### XStudio Mental Model — One Page

#### What XStudio is

XStudio is a **low-code MES/ERP platform** where configuration stored in SQL Server metadata drives the application UI, runtime behavior, and generated SQL objects.

Older notes often say “configuration is created by inserting rows into config tables.” That is only partly true. The corrected rule is:

```text
Use XStudio stored procedures wherever a suitable XStudio SP exists.
If no suitable SP exists, preserve the config-table operation as a documented no-SP exception with live schema inspection, guards, real user audit values, and postflight checks.
```

Configuration is still the application, but the preferred write path is the platform SP surface.

#### The 4-database layout

```text
XStudio_Configuration              — Shared: users, roles, data types, global params, shared SPs
XStudio_Configuration_[System]     — System config: entities, pages, menus, LVs, edits
XStudio_[System]                   — Operational data: the actual business tables and generated LV views
XStudio_[System]_Audit             — Change history when IsAuditLog=1
```

For XMES:

```text
XStudio_Configuration_XMES
XStudio_XMES
XStudio_XMES_Audit, where audit is enabled
```

Always resolve the live configuration/data DBs from `SystemID` before writing SQL.

#### The object hierarchy

```text
System
└── Application (DEFAULT)
    ├── Entity → creates/owns a real SQL table in XStudio_[System]
    │   ├── Attribute → creates/owns a physical SQL column
    │   │   └── AttributeProperties → required for dropdown and other advanced behavior
    │   └── EntityRelation → defines dropdown/FK-style relation metadata
    ├── LV (List View / Grid)
    │   └── LvColumns → each column in the grid
    ├── Edit (Form)
    │   └── EditControls → each field in the form
    ├── Page
    │   └── PageControls → places LVs, Edits, tabs, inline grids, HTML controls, etc. onto a Page
    ├── Menu → links to a Page
    │   └── MenuRights → role visibility
    ├── Workflow → statuses, actors/transitions, action assignments
    ├── HTML controls / API / DataSource / SP-backed surfaces
    └── Schedule / Summary / Task runtime objects
```

#### How configuration becomes a UI screen

1. Engineer creates configuration using XStudio SPs where available.
2. For no-SP surfaces, engineer uses guarded config-table writes after live schema inspection.
3. XStudio reads the config and renders a Kendo-based SPA in the browser.
4. When a user saves data, XStudio inserts/updates the physical data table.
5. LV grids usually read from generated SQL views in the data DB:
   ```text
   XStudio_[System].dbo.XStudio_[LVName]_Vw
   ```

#### Complete module rule

A usable XStudio module is not only a physical SQL table. A production-ready module normally includes:

```text
Entity
-> attributes
-> dropdown AttributeProperties, where needed
-> EntityRelations, where needed
-> LV and LV columns
-> regenerated LV view
-> edit and edit controls
-> pages and PageControls
-> menu and MenuRights
-> optional workflow/action hooks
-> postflight checks
```

#### Correct write-path model

| Area | Preferred write path |
|---|---|
| Entity create/update | `XStudio_SaveEntity_Usp` |
| Attribute add | `XStudio_AddAttribute_Usp` |
| Dropdown properties + relation | `XStudio_Add_DropDown_Attribute_Properties_And_Relation_Usp` first, then verify/complete six-property contract through the approved template |
| PageControl binding | `XStudio_Save_Page_Control_Configuration_Usp` |
| Menu | `XStudio_Save_Menu_Configuration_Usp` |
| MenuRights | `XStudio_Save_Menu_Rights_Usp` |
| Menu sequence | `XStudio_Save_Menu_Sequence_Usp` |
| Tabs | `XStudio_Save_Tab_Configuration_Usp` and `XStudio_Save_Tab_Control_Configuration_Usp` |
| HTML controls | `XStudio_Save_HTMLControl_Usp` and `XStudio_Save_HTMLControl_DataSource_Usp` |
| LV generated view | `XStudio_CreateViewForLV_Usp` |
| Edit controls | `Xstudio_Insert_Edit_Columns_Info_Usp`, followed by safe JSON completion |
| Edit logic/validation | `XStudio_Save_Edit_Logic_And_Validation_USP` |

#### Known no-SP exceptions that must not be erased

The following knowledge is still operationally important. Keep it, but handle it safely.

| Area | Why it remains a no-SP exception |
|---|---|
| Brand-new page header | No generic page-create SP is confirmed; PageControls/Menu/Rights do have SPs |
| Brand-new LV header | Copy/duplicate SPs exist, but no generic save-LV-header SP is confirmed |
| Normal LV columns | No generic save-LV-column SP is confirmed |
| LV `FilterCondition` | No generic LV-header save SP is confirmed |
| Brand-new edit header | Copy/duplicate SPs exist, but no generic create-edit-header SP is confirmed |
| Edit save/cancel/save-and-continue navigation | No generic edit-navigation save SP is confirmed |
| Edit-control JSON completion | Required because the edit-control generation SP may not produce full renderer-safe JSON |

For these exceptions, use:

```text
sys.columns inspection
NOT EXISTS / exact target guard
transaction for grouped changes
real @UserID, never 'sa'
postflight checks
```

#### The most important rule

```text
Attribute.Name = physical SQL column name
```

Changing it can rename the physical column and break stored procedures, views, and application code. Use `DisplayName` to change UI labels.

The same principle applies to entity names: changing `XStudio_Entities_Mst_Tbl.Name` can rename the physical table.

#### Dropdown rule

A dropdown attribute is not complete until it has:

```text
base attribute
physical column
six active dropdown property rows
one active EntityRelation row
renderer-safe edit-control JSON if shown on an edit form
correct LV relation/view handling if shown in a grid
```

Required properties:

```text
DropdownEntity
DropdownDatabase
DropdownTextField
DropdownValueField
DropdownEntryMode
TextCharacterLength
```

#### LV rule

After adding or changing LV columns, regenerate and verify the view through:

```text
XStudio_CreateViewForLV_Usp
```

Pass explicit non-null:

```text
@ColumnQuery
@RelationQuery
@GroupByQuery
@WhereCondition
@IsCalculatedView
```

Do not rely on older shortened or null-argument examples unless the live SP text proves that local build supports them.

#### Edit-form rule

Active edit controls must have renderer-safe `ControlProperties` JSON. Dropdown controls must align both physical dropdown columns, where present, and JSON keys. Use:

```text
Xstudio_Insert_Edit_Columns_Info_Usp
edit_control_safe_json_template.sql
```

#### XStudio-native MES architecture

For XMES-like products, split responsibilities deliberately:

| Layer | Owns |
|---|---|
| Asset Structure | Equipment hierarchy, equipment types/templates/instances, tag mapping, historian current/block data, event state detection |
| XMES transaction tables | Orders, campaigns, work orders, production, consumption, quality, inventory, genealogy, dispatch, SAP posting truth |
| XMES stored procedures | Domain rules, lot transformations, reconciliation, posting chains, workflow side effects |
| XStudio workflow | User-visible lifecycle, transitions, actor/role gating |
| Summary/views/LVs/pages | Aggregated KPIs, operational screens, Sankey, Gantt, SPC, OEE/TEEP, digital passports |
| Postflight checks | Proof that metadata, physical objects, generated views, UI bindings, and SPs line up |

#### Retrieval pattern for agents

```text
User task
  -> task_router.md or task_router.json
  -> load 01_safety_rules.md
  -> load the relevant playbook from 02_TASK_PLAYBOOKS/
  -> load SQL template from 03_SQL_TEMPLATES/ if writing SQL
  -> load a working example from 06_EXAMPLES/ if uncertain
  -> run postflight_checks.sql before declaring done
```

#### Model loading guide

| Model | Load | Use for |
|---|---|---|
| Haiku | mental_model.md + task_router.json + one small playbook | Classification, validation, checklists |
| Sonnet | safety_rules.md + playbook + SQL template + one example | Module generation, SQL drafts, debugging |
| Opus | mental_model.md + safety_rules.md + canonical refs + domain docs | Architecture, migration, hard debugging |
| GPT-5.5 | Opus/Sonnet hybrid — playbooks first, references second | Planning + execution |

## 2. Safety Rules

**Source file:** `00_START_HERE/01_safety_rules.md`  
**File type:** `.md`  
**Size:** `11631` bytes  
**Modified on:** `2026-05-12 15:04:40`  
**SHA256:** `709b971fcb4c`  
**Encoding used:** `utf-8`

> Start of imported document.

### Safety Rules — Read Before Writing SQL or XStudio Configuration

These rules prevent irreversible damage to XMES/XStudio systems.

The central rule is:

```text
Use XStudio stored procedures wherever a suitable SP exists.
Where no suitable SP exists, preserve the config-table operation as a documented no-SP exception with live schema inspection, guards, real user audit values, transactions where appropriate, and postflight checks.
```

Do not delete operational knowledge just because it uses table writes. Convert it to the correct write path.

---

#### RULE 1 — Never casually change `XStudio_Attribute_Mst_Tbl.Name`

`Name` is the physical SQL column name.

Changing `Name` can cause XStudio to rename the physical column in the data table. This can break stored procedures, views, generated SQL, LVs, edits, and domain code.

**Incident:** Setting `Name = 'Work Order'` with a space renamed the physical column from `WorkOrderID` to `Work Order`, breaking stored procedures that referenced `WorkOrderID`. Reverting triggered a second rename back.

Safe:

```text
Update DisplayName to change UI labels.
```

Dangerous:

```text
Update Name on existing attributes.
```

Preferred creation path:

```text
XStudio_AddAttribute_Usp
```

---

#### RULE 2 — Never casually change `XStudio_Entities_Mst_Tbl.Name`

Changing entity `Name` can rename the physical table. This can break every SP, view, LV, edit, workflow action, integration, and report that references it.

Safe:

```text
Change display labels or menu/page labels where appropriate.
```

Dangerous:

```text
Update entity Name on existing entities.
```

Preferred creation/update path:

```text
XStudio_SaveEntity_Usp
```

---

#### RULE 3 — Use SPs for entity and attribute creation

Do not directly insert into:

```text
XStudio_Entities_Mst_Tbl
XStudio_Attribute_Mst_Tbl
```

when creating normal entities/attributes.

Use:

```text
XStudio_SaveEntity_Usp
XStudio_AddAttribute_Usp
```

These SPs are responsible for keeping metadata and physical table/column state aligned.

Do not manually `CREATE TABLE` or `ALTER TABLE` for normal entity/attribute creation unless a specific no-SP exception has been documented and approved.

---

#### RULE 4 — Always create and verify all 6 dropdown property rows

A `Dropdown` attribute requires exactly one active row for each property:

| Property | Why required |
|---|---|
| `DropdownEntity` | Target entity for lookup |
| `DropdownDatabase` | Source database for lookup |
| `DropdownTextField` | Display expression/field |
| `DropdownValueField` | Stored value field |
| `DropdownEntryMode` | Single/multi behavior |
| `TextCharacterLength` | Usually `36` for GUID FK |

Also required:

```text
one active XStudio_EntityRelations_Mst_Tbl row per dropdown attribute
```

Use:

```text
XStudio_Add_DropDown_Attribute_Properties_And_Relation_Usp
```

but do not trust it blindly. Always verify the full six-property contract afterward. If the SP leaves the contract incomplete, complete it only through the approved dropdown template fallback.

---

#### RULE 5 — Always use renderer-safe `ControlProperties` JSON

The edit-form renderer can call string methods such as `.toLowerCase()` on JSON values. Missing keys, SQL NULL, blank strings, or invalid JSON can crash the UI.

Never leave active edit controls with:

```text
ControlProperties = NULL
ControlProperties = ''
ControlProperties = '{}'
invalid JSON
```

Use:

```text
Xstudio_Insert_Edit_Columns_Info_Usp
edit_control_safe_json_template.sql
```

For dropdown controls, align both physical dropdown columns where present and JSON fields:

```text
DropdownType
DropDownEntityID
DropdownTextField
DropdownValueField
DropdownDatabase

ControlProperties.DropdownType
ControlProperties.DropdownDatabase
ControlProperties.DropdownEntityID
ControlProperties.DropdownTextField
ControlProperties.DropdownValueField
ControlProperties.DropdownEntryMode
```

---

#### RULE 6 — `ControlType` must match datatype

Never default every edit control to `textbox`.

| Attribute datatype | Expected ControlType |
|---|---|
| Text | `textbox` |
| Number | `number` |
| Decimal | `number` |
| Bool | `checkbox` |
| DateTime | `datetimepicker` |
| Date | `datepicker` or platform date control |
| Dropdown | `selectbox` |

Dropdown attributes using `textbox` will not show options correctly and may fail to save a GUID FK value.

---

#### RULE 7 — Always verify/regenerate the generated LV view after LV changes

LV grids commonly read from:

```text
XStudio_[System].dbo.XStudio_[LVName]_Vw
```

After adding or modifying LV columns, regenerate the view with the live signature of:

```text
XStudio_CreateViewForLV_Usp
```

Confirmed shared-config shape:

```sql
EXEC XStudio_Configuration.dbo.XStudio_CreateViewForLV_Usp
    @LvID             = '<LV_GUID>',
    @LvName           = '<LV_NAME>',
    @DataDatabase     = 'XStudio_XMES',
    @ColumnQuery      = '<SELECT ...>',
    @RelationQuery    = '<FROM/JOIN ...>',
    @GroupByQuery     = '',
    @WhereCondition   = '<WHERE ...>',
    @IsCalculatedView = 0;
```

Do not pass `NULL` query fragments unless the live SP definition proves that this local build handles them safely.

Always verify:

```sql
SELECT TOP 1 *
FROM XStudio_XMES.dbo.XStudio_<LVName>_Vw;
```

---

#### RULE 8 — Custom LV action columns are links, not physical fields

Edit/Delete/Open/Drilldown columns must be custom link/action columns, not physical data columns.

| Field | Required pattern |
|---|---|
| `Type` | `link` |
| `IsCustomField` | `1` |
| `AttributeID` | null/blank |
| `RelationID` | null/blank |
| `RelationIDList` | null/blank |
| `LinkType` | `fixedtext` or platform-specific action type |
| `LinkValue` | visible label such as `Open`, `Edit`, `Delete` |
| `CustomLink` / `ColumnCustomLink` | page ID or URL/token pattern |
| `CustomLinkTargetMode` | `Inside` / `Popup`, matching live examples |
| `ColumnProperties` | valid JSON |

If an action column is bound to a deleted/missing attribute, the generated view can try to select a non-existent physical column.

Use:

```text
lv_link_column_template.sql
```

where possible.

---

#### RULE 9 — Use PageControl/Menu/MenuRights SPs

Do not directly insert normal PageControl, Menu, or MenuRights rows when the SPs exist.

Use:

| Action | Required SP |
|---|---|
| Bind a control to a page | `XStudio_Save_Page_Control_Configuration_Usp` |
| Delete a PageControl | `XStudio_Delete_Page_Control_Configuration_Usp` |
| Create/update a menu item | `XStudio_Save_Menu_Configuration_Usp` |
| Add menu rights | `XStudio_Save_Menu_Rights_Usp` |
| Delete menu rights | `XStudio_Delete_Menu_Rights_Usp` |
| Save menu sequence | `XStudio_Save_Menu_Sequence_Usp` |

Known no-SP exception:

```text
Brand-new Page header creation may require guarded direct insert if no generic page-create SP exists.
```

Do not confuse:

```text
Page = container/header
PageControls = actual Page -> Control bindings
Menu = navigation row pointing to a Page
MenuRights = role visibility
```

---

#### RULE 10 — Tabs are optional, not default

Do not force tab containers into every page.

Use tab SPs only when the design actually requires a tab container:

```text
XStudio_Save_Tab_Configuration_Usp
XStudio_Save_Tab_Control_Configuration_Usp
```

A generic page may bind an LV, edit form, inline grid, HTML control, dashboard, or other control directly through PageControls without tabs.

---

#### RULE 11 — Always check live schema before no-SP table writes

Before any documented no-SP exception, inspect the live schema.

Example:

```sql
SELECT
    COLUMN_NAME,
    DATA_TYPE,
    CHARACTER_MAXIMUM_LENGTH
FROM XStudio_Configuration_XMES.INFORMATION_SCHEMA.COLUMNS
WHERE TABLE_NAME = 'XStudio_LvColumns_Mst_Tbl'
ORDER BY ORDINAL_POSITION;
```

Known confirmed examples:

```text
XStudio_LvColumns_Mst_Tbl: SerialNumber, not SrNo
XStudio_LvColumns_Mst_Tbl: Type, not DataType
XStudio_Attribute_Mst_Tbl: Name = physical column, DisplayName = label
XStudio_LV_Mst_Tbl: FilterCondition, SPName
```

---

#### RULE 12 — Never blindly insert; always guard with stable keys

Duplicate config rows cause duplicate menus, duplicate grid columns, duplicate edit controls, duplicate rights, and confusing UI behavior.

Use guards such as:

```sql
IF NOT EXISTS
(
    SELECT 1
    FROM XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_LV_Mst_Tbl
    WHERE Name = @LVName
      AND ParentID = @EntityID
      AND IsDeleted = 0
)
BEGIN
    -- guarded no-SP exception insert
END;
```

For SP calls, still guard where duplicate calls can create duplicates.

---

#### RULE 13 — Use a real deployment user, never `sa`

Do not write:

```text
CreatedBy = 'sa'
ModifiedBy = 'sa'
```

Use a real XStudio user GUID:

```text
@UserID = '<deployment_user_guid>'
```

Use `@IPAddress` / `HostAddress` where supported.

---

#### RULE 14 — Workflow side effects are not actor rows by default

Do not place SP side-effect assumptions into `XStudio_Approval_Actor_Mst_Tbl` unless the live schema proves that field exists and is used in the current system.

Correct model:

```text
workflow header/status/actor/transition metadata
+
workflow action and action-assignment metadata
+
data DB stored procedure with validated signature
```

Status lifecycle and business side effects are related, but they are not the same layer.

Review the workflow/action SPs before replacing workflow table writes. Preserve workflow field knowledge when no SP exists.

---

#### RULE 15 — No physical SQL Server FKs in XMES generated packs

Use GUID columns plus XStudio `EntityRelations` metadata and dropdown properties. Physical SQL Server FK constraints create deployment-order and generator conflicts in XStudio systems.

---

#### RULE 16 — Use SQLCMD variables and live preflight

Deployment scripts should use SQLCMD variables for DB names, include DB/table/column preflight, run idempotently, use transactions for grouped changes, and include postflight checks.

Hardcoded DB names are allowed only in variable blocks or diagnostics.

---

#### Quick reference: operations and write paths

| Operation | Correct classification |
|---|---|
| Update attribute `DisplayName` | Usually safe label change; still guard exact row |
| Update attribute `Name` | Dangerous; renames physical column |
| Create entity | Use `XStudio_SaveEntity_Usp` |
| Add attribute | Use `XStudio_AddAttribute_Usp` |
| Insert dropdown properties | Use dropdown SP/template first; direct fallback only inside approved template |
| Insert entity relation for dropdown | Use dropdown SP/template first; direct fallback only inside approved template |
| Update edit-control `ControlType` / `ControlProperties` | Use edit-control SP + safe JSON template; direct fallback only where no complete SP exists |
| Create LV header | No confirmed save SP; guarded no-SP exception |
| Insert normal LV columns | No confirmed save SP; guarded no-SP exception |
| Update LV `FilterCondition` | No confirmed save SP; guarded no-SP exception |
| Regenerate LV view | Use `XStudio_CreateViewForLV_Usp` |
| Create PageControl | Use `XStudio_Save_Page_Control_Configuration_Usp` |
| Create menu | Use `XStudio_Save_Menu_Configuration_Usp` |
| Add menu rights | Use `XStudio_Save_Menu_Rights_Usp` |
| Update entity `Name` | Dangerous; renames physical table |
| Direct `ALTER TABLE` on XStudio data tables | Dangerous unless a documented no-SP exception and impact check exists |
| Physical SQL FK constraints in XMES packs | Dangerous; conflicts with generator/runtime flexibility |
| Custom LV link column with nonblank `AttributeID` | Dangerous; can generate invalid physical-column SQL |

> End of imported document.

## 3. Task Router

### Task Router

Use this to identify which playbook and template to load for a given task.

Always load:

```text
00_mental_model.md
01_safety_rules.md
```

Then load the relevant playbook/template below.

#### Decision tree

```text
What is the user trying to do?

Explore the DB / understand what already exists / look up GUIDs
  -> explore_database.md
  -> run this FIRST before any config task

Create a true XStudio system / product database set
  -> database_architecture.md
  -> system bootstrap runbook/script

Create a new module: entity + LV + edit + page + menu
  -> create_entity_module.md
  -> entity_create_template.sql
  -> dropdown_properties_template.sql, if dropdowns exist
  -> create_lv_grid.md / lv_grid_template.sql when available
  -> create_edit_form.md / edit_form_template.sql when available
  -> page_menu_template.sql
  -> postflight_checks.sql

Add a dropdown/FK attribute to an existing entity
  -> create_dropdown_attribute.md
  -> entity_create_template.sql or dedicated XStudio_AddAttribute_Usp call
  -> dropdown_properties_template.sql
  -> edit_control_safe_json_template.sql, if field appears in edit form

Create or configure a list view/grid
  Normal grid columns
    -> create_lv_grid.md
    -> XStudio_CreateViewForLV_Usp
  Navigation/action link column
    -> create_lv_link_column.md
    -> lv_link_column_template.sql

Create or fix an edit form
  -> create_edit_form.md
  -> edit_control_safe_json_template.sql
  -> configure_save_and_continue.md if navigation is involved

Add a page, bind controls, create menu, add rights
  -> create_page_bind_controls_menu_playbook.md
  -> page_menu_template.sql

Configure workflow / approval transitions
  -> create_workflow_action.md
  -> workflow/approval SP references
  -> preserve field knowledge where no SP exists

Build parent -> child drill-down navigation
  -> create_parent_child_flow.md
  -> create_lv_link_column.md
  -> configure_save_and_continue.md
  -> page_menu_template.sql

Add an inline grid inside a parent edit/page
  -> create_inline_grid.md
  -> page_menu_template.sql for PageControl binding

Configure Save/Cancel/Save & Continue navigation
  -> configure_save_and_continue.md

Set up WO execution: Production / Consumption / Events
  -> configure_wo_execution.md
  -> XMES domain files
  -> relevant SP references

Design an integrated steel MES product / digital thread
  -> xmes_domain_model.md
  -> asset_structure.md
  -> work_order_execution.md

Design genealogy, Sankey, passport, OEE/TEEP, SPC, Gantt
  -> xmes_domain_model.md
  -> plan_vs_actual.md
  -> relevant report/dashboard archetype

Debug a crash or rendering failure
  Renderer crash: toLowerCase null, BindControls.js
    -> renderer_crashes.md
    -> edit_control_safe_json_template.sql
  Dropdown not showing options
    -> dropdown_failures.md
    -> dropdown_properties_template.sql
  LV grid not showing data or wrong columns
    -> lv_view_failures.md
    -> create_lv_grid.md
  Link column not navigating
    -> broken_link_columns.md
    -> lv_link_column_template.sql
  Object exists in config but not visible in UI
    -> orphaned_objects.md
    -> postflight_checks.sql

Configure DOW / Digital Operator Workbench / Logsheets
  -> dow_logsheet_feature.md

Configure DownTime monitoring
  -> downtime_feature.md

Look up which SP to use for a platform operation
  -> sp_catalog_complete.md

Architecture / migration / XNEO design
  -> mental_model.md
  -> safety_rules.md
  -> canonical references
  -> domain docs
```

#### Quick lookup: task -> objects touched and write path

| Task | Primary objects | Write path |
|---|---|---|
| Explore existing config | `INFORMATION_SCHEMA.COLUMNS`, all `*_Mst_Tbl` | Read only |
| Create entity | `XStudio_Entities_Mst_Tbl`, physical data table | `XStudio_SaveEntity_Usp` |
| Add attribute | `XStudio_Attribute_Mst_Tbl`, physical data column | `XStudio_AddAttribute_Usp` |
| Add dropdown attribute | Attribute + six AttributeProperties + EntityRelation | `XStudio_AddAttribute_Usp`, then dropdown SP/template |
| Create LV | `XStudio_LV_Mst_Tbl`, `XStudio_LvColumns_Mst_Tbl`, generated view | LV header/columns are no-SP exceptions; view uses `XStudio_CreateViewForLV_Usp` |
| Create LV link column | `XStudio_LvColumns_Mst_Tbl`, generated view | no confirmed save-column SP; use `lv_link_column_template.sql`, then `XStudio_CreateViewForLV_Usp` |
| Create edit form | `XStudio_Edit_Mst_Tbl`, `XStudio_EditControls_Mst_Tbl` | edit header is no-SP exception; controls use `Xstudio_Insert_Edit_Columns_Info_Usp` + safe JSON template |
| Configure edit navigation | `XStudio_Edit_Mst_Tbl` navigation fields | no confirmed generic SP; guarded no-SP exception |
| Create page header | `XStudio_Page_Mst_Tbl` | no confirmed generic page-create SP; guarded no-SP exception |
| Bind control to page | `XStudio_PageControls_Mst_Tbl` | `XStudio_Save_Page_Control_Configuration_Usp` |
| Create menu | `XStudio_Menu_Mst_Tbl` | `XStudio_Save_Menu_Configuration_Usp` |
| Add menu rights | `XStudio_MenuRights_Mst_Tbl` | `XStudio_Save_Menu_Rights_Usp` |
| Save menu order | menu sequence | `XStudio_Save_Menu_Sequence_Usp` |
| Configure tabs | tab metadata and tab controls | `XStudio_Save_Tab_Configuration_Usp`, `XStudio_Save_Tab_Control_Configuration_Usp` |
| Configure HTML control | HTML control and datasource | `XStudio_Save_HTMLControl_Usp`, `XStudio_Save_HTMLControl_DataSource_Usp` |
| Configure workflow | approval/workflow/action objects | Use confirmed workflow SPs where available; preserve no-SP metadata operations |
| Configure WO execution | config + XMES data DB SPs | Domain SP-backed where available; preserve XMES runtime field knowledge |
| Configure DOW logsheet | `Logsheet_Mst_Tbl`, version, sections, items in data DB | DOW SPs/templates where available |
| Configure DownTime | downtime config/tag/condition tables in data DB | DownTime SPs/templates where available |
| Asset Structure: equipment type | `Equipment_Type_Mst_Tbl` and generated child tables | Asset Structure playbooks/triggers |
| Asset Structure: template | `<EquipmentType>_Attribute_Tag_Template_Mst_Tbl` | Asset Structure playbooks/triggers |
| Asset Structure: tag mapping | `<EquipmentType>_Tag_Mapping_Tbl` | Asset Structure playbooks/triggers |

#### Core routing

| User task | Load first | Then load |
|---|---|---|
| Understand XStudio platform | `00_mental_model.md` | `database_architecture.md`, `object_hierarchy.md` |
| Create entity/module | `create_entity_module.md` | `01_safety_rules.md`, SQL templates, examples |
| Create dropdown/FK field | `create_dropdown_attribute.md` | `dropdown_failures.md`, `dropdown_properties_template.sql` |
| Create LV/grid | `create_lv_grid.md` | `lv_view_failures.md`, LV templates |
| Create LV link/action column | `create_lv_link_column.md` | `lv_link_column_template.sql`, `broken_link_columns.md` |
| Create edit form | `create_edit_form.md` | `renderer_crashes.md`, `edit_control_safe_json_template.sql` |
| Configure save/cancel/save-and-continue | `configure_save_and_continue.md` | `create_edit_form.md`, page/menu docs |
| Create page/menu | `create_page_bind_controls_menu_playbook.md` | `page_menu_template.sql` |
| Create workflow action | `create_workflow_action.md` | approval/workflow SP references |
| Configure WO execution | `configure_wo_execution.md` | XMES domain files |

#### Asset Structure routing

| User task | Load first | Then load |
|---|---|---|
| Understand Asset Structure | `asset_structure.md` | `asset_structure_deep_dive.md` |
| Create equipment type | `create_asset_structure_equipment_type.md` | `asset_structure_triggers_and_mapping.md`, postflight SQL |
| Create template | `create_asset_structure_template.md` | `asset_structure_triggers_and_mapping.md` |
| Create equipment instances | `create_asset_structure_equipment_instances.md` | `asset_structure_runtime_views.md` |
| Map historian tags | `map_asset_structure_tags.md` | `asset_structure_triggers_and_mapping.md`, `asset_structure_api_surface.md` |
| Configure events | `configure_asset_structure_events.md` | `asset_structure_deep_dive.md` |
| Debug generation failure | `asset_structure_generation_failures.md` | SP runtime surface, postflight SQL |
| Debug tag mapping | `asset_structure_tag_mapping_failures.md` | runtime views, tag mapping playbook |
| Debug block/historian capture | `asset_structure_block_historian_failures.md` | historian blocks reference |
| Model LMEL assets | `lmel_asset_structure_model.md` | EAF example, equipment type/template playbooks |

#### Critical Asset Structure rule

Do not design plant equipment as a disconnected custom equipment master. XStudio Asset Structure should be the equipment backbone. MES transaction tables should reference generated Asset Structure equipment instances or consolidated equipment views.

#### Product build sequence for XMES-like systems

Route product creation in this order:

```text
00 true XStudio system/bootstrap
01 helper/log/registry objects
02 Asset Structure foundation and equipment backbone
03 master data
04 planning/campaign/BOM
05 core execution
06 plant-specific execution
07 quality
08 inventory/genealogy
09 workflow/status/action metadata
10 business SPs
11 metadata repair/relations/dropdowns
12 seed data
13 views/analytics
14 pages/LVs/edits/menus/rights
15 postflight validation
```

Stop if an upstream layer is missing, live schema does not match assumptions, a required SP/template is missing, or postflight fails.

#### SQL connection note

Use project/user-provided connection details only in local execution contexts. Do not embed production credentials in reusable KB files.

When using `sqlcmd`, remember that shell quoting matters. Passwords containing `$` must be quoted safely for the shell in use.

Known XMES IDs:

```text
System ID: 6C8C0070-60B5-4474-BEC8-5C02197B32E6
Application ID (DEFAULT): 29123B0B-3872-41CF-B5E1-67C4A8F0D4BC
```

## 4. Asset Structure

**Source file:** `01_CANONICAL_REFERENCE/asset_structure.md`  
**File type:** `.md`  
**Size:** `42323` bytes  
**Modified on:** `2026-05-04 18:02:54`  
**SHA256:** `fa6403a09de5`  
**Encoding used:** `utf-8`

> Start of imported document.

### XStudio Asset Structure — Agent KB

#### Purpose

This document is the canonical agent-facing knowledge base for XStudio Asset Structure.

It explains how agents must reason about hierarchy, equipment types, generated equipment modules, templates, tag mapping, block/historian capture, events, consolidated views, and LMEL MES integration.

Asset Structure is the platform feature to use for LMEL plant asset modeling. MES transaction entities should reference Asset Structure equipment instances instead of creating an isolated custom equipment master.

Batch table creation is intentionally out of scope in this document. Do not generate batch-table design, batch-table scripts, or batch-specific recommendations from this KB.

---

#### 1. Scope

##### In scope

Agents may use this document for:

```text
Asset hierarchy understanding
Equipment type lifecycle
Generated table family understanding
Template and template-detail behavior
Equipment instance behavior
Tag mapping behavior
Consolidated runtime views
Block/historian runtime behavior
Event schema behavior
LMEL equipment modeling rules
MES transaction reference patterns
Postflight checks
Stored procedure dependency reasoning
```

##### Out of scope

Agents must not use this document to generate:

```text
XStudio_Create_Batch_Table_USP analysis
Batch table scripts
Batch table schema
Batch-specific LMEL recommendations
Custom duplicate equipment master as the primary source of assets
Manual CREATE TABLE scripts for generated equipment-type tables in production
```

---

#### 2. Mandatory Agent Rules

```text
1. Treat Asset Structure as the source of truth for plant assets.
2. Do not create a standalone XMES_Equipment_Mst_Tbl as the primary equipment master.
3. Do not manually create <EquipmentType> generated tables in production.
4. Equipment_Type_Mst_Tbl insert/update is structural and fires generator logic.
5. Equipment type Name becomes the physical table prefix; use simple PascalCase nouns.
6. Frequency/block choice is structural and should be fixed at creation.
7. Define hierarchy before equipment types.
8. Create templates before equipment instances where possible.
9. Equipment instances select TemplateID and AreaID.
10. Equipment instance creation auto-creates mapping rows.
11. Template detail changes propagate ranges/colors/documents into mapping rows.
12. Tag mapping is per equipment instance + attribute + datasource.
13. Use generated spelling exactly: EquipmetTypeID.
14. Event schema is optional and controlled by IsEventCheck.
15. Do not enable event schema for passive assets unless required.
16. Never delete an equipment type with live production or historian data.
17. MES transactions should reference Asset Structure equipment instances.
18. Batch table behavior is out of scope.
19. Do not invent Asset Structure API paths; use known Swagger paths exactly, including misspellings.
20. Do not assume generated views use corrected column names.
```

---

#### 3. Working Mental Model

```text
Asset_Hierarchy_Mst_Tbl
  defines ordered hierarchy levels

Area_Mst_Tbl
  anchors plant/area placement

Equipment_Type_Mst_Tbl
  defines equipment classes and generator flags

<EquipmentType>_Mst_Tbl
  stores physical equipment instances

<EquipmentType>_Attribute_Tag_Template_Mst_Tbl
  stores reusable template headers

<EquipmentType>_Attribute_Tag_Template_Dtl_Tbl
  stores template attribute/tag definitions, limits, colors, documents, formulas

<EquipmentType>_Tag_Mapping_Tbl
  maps equipment instance + attribute to datasource/tag

<EquipmentType>_Data
  stores or exposes current equipment data

<EquipmentType>_<Frequency> / <EquipmentType>_Block
  stores interval/block historian rows

<EquipmentType>_Event_*
  stores event detection, state, action, and error configuration

XStudio_Equipment_Mst_Vw
XStudio_Equipment_Template_Mst_Vw
XStudio_Equipment_Tag_Mst_Vw
  provide cross-equipment runtime selectors
```

For LMEL MES:

```text
Asset Structure = physical plant and historian backbone
XMES transactions = production, quality, inventory, genealogy, SAP execution records
```

The two layers must be linked, not duplicated.

---

#### 4. Asset Structure Object Model

Asset Structure is XStudio's built-in generator for plant equipment modeling. It is not a normal master table and not only a hierarchy screen.

It creates a generated module family for every equipment class/type created through the Asset Structure equipment type flow.

```text
Hierarchy
→ Equipment Type
→ Generated Equipment Type Tables
→ Template
→ Template Detail Attributes
→ Equipment Instance
→ Tag Mapping
→ Current Data / Block Data
→ Event State and Event Tag Mapping
```

The key point is that saving an equipment type is structural. It creates physical SQL tables, XStudio metadata, triggers, views, block/historian structures, and optionally event structures.

| Layer | Object | Purpose |
|---|---|---|
| Hierarchy | `Asset_Hierarchy_Mst_Tbl` | Ordered hierarchy level definitions |
| Area anchor | `Area_Mst_Tbl` | Plant/area grouping used by equipment instances |
| Equipment type | `Equipment_Type_Mst_Tbl` | One row per equipment class/type |
| Instance table | `<EquipmentType>_Mst_Tbl` | Physical equipment instances |
| Template header | `<EquipmentType>_Attribute_Tag_Template_Mst_Tbl` | Reusable template names/configurations |
| Template detail | `<EquipmentType>_Attribute_Tag_Template_Dtl_Tbl` | Attribute/tag definitions, ranges, colors, documents, formulas, retrieval behavior |
| Tag mapping | `<EquipmentType>_Tag_Mapping_Tbl` | Equipment instance + attribute + datasource/tag mapping |
| Current data | `<EquipmentType>_Data` | Auto-refresh current equipment data entity |
| Block data | `<EquipmentType>_<Frequency>` or `<EquipmentType>_Block` | Historian interval/block rows |
| Event schema | `<EquipmentType>_Event_*` | Event states/actions/errors if event check is enabled |
| Consolidated views | `XStudio_Equipment_*_Vw` | Cross-equipment selectors and browsers |

---

#### 5. Lifecycle Dependency Order

Agents must use this order when generating plans, prompts, scripts, validation, or runbooks:

```text
1. Bootstrap / verify Asset Structure base entities
2. Define hierarchy levels
3. Materialize hierarchy entities/configuration
4. Create equipment type
5. Let generator create generated table family
6. Verify generated entities, tables, triggers, views
7. Create template header
8. Let template master trigger create template detail rows
9. Add/adjust template detail rows
10. Create equipment instances
11. Let equipment instance trigger create mapping rows
12. Map datasource/tag names
13. Configure event schema if IsEventCheck = 1
14. Configure block/historian validation
15. Reference equipment instances from MES transactions
```

Do not reverse this order unless debugging a specific existing object.

---

#### 6. Hierarchy Layer

The hierarchy layer defines how plant assets are organized.

Recommended logical hierarchy for LMEL:

```text
Enterprise
→ Site
→ Plant / Area
→ Shop / Process Line
→ Work Center
→ Equipment
→ Sub-equipment / Instrumented Unit
```

`Asset_Hierarchy_Mst_Tbl` stores hierarchy level definitions ordered by `SerialNumber`.

Typical read pattern:

```sql
SELECT ID, Name, SerialNumber
FROM XStudio_XMES.dbo.Asset_Hierarchy_Mst_Tbl
WHERE IsDeleted = 0
ORDER BY SerialNumber ASC;
```

Important hierarchy procedures:

| Stored Procedure | Purpose |
|---|---|
| `XStudio_AssetStructure_Create_Base_Entities_Usp` | Creates base Asset Structure entities during system setup |
| `XStudio_AssetStructure_Get_Hierarchy_Usp` | Reads hierarchy levels and ensures base hierarchy entity exists |
| `XStudio_AssetStructure_Create_Hierarchy_Entity_Usp` | Materializes hierarchy configuration into usable entities/configuration |
| `XStudio_AssetStructure_Update_Hierarchy_Menu_Usp` | Updates navigation menus after hierarchy changes |
| `XStudio_Save_Hierarchy_Configuration_Usp` | Saves hierarchy configuration |
| `XStudio_Delete_Hierarchy_Usp` | Deletes hierarchy configuration |

Important API endpoints:

```text
GET  /api/Rest/{systemId}/AssetStructure/Hierarchy
POST /api/Rest/{systemId}/AssetStructure/Hierarchy/Save
```

Operational rule:

```text
Define hierarchy before creating equipment types and instances.
```

Reason: equipment instances are placed into plant/area/hierarchy context through generated attributes such as `AreaID` and hierarchy-linked objects.

---

#### 7. Equipment Type Layer

`Equipment_Type_Mst_Tbl` stores one row per equipment class, not one row per physical instance.

Examples:

```text
EAF
LRF
VD
CCM
CCMStrand
BlastFurnace
DRIKiln
CokeOvenBattery
RollingMillStand
Boiler
Turbine
Generator
```

The equipment type name becomes the physical table prefix for the generated table family.

Example:

```text
Equipment Type: EAF
Generated tables:
  EAF_Mst_Tbl
  EAF_Tag_Mapping_Tbl
  EAF_Attribute_Tag_Template_Mst_Tbl
  EAF_Attribute_Tag_Template_Dtl_Tbl
  EAF_Data
  EAF_5               -- if frequency = 5
  EAF_Event_*         -- if IsEventCheck = 1
```

##### Equipment Type Form Fields

| UI field | Backend meaning |
|---|---|
| Equipment Type | Equipment class name; becomes generated table prefix |
| Block Type | Determines block table behavior |
| Summary Hierarchy | Links equipment type to summary/hierarchy grouping |
| Icon | Display icon/file reference |
| Is Event Check? | Enables generated event schema |
| Is Batching Entity? | Batch path; intentionally out of scope here |
| Data Source | Historian/source context used for mapping |

Critical rule:

```text
Creating or updating an equipment type is a heavy generator operation.
```

The row insert/update fires `Equipment_Type_TRG`, which calls `XStudio_Create_Equipment_Type_Table_Usp` and regenerates consolidated views.

---

#### 8. Equipment Type Save Lifecycle

##### 8.1 System Bootstrap

```text
System generation
→ XStudio_AssetStructure_Create_Base_Entities_Usp
→ Base asset entities created
→ Equipment_Type_Mst_Tbl available
→ Area_Mst_Tbl available
→ Asset_Hierarchy_Mst_Tbl available
→ Equipment type trigger created
```

##### 8.2 Hierarchy Configuration

```text
User saves hierarchy levels
→ Asset_Hierarchy_Mst_Tbl updated
→ XStudio_AssetStructure_Create_Hierarchy_Entity_Usp
→ hierarchy entities/configuration materialized
→ XStudio_AssetStructure_Update_Hierarchy_Menu_Usp
→ menus/navigation updated
```

##### 8.3 Equipment Type Save

```text
User creates Equipment Type
→ INSERT / UPDATE Equipment_Type_Mst_Tbl
→ Equipment_Type_TRG fires
→ if IsDeleted <> 1:
      XStudio_Create_Equipment_Type_Table_Usp
  else:
      XStudio_Delete_Equipment_Type_Related_Table_Usp
→ XStudio_Create_Equipment_View_Usp
→ XStudio_Create_Equipment_Template_View_Usp
→ XStudio_Create_Equipment_Tag_View_Usp
```

The trigger skips rows whose `Source` is:

```text
Synchronizer
HandHeld
```

##### 8.4 Generated Module Family

The generator creates:

```text
tables
entities
attributes
dropdown metadata
entity relations
triggers
block structures
event structures if enabled
list views/views
historian-related objects
```

##### 8.5 Template Creation

```text
User creates template header
→ INSERT into <EquipmentType>_Attribute_Tag_Template_Mst_Tbl
→ template master trigger fires
→ template detail rows are auto-populated from equipment/tag attributes
```

##### 8.6 Equipment Instance Creation

```text
User creates physical equipment instance
→ INSERT into <EquipmentType>_Mst_Tbl
→ equipment instance trigger fires
→ tag mapping rows are created for the selected template attributes
```

##### 8.7 Tag Mapping

```text
User maps attributes to historian tags
→ XStudio_AssetStructure_Update_Equipment_And_Attribute_Mapping_Usp
→ <EquipmentType>_Tag_Mapping_Tbl updated
→ datasource and tag name stored per equipment instance + attribute
```

##### 8.8 Runtime Data Capture

```text
Historian / OPC / IIoT source
→ XStudio tag master / tag transaction data
→ equipment tag mapping
→ current data / block table updates
→ summary aggregation and event logic
```

---

#### 9. Generated Tables Per Equipment Type

`XStudio_Create_Equipment_Type_Table_Usp` derives table names from the equipment type name.

```sql
@TypeTableName           = @Name + '_Mst_Tbl'
@MappingTableName        = @Name + '_Tag_Mapping_Tbl'
@TemplateMasterTableName = @Name + '_Attribute_Tag_Template_Mst_Tbl'
@TemplateDetailTableName = @Name + '_Attribute_Tag_Template_Dtl_Tbl'
@CurrentDataTableName    = @Name + '_Data'
```

Block table naming:

```sql
-- if Frequency is set
@BlockTableName = @Name + '_' + @Frequency

-- if block structure is needed without frequency
@BlockTableName = @Name + '_Block'
```

Examples:

```text
EAF_5
BlastFurnace_5
RollingMillStand_1
Turbine_15
```

---

#### 10. Generated Table Roles

##### 10.1 `<EquipmentType>_Mst_Tbl`

Purpose:

```text
Stores actual physical equipment instances for one equipment type.
```

Example:

```text
EAF_Mst_Tbl
  EAF-1
  EAF-2

BlastFurnace_Mst_Tbl
  BF-1

CCMStrand_Mst_Tbl
  CCM-1-Strand-1
  CCM-1-Strand-2
```

Important generated attributes:

| Attribute | Purpose |
|---|---|
| `EquipmentTypeID` | Back-reference to `Equipment_Type_Mst_Tbl` |
| `TemplateID` | Template used by this equipment instance |
| `AreaID` | Plant/area/hierarchy placement |
| `AssetIdentificationProperties` | Flexible asset identity/config properties |
| `RoleIDList` | Role visibility/ownership |
| `PhysicalTag` | Physical or historian-side asset reference |
| `MaterialID` | Conditional; appears for storage/XBatch-like equipment paths |

`TemplateID` and `AreaID` are dropdown/reference attributes and require XStudio dropdown properties and entity relations.

##### 10.2 `<EquipmentType>_Attribute_Tag_Template_Mst_Tbl`

Purpose:

```text
Stores reusable templates for one equipment type.
```

Examples:

```text
EAF_100T_Standard
EAF_80T_Standard
LRF_45T_Standard
BF_Standard
Turbine_30MW_Standard
```

Multiple equipment instances can share the same template.

Example:

```text
EAF-1 → EAF_100T_Standard
EAF-2 → EAF_100T_Standard
```

##### 10.3 `<EquipmentType>_Attribute_Tag_Template_Dtl_Tbl`

Purpose:

```text
Stores template-level measurable attributes/tags and their behavior.
```

Example attributes for EAF:

```text
TapTemperature
PowerKWh
OxygenFlow
ElectrodeCurrent
FurnacePressure
ChargeWeight
IsStartHeat
IsRunHeat
IsDoneHeat
ErrorCode
```

Important fields include:

```text
Attribute
HHRange
HRange
LRange
LLRange
ColorGTHH
ColorBWHHH
ColorBWHL
ColorBWLLL
ColorLTLL
DocumentGTHH
DocumentBWHHH
DocumentBWHL
DocumentBWLLL
DocumentLTLL
ExtendRetrievalType
ExtendEquationType
ExtendEquation
TransactionRetrievalType
Procedure
PWherecolumn
POperator
PConditionType
PMSTAttribute
PWhereValue
IType
TransactionEquationType
TransactionEquation
```

This table is the control point for:

```text
tag mapping generation
range/limit propagation
alarm color behavior
SOP/document association
historian attribute behavior
transaction retrieval behavior
computed/formula behavior
summary aggregation configuration
```

##### 10.4 `<EquipmentType>_Tag_Mapping_Tbl`

Purpose:

```text
Maps equipment instance + template attribute to actual historian/source tag.
```

Example:

```text
EAF-1 + TapTemperature + OSI_PI → HIST.SMS.EAF1.TAP_TEMP
EAF-1 + PowerKWh       + OSI_PI → HIST.SMS.EAF1.POWER_KWH
BF-1  + HotBlastTemp   + OSI_PI → HIST.BF.BF1.HOT_BLAST_TEMP
```

Important columns include:

```text
EquipmentID
Attribute
DataSourceID
TagName
InstrumentTag
HHRange
HRange
LRange
LLRange
ColorGTHH
ColorBWHHH
ColorBWHL
ColorBWLLL
ColorLTLL
DocumentGTHH
DocumentBWHHH
DocumentBWHL
DocumentBWLLL
DocumentLTLL
Type
IsXBatchTag
```

Template detail changes propagate into this table for all equipment instances using that template.

##### 10.5 `<EquipmentType>_Data`

Purpose:

```text
Auto-refresh current data entity for an equipment type.
```

Generated behavior:

```text
IsAutoRefresh = 1
RefreshInterval = 5
IsEquipmentEntity = 1
EquipmentType = current equipment type ID
```

Use cases:

```text
current equipment data screen
SCADA-style screens
dashboards
runtime status panels
```

##### 10.6 `<EquipmentType>_<Frequency>` or `<EquipmentType>_Block`

Purpose:

```text
Periodic historian/block capture table.
```

Frequency is structural. It must be selected at equipment type creation and should not be changed afterward because it affects:

```text
physical table name
block trigger
summary configuration
historian generated SPs
aggregation schedule
runtime block generation
```

---

#### 11. Generated Event Schema

If `IsEventCheck = 1`, `XStudio_Create_Equipment_Event_Schema_USP` creates a full event subsystem for the equipment type.

Generated event tables:

```text
<EquipmentType>_Event_Mst_Tbl
<EquipmentType>_Event_Attribute_Mst_Tbl
<EquipmentType>_Event_Tag_Mapping_Tbl
<EquipmentType>_Event_Configuration_Mst_Tbl
<EquipmentType>_Event_State_Mst_Tbl
<EquipmentType>_Event_Error_Condition_Mst_Tbl
<EquipmentType>_Event_Error_Configuration_Mst_Tbl
<EquipmentType>_Event_Action_Mst_Tbl
```

##### 11.1 Event Master

`<EquipmentType>_Event_Mst_Tbl` defines event categories/types.

Examples:

```text
EAF Power On
EAF Charging Start
EAF Tapping Start
EAF Tapping End
BF Tap Start
BF Tap End
Rolling Mill Cobble
Turbine Trip
```

##### 11.2 Event Attribute

`<EquipmentType>_Event_Attribute_Mst_Tbl` defines event-detecting attributes at template level.

Example:

```text
Template = EAF_100T_Standard
AttributeName = FurnacePowerStatus
TagType = Digital
```

##### 11.3 Event Tag Mapping

`<EquipmentType>_Event_Tag_Mapping_Tbl` maps event attributes to actual tags per equipment instance.

Example:

```text
EAF-1 + PowerStatus = OPC.SMS.EAF1.POWER_ON
EAF-1 + TapStatus   = OPC.SMS.EAF1.TAP_ON
```

##### 11.4 Event Configuration

`<EquipmentType>_Event_Configuration_Mst_Tbl` links event definitions to transaction entities and active configuration.

Example:

```text
EAF Tap Start
→ TransactionEntity = SMS heat/event transaction
```

##### 11.5 Event State

`<EquipmentType>_Event_State_Mst_Tbl` defines state machine behavior.

Important attributes:

```text
StateName
StateSequence
StateCondition
IsErrorState
StateOnWorkFlow
StateOffWorkFlow
IsActive
WorkFlowAttribute
IsWorkFlowEnable
StateOnDelay
StateOffDelay
IsDifferentOffCondition
StateOffCondition
```

Example:

```text
StateName = Running
StateCondition = PowerStatus = 1
StateOffCondition = PowerStatus = 0

StateName = TapInProgress
StateCondition = TapStatus = 1
StateOffCondition = TapStatus = 0
```

##### 11.6 Event Error Configuration

Tables:

```text
<EquipmentType>_Event_Error_Configuration_Mst_Tbl
<EquipmentType>_Event_Error_Condition_Mst_Tbl
```

Purpose:

```text
Detect abnormal or invalid event-state conditions and map them to error messages/codes.
```

Example:

```text
PowerStatus = 1 but FurnaceReady = 0
→ Error: EAF power on when not ready
```

##### 11.7 Event Action

`<EquipmentType>_Event_Action_Mst_Tbl` defines actions fired when an event state occurs.

Important attributes:

```text
StateID
Configuration
ActionType
StateMode
IsActive
```

Likely use cases:

```text
create transaction
update transaction
trigger workflow
raise alert
execute configured action
```

Important API endpoints:

```text
POST /api/Rest/{systemId}/AssetStructure/SaveEventEntity
POST /api/Rest/{systemId}/AssetStructure/SaveEventState/{equipmentTypeId}
POST /api/Rest/{systemId}/AssetStructure/SaveEventErrorConfiguration/{equipmentTypeId}
GET  /api/Rest/{systemId}/AssetStructure/GetEventActions/{equipmentTypeId}/{eventStateId}
GET  /api/Rest/{systemId}/AssetStructure/GetEventEntityColumns/{equipmentTypeId}/{eventId}
```

---

#### 12. Triggers Created for Equipment Modules

`XStudio_Create_Equipment_Mst_And_Template_Dtl_Trigger_Usp` creates three important triggers per equipment type.

```text
XStudio_<EquipmentType>_Mst_Tbl_TRG
XStudio_<EquipmentType>_Attribute_Tag_Template_Mst_Tbl_TRG
XStudio_<EquipmentType>_Attribute_Tag_Template_Dtl_Tbl_TRG
```

##### 12.1 Equipment Instance Trigger

Trigger:

```text
XStudio_<EquipmentType>_Mst_Tbl_TRG
```

Fires on:

```text
INSERT, UPDATE
```

Behavior:

```text
<EquipmentType>_Mst_Tbl insert/update
→ if IsDeleted = 0:
      XStudio_Add_Attribute_Equipment_Mapping_Usp
      creates or updates mapping rows for that equipment instance
→ if IsDeleted = 1:
      XStudio_Delete_Attribute_Equipment_Mapping_Usp
      deletes/soft-deletes mapping rows
```

It skips rows whose source is:

```text
Synchronizer
HandHeld
```

Meaning:

```text
Creating an equipment instance automatically creates tag mapping rows for that instance.
```

##### 12.2 Template Master Trigger

Trigger:

```text
XStudio_<EquipmentType>_Attribute_Tag_Template_Mst_Tbl_TRG
```

Behavior:

```text
New template header
→ reads XStudio_Attribute_Mst_Tbl
→ finds attributes for entities linked to this equipment type
→ filters attributes by equipment/tag datatype
→ inserts template detail rows
→ applies default alarm colors
```

Critical datatype:

```text
EC8BE677-39DB-4945-A62F-70D4A4F9CE78
```

This appears to identify equipment/historian tag attributes. Only attributes with this datatype are auto-added to template details.

Rule:

```text
If an attribute must appear in template details and tag mapping, create it using the correct equipment/tag datatype.
```

##### 12.3 Template Detail Trigger

Trigger:

```text
XStudio_<EquipmentType>_Attribute_Tag_Template_Dtl_Tbl_TRG
```

Behavior A — update mapping rows:

```text
When template detail ranges/colors/documents change:
→ update matching <EquipmentType>_Tag_Mapping_Tbl rows
→ for all equipment instances using that template
```

Behavior B — insert missing mapping rows:

```text
When a new template attribute is added:
→ insert missing mapping rows
→ for all existing equipment instances using that template
```

Example:

```text
Template: EAF_Standard
New attribute: ElectrodeCurrent
Existing equipment: EAF-1, EAF-2

Trigger inserts:
EAF-1 + ElectrodeCurrent
EAF-2 + ElectrodeCurrent
into EAF_Tag_Mapping_Tbl
```

---

#### 13. Consolidated Runtime Views

Because each equipment type has its own physical tables, XStudio creates consolidated union views.

##### 13.1 `XStudio_Equipment_Mst_Vw`

Created by:

```text
XStudio_Create_Equipment_View_Usp
```

Purpose:

```text
Cross-equipment instance selector/browser.
```

Output columns:

```text
ID
EquipmentName
EquipmetTypeID
EquipmentTypeName
TemplateID
TemplateName
AreaID
MaterialID
MaterialName
```

Important:

```text
The generated column is spelled EquipmetTypeID, not EquipmentTypeID.
Do not correct this spelling in downstream SQL if binding to this view.
```

##### 13.2 `XStudio_Equipment_Template_Mst_Vw`

Created by:

```text
XStudio_Create_Equipment_Template_View_Usp
```

Purpose:

```text
Cross-equipment template selector/browser.
```

Output columns:

```text
EquipmetTypeID
EquipmentTypeName
ID
Name
```

##### 13.3 `XStudio_Equipment_Tag_Mst_Vw`

Created by:

```text
XStudio_Create_Equipment_Tag_View_Usp
```

Purpose:

```text
Cross-equipment tag mapping selector/browser.
```

Important columns:

```text
ID
Name
EquipmentID
EquipmentName
Attribute
DataSourceID
TagName
InstrumentTag
HHRange
HRange
LRange
LLRange
ColorGTHH
ColorBWHHH
ColorBWHL
ColorBWLLL
ColorLTLL
DocumentGTHH
DocumentBWHHH
DocumentBWHL
DocumentBWLLL
DocumentLTLL
Type
IsXBatchTag
TagType
FunctionType
```

##### 13.4 Tag Classification Logic

`XStudio_Equipment_Tag_Mst_Vw` classifies tags based on name patterns.

| Pattern in mapping row name | TagType | FunctionType |
|---|---|---|
| contains `Weight` | Weight | Read |
| contains `IsStart`, `IsHold`, `IsRestart`, `IsAbort`, `IsReset` | Command | Write |
| contains `IsReady`, `IsHeld`, `IsRun`, `IsDone`, `IsInterlock`, `IsAborted` | Status | Read |
| contains `Error` | Interlock | Read |
| else | Parameter | Parameter |

Template/tag naming matters.

Recommended naming examples:

```text
IsStartHeat      → Command / Write
IsRunHeat        → Status / Read
IsDoneHeat       → Status / Read
IsInterlockReady → Status / Read
ErrorCode        → Interlock / Read
ChargeWeight     → Weight / Read
TapTemperature   → Parameter / Parameter
```

---

#### 14. Block / Historian Runtime

Block tables are interval-based historian capture tables for equipment data.

##### 14.1 Block Row Generation

`XStudio_Generate_Block_Entry_USP` creates expected interval rows for all active equipment instances.

`XStudio_Insert_Block_Equipment_data_usp` creates missing block rows for one specific equipment instance.

Runtime pattern:

```text
Block entity metadata
→ equipment type resolved
→ active equipment instances found
→ one row per equipment instance per interval inserted
→ EquipmentID, EntryDateTime, ReportDate, Source stored
```

Core insert pattern:

```sql
INSERT INTO <BlockTable>
(
    EquipmentID,
    EntryDateTime,
    ReportDate,
    Source
)
SELECT
    ID,
    @EntryDateTime,
    @ReportDate,
    'T-SQL'
FROM <EquipmentType>_Mst_Tbl
WHERE IsDeleted = 0
AND NOT EXISTS (
    -- same EquipmentID + EntryDateTime + ReportDate
);
```

##### 14.2 ReportDate Logic

`ReportDate` is operational-day aware.

If system `DayStartTime` exists:

```text
If EntryDateTime time is between 00:00 and DayStartTime
→ ReportDate = previous calendar date

Else
→ ReportDate = EntryDateTime date
```

This matters for steel plant shift/day accounting where the production day may start at 06:00 instead of midnight.

##### 14.3 Block Query

`XStudio_Find_Block_Data_Usp` reads block data by resolving LV/entity/block metadata and then querying the relevant block tables.

Inputs include:

```text
SystemID
LVID
StartDate
EndDate
Type
Filter
EquipmentID
```

Behavior:

```text
Resolve LV/entity
Resolve block entities
Build dynamic query
Filter by date/equipment/filter
Return rows, usually TOP 10000 descending
Support archive mode where applicable
```

##### 14.4 Recalculation

`XStudio_Historian_Block_Recalculate_USP` recalculates block/historian values.

Use cases:

```text
late historian data
wrong tag mapping
historian outage
formula change
manual correction
missed interval processing
```

Supported recalculation patterns include:

```text
all attributes over date range
specific attribute
specific equipment
specific equipment + attribute
```

Generated historian SP pattern:

```text
Xstudio_Historian_<EntityName>_usp
```

##### 14.5 OSI PI Tag Flow

Relevant SPs:

| Stored Procedure | Purpose |
|---|---|
| `XStudio_Get_OSI_Datasource_USP` | Gets selected OSI datasource configuration |
| `XStudio_Get_OSI_Tag_USP` | Lists active OSI PI tags from tag master |
| `XStudio_Save_OSIPI_Tag_Data_Usp` | Saves incoming OSI PI tag values into tag transaction table |
| `XStudio_Update_Historian_Data_In_Entity_Usp` | Bulk-updates target entity attribute values from tag data |

Runtime flow:

```text
OSI PI source
→ XStudio_Tag_Mst_Tbl
→ XStudio_Tag_Trn_Tbl
→ historian processing
→ equipment current/block entities
```

---

#### 15. Template Aggregation

`XStudio_AssetStructure_Template_Aggregation_Usp` copies summary aggregation rows from one template to another.

Inputs:

```text
@SystemId
@EntityID
@FromTemID
@ToTempID
```

Behavior:

```text
Read XStudio_Summary_Aggregation_Dtl_Tbl
where TemplateID = @FromTemID
  and EntityID = @EntityID
  and HierarchyTableType = 'block'
  and IsDeleted = 0

Insert equivalent rows for @ToTempID
Avoid duplicates
```

Meaning:

```text
Templates do not only define tags.
Templates can also own block-summary aggregation configuration.
```

`XStudio_AssetStructure_Template_GetPreviousTable_Usp` resolves source/previous table in hierarchy-aware summary configuration.

`XStudio_AssetStructure_Template_Update_SummaryHierarchy_SourceType_Details` bulk-updates summary hierarchy source-type details using the previous-table logic.

---

#### 16. API Surface Summary

Important Asset Structure endpoints:

```text
GET  /api/Rest/{systemId}/AssetStructure/Hierarchy
POST /api/Rest/{systemId}/AssetStructure/Hierarchy/Save

GET  /api/Rest/{systemId}/AssetStructure/EquipmentType
POST /api/Rest/{systemId}/AssetStructure/EquipmentType/Save

POST /api/Rest/{systemId}/AssetStructure/Equipment/Nodes
POST /api/Rest/{systemId}/AssetStructure/Equipment/Mapping/{mappingType}
POST /api/Rest/{systemId}/AssetStructure/Equipment/SaveMapping/{euiqmentType}/{mappingType}
POST /api/Rest/{systemId}/AssetStructure/Equipment/Tags/Validate/{datasourceId}
POST /api/Rest/{systemId}/AssetStructure/Equipment/Tags/ValidateTagMapping
POST /api/Rest/{systemId}/AssetStructure/Equipment/Entities
POST /api/Rest/{systemId}/AssetStructure/Equipment/DeleteNode

GET  /api/Rest/{systemId}/AssetStructure/{equipmentType}/Instance
POST /api/Rest/{systemId}/AssetStructure/{equipmentTypeId}/Template/Save
GET  /api/Rest/{systemId}/AssetStructure/{equipmentTypeId}/TemplateDetail/{recordId}
POST /api/Rest/{systemId}/AssetStructure/TemplateDetails/Save
POST /api/Rest/{systemId}/AssetStructure/{equipmentTypeId}/DuplicateTemplate/Save

POST /api/Rest/{systemId}/AssetStructure/SaveEventEntity
POST /api/Rest/{systemId}/AssetStructure/SaveEventState/{equipmentTypeId}
POST /api/Rest/{systemId}/AssetStructure/SaveEventErrorConfiguration/{equipmentTypeId}
GET  /api/Rest/{systemId}/AssetStructure/GetEventActions/{equipmentTypeId}/{eventStateId}
GET  /api/Rest/{systemId}/AssetStructure/GetEventEntityColumns/{equipmentTypeId}/{eventId}

GET  /api/Rest/{systemId}/AssetStructure/Documents
```

Known spelling issue in API path:

```text
/api/Rest/{systemId}/AssetStructure/Equipment/SaveMapping/{euiqmentType}/{mappingType}
```

The path parameter is misspelled as `euiqmentType`. Use the actual platform path as defined.

---

#### 17. Deletion Behavior

`XStudio_Delete_Equipment_Type_Related_Table_Usp` is called when an equipment type is soft-deleted through the equipment type trigger.

It can:

```text
drop block data tables
delete summary configuration
delete entity attributes and properties
drop physical generated tables
drop generated LV views
drop generated historian SPs
soft-delete generated entities
delete equipment-type menu items
regenerate consolidated views
```

Rule:

```text
Never delete an equipment type with live production or historian data.
```

For LMEL, equipment types should be treated as structural configuration. If a wrong type is created during experimentation, fix it in a sandbox first rather than deleting in production.

---

#### 18. LMEL MES Integration Pattern

Asset Structure owns equipment identity, hierarchy, templates, tag mappings, historian/block capture, and event schema.

MES transaction entities should reference Asset Structure equipment instances.

##### 18.1 Generic transaction/event tables

Use when a record can refer to multiple equipment types:

```text
EquipmentTypeID
EquipmentID
```

Examples:

```text
XMES_Equipment_Event_Trn_Tbl
XMES_Delay_Event_Trn_Tbl
XMES_Operator_Log_Trn_Tbl
```

##### 18.2 Plant-specific transaction tables

Use typed equipment fields where domain clarity matters.

Examples:

```text
XMES_SMS_Heat_Trn_Tbl.EAFID       → EAF_Mst_Tbl.ID
XMES_SMS_Heat_Trn_Tbl.LRFID       → LRF_Mst_Tbl.ID
XMES_SMS_CCM_Cast_Trn_Tbl.CCMID   → CCM_Mst_Tbl.ID
XMES_BF_Tap_Trn_Tbl.BFID          → BlastFurnace_Mst_Tbl.ID
XMES_RM_Rolling_Trn_Tbl.StandID   → RollingMillStand_Mst_Tbl.ID
XMES_CPP_Generation_Trn_Tbl.TGID  → Turbine_Mst_Tbl.ID
```

##### 18.3 Generic equipment selector

Use:

```text
XStudio_Equipment_Mst_Vw
```

when a screen needs to browse all equipment types.

Important:

```text
Do not create a separate standalone XMES_Equipment_Mst_Tbl as the primary asset master.
```

A lightweight MES reference/wrapper can exist only if needed for execution convenience, but Asset Structure remains the source of truth.

---

#### 19. Recommended LMEL Equipment Types

| Equipment Type | Example Instances | Event Check | Block Data | Notes |
|---|---|---:|---:|---|
| `CokeOvenBattery` | Battery-1, Battery-2 | Yes | Yes | Battery-level operating events and process data |
| `CokeOvenChamber` | Chamber-01..72 | Yes | Optional | Usually instance-heavy; avoid unnecessary block load |
| `BlastFurnace` | BF-1 | Yes | Yes | Tap events, hot blast, burden/process values |
| `DRIKiln` | DRI-Kiln-1, DRI-Kiln-2 | Yes | Yes | Kiln run state, feed, discharge, temperature zones |
| `PelletBallingDisc` | Disc-1, Disc-2 | Yes | Yes | Balling process values |
| `PelletIndurationFurnace` | Induration-1 | Yes | Yes | Furnace temperature/zone values |
| `EAF` | EAF-1, EAF-2 | Yes | Yes | Heat execution, power, oxygen, tapping |
| `LRF` | LRF-1, LRF-2 | Yes | Yes | Heating, alloying, ladle treatment |
| `VD` | VD-1 | Yes | Yes | Vacuum cycle data |
| `CCM` | CCM-1 | Yes | Yes | Cast/sequence state |
| `CCMStrand` | CCM-1-S1, CCM-1-S2 | Yes | Optional | Strand-specific values/events |
| `Ladle` | Ladle-001..030 | Optional | No | May be better handled through MES transaction lifecycle |
| `ReheatingFurnace` | RHF-1, RHF-2 | Yes | Yes | Charging/discharging, zones, fuel |
| `RollingMillStand` | WRM-S1..S20 | Yes | Yes | Stand status, speed, load |
| `CoolingBed` | CB-1 | Optional | Optional | Depends on automation scope |
| `Boiler` | Boiler-1, Boiler-2 | Yes | Yes | CPP steam/fuel/process values |
| `Turbine` | TG-1, TG-2 | Yes | Yes | CPP generation/load/trip events |
| `Generator` | Gen-1, Gen-2 | Yes | Yes | Electrical generation values |
| `Compressor` | AC-1, AC-2 | Yes | Yes | Utility equipment |
| `Pump` | Pump-1..N | Optional | Optional | Only model critical pumps initially |
| `Weighbridge` | WB-1, WB-2 | No | Optional | Mostly transactional integration, not event-heavy |

---

#### 20. Suggested Block Frequency Strategy

Frequency creates physical tables. Do not create unnecessary frequency variants.

Recommended standard options:

| Frequency | Use |
|---:|---|
| 1 minute | High-frequency critical equipment where required |
| 5 minutes | Normal process equipment data |
| 15 minutes | Utilities, CPP, slower balances |
| 60 minutes | Low-frequency balances and periodic reporting |

Recommended Day-1 approach:

```text
SMS/EAF/LRF/CCM     → 1 or 5 min
BF/DRI/Pellet       → 5 or 15 min
RM critical stands  → 1 or 5 min
CPP                 → 5 or 15 min
Yard/static assets  → no block unless required
```

---

#### 21. Agent Do / Do Not

##### Do

```text
Use Asset Structure for plant equipment identity.
Reference generated equipment instances from XMES transactions.
Use EquipmentTypeID + EquipmentID for generic equipment references.
Use typed equipment fields for domain-specific transaction tables.
Use XStudio_Equipment_Mst_Vw for generic selectors.
Use exact generated column names.
Validate generated triggers and views after equipment type creation.
Validate tag mapping rows after template/instance creation.
Keep event schema only for assets that need event/state behavior.
Keep frequency choices limited and intentional.
```

##### Do not

```text
Do not create XMES_Equipment_Mst_Tbl as the primary asset master.
Do not manually create <EquipmentType>_Mst_Tbl or related generated tables in production.
Do not correct EquipmetTypeID spelling in generated view bindings.
Do not enable block tables for every small/passive asset.
Do not enable event schema for every equipment type by default.
Do not delete equipment types with live data.
Do not generate batch table details from this KB.
Do not invent unverified API endpoints.
```

---

#### 22. Postflight Checks for Agents

After equipment type creation, verify:

```sql
-- 1. Equipment type exists
SELECT ID, Name, IsDeleted
FROM XStudio_XMES.dbo.Equipment_Type_Mst_Tbl
WHERE Name = '<EquipmentType>';

-- 2. Generated physical tables exist
SELECT TABLE_NAME
FROM XStudio_XMES.INFORMATION_SCHEMA.TABLES
WHERE TABLE_NAME IN (
    '<EquipmentType>_Mst_Tbl',
    '<EquipmentType>_Tag_Mapping_Tbl',
    '<EquipmentType>_Attribute_Tag_Template_Mst_Tbl',
    '<EquipmentType>_Attribute_Tag_Template_Dtl_Tbl',
    '<EquipmentType>_Data'
);

-- 3. Generated entities exist
SELECT Name, TableType, IsEquipmentEntity, EquipmentType
FROM XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_Entities_Mst_Tbl
WHERE Name LIKE '<EquipmentType>%'
  AND IsDeleted = 0;

-- 4. Equipment triggers exist
SELECT name
FROM XStudio_XMES.sys.triggers
WHERE name IN (
    'XStudio_<EquipmentType>_Mst_Tbl_TRG',
    'XStudio_<EquipmentType>_Attribute_Tag_Template_Mst_Tbl_TRG',
    'XStudio_<EquipmentType>_Attribute_Tag_Template_Dtl_Tbl_TRG'
);

-- 5. Consolidated views exist
SELECT TABLE_NAME
FROM XStudio_XMES.INFORMATION_SCHEMA.VIEWS
WHERE TABLE_NAME IN (
    'XStudio_Equipment_Mst_Vw',
    'XStudio_Equipment_Template_Mst_Vw',
    'XStudio_Equipment_Tag_Mst_Vw'
);
```

After template creation, verify:

```sql
SELECT ID, Name
FROM XStudio_XMES.dbo.<EquipmentType>_Attribute_Tag_Template_Mst_Tbl
WHERE IsDeleted = 0;

SELECT ParentID, Attribute, HHRange, HRange, LRange, LLRange
FROM XStudio_XMES.dbo.<EquipmentType>_Attribute_Tag_Template_Dtl_Tbl
WHERE IsDeleted = 0;
```

After equipment instance creation, verify:

```sql
SELECT ID, Name, TemplateID, AreaID
FROM XStudio_XMES.dbo.<EquipmentType>_Mst_Tbl
WHERE IsDeleted = 0;

SELECT EquipmentID, Attribute, DataSourceID, TagName
FROM XStudio_XMES.dbo.<EquipmentType>_Tag_Mapping_Tbl
WHERE IsDeleted = 0;
```

For generic selector verification:

```sql
SELECT TOP 20
    ID,
    EquipmentName,
    EquipmetTypeID,
    EquipmentTypeName,
    TemplateID,
    TemplateName,
    AreaID
FROM XStudio_XMES.dbo.XStudio_Equipment_Mst_Vw;
```

For tag classification verification:

```sql
SELECT TOP 50
    EquipmentName,
    Attribute,
    TagName,
    TagType,
    FunctionType
FROM XStudio_XMES.dbo.XStudio_Equipment_Tag_Mst_Vw
WHERE EquipmentTypeName = '<EquipmentType>';
```

For block verification:

```sql
SELECT TABLE_NAME
FROM XStudio_XMES.INFORMATION_SCHEMA.TABLES
WHERE TABLE_NAME IN ('<EquipmentType>_1', '<EquipmentType>_5', '<EquipmentType>_15', '<EquipmentType>_60', '<EquipmentType>_Block');
```

For event schema verification:

```sql
SELECT TABLE_NAME
FROM XStudio_XMES.INFORMATION_SCHEMA.TABLES
WHERE TABLE_NAME IN (
    '<EquipmentType>_Event_Mst_Tbl',
    '<EquipmentType>_Event_Attribute_Mst_Tbl',
    '<EquipmentType>_Event_Tag_Mapping_Tbl',
    '<EquipmentType>_Event_Configuration_Mst_Tbl',
    '<EquipmentType>_Event_State_Mst_Tbl',
    '<EquipmentType>_Event_Error_Condition_Mst_Tbl',
    '<EquipmentType>_Event_Error_Configuration_Mst_Tbl',
    '<EquipmentType>_Event_Action_Mst_Tbl'
);
```

---

#### 24. Agent Output Contract for Asset Structure Tasks

When an agent uses this KB to produce scripts or plans, the output must include:

```text
1. Scope statement
2. Assumptions
3. Objects touched
4. Dependency order
5. Script or steps
6. Side effects / generated objects
7. Postflight checks
8. Rollback / non-rollback warning
9. Explicit note if any generated table/view/trigger is platform-created rather than manually created
```

For any task involving equipment type creation, the agent must explicitly state:

```text
Equipment_Type_Mst_Tbl save fires generator logic.
Generated equipment tables must be created by platform generator.
The script must not manually CREATE generated equipment type tables unless operating in a controlled reproduction/sandbox mode.
```

## 5. Asset Structure Api Surface

**Source file:** `01_CANONICAL_REFERENCE/asset_structure_api_surface.md`  
**File type:** `.md`  
**Size:** `1984` bytes  
**Modified on:** `2026-05-04 17:49:24`  
**SHA256:** `6b3566787729`  
**Encoding used:** `utf-8`

> Start of imported document.

### Asset Structure API Surface

Asset Structure has dedicated API endpoints. The frontend generally uses APIs rather than directly editing every generated table.

#### Main endpoints

```text
GET  /api/Rest/{systemId}/AssetStructure/Hierarchy
POST /api/Rest/{systemId}/AssetStructure/Hierarchy/Save
GET  /api/Rest/{systemId}/AssetStructure/EquipmentType
POST /api/Rest/{systemId}/AssetStructure/EquipmentType/Save
POST /api/Rest/{systemId}/AssetStructure/Equipment/Nodes
POST /api/Rest/{systemId}/AssetStructure/Equipment/Mapping/{mappingType}
POST /api/Rest/{systemId}/AssetStructure/Equipment/SaveMapping/{euiqmentType}/{mappingType}
POST /api/Rest/{systemId}/AssetStructure/Equipment/Tags/Validate/{datasourceId}
POST /api/Rest/{systemId}/AssetStructure/Equipment/Tags/ValidateTagMapping
POST /api/Rest/{systemId}/AssetStructure/Equipment/Entities
POST /api/Rest/{systemId}/AssetStructure/Equipment/DeleteNode
GET  /api/Rest/{systemId}/AssetStructure/{equipmentType}/Instance
POST /api/Rest/{systemId}/AssetStructure/{equipmentTypeId}/Template/Save
GET  /api/Rest/{systemId}/AssetStructure/{equipmentTypeId}/TemplateDetail/{recordId}
POST /api/Rest/{systemId}/AssetStructure/TemplateDetails/Save
POST /api/Rest/{systemId}/AssetStructure/{equipmentTypeId}/DuplicateTemplate/Save
```

Note: the API path uses the spelling `euiqmentType` in `SaveMapping`; preserve this when calling the endpoint.

#### Conceptual API flow

```text
Hierarchy/Save
→ EquipmentType/Save
→ Template/Save
→ TemplateDetails/Save
→ Equipment/Nodes
→ Equipment/Mapping
→ Equipment/SaveMapping
```

#### Mapping validation

Before saving tag mappings, use the validation endpoints where available:

```text
Equipment/Tags/Validate/{datasourceId}
Equipment/Tags/ValidateTagMapping
```

#### When to prefer SP/SQL

Use direct SP/SQL only for controlled deployment, postflight checks, or repair. For normal UI behavior, follow the API path because it triggers platform-side validation and generator behavior.

## 6. Asset Structure Deep Dive

**File type:** `.md`
