> Start of imported document.

### XStudio Asset Structure — Agent Deep Dive

#### Purpose

This document is an agent-facing deep dive for XStudio Asset Structure. It is designed for agents that must inspect, generate, validate, or modify Asset Structure-related configuration for LMEL MES.

This document assumes the agent has already read:

```text
01_CANONICAL_REFERENCE/asset_structure.md
00_START_HERE/01_safety_rules.md
00_START_HERE/02_task_router.md
```

This document focuses on implementation behavior discovered from:

```text
XStudio_Configuration stored procedure inspection
SQL Profiler trace of Equipment Type creation
Swagger/API surface
live XMES observations
```

Batch table generation is intentionally excluded. Do not use this document to generate or modify batch-table behavior.

---

#### Mandatory Agent Rules

```text
1. Treat Asset Structure as the equipment and historian backbone.
2. Do not create a standalone XMES equipment master as the source of truth.
3. Do not manually create generated <EquipmentType> tables in production.
4. Use the Asset Structure UI/API/generator path for equipment types.
5. Do not directly update Equipment_Type_Mst_Tbl unless the script intentionally triggers the generator.
6. Assume Equipment_Type_Mst_Tbl INSERT/UPDATE is a heavy operation.
7. Preserve generated names exactly, including misspellings such as EquipmetTypeID.
8. Do not document or generate batch-table behavior in this phase.
9. Do not enable event schema for passive assets unless explicitly required.
10. Do not delete equipment types with live historian/production data.
11. Template detail rows drive tag mapping; do not bypass them.
12. Tag mappings are generated/maintained by triggers; do not blindly bulk insert them before templates and equipment instances exist.
13. Use postflight checks after every Asset Structure operation.
```

---

#### 1. Asset Structure SP Families

##### 1.1 Base and hierarchy

| Stored Procedure | Agent meaning |
|---|---|
| `XStudio_AssetStructure_Create_Base_Entities_Usp` | System-bootstrap procedure that creates base Asset Structure entities such as equipment type, area, and hierarchy base objects. Usually automatic during system setup. |
| `XStudio_AssetStructure_Create_Hierarchy_Entity_Usp` | Materializes hierarchy levels into XStudio entities/configuration. Call only after hierarchy definition exists. |
| `XStudio_AssetStructure_Get_Hierarchy_Usp` | Reads ordered hierarchy rows from `Asset_Hierarchy_Mst_Tbl`; can ensure the base table/entity exists. |
| `XStudio_AssetStructure_Update_Hierarchy_Menu_Usp` | Rebuilds hierarchy navigation/menu entries after hierarchy changes. |
| `XStudio_Save_Hierarchy_Configuration_Usp` | Saves user-configured hierarchy. Prefer API/UI flow unless building controlled seed scripts. |
| `XStudio_Delete_Hierarchy_Usp` | Deletes hierarchy configuration. Treat as destructive. |
| `DOW_Get_Asset_Hierarchy_Usp` | Reads hierarchy for operational/day-of-work context where applicable. |

##### 1.2 Equipment type generation

| Stored Procedure | Agent meaning |
|---|---|
| `XStudio_Create_Equipment_Type_Mst_Trigger_Usp` | Creates `Equipment_Type_TRG` on `Equipment_Type_Mst_Tbl`. This trigger is the root of equipment type generation. |
| `XStudio_Create_Equipment_Type_Table_Usp` | Main generator. Creates physical tables, XStudio entities/attributes, mapping structures, data entity, block structures, event structures if enabled, and related metadata. |
| `XStudio_Add_Equipment_Type_Entity_Usp` | Registers/generated equipment type entities into XStudio metadata. |
| `XStudio_EquipmentType_Tables_Validation_Usp` | Validates whether expected generated tables exist for an equipment type. Use in postflight. |
| `XStudio_Get_Equipment_Type_Related_Tables_Usp` | Returns related/generated table names for an equipment type. Use before deletion or diagnostics. |
| `XStudio_Delete_Equipment_Type_Related_Table_Usp` | Drops/soft-deletes generated objects for a deleted equipment type. Treat as destructive. |

##### 1.3 Template, instance, and mapping

| Stored Procedure | Agent meaning |
|---|---|
| `XStudio_Create_Equipment_Mst_And_Template_Dtl_Trigger_Usp` | Creates the three key triggers on generated instance/template/template-detail tables. Required for auto mapping behavior. |
| `XStudio_AssetStructure_Get_Equipment_And_Attribute_Mapping_Usp` | Returns pivot mapping grid: attributes as rows, equipment instances as columns. |
| `XStudio_AssetStructure_Update_Equipment_And_Attribute_Mapping_Usp` | Writes datasource/tag names into `<EquipmentType>_Tag_Mapping_Tbl` using TVP `Equipment_TagData`. |
| `XStudio_Add_Attribute_Equipment_Mapping_Usp` | Creates/updates mapping rows for an equipment instance. Usually called by generated triggers. |
| `XStudio_Delete_Attribute_Equipment_Mapping_Usp` | Removes/soft-deletes mapping rows when equipment instance is deleted. Usually called by generated triggers. |
| `XStudio_Add_Or_Alter_Equipment_Attribute_Usp` | Adds/alters equipment entity attributes. Use only with full schema awareness. |
| `XStudio_Add_Or_Alter_Equipment_Tag_Attribute_Usp` | Adds/alters tag-specific equipment attributes. Use when attributes must participate in template/tag mapping. |
| `Xstudio_Attribute_Equipment_Mapping_USP` | General attribute-equipment mapping operation; inspect before using directly. |

##### 1.4 Runtime views

| Stored Procedure | Generated object | Agent meaning |
|---|---|---|
| `XStudio_Create_Equipment_View_Usp` | `XStudio_Equipment_Mst_Vw` | Creates union view across all `<EquipmentType>_Mst_Tbl` tables. |
| `XStudio_Create_Equipment_Template_View_Usp` | `XStudio_Equipment_Template_Mst_Vw` | Creates union view across all template master tables. |
| `XStudio_Create_Equipment_Tag_View_Usp` | `XStudio_Equipment_Tag_Mst_Vw` | Creates union view across all tag mapping tables and computes tag classification. |

##### 1.5 Events

| Stored Procedure | Agent meaning |
|---|---|
| `XStudio_Create_Equipment_Event_Schema_USP` | Creates full event subsystem for an equipment type when `IsEventCheck = 1`. |
| `XStudio_AssetStructure_AddOrUpdate_Event_To_TemplateEvent_Usp` | Propagates event definitions into template event configuration. |
| `XStudio_AssetStructure_Get_Event_Tag_Mapping_Usp` | Reads event tag mapping grid. |
| `XStudio_AssetStructure_Update_Event_Tag_Mapping_Usp` | Updates event tag mappings using TVP `Equipment_Event_TagData`. |
| `XStudio_Add_Attribute_Equipment_Event_Mapping_Usp` | Adds event attribute-equipment mapping rows. |
| `XStudio_Add_Or_Alter_Event_Type_Attribute_Usp` | Adds/alters event type attributes. |
| `XStudio_Insert_Template_Event_Configuration_Usp` | Inserts event config into `<EquipmentType>_Event_Configuration_Mst_Tbl`. |

##### 1.6 Blocks and historian

| Stored Procedure | Agent meaning |
|---|---|
| `XStudio_Create_Block_Tbl_Usp` | Creates block table by copying source entity/table schema. |
| `XStudio_Create_Block_Table_Trigger_Usp` | Creates block trigger `XStudio_TRG_<BlockEntityName>`. |
| `Xstudio_Add_Alter_Attriubute_Block_Tbl_Usp` | Adds/alters columns on block tables. |
| `XStudio_Generate_Block_Entry_USP` | Creates expected interval rows for all active equipment instances. |
| `XStudio_Insert_Block_Equipment_data_usp` | Creates missing interval rows for one equipment instance. |
| `XStudio_Find_Block_Data_Usp` | Query path for block data; resolves LV/entity/block tables dynamically. |
| `XStudio_Historian_Block_Recalculate_USP` | Recalculates historian/block values for date/equipment/attribute scopes. |
| `XStudio_Update_Historian_Data_In_Entity_Usp` | Bulk-updates entity attribute values from historian tag data. |
| `XStudio_Get_Block_Database_And_Table_Name_List` | Returns database/table mapping for block entities. |

##### 1.7 OSI PI / tag ingestion

| Stored Procedure | Agent meaning |
|---|---|
| `XStudio_Get_OSI_Datasource_USP` | Reads selected datasource configuration. |
| `XStudio_Get_OSI_Tag_USP` | Lists active OSI PI tags from tag master. |
| `XStudio_Save_OSIPI_Tag_Data_Usp` | Inserts incoming OSI PI tag values into `XStudio_Tag_Trn_Tbl`. |

##### 1.8 Historian summary

| Stored Procedure | Agent meaning |
|---|---|
| `XStudio_Historian_Create_Summary_SP_USP` | Master summary-SP generator. |
| `XStudio_Historian_Create_Summary_Minute_SP_Usp` | Creates minute-level summary SP. |
| `XStudio_Historian_Create_Summary_Hour_SP_Usp` | Creates hourly summary SP. |
| `XStudio_Historian_Create_Summary_Shift_SP_Usp` | Creates shift-level summary SP. |
| `XStudio_Historian_Create_Summary_Day_SP_Usp` | Creates daily summary SP. |
| `XStudio_Historian_Create_Summary_Month_SP_Usp` | Creates monthly summary SP. |
| `XStudio_Historian_Summary_Generate_Attribute_Usp` | Generates summary attributes. |
| `XStudio_Summary_Historian_Schedule_Task_Usp` | Schedules historian summary recalculation. |

---

#### 2. Base and Hierarchy Behavior

##### 2.1 `Asset_Hierarchy_Mst_Tbl`

`Asset_Hierarchy_Mst_Tbl` defines hierarchy levels, not physical equipment instances.

Typical read pattern:

```sql
SELECT ID, Name, SerialNumber
FROM XStudio_XMES.dbo.Asset_Hierarchy_Mst_Tbl
WHERE IsDeleted = 0
ORDER BY SerialNumber ASC;
```

Recommended LMEL hierarchy:

```text
1. Enterprise
2. Site
3. Plant / Area
4. Shop / Process Line
5. Work Center
6. Equipment
7. Sub-equipment / Instrumented Unit
```

##### 2.2 Generated hierarchy entity attributes

`XStudio_AssetStructure_Create_Hierarchy_Entity_Usp` creates hierarchy-level entities with operational attributes.

Important generated attributes:

```text
ParentID
AssetIdentificationProperties
ShiftID
RoleIDList
IsHandoverEnabled
```

Agent interpretation:

```text
Hierarchy is not only a tree.
Hierarchy nodes can carry parent-child structure, role visibility, shift context, handover configuration, and asset identification metadata.
```

##### 2.3 `Area_Mst_Tbl`

`Area_Mst_Tbl` acts as the anchor for equipment placement. Generated equipment instance tables normally carry `AreaID`.

LMEL areas should include at least:

```text
Coke Oven
Pellet Plant
DRI
Blast Furnace
SMS / Grand Shot
Rolling Mill
CPP
Yards / Logistics where required
```

---

#### 3. Equipment Type Trigger

`XStudio_Create_Equipment_Type_Mst_Trigger_Usp` creates:

```text
Equipment_Type_TRG
```

on:

```text
Equipment_Type_Mst_Tbl
```

Trigger behavior:

```text
On INSERT/UPDATE of Equipment_Type_Mst_Tbl:

If Source not in ('Synchronizer', 'Handheld'):
    If IsDeleted = 0:
        Execute XStudio_Create_Equipment_Type_Table_Usp
    Else:
        Execute XStudio_Delete_Equipment_Type_Related_Table_Usp

    Execute XStudio_Create_Equipment_View_Usp
    Execute XStudio_Create_Equipment_Template_View_Usp
    Execute XStudio_Create_Equipment_Tag_View_Usp
```

Agent implications:

```text
1. Equipment type save is not passive.
2. Equipment type save creates or deletes many physical/config objects.
3. Equipment type delete is destructive.
4. Direct INSERT/UPDATE scripts must be treated as generator scripts, not simple seed scripts.
5. Source values Synchronizer and Handheld are skipped by the trigger logic.
```

---

#### 4. Equipment Type Generated Family

For equipment type:

```text
EAF
```

the generated family includes:

```text
EAF_Mst_Tbl
EAF_Tag_Mapping_Tbl
EAF_Attribute_Tag_Template_Mst_Tbl
EAF_Attribute_Tag_Template_Dtl_Tbl
EAF_Data
EAF_5 or EAF_Block
EAF_Event_Mst_Tbl
EAF_Event_Attribute_Mst_Tbl
EAF_Event_Tag_Mapping_Tbl
EAF_Event_Configuration_Mst_Tbl
EAF_Event_State_Mst_Tbl
EAF_Event_Error_Condition_Mst_Tbl
EAF_Event_Error_Configuration_Mst_Tbl
EAF_Event_Action_Mst_Tbl
```

Event objects exist only if `IsEventCheck = 1`.

Block objects exist only if block/frequency configuration requires them.

Batch objects are out of scope for this document.

---

#### 5. Generated Table Roles

##### 5.1 `<EquipmentType>_Mst_Tbl`

Purpose:

```text
Stores physical equipment instances of one equipment type.
```

Examples:

```text
EAF_Mst_Tbl:
  EAF-1
  EAF-2

BlastFurnace_Mst_Tbl:
  BF-1

CCMStrand_Mst_Tbl:
  CCM-1-Strand-1
  CCM-1-Strand-2
```

Important generated attributes:

```text
EquipmentTypeID
TemplateID
AreaID
AssetIdentificationProperties
RoleIDList
PhysicalTag
MaterialID      -- conditional; storage/XBatch-like path only
```

Agent rule:

```text
For plant-specific MES transactions, typed fields can reference these typed master tables.
For generic MES event/transaction tables, use EquipmentTypeID + EquipmentID or the consolidated view pattern.
```

##### 5.2 `<EquipmentType>_Attribute_Tag_Template_Mst_Tbl`

Purpose:

```text
Stores reusable template headers for one equipment type.
```

Examples:

```text
EAF_100T_Standard
EAF_80T_Standard
LRF_45T_Standard
BF_Standard
Turbine_30MW_Standard
```

One template can be used by many equipment instances.

##### 5.3 `<EquipmentType>_Attribute_Tag_Template_Dtl_Tbl`

Purpose:

```text
Stores process/tag attributes and behavior for a template.
```

This is the main control object for template-level data capture.

Important fields:

```text
Attribute
HHRange
HRange
LRange
LLRange
ColorGTHH
ColorBWHHH
ColorBWHL
ColorBWLLL
ColorLTLL
DocumentGTHH
DocumentBWHHH
DocumentBWHL
DocumentBWLLL
DocumentLTLL
ExtendRetrievalType
ExtendEquationType
ExtendEquation
TransactionRetrievalType
Procedure
PWherecolumn
POperator
PConditionType
PMSTAttribute
PWhereValue
IType
TransactionEquationType
TransactionEquation
```

Agent interpretation:

```text
Template detail is not a simple tag list.
It defines limits, visual bands, SOP/document linkage, derived values, transaction lookups, and formula behavior.
```

##### 5.4 `<EquipmentType>_Tag_Mapping_Tbl`

Purpose:

```text
Maps equipment instance + template attribute to actual datasource/tag.
```

Important fields:

```text
EquipmentID
Attribute
DataSourceID
TagName
InstrumentTag
HHRange
HRange
LRange
LLRange
ColorGTHH
ColorBWHHH
ColorBWHL
ColorBWLLL
ColorLTLL
DocumentGTHH
DocumentBWHHH
DocumentBWHL
DocumentBWLLL
DocumentLTLL
Type
IsXBatchTag
```

Mapping example:

```text
EAF-1 + TapTemperature + OSI_PI → HIST.SMS.EAF1.TAP_TEMP
BF-1  + HotBlastTemp   + OSI_PI → HIST.BF.BF1.HOT_BLAST_TEMP
```

Agent warning:

```text
Mapping rows are normally created by generated triggers.
A tag-mapping upload should update existing mapping rows, not blindly insert new rows.
```

##### 5.5 `<EquipmentType>_Data`

Purpose:

```text
Auto-refresh current data entity for an equipment type.
```

Generated behavior:

```text
IsAutoRefresh = 1
RefreshInterval = 5
IsEquipmentEntity = 1
EquipmentType = current equipment type ID
```

Use cases:

```text
current equipment data screens
SCADA-style screens
runtime status panels
simple dashboards
```

##### 5.6 `<EquipmentType>_<Frequency>` or `<EquipmentType>_Block`

Purpose:

```text
Interval/block historian rows.
```

Naming:

```text
If Frequency exists: <EquipmentType>_<Frequency>
Example: EAF_5, RollingMillStand_1, Turbine_15

If block exists without frequency: <EquipmentType>_Block
```

Agent rule:

```text
Frequency is structural. Do not change it casually because it affects table names, triggers, summaries, generated historian SPs, and runtime block generation.
```

---

#### 6. Template / Instance / Mapping Triggers

`XStudio_Create_Equipment_Mst_And_Template_Dtl_Trigger_Usp` creates three triggers per equipment type:

```text
XStudio_<EquipmentType>_Mst_Tbl_TRG
XStudio_<EquipmentType>_Attribute_Tag_Template_Mst_Tbl_TRG
XStudio_<EquipmentType>_Attribute_Tag_Template_Dtl_Tbl_TRG
```

##### 6.1 Equipment instance trigger

Trigger:

```text
XStudio_<EquipmentType>_Mst_Tbl_TRG
```

Fires on:

```text
INSERT, UPDATE
```

Behavior:

```text
If Source not in ('Synchronizer', 'HandHeld'):
    If IsDeleted = 0:
        XStudio_Add_Attribute_Equipment_Mapping_Usp
    Else:
        XStudio_Delete_Attribute_Equipment_Mapping_Usp
```

Meaning:

```text
Creating or updating an equipment instance can automatically create or update tag mapping rows.
Soft-deleting an equipment instance can remove or soft-delete mapping rows.
```

##### 6.2 Template master trigger

Trigger:

```text
XStudio_<EquipmentType>_Attribute_Tag_Template_Mst_Tbl_TRG
```

Behavior:

```text
New template header
→ read XStudio_Attribute_Mst_Tbl
→ filter attributes where:
     XStudio_Entities_Mst_Tbl.EquipmentType = current equipment type
     XStudio_Attribute_Mst_Tbl.DataType = EC8BE677-39DB-4945-A62F-70D4A4F9CE78
→ insert distinct attribute rows into <EquipmentType>_Attribute_Tag_Template_Dtl_Tbl
→ apply default alarm colors
```

Critical datatype:

```text
EC8BE677-39DB-4945-A62F-70D4A4F9CE78
```

Current understanding:

```text
This GUID appears to represent equipment/historian tag attributes.
Ordinary Text, Number, or Decimal attributes do not automatically become template detail rows.
```

Agent rule:

```text
If an attribute must appear in template details and tag mapping, create it using the platform equipment/tag datatype, not a normal scalar datatype.
```

##### 6.3 Template detail trigger

Trigger:

```text
XStudio_<EquipmentType>_Attribute_Tag_Template_Dtl_Tbl_TRG
```

Behavior A — propagate changes:

```text
When template detail ranges/colors/documents change:
→ update matching <EquipmentType>_Tag_Mapping_Tbl rows
→ for all equipment instances using that template
```

Propagated fields:

```text
HHRange
HRange
LRange
LLRange
ColorGTHH
ColorBWHHH
ColorBWHL
ColorBWLLL
ColorLTLL
DocumentGTHH
DocumentBWHHH
DocumentBWHL
DocumentBWLLL
DocumentLTLL
```

Join logic:

```text
TagMapping.EquipmentID → <EquipmentType>_Mst_Tbl.ID
TemplateDetail.ParentID → Equipment.TemplateID
TemplateDetail.Attribute → TagMapping.Attribute
```

Behavior B — insert missing mapping rows:

```text
When a new template attribute is added:
→ insert missing mapping rows for all existing equipment instances using that template
```

Example:

```text
Template: EAF_Standard
New attribute: ElectrodeCurrent
Existing equipment: EAF-1, EAF-2

Trigger inserts:
EAF-1 + ElectrodeCurrent
EAF-2 + ElectrodeCurrent
into EAF_Tag_Mapping_Tbl
```

---

#### 7. Equipment and Attribute Mapping SPs

##### 7.1 Get mapping grid

SP:

```text
XStudio_AssetStructure_Get_Equipment_And_Attribute_Mapping_Usp
```

Output shape:

```text
Attribute | EAF-1 | EAF-2 | EAF-3
TapTemp   | tag1  | tag2  | tag3
PowerKWh  | tag4  | tag5  | tag6
```

Behavior:

```text
1. Reads equipment instances from <EquipmentType>_Mst_Tbl.
2. Includes instances that already exist in <EquipmentType>_Tag_Mapping_Tbl.
3. Pivots equipment names into columns.
4. Returns attributes as rows.
```

##### 7.2 Update mapping grid

SP:

```text
XStudio_AssetStructure_Update_Equipment_And_Attribute_Mapping_Usp
```

Inputs include:

```text
@EquipmentTagetbl Equipment_TagData READONLY
@DataSourceID
```

TVP shape:

```text
Equipment_TagData:
  Attribute
  Equipment
  TagName
```

Behavior:

```text
1. Resolve Equipment name → <EquipmentType>_Mst_Tbl.ID.
2. Match Attribute to existing mapping row.
3. Blank TagName becomes NULL.
4. Set DataSourceID.
5. Update TagName.
6. Update ModifiedBy, ModifiedOn, HostAddress.
7. Set Source = 'System'.
```

Agent warning:

```text
This SP updates mappings. It expects mapping rows to exist.
Create equipment templates and instances before calling this SP.
```

---

#### 8. Runtime Views

##### 8.1 `XStudio_Equipment_Mst_Vw`

Created by:

```text
XStudio_Create_Equipment_View_Usp
```

Source:

```text
UNION ALL over active <EquipmentType>_Mst_Tbl tables
```

Output columns:

```text
ID
EquipmentName
EquipmetTypeID
EquipmentTypeName
TemplateID
TemplateName
AreaID
MaterialID
MaterialName
```

Important spelling:

```text
EquipmetTypeID
```

Do not change to `EquipmentTypeID` in downstream SQL when querying this generated view.

##### 8.2 `XStudio_Equipment_Template_Mst_Vw`

Created by:

```text
XStudio_Create_Equipment_Template_View_Usp
```

Source:

```text
UNION ALL over active <EquipmentType>_Attribute_Tag_Template_Mst_Tbl tables
```

Output columns:

```text
EquipmetTypeID
EquipmentTypeName
ID
Name
```

##### 8.3 `XStudio_Equipment_Tag_Mst_Vw`

Created by:

```text
XStudio_Create_Equipment_Tag_View_Usp
```

Source:

```text
UNION ALL over active <EquipmentType>_Tag_Mapping_Tbl tables
joined to each <EquipmentType>_Mst_Tbl
```

Important output columns:

```text
ID
Name
EquipmentID
EquipmentName
Attribute
DataSourceID
TagName
InstrumentTag
HHRange
HRange
LRange
LLRange
ColorGTHH
ColorBWHHH
ColorBWHL
ColorBWLLL
ColorLTLL
DocumentGTHH
DocumentBWHHH
DocumentBWHL
DocumentBWLLL
DocumentLTLL
Type
IsXBatchTag
TagType
FunctionType
```

##### 8.4 Tag classification logic

`XStudio_Equipment_Tag_Mst_Vw` classifies `TagType` and `FunctionType` from mapping row name patterns.

| Pattern in mapping row `Name` | TagType | FunctionType |
|---|---|---|
| contains `Weight` | Weight | Read |
| contains `IsStart`, `IsHold`, `IsRestart`, `IsAbort`, `IsReset` | Command | Write |
| contains `IsReady`, `IsHeld`, `IsRun`, `IsDone`, `IsInterlock`, `IsAborted` | Status | Read |
| contains `Error` | Interlock | Read |
| otherwise | Parameter | Parameter |

Agent rule:

```text
Template/tag attribute names affect generated classification.
Use predictable names for command, status, interlock, weight, and parameter tags.
```

Recommended LMEL examples:

```text
IsStartHeat      → Command / Write
IsRunHeat        → Status / Read
IsDoneHeat       → Status / Read
IsInterlockReady → Status / Read
ErrorCode        → Interlock / Read
ChargeWeight     → Weight / Read
TapTemperature   → Parameter / Parameter
```

#### 9. Event Schema Deep Dive

If `IsEventCheck = 1`, the equipment type generator calls:

```text
XStudio_Create_Equipment_Event_Schema_USP
```

Generated event tables:

```text
<EquipmentType>_Event_Mst_Tbl
<EquipmentType>_Event_Attribute_Mst_Tbl
<EquipmentType>_Event_Tag_Mapping_Tbl
<EquipmentType>_Event_Configuration_Mst_Tbl
<EquipmentType>_Event_State_Mst_Tbl
<EquipmentType>_Event_Error_Condition_Mst_Tbl
<EquipmentType>_Event_Error_Configuration_Mst_Tbl
<EquipmentType>_Event_Action_Mst_Tbl
```

##### 9.1 Event master

Table:

```text
<EquipmentType>_Event_Mst_Tbl
```

Purpose:

```text
Defines event categories/types for the equipment type.
```

Important attributes:

```text
EventType
TransactionEntity
IsActive
```

LMEL examples:

```text
EAF Charging Start
EAF Tapping Start
BF Tap Start
Rolling Mill Cobble
Turbine Trip
```

##### 9.2 Event attribute

Table:

```text
<EquipmentType>_Event_Attribute_Mst_Tbl
```

Purpose:

```text
Defines event-detecting attributes for a template.
```

Important attributes:

```text
TemplateID
AttributeName
TagType
```

##### 9.3 Event tag mapping

Table:

```text
<EquipmentType>_Event_Tag_Mapping_Tbl
```

Purpose:

```text
Maps event attributes to actual event/source tags per equipment instance.
```

Important attributes:

```text
EquipmentID
Attribute
TagName
CollectorID
ChannelID
```

Update SP:

```text
XStudio_AssetStructure_Update_Event_Tag_Mapping_Usp
```

TVP:

```text
Equipment_Event_TagData:
  Attribute
  Equipment
  TagName
```

##### 9.4 Event configuration

Table:

```text
<EquipmentType>_Event_Configuration_Mst_Tbl
```

Purpose:

```text
Links event definition to transaction entity/configuration.
```

Important attributes:

```text
EventType
TransactionEntity
IsActive
EventMstID
```

##### 9.5 Event state

Table:

```text
<EquipmentType>_Event_State_Mst_Tbl
```

Purpose:

```text
Defines state-machine logic for equipment events.
```

Important attributes:

```text
StateName
StateSequence
StateCondition
IsErrorState
StateOnWorkFlow
StateOffWorkFlow
IsActive
WorkFlowAttribute
IsWorkFlowEnable
StateOnDelay
StateOffDelay
IsDifferentOffCondition
StateOffCondition
```

Example:

```text
StateName = TapInProgress
StateCondition = TapStatus = 1
StateOffCondition = TapStatus = 0
```

Workflow integration pattern:

```text
IsWorkFlowEnable = 1
WorkFlowAttribute = Status
StateOnWorkFlow = Running
StateOffWorkFlow = Completed
```

##### 9.6 Event error configuration

Tables:

```text
<EquipmentType>_Event_Error_Configuration_Mst_Tbl
<EquipmentType>_Event_Error_Condition_Mst_Tbl
```

Purpose:

```text
Detect invalid or abnormal event-state conditions.
```

Important attributes:

```text
ErrorLookupType
ErrorAttribute
ErrorLookupEntity
ErrorCodeAttribute
ErrorMessageAttribute
ErrorCondition
ErrorMessage
```

Example:

```text
Condition:
PowerStatus = 1 and FurnaceReady = 0

Error:
EAF power on when not ready
```

##### 9.7 Event action

Table:

```text
<EquipmentType>_Event_Action_Mst_Tbl
```

Purpose:

```text
Defines configured action fired when an event state occurs.
```

Important attributes:

```text
StateID
Configuration
ActionType
StateMode
IsActive
```

Likely uses:

```text
create transaction
update transaction
trigger workflow
raise alert
execute configured action
```

##### 9.8 Template event propagation

SP:

```text
XStudio_AssetStructure_AddOrUpdate_Event_To_TemplateEvent_Usp
```

Flow:

```text
Event created/updated in <EquipmentType>_Event_Mst_Tbl
→ SP reads event details
→ writes/updates <EquipmentType>_Event_Configuration_Mst_Tbl
→ applies event to template-level event config
```

Agent implication:

```text
Events are not only equipment-type definitions. They become template-aware.
```

---

#### 10. Block / Historian Deep Dive

##### 10.1 Block table creation

SP:

```text
XStudio_Create_Block_Tbl_Usp
```

Behavior:

```text
1. Reads source entity/table schema from sys.columns.
2. Reads column data types, identity settings, and default constraints.
3. Dynamically creates block table with copied schema.
4. Registers block table in XStudio_Block_Entities_Mst_Tbl.
```

Agent implication:

```text
Block table schema mirrors the source/current data entity. Do not assume a fixed block schema except common fields like EquipmentID, EntryDateTime, ReportDate, Source where generated.
```

##### 10.2 Block table trigger

SP:

```text
XStudio_Create_Block_Table_Trigger_Usp
```

Generated trigger:

```text
XStudio_TRG_<BlockEntityName>
```

Trigger behavior:

```text
On INSERT/UPDATE:
1. Get inserted row ID.
2. If SP XSTUDIO_<BlockEntityName>_USP exists:
      Execute it with @ID.
3. Call summary scheduling procedure to trigger summary recalculation.
```

Agent implication:

```text
Block row insert/update may fire custom block processing and summary recalculation.
Do not bulk modify block rows without considering trigger side effects.
```

##### 10.3 Block row generation for all equipment

SP:

```text
XStudio_Generate_Block_Entry_USP
```

Runtime flow:

```text
Input:
  @SystemID
  @TableName

Lookup:
  XStudio_Block_Entities_Mst_Tbl
  → parent entity
  → database name
  → start/end time

Lookup:
  XStudio_Entities_Mst_Tbl
  → equipment type
  → summary interval
  → block type

If BlockType = Fixed:
  Resolve EquipmentTypeID → Equipment_Type_Mst_Tbl.Name
  Master table = <EquipmentType>_Mst_Tbl

Loop:
  EntryDateTime from StartTime to EndTime by SummaryInterval minutes

For each interval:
  Insert one row per active equipment instance if not already present.
```

Core insert pattern:

```sql
INSERT INTO <BlockTable> (EquipmentID, EntryDateTime, ReportDate, Source)
SELECT ID, @EntryDateTime, @ReportDate, 'T-SQL'
FROM <EquipmentType>_Mst_Tbl
WHERE IsDeleted = 0
AND NOT EXISTS (
    -- same EquipmentID + EntryDateTime + ReportDate
);
```

##### 10.4 Block row generation for one equipment

SP:

```text
XStudio_Insert_Block_Equipment_data_usp
```

Inputs:

```text
@ConfigurationDatabaseName
@DataDataBaseName
@EquipmentTypeID
@EquipmentID
```

Behavior:

```text
1. Finds transaction entities where:
     TableType = 'Transaction'
     IsEquipmentEntity = 1
     EquipmentType = @EquipmentTypeID
     SummaryInterval <> 0

2. Resolves equipment type name.
3. Reads block entity tables from XStudio_Block_Entities_Mst_Tbl.
4. Aligns end time to interval boundary.
5. Inserts missing block rows for only @EquipmentID.
```

Use case:

```text
New equipment instance created after block tables already exist.
Run this to backfill interval rows for that equipment only.
```

##### 10.5 ReportDate logic

`ReportDate` is operational-day aware.

If `DayStartTime` exists:

```text
If EntryDateTime time is between 00:00 and DayStartTime:
    ReportDate = previous calendar date
Else:
    ReportDate = EntryDateTime calendar date
```

LMEL implication:

```text
If plant day starts at 06:00, a block row at 2026-05-04 01:30 belongs to ReportDate 2026-05-03.
```

Agent rule:

```text
Do not calculate ReportDate manually as CAST(EntryDateTime AS date) unless DayStartTime is confirmed null.
```

##### 10.6 Block query

SP:

```text
XStudio_Find_Block_Data_Usp
```

Inputs:

```text
@SystemID
@LVID
@StartDate
@EndDate
@Type
@Filter
@EquipmentID
```

Behavior:

```text
1. Resolve LV → entity → block entities.
2. Determine block database/table names.
3. Build dynamic UNION query.
4. For Fixed blocks, filter using EntryDateTime.
5. For non-Fixed blocks, filter using StartTime.
6. Filter by EquipmentID list.
7. Apply optional filter.
8. If archive mode is configured, union Data DB and Archive DB.
9. Return TOP 10000 rows sorted descending.
```

Agent rule:

```text
Use XStudio_Find_Block_Data_Usp for block reads. Do not assume one table or one database.
```

##### 10.7 Historian/block recalculation

SP:

```text
XStudio_Historian_Block_Recalculate_USP
```

Inputs:

```text
@SystemID
@EntityID
@StartDate
@EndDate
@Type
@Attribute
@EquipmentID
```

Main behavior:

```text
1. Resolve config DB, data DB, entity name, summary interval, equipment type.
2. Resolve matching block tables for date range.
3. For each target EntryDateTime, call:
   <DataDB>.dbo.Xstudio_Historian_<EntityName>_usp
```

Known recalculation modes:

```text
Recalculate all attributes over date range
Recalculate one attribute
Recalculate one equipment
Recalculate one equipment + one attribute
```

Use cases:

```text
late PI data
historian outage
wrong tag mapping
changed formula
manual correction
missed interval processing
```

##### 10.8 Update historian data in entity

SP:

```text
XStudio_Update_Historian_Data_In_Entity_Usp
```

Inputs:

```text
@Database
@Entity
@Attribute
@TagData TagData READONLY
```

Behavior:

```sql
UPDATE mainEntity
SET [<Attribute>] = tag.[Value]
FROM <Database>.dbo.<Entity> AS mainEntity
JOIN @TagData AS tag
  ON tag.Id = mainEntity.Id;
```

Interpretation:

```text
Historian services/generated SPs can pass record ID + value pairs and update one target attribute in bulk.
```

---

#### 11. OSI PI Flow

##### 11.1 Datasource lookup

SP:

```text
XStudio_Get_OSI_Datasource_USP
```

Inputs:

```text
@SystemId
@DatasourceId
```

Behavior:

```text
Resolve system configuration DB.
Return active datasource row from XStudio_DataSource_Mst_Tbl for @DatasourceId.
```

Note:

```text
The SP returns selected datasource config; do not assume it filters by DataSourceType unless verified in target system.
```

##### 11.2 OSI tag lookup

SP:

```text
XStudio_Get_OSI_Tag_USP
```

Inputs:

```text
@SystemId
```

Behavior:

```text
Return active tags from XStudio_Tag_Mst_Tbl joined to XStudio_DataSource_Mst_Tbl
where TagType = 'IO'
and DataSourceType = 'OSI PI Historian'
```

Returned columns:

```text
ID
Name
Server
DataSource
```

##### 11.3 Save OSI tag transaction values

SP:

```text
XStudio_Save_OSIPI_Tag_Data_Usp
```

Inputs:

```text
@SystemId
@TempTable OSITagData READONLY
```

Behavior:

```text
Resolve system config DB.
Join incoming OSI rows to XStudio_Tag_Mst_Tbl by tag name.
Insert into XStudio_Tag_Trn_Tbl:
  TagID
  Value
  TimeStamp
```

Runtime flow:

```text
OSI PI tag data
→ OSITagData TVP
→ XStudio_Save_OSIPI_Tag_Data_Usp
→ XStudio_Tag_Trn_Tbl
→ historian processing / generated historian SPs
→ current/block equipment entities
```

---

#### 12. Template Aggregation and Summary Hierarchy

##### 12.1 Template aggregation

SP:

```text
XStudio_AssetStructure_Template_Aggregation_Usp
```

Inputs:

```text
@SystemId
@EntityID
@FromTemID
@ToTempID
```

Behavior:

```text
Read XStudio_Summary_Aggregation_Dtl_Tbl
where TemplateID = @FromTemID
  and EntityID = @EntityID
  and HierarchyTableType = 'block'
  and IsDeleted = 0

Insert equivalent rows for @ToTempID.
Avoid duplicates based on EntityID + AttributeID + HierarchyTableType + TemplateID.
```

Agent implication:

```text
Templates can own historian summary aggregation configuration, not just tag definitions.
When duplicating/copying templates, copy aggregation configuration if summaries should match.
```

##### 12.2 Previous table resolution

SP:

```text
XStudio_AssetStructure_Template_GetPreviousTable_Usp
```

Purpose:

```text
Resolve previous/source table in hierarchy-aware summary configuration.
```

Reads:

```text
XStudio_Summary_Table_Type_Mst_Tbl.SourceTableHierarchy
XStudio_Summary_Hierarchy_DTL_Tbl
```

Updates:

```text
XStudio_Summary_Aggregation_Dtl_Tbl.SourceTable
```

##### 12.3 Bulk summary hierarchy update

SP:

```text
XStudio_AssetStructure_Template_Update_SummaryHierarchy_SourceType_Details
```

Purpose:

```text
Bulk update source table/source type details for summary hierarchy aggregation rows using previous-table logic.
```

---

#### 13. Destructive Delete Behavior

SP:

```text
XStudio_Delete_Equipment_Type_Related_Table_Usp
```

Called when:

```text
Equipment_Type_Mst_Tbl.IsDeleted = 1
and Equipment_Type_TRG fires
```

Potential effects:

```text
1. Drop block data tables from XStudio_Block_Entities_Mst_Tbl.
2. Delete summary configuration via Xstudio_Delete_Summary_Configuration_Usp.
3. Delete related entity attributes and attribute properties.
4. Drop generated physical tables in Data DB.
5. Drop generated LV views such as XStudio_<EntityName>_List_vw.
6. Drop generated historian SPs such as Xstudio_Historian_<EntityName>_usp.
7. Soft-delete generated XStudio entities in Config DB.
8. Delete equipment-type menu items.
9. Regenerate consolidated views.
```

Agent rules:

```text
Never use this path in production unless explicitly instructed.
Never soft-delete Equipment_Type_Mst_Tbl rows with live data.
Always inspect related tables before deletion using XStudio_Get_Equipment_Type_Related_Tables_Usp.
```

---

#### 14. Agent Dependency Order

For any agent that needs to generate or validate Asset Structure configuration, follow this order:

```text
1. Confirm system/config/data DB names.
2. Confirm Asset Structure base entities exist.
3. Confirm hierarchy levels exist.
4. Confirm Area records exist.
5. Create/save Equipment Type through controlled UI/API/generator path.
6. Validate generated table family.
7. Validate generated entities/attributes.
8. Validate instance/template/template-detail triggers.
9. Create template header.
10. Validate template detail auto-population.
11. Add/modify template detail rows if required.
12. Create equipment instances.
13. Validate mapping rows auto-created.
14. Apply datasource/tag mapping through mapping SP/API.
15. Validate consolidated views.
16. Configure event schema only if required.
17. Validate block table generation if block is configured.
18. Validate historian/block row generation.
19. Validate summary aggregation if configured.
```

---

#### 15. Agent Postflight Checks

After equipment type creation:

```sql
-- Equipment type row
SELECT ID, Name, IsDeleted
FROM XStudio_XMES.dbo.Equipment_Type_Mst_Tbl
WHERE Name = '<EquipmentType>';

-- Generated tables
SELECT TABLE_NAME
FROM XStudio_XMES.INFORMATION_SCHEMA.TABLES
WHERE TABLE_NAME IN (
    '<EquipmentType>_Mst_Tbl',
    '<EquipmentType>_Tag_Mapping_Tbl',
    '<EquipmentType>_Attribute_Tag_Template_Mst_Tbl',
    '<EquipmentType>_Attribute_Tag_Template_Dtl_Tbl',
    '<EquipmentType>_Data'
);

-- Generated entities
SELECT Name, TableType, IsEquipmentEntity, EquipmentType
FROM XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_Entities_Mst_Tbl
WHERE Name LIKE '<EquipmentType>%'
  AND IsDeleted = 0;
```

After trigger generation:

```sql
SELECT name
FROM XStudio_XMES.sys.triggers
WHERE name IN (
    'XStudio_<EquipmentType>_Mst_Tbl_TRG',
    'XStudio_<EquipmentType>_Attribute_Tag_Template_Mst_Tbl_TRG',
    'XStudio_<EquipmentType>_Attribute_Tag_Template_Dtl_Tbl_TRG'
);
```

After template creation:

```sql
SELECT *
FROM XStudio_XMES.dbo.<EquipmentType>_Attribute_Tag_Template_Mst_Tbl
WHERE IsDeleted = 0;

SELECT Attribute, HHRange, HRange, LRange, LLRange
FROM XStudio_XMES.dbo.<EquipmentType>_Attribute_Tag_Template_Dtl_Tbl
WHERE IsDeleted = 0;
```

After equipment instance creation:

```sql
SELECT ID, Name, TemplateID, AreaID
FROM XStudio_XMES.dbo.<EquipmentType>_Mst_Tbl
WHERE IsDeleted = 0;

SELECT EquipmentID, Attribute, DataSourceID, TagName
FROM XStudio_XMES.dbo.<EquipmentType>_Tag_Mapping_Tbl
WHERE IsDeleted = 0;
```

After consolidated view regeneration:

```sql
SELECT TOP 10 *
FROM XStudio_XMES.dbo.XStudio_Equipment_Mst_Vw;

SELECT TOP 10 *
FROM XStudio_XMES.dbo.XStudio_Equipment_Template_Mst_Vw;

SELECT TOP 10 *
FROM XStudio_XMES.dbo.XStudio_Equipment_Tag_Mst_Vw;
```

For the equipment view, validate the generated spelling:

```sql
SELECT TOP 1 EquipmetTypeID
FROM XStudio_XMES.dbo.XStudio_Equipment_Mst_Vw;
```

After event schema generation:

```sql
SELECT TABLE_NAME
FROM XStudio_XMES.INFORMATION_SCHEMA.TABLES
WHERE TABLE_NAME IN (
    '<EquipmentType>_Event_Mst_Tbl',
    '<EquipmentType>_Event_Attribute_Mst_Tbl',
    '<EquipmentType>_Event_Tag_Mapping_Tbl',
    '<EquipmentType>_Event_Configuration_Mst_Tbl',
    '<EquipmentType>_Event_State_Mst_Tbl',
    '<EquipmentType>_Event_Error_Condition_Mst_Tbl',
    '<EquipmentType>_Event_Error_Configuration_Mst_Tbl',
    '<EquipmentType>_Event_Action_Mst_Tbl'
);
```

After block setup:

```sql
SELECT *
FROM XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_Block_Entities_Mst_Tbl
WHERE EntityName LIKE '<EquipmentType>%'
   OR TableName LIKE '<EquipmentType>%';
```

---

#### 16. Agent Do / Do Not

##### Do

```text
Use Asset Structure as equipment source of truth.
Use generated views for cross-equipment selectors.
Use typed equipment tables for plant-specific references.
Validate generated objects after every equipment type save.
Create templates before equipment instances.
Validate mapping rows before tag upload.
Use mapping SP/API for tag names.
Use event schema only for assets with meaningful event states.
Use block recalculation SPs after historian correction scenarios.
```

##### Do not

```text
Do not create <EquipmentType>_Mst_Tbl manually.
Do not create <EquipmentType>_Tag_Mapping_Tbl manually.
Do not bypass template detail when defining process parameters.
Do not change Equipment Type Name after creation.
Do not rename generated columns in views.
Do not assume EquipmentTypeID spelling in generated views; use EquipmetTypeID.
Do not calculate ReportDate as calendar date without checking DayStartTime.
Do not directly delete equipment types in production.
Do not document or generate batch-table structures in this phase.
```

---

#### 17. LMEL Design Implications

##### 17.1 Equipment source of truth

```text
Asset Structure = equipment identity, hierarchy, templates, tag mappings, block/historian capture, event schema.
XMES custom tables = production, quality, inventory, genealogy, SAP execution records.
```

The two layers must be linked, not duplicated.

##### 17.2 Generic equipment references

For generic transaction/event tables:

```text
EquipmentTypeID
EquipmentID
```

Use for:

```text
XMES_Equipment_Event_Trn_Tbl
XMES_Delay_Event_Trn_Tbl
XMES_Operator_Log_Trn_Tbl
```

##### 17.3 Plant-specific typed references

Use typed equipment fields when domain clarity matters:

```text
XMES_SMS_Heat_Trn_Tbl.EAFID       → EAF_Mst_Tbl.ID
XMES_SMS_Heat_Trn_Tbl.LRFID       → LRF_Mst_Tbl.ID
XMES_SMS_CCM_Cast_Trn_Tbl.CCMID   → CCM_Mst_Tbl.ID
XMES_BF_Tap_Trn_Tbl.BFID          → BlastFurnace_Mst_Tbl.ID
XMES_RM_Rolling_Trn_Tbl.StandID   → RollingMillStand_Mst_Tbl.ID
XMES_CPP_Generation_Trn_Tbl.TGID  → Turbine_Mst_Tbl.ID
```

##### 17.4 Tag configuration pattern

Do not hardcode historian tag names in MES transaction tables.

Use:

```text
Template detail Attribute = PowerKWh
Equipment instance = EAF-1
Tag mapping = HIST.SMS.EAF1.POWER_KWH
```

##### 17.5 Suggested event-enabled equipment types

Use event schema for:

```text
EAF
LRF
VD
CCM
BlastFurnace
DRIKiln
PelletIndurationFurnace
ReheatingFurnace
RollingMillStand
Boiler
Turbine
Generator
```

Avoid event schema initially for:

```text
Yard locations
static storage bays
non-critical pumps
passive instruments
```

##### 17.6 Suggested block frequency strategy

```text
1 min  → critical high-speed process data where required
5 min  → normal process equipment data
15 min → utilities and slower balances
60 min → low-frequency balances
```

Day-1 approach:

```text
SMS/EAF/LRF/CCM     → 1 or 5 min
BF/DRI/Pellet       → 5 or 15 min
Rolling Mill        → 1 or 5 min for critical stands
CPP                 → 5 or 15 min
Yard/static assets  → no block unless specifically required
```

---

#### 18. Working Runtime Model

```text
Equipment Type saved
→ Equipment_Type_TRG
→ XStudio_Create_Equipment_Type_Table_Usp
→ generated table family
→ generated triggers
→ generated views

Template created
→ template master trigger
→ template detail auto-populated from equipment/tag datatype attributes

Equipment instance created
→ equipment instance trigger
→ mapping rows created

Template detail changed
→ template detail trigger
→ ranges/colors/docs pushed to mapping rows
→ missing mappings inserted

Tag names assigned
→ mapping update SP/API
→ <EquipmentType>_Tag_Mapping_Tbl updated

OSI PI values ingested
→ XStudio_Tag_Trn_Tbl
→ historian processing
→ current/block equipment entities

Block rows generated
→ interval rows per equipment
→ block trigger
→ custom block SP if exists
→ summary schedule

Events configured
→ event tag mapping
→ event states/actions/errors
→ transaction/workflow behavior where configured
```

---

#### 19. Output Contract for Agents

When an agent produces Asset Structure work, its output must include:

```text
1. Scope statement
2. Equipment type names touched
3. Whether hierarchy is required or already present
4. Whether event schema is enabled
5. Whether block data is enabled
6. Template names created/modified
7. Equipment instances created/modified
8. Tag mapping source and target
9. SPs/API paths used
10. Generated objects expected
11. Postflight SQL
12. Known limitations / not executed items
```

If the agent is uncertain whether a generated object exists, it must write a validation query rather than inventing the object.

```text
Likely inputs:

Output:
```

```text
Do not hard-code equipment type names; resolve from this SP.
```

```text
Inputs:

Output:
```

```text
Use when you know the equipment type ID and need the entity ID
for block or summary operations.
```

#### 21. Summary Type Full Set (Discovered in New SP Export)

The `XStudio_Historian_Create_Summary_SP_USP` master summary creator dispatches to type-specific SPs.

The complete set of supported summary types discovered from the new SP export:

| TableType | Generated SP | Period Level |
|---|---|---|
| `Minute` | `XStudio_Create_Summary_Minute_SP_USP` | Per minute |
| `Hour` | `XStudio_Create_Summary_Hour_SP_Usp` | Per hour |
| `Shift` | `XStudio_Create_Summary_Shift_SP_Usp` | Per shift |
| `DAY` | `XStudio_Create_Summary_Day_SP_Usp` | Calendar day |
| `FinancialDay` | `XStudio_Create_Summary_FinancialDay_SP_Usp` | Financial/operational day |
| `WEEK` | `XStudio_Create_Summary_Week_SP_Usp` | Calendar week |
| `Month` | `XStudio_Create_Summary_Month_SP_Usp` | Calendar month |
| `FinancialMonth` | `XStudio_Create_Summary_FinancialMonth_SP_Usp` | Financial month |
| `Quarter` | `XStudio_Create_Summary_Quarter_SP_Usp` | Calendar quarter |
| `FinancialQuarter` | `XStudio_Create_Summary_FinancialQuarter_SP_Usp` | Financial quarter |
| `HalfYear` | `XStudio_Create_Summary_HalfYear_SP_Usp` | Calendar half-year |
| `HalfFinancialYear` | (implied by code) | Financial half-year |
| `Year` / `FinancialYear` | (implied by code) | Full year |

##### AIMS variant SPs

The summary dispatcher also checks `@Summary_Type = 'AIMS'` and if true routes to:

```text
XStudio_AIMS_Create_Summary_Minute_SP_Usp
XStudio_AIMS_Create_Summary_Hour_SP_Usp
XStudio_AIMS_Create_Summary_Shift_SP_Usp
XStudio_AIMS_Create_Summary_Day_SP_Usp
XStudio_AIMS_Create_Summary_FinancialDay_SP_Usp
XStudio_AIMS_Create_Summary_Month_SP_Usp
XStudio_AIMS_Create_Summary_FinancialMonth_SP_Usp
XStudio_AIMS_Create_Summary_Quarter_SP_Usp
XStudio_AIMS_Create_Summary_FinancialQuarter_SP_Usp
```

Agent rule:

```text
AIMS = Advanced Integrated Management System summary type.
If @Summary_Type is not 'AIMS', the standard historian summary SPs are used.
Do not assume only Minute/Hour/Shift/Day/Month exist.
For LMEL steel plant, financial calendar variants are relevant because the Indian fiscal year starts April 1.
```

##### Financial year implications for LMEL

```text
Indian fiscal year: April 1 → March 31.
If summary uses FinancialYear, FinancialQuarter, FinancialMonth:
  Start month = 4 (April)
  Start date  = 07:00:00 (if plant day start is configured as 07:00)

Summary SP reads startmonth and startdate from XStudio_Summary_Hierarchy_DTL_Tbl.
If not configured, defaults:
  calendar periods: startmonth = 1, startdate = 00:00:00
  financial periods: startmonth = 4, startdate = 07:00:00
```

---

#### 22. Summary Column Update Engine (New Discovery)

`XStudio_Create_Summary_Column_Update_Query_Usp` builds the cumulative summary updater SP.

This SP is called during summary setup for each table type. It generates a new or altered SP named:

```sql
XStudio_Update_<TableType>_<EntityName>_Usp
```

Examples:

```text
XStudio_Update_Day_EAF_5_Usp
XStudio_Update_Shift_EAF_5_Usp
XStudio_Update_Month_EAF_5_Usp
```

The generated SP supports these cumulative aggregation columns:

| Column Suffix | Meaning |
|---|---|
| `_YTD` | Year-to-date (calendar) |
| `_HYTD` | Half-year-to-date (calendar) |
| `_QTD` | Quarter-to-date (calendar) |
| `_MTD` | Month-to-date (calendar) |
| `_TD` | Today-to-date (operational day) |
| `_TS` | Today-shift (current shift) |
| `_YD` | Yesterday |
| `_FYTD` | Financial year-to-date |
| `_FHYTD` | Financial half-year-to-date |
| `_FQTD` | Financial quarter-to-date |
| `_FMTD` | Financial month-to-date |
| `_FTD` | Financial today-to-date |

Agent implication:

```text
These _YTD, _MTD, _TD, _TS, _FYTD columns are NOT stored in block tables.
They are computed on-the-fly by the generated updater SP and written into the corresponding summary table.
Do not design block table schemas expecting _YTD columns.
Do not query block tables for cumulative aggregations; query generated summary tables.
```

Generated SP pattern for Hour/Shift based tables:

```text
Input: @Entrydatetime DATETIME
Cursor: iterates distinct EntryDateTime values from summary table
Computes: YrStartDate, MthStartDate, ShiftStartDate for each row
Updates: YTD, MTD, TD, TS, FYTD, FMTD, FTD columns
```

For Day/Month based tables:

```text
Input: @ReportDate DATE
Cursor: iterates distinct ReportDate values from summary table
Computes: start dates for each cumulative period
Updates: corresponding summary table rows
```

---

#### 23. Equipment Page Update SP

`XStudio_Update_Equipment_Page_Details_Usp` — **previously not documented**.

```text
Purpose:
  Updates UI page configuration (page controls, dropdowns, etc.)
  when equipment type configuration changes after initial generation.

Called when:
  Equipment type name, template, or attribute configuration
  changes in ways that affect generated UI pages/screens.

Agent warning:
  Do not call this SP casually.
  It modifies XStudio page and control configuration for the equipment entity.
  Incorrect use can break asset configuration screens.
  Only call through platform UI flow.
```

#### 24. Equipment Type Delete Cascade — Full Sequence

`XStudio_Delete_Equipment_Type_Related_Table_Usp` performs a **full cascade delete** in this order:

```text
1. Resolve SystemID and DataDatabaseName from ConfigurationDatabaseName.

2. Get full related table list via Xstudio_Get_Equipment_type_Related_Tables_Usp.

3. Clean up schedule task:
   - Delete from XStudio_ScheduleTask_Mst_Tbl where ParentID = SummaryScheduleID.
   - If no remaining schedule tasks, disable XStudio_Schedule_Mst_Tbl and reset IsReset parameter.

4. Cursor over XStudio_Block_Entities_Mst_Tbl for all block entities where EquipmentType = @equipmenttypeid:
   - DROP TABLE IF EXISTS on block table in data database.
   - DELETE row from XStudio_Block_Entities_Mst_Tbl.

5. Cursor over XStudio_Summary_Mst_Tbl for all summaries linked to this equipment type:
   - Execute Xstudio_Delete_Summary_Configuration_Usp for each summary.

6. Cursor over XStudio_Entities_Mst_Tbl for all entities linked to this equipment type:
   - DELETE from XStudio_AttributeProperties_Mst_Tbl (attribute properties).
   - DELETE from XStudio_Attribute_Mst_Tbl (attribute definitions).
   - DROP TABLE IF EXISTS (physical data table in data database).
   - DROP VIEW IF EXISTS (generated LV list view).
   - DROP PROCEDURE IF EXISTS (generated historian SP).
   - UPDATE XStudio_Entities_Mst_Tbl.IsDeleted = 1 (soft delete entity).
   - DELETE generated menu items.
```

Agent rule:

```text
Equipment type delete is a multi-step cascade.
It touches: schedule tasks, block tables, summary config, attribute metadata, physical tables, views, SPs, menu items.
There is NO undo.
Never soft-delete an Equipment_Type_Mst_Tbl row with production/historian data.
```

---

#### 25. `XStudio_Attribute_Equipment_Mapping_USP` — Corrected Note

In earlier documentation, `Xstudio_Attribute_Equipment_Mapping_USP` was listed as "general purpose / inspect before using".

From the SP line list, this is the **general equipment attribute mapping operation** used when mapping attributes that do not go through the specialized tag-mapping TVP path.

Confirmed behavior pattern:

```text
Resolves @EquipmentTypeID → Equipment_Type_Mst_Tbl → generated tables.
Operates on <EquipmentType>_Tag_Mapping_Tbl.
Used for single-attribute / single-equipment mapping operations.
```

Agent rule:

```text
Prefer XStudio_AssetStructure_Update_Equipment_And_Attribute_Mapping_Usp
(TVP-based batch mapping) for bulk tag load operations.
Use Xstudio_Attribute_Equipment_Mapping_USP only for single-attribute operations
where the TVP path is not available.
```

#### 26. Add/Alter Equipment Attribute SPs — Full Pair

Two SPs handle attribute management on generated equipment entities:

##### 26.1 `XStudio_Add_Or_Alter_Equipment_Attribute_Usp`

```text
Purpose:
  Adds or modifies a general attribute on the generated equipment entity.
  Use when you need to add a non-tag-mapped attribute to the equipment master.

Example use cases:
  Adding a status text attribute to EAF_Mst_Tbl entity.

Warning:
```

```text
Purpose:
  Adds or modifies a tag-specific attribute on the generated equipment entity.

  and tag mapping via the equipment/tag datatype.

When to use:
  When you need a new process/historian tag attribute to appear in
  template details and tag mapping for an existing equipment type.

Required datatype for tag mapping participation:
  EC8BE677-39DB-4945-A62F-70D4A4F9CE78
```

## 7. Asset Structure Historian Blocks

**Source file:** `01_CANONICAL_REFERENCE/asset_structure_historian_blocks.md`  
**File type:** `.md`  
**Size:** `15906` bytes  
**Modified on:** `2026-05-04 18:17:49`  
**SHA256:** `46af68ab54e7`  
**Encoding used:** `utf-8`

> Start of imported document.

### Asset Structure Historian Blocks

#### Purpose

Historian block tables store interval-based equipment data for XStudio Asset Structure equipment types.

They are generated from equipment type configuration and populated by XStudio historian/block processes. Agents must treat block tables as platform-generated runtime structures, not as normal custom MES transaction tables.

Use this document when an agent needs to understand, validate, troubleshoot, or design around:

```text
<EquipmentType>_<Frequency>
<EquipmentType>_Block
XStudio_Block_Entities_Mst_Tbl
XStudio_Generate_Block_Entry_USP
XStudio_Insert_Block_Equipment_data_usp
XStudio_Find_Block_Data_Usp
XStudio_Historian_Block_Recalculate_USP
```

Batch table behavior is intentionally out of scope.

---

#### Mandatory Agent Rules

```text
1. Do not manually create block tables unless reproducing XStudio generator behavior in a controlled migration script.
2. Do not directly insert production values into block tables unless the specific XStudio runtime/write path requires it.
3. Do not assume ReportDate = calendar date.
4. Do not query a block table directly for UI behavior; use XStudio_Find_Block_Data_Usp or the platform-generated LV path.
5. Do not create unnecessary frequencies. Frequency creates physical tables and runtime load.
6. Do not rename generated block tables.
7. Do not change equipment type frequency after live data exists.
8. Always validate block metadata in XStudio_Block_Entities_Mst_Tbl.
9. Always validate that EquipmentID points to active rows in <EquipmentType>_Mst_Tbl.
10. Use recalculation SPs after tag remapping, outage recovery, or formula changes.
```

---

#### Where Blocks Fit in Asset Structure

Asset Structure flow:

```text
Equipment Type
-> Generated current data entity: <EquipmentType>_Data
-> Generated block entity/table: <EquipmentType>_<Frequency> or <EquipmentType>_Block
-> Block row generation
-> Historian/value calculation
-> Summary scheduling
-> Runtime block query
```

Block tables are the interval-level storage layer used for historian-backed equipment data, summary calculation, and time-window analysis.

---

#### Naming Convention

If frequency is configured during Equipment Type creation:

```text
<EquipmentType>_<Frequency>
```

Examples:

```text
EAF_5
BlastFurnace_5
RollingMillStand_1
Turbine_15
```

If no frequency suffix applies but block behavior is generated:

```text
<EquipmentType>_Block
```

Important:

```text
Frequency is structural.
It affects physical table name, block trigger, generated historian SPs, summary configuration, and runtime scheduling.
```

Do not treat frequency as a cosmetic UI value.

---

#### Main Stored Procedures

| Stored Procedure | Role |
|---|---|
| `XStudio_Create_Block_Tbl_Usp` | Creates the physical block table by copying schema from a source entity/table |
| `XStudio_Create_Block_Table_Trigger_Usp` | Creates `XStudio_TRG_<BlockEntityName>` on the block table |
| `XStudio_Generate_Block_Entry_USP` | Generates expected interval rows for all active equipment instances |
| `XStudio_Insert_Block_Equipment_data_usp` | Generates or backfills interval rows for one equipment instance |
| `XStudio_Find_Block_Data_Usp` | Runtime read/query path for block data |
| `XStudio_Historian_Block_Recalculate_USP` | Recalculates block/historian values |
| `XStudio_Update_Historian_Data_In_Entity_Usp` | Bulk-updates generated entity attributes from historian tag data |

---

#### Block Table Creation

`XStudio_Create_Block_Tbl_Usp` creates the block table.

Observed behavior:

```text
1. Read the source entity/table schema.
2. Inspect sys.columns, data types, identity/default metadata.
3. Generate CREATE TABLE dynamically for the block entity/table.
4. Register block metadata in XStudio_Block_Entities_Mst_Tbl.
5. Create or attach block-related trigger behavior.
```

Agent implication:

```text
Block table schema is derived from the generated equipment data entity.
Do not design block schema independently from <EquipmentType>_Data / equipment entity metadata.
```

---

#### Block Row Generation

##### All-equipment generation

`XStudio_Generate_Block_Entry_USP` generates interval rows for all active equipment instances of an equipment type.

Runtime resolution:

```text
Input:
  @SystemID
  @TableName

Resolve:
  XStudio_Block_Entities_Mst_Tbl
  -> parent/source entity
  -> block table/database
  -> StartTime
  -> EndTime

Resolve:
  XStudio_Entities_Mst_Tbl
  -> EquipmentType
  -> SummaryInterval
  -> BlockType

Resolve:
  EquipmentTypeID
  -> Equipment_Type_Mst_Tbl.Name
  -> <EquipmentType>_Mst_Tbl
  -> active equipment rows
```

For each interval, XStudio inserts one row per active equipment instance if the row does not already exist.

Typical insert shape:

```sql
INSERT INTO <BlockTable>
(
    EquipmentID,
    EntryDateTime,
    ReportDate,
    Source
)
SELECT
    ID,
    @EntryDateTime,
    @ReportDate,
    'T-SQL'
FROM <EquipmentType>_Mst_Tbl
WHERE IsDeleted = 0
AND NOT EXISTS
(
    -- same EquipmentID + EntryDateTime + ReportDate already exists
);
```

##### Single-equipment generation / backfill

`XStudio_Insert_Block_Equipment_data_usp` generates or backfills interval rows for one equipment instance.

Use cases:

```text
new equipment instance added after block tables exist
commissioning backfill
missing interval recovery for one equipment
controlled repair after metadata correction
```

Typical behavior:

```text
EquipmentTypeID + EquipmentID
-> resolve <EquipmentType>_Mst_Tbl
-> find relevant block entities for that equipment type
-> calculate aligned interval end time
-> insert missing EquipmentID + EntryDateTime + ReportDate rows
```

---

#### Typical Block Columns

Minimum expected operational columns:

```text
EquipmentID
EntryDateTime
ReportDate
Source
```

Common platform columns may also exist depending on generated schema:

```text
ID
Name
ParentID
CreatedBy
CreatedOn
ModifiedBy
ModifiedOn
IsDeleted
IsSystem
HostAddress
IsProcessed
```

Process-value columns depend on generated equipment/tag attributes.

Example for `EAF_5`:

```text
EquipmentID
EntryDateTime
ReportDate
TapTemperature
PowerKWh
OxygenFlow
ElectrodeCurrent
FurnacePressure
Source
```

Do not assume every block table has the same process columns.

---

#### ReportDate Logic

`ReportDate` is operational-day aware.

It is not always:

```sql
CAST(EntryDateTime AS date)
```

If the system has a configured `DayStartTime`:

```text
If EntryDateTime time is between 00:00 and DayStartTime
-> ReportDate = previous calendar date

Else
-> ReportDate = EntryDateTime date
```

Example:

```text
DayStartTime = 06:00
EntryDateTime = 2026-05-04 01:30
ReportDate = 2026-05-03
```

Reason:

```text
Steel plant operational days often start at 06:00, not midnight.
Shift, day, heat, campaign, and utility summaries must follow operational day logic.
```

Agent rule:

```text
When writing validation or reports over block data, group by ReportDate, not only by CAST(EntryDateTime AS date).
```

---

#### Block Trigger

`XStudio_Create_Block_Table_Trigger_Usp` creates:

```text
XStudio_TRG_<BlockEntityName>
```

on the generated block table.

On insert/update:

```text
1. Read inserted row ID.
2. If a custom SP named XSTUDIO_<BlockEntityName>_USP exists:
      execute it for the inserted ID.
3. If summary scheduling applies:
      call the XStudio summary scheduling path.
```

Meaning:

```text
Block row insertion can trigger custom block processing and summary recalculation.
```

Agent warning:

```text
Bulk repair scripts that insert/update block rows may trigger downstream processing.
Always account for trigger side effects.
```

---

#### Reading Block Data

`XStudio_Find_Block_Data_Usp` is the runtime block read path.

Inputs include:

```text
@SystemID
@LVID
@StartDate
@EndDate
@Type
@Filter
@EquipmentID
```

Runtime behavior:

```text
1. Resolve LV metadata.
2. Resolve entity metadata.
3. Resolve block entity/table metadata.
4. Build dynamic query across matching block tables.
5. Filter by date window.
6. Filter by EquipmentID when supplied.
7. Apply additional filter when supplied.
8. Return rows, typically capped and sorted descending.
```

Important distinction:

```text
Fixed blocks use EntryDateTime for time filtering.
Non-fixed block paths may use StartTime or other configured time fields.
```

Agent rule:

```text
For UI/LV behavior, validate XStudio_Find_Block_Data_Usp path rather than directly selecting from <EquipmentType>_<Frequency>.
```

---

#### Historian Value Flow

Block row generation creates interval rows. Historian/value processing fills or recalculates values.

Simplified runtime flow:

```text
Historian / OSI PI / OPC source
-> XStudio_Tag_Mst_Tbl
-> XStudio_Tag_Trn_Tbl
-> equipment tag mapping
-> generated historian SP
-> <EquipmentType>_Data and/or <EquipmentType>_<Frequency>
-> summary aggregation
```

Relevant SPs:

| SP | Role |
|---|---|
| `XStudio_Get_OSI_Datasource_USP` | Reads selected OSI datasource configuration |
| `XStudio_Get_OSI_Tag_USP` | Lists active OSI PI tags from tag master |
| `XStudio_Save_OSIPI_Tag_Data_Usp` | Saves incoming OSI PI tag values into tag transaction table |
| `XStudio_Update_Historian_Data_In_Entity_Usp` | Updates target generated entity attributes from tag data |

Generated historian SP pattern:

```text
Xstudio_Historian_<EntityName>_usp
```

Example:

```text
Xstudio_Historian_EAF_5_usp
Xstudio_Historian_BlastFurnace_5_usp
```

---

#### Recalculation

`XStudio_Historian_Block_Recalculate_USP` recalculates block/historian values.

Supported scenarios:

```text
all rows in date range
single attribute
single equipment
single equipment + attribute
```

Use recalculation after:

```text
historian outage
late PI values
tag remapping
formula correction
template attribute correction
incorrect range/value processing
missed interval processing
commissioning data repair
```

Generic recalculation concept:

```text
Block rows already exist
-> identify EntryDateTime intervals
-> execute generated Xstudio_Historian_<EntityName>_usp
-> recompute requested attributes/equipment/date range
```

Agent rule:

```text
If tag mapping or formula logic changes after data exists, block row regeneration alone is insufficient.
Run or recommend historian block recalculation.
```

---

#### Summary Interaction

Block tables feed XStudio summary aggregation.

Summary-related behavior:

```text
Block trigger can schedule summary recalculation.
Summary aggregation can be template-aware.
Summary rows may aggregate minute/hour/shift/day/month values.
```

Relevant summary SP families:

```text
XStudio_Historian_Create_Summary_SP_USP
XStudio_Historian_Create_Summary_Minute_SP_Usp
XStudio_Historian_Create_Summary_Hour_SP_Usp
XStudio_Historian_Create_Summary_Shift_SP_Usp
XStudio_Historian_Create_Summary_Day_SP_Usp
XStudio_Historian_Create_Summary_Month_SP_Usp
XStudio_Summary_Historian_Schedule_Task_Usp
```

Agent implication:

```text
When block data changes historically, summary data may also need recalculation.
```

---

#### LMEL Block Frequency Strategy

Frequency creates physical objects and runtime load. Keep frequencies standardized.

Recommended options:

| Frequency | Use |
|---:|---|
| 1 minute | High-frequency critical equipment where required |
| 5 minutes | Normal process equipment data |
| 15 minutes | Utilities, CPP, slower process balances |
| 60 minutes | Low-frequency balances and periodic accounting |

Recommended Day-1 approach:

| Area / Equipment | Suggested Frequency |
|---|---:|
| EAF / LRF / VD / CCM | 1 or 5 min |
| Blast Furnace | 5 or 15 min |
| DRI Kiln | 5 or 15 min |
| Pellet Plant major equipment | 5 or 15 min |
| Rolling Mill critical stands | 1 or 5 min |
| CPP Boiler / Turbine / Generator | 5 or 15 min |
| Yard/static assets | no block unless required |

Agent rule:

```text
Do not create separate frequencies for minor convenience.
Each frequency variant creates additional generated objects and maintenance load.
```

---

#### Postflight Checks

Use these checks after equipment type/block generation or during troubleshooting.

##### 1. Confirm block entity metadata exists

```sql
SELECT *
FROM XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_Block_Entities_Mst_Tbl
WHERE IsDeleted = 0
ORDER BY CreatedOn DESC;
```

##### 2. Confirm generated block table exists

```sql
SELECT TABLE_SCHEMA, TABLE_NAME
FROM XStudio_XMES.INFORMATION_SCHEMA.TABLES
WHERE TABLE_NAME IN ('EAF_5', 'BlastFurnace_5', 'RollingMillStand_1');
```

##### 3. Confirm active equipment rows exist

```sql
SELECT ID, Name, TemplateID, AreaID, IsDeleted
FROM XStudio_XMES.dbo.EAF_Mst_Tbl
WHERE IsDeleted = 0;
```

##### 4. Confirm block rows exist for equipment

```sql
SELECT EquipmentID, EntryDateTime, ReportDate, Source, COUNT(*) AS RowCount
FROM XStudio_XMES.dbo.EAF_5
WHERE IsDeleted = 0
GROUP BY EquipmentID, EntryDateTime, ReportDate, Source
ORDER BY EntryDateTime DESC;
```

##### 5. Detect duplicate block rows

```sql
SELECT EquipmentID, EntryDateTime, ReportDate, COUNT(*) AS DuplicateCount
FROM XStudio_XMES.dbo.EAF_5
WHERE IsDeleted = 0
GROUP BY EquipmentID, EntryDateTime, ReportDate
HAVING COUNT(*) > 1;
```

##### 6. Validate operational-day grouping

```sql
SELECT TOP 100
    EquipmentID,
    EntryDateTime,
    ReportDate
FROM XStudio_XMES.dbo.EAF_5
WHERE CAST(EntryDateTime AS time) < '06:00'
ORDER BY EntryDateTime DESC;
```

##### 7. Confirm generated historian SP exists

```sql
SELECT name
FROM XStudio_XMES.sys.procedures
WHERE name LIKE 'Xstudio_Historian_EAF%';
```

##### 8. Confirm block trigger exists

```sql
SELECT name
FROM XStudio_XMES.sys.triggers
WHERE name = 'XStudio_TRG_EAF_5';
```

---

#### Common Failure Modes

| Symptom | Likely Cause | Agent Action |
|---|---|---|
| No block rows generated | No active equipment instances | Check `<EquipmentType>_Mst_Tbl` |
| No block rows generated | Missing `XStudio_Block_Entities_Mst_Tbl` metadata | Validate block entity registration |
| Rows generated with wrong date | Misunderstood `ReportDate` logic | Check system `DayStartTime` |
| Duplicate rows | Broken NOT EXISTS condition or manual insert | Run duplicate check by EquipmentID + EntryDateTime + ReportDate |
| Values are NULL | Rows exist but historian values not processed | Check tag mapping and generated historian SP |
| One new equipment has no block rows | Equipment was created after block setup | Use/check `XStudio_Insert_Block_Equipment_data_usp` |
| Recalculated values not reflected | Summary not recalculated | Check summary scheduler/summary SPs |
| UI block grid missing data | LV/entity/block metadata resolution issue | Test `XStudio_Find_Block_Data_Usp` path |

---

#### Do / Do Not

##### Do

```text
Use block metadata to discover generated block tables.
Use XStudio_Find_Block_Data_Usp for runtime block reads.
Use ReportDate for operational-day summaries.
Use recalculation after historian/tag/formula changes.
Keep frequency strategy simple and standardized.
Validate generated historian SPs and triggers.
```

##### Do Not

```text
Do not treat block tables as ordinary transaction tables.
Do not manually create <EquipmentType>_<Frequency> tables in production.
Do not assume calendar date equals ReportDate.
Do not create many frequency variants without a real operational reason.
Do not bypass tag mapping and write historian tag names into MES transaction tables.
Do not ignore trigger and summary side effects during repair scripts.
```

---

#### Agent Output Contract

When an agent writes Asset Structure block/historian documentation, scripts, or validation notes, it must include:

```text
1. EquipmentType name
2. Expected block table name
3. Frequency / SummaryInterval
4. Whether block metadata exists
5. Source equipment instance table
6. Required active equipment rows
7. Expected block trigger name
8. Expected generated historian SP name
9. ReportDate handling note
10. Recalculation recommendation if mapping/formula/tag changes are involved
```

> End of imported document.
