## 8. Asset Structure Runtime Views

**Source file:** `01_CANONICAL_REFERENCE/asset_structure_runtime_views.md`  
**File type:** `.md`  
**Size:** `11487` bytes  
**Modified on:** `2026-05-04 18:19:29`  
**SHA256:** `eb778a0bce89`  
**Encoding used:** `utf-8`

> Start of imported document.

### Asset Structure Runtime Views

#### Purpose

XStudio generates consolidated runtime views because every equipment type owns its own physical table family.

Example:

```text
EAF_Mst_Tbl
LRF_Mst_Tbl
BlastFurnace_Mst_Tbl
RollingMillStand_Mst_Tbl
```

A generic selector, mapping screen, dashboard, or cross-plant lookup cannot bind directly to one equipment-type table. XStudio solves this by regenerating union views across all active equipment types.

Agent rule:

```text
Use generated runtime views for generic cross-equipment lookup.
Use typed equipment tables only when the transaction is plant-specific and the equipment type is known.
```

---

#### Generated Runtime View Set

| View | Generated By | Purpose |
|---|---|---|
| `XStudio_Equipment_Mst_Vw` | `XStudio_Create_Equipment_View_Usp` | Global equipment instance selector |
| `XStudio_Equipment_Template_Mst_Vw` | `XStudio_Create_Equipment_Template_View_Usp` | Global template selector |
| `XStudio_Equipment_Tag_Mst_Vw` | `XStudio_Create_Equipment_Tag_View_Usp` | Global tag mapping selector/browser |

These views are regenerated after equipment type insert/update through the `Equipment_Type_TRG` trigger.

Do not manually rename these views or correct their generated column names.

---

#### 1. `XStudio_Equipment_Mst_Vw`

##### Purpose

Union view over all active `<EquipmentType>_Mst_Tbl` equipment instance tables.

Use this view when a screen or stored procedure needs to select equipment across equipment types.

Examples:

```text
operator equipment selector
cross-plant asset browser
delay/event equipment selector
maintenance lookup
historian mapping lookup
```

##### Source Pattern

For each active equipment type, XStudio builds a query similar to:

```sql
SELECT
    dm.ID,
    dm.Name AS EquipmentName,
    '<EquipmentTypeID>' AS EquipmetTypeID,
    '<EquipmentTypeName>' AS EquipmentTypeName,
    dm.TemplateID,
    dtm.Name AS TemplateName,
    dm.AreaID,
    dm.MaterialID,
    NULL AS MaterialName
FROM <EquipmentType>_Mst_Tbl dm
LEFT JOIN <EquipmentType>_Attribute_Tag_Template_Mst_Tbl dtm
    ON dm.TemplateID = dtm.ID
WHERE ISNULL(dm.IsDeleted, 0) = 0
```

Actual generated SQL may include XBatch material joins for storage/batch-enabled equipment paths. Batch behavior is out of scope for this KB document.

##### Expected Columns

```text
ID
EquipmentName
EquipmetTypeID
EquipmentTypeName
TemplateID
TemplateName
AreaID
MaterialID
MaterialName
```

Critical spelling rule:

```text
Column name is EquipmetTypeID, not EquipmentTypeID.
```

Agents must preserve this spelling when binding to the generated view.

##### Recommended Use

Use `XStudio_Equipment_Mst_Vw` for generic equipment selection.

```text
Generic table pattern:
  EquipmentTypeID / EquipmetTypeID from runtime view
  EquipmentID
```

Use typed fields when the equipment type is fixed by domain logic.

```text
XMES_SMS_Heat_Trn_Tbl.EAFID       → EAF_Mst_Tbl.ID
XMES_SMS_Heat_Trn_Tbl.LRFID       → LRF_Mst_Tbl.ID
XMES_BF_Tap_Trn_Tbl.BFID          → BlastFurnace_Mst_Tbl.ID
XMES_RM_Rolling_Trn_Tbl.StandID   → RollingMillStand_Mst_Tbl.ID
XMES_CPP_Generation_Trn_Tbl.TGID  → Turbine_Mst_Tbl.ID
```

---

#### 2. `XStudio_Equipment_Template_Mst_Vw`

##### Purpose

Union view over all active `<EquipmentType>_Attribute_Tag_Template_Mst_Tbl` template header tables.

Use this view when a screen or SP needs to list templates across equipment types.

Examples:

```text
global template selector
template copy utility
asset configuration screen
summary aggregation setup
```

##### Expected Columns

```text
EquipmetTypeID
EquipmentTypeName
ID
Name
```

Critical spelling rule:

```text
Column name is EquipmetTypeID, not EquipmentTypeID.
```

##### Agent Use

Use this view for cross-type template lookup only.

If an agent is configuring template details for a specific equipment type, it should use the typed table:

```text
<EquipmentType>_Attribute_Tag_Template_Dtl_Tbl
```

Example:

```text
Global template lookup:
  XStudio_Equipment_Template_Mst_Vw

EAF template detail edit:
  EAF_Attribute_Tag_Template_Dtl_Tbl
```

---

#### 3. `XStudio_Equipment_Tag_Mst_Vw`

##### Purpose

Union view over all active `<EquipmentType>_Tag_Mapping_Tbl` tables.

Use this view when a screen or process needs to inspect tag mappings across equipment types.

Examples:

```text
historian tag browser
asset tag validation
tag mapping audit
cross-plant tag search
SCADA/PI integration diagnostics
```

##### Important Columns

```text
ID
Name
ParentID
CreatedBy
ModifiedBy
CreatedOn
ModifiedOn
IsDeleted
IsSystem
AssignedUserID
HostAddress
DbSyncStatus
MobileSyncStatus
Source
EquipmentID
EquipmentName
Attribute
DataSourceID
TagName
InstrumentTag
HHRange
HRange
LRange
LLRange
ColorGTHH
ColorBWHHH
ColorBWHL
ColorBWLLL
ColorLTLL
DocumentGTHH
DocumentBWHHH
DocumentBWHL
DocumentBWLLL
DocumentLTLL
Type
IsXBatchTag
TagType
FunctionType
```

##### Agent Use

Use `XStudio_Equipment_Tag_Mst_Vw` for read-only cross-equipment mapping inspection.

Do not update this view directly.

To update tag names, use the platform mapping flow or the mapping SP:

```text
XStudio_AssetStructure_Update_Equipment_And_Attribute_Mapping_Usp
```

For event tag mappings, use:

```text
XStudio_AssetStructure_Update_Event_Tag_Mapping_Usp
```

---

#### 4. Tag Classification Logic

`XStudio_Equipment_Tag_Mst_Vw` derives `TagType` and `FunctionType` from the mapping row name pattern.

| Mapping Row Name Pattern | Generated TagType | Generated FunctionType |
|---|---|---|
| contains `Weight` | `Weight` | `Read` |
| contains `IsStart` | `Command` | `Write` |
| contains `IsHold` | `Command` | `Write` |
| contains `IsRestart` | `Command` | `Write` |
| contains `IsAbort` | `Command` | `Write` |
| contains `IsReset` | `Command` | `Write` |
| contains `IsReady` | `Status` | `Read` |
| contains `IsHeld` | `Status` | `Read` |
| contains `IsRun` | `Status` | `Read` |
| contains `IsDone` | `Status` | `Read` |
| contains `IsInterlock` | `Status` | `Read` |
| contains `IsAborted` | `Status` | `Read` |
| contains `Error` | `Interlock` | `Read` |
| any other name | `Parameter` | `Parameter` |

Agent rule:

```text
Attribute names are not cosmetic. They affect generated tag classification.
```

---

#### 5. LMEL Naming Implication

For LMEL templates, use deliberate attribute names so the generated tag view classifies tags correctly.

Recommended examples:

```text
IsStartHeat        → Command / Write
IsRunHeat          → Status / Read
IsDoneHeat         → Status / Read
IsAbortHeat        → Command / Write
IsInterlockReady   → Status / Read
ErrorCode          → Interlock / Read
ChargeWeight       → Weight / Read
TapWeight          → Weight / Read
TapTemperature     → Parameter / Parameter
PowerKWh           → Parameter / Parameter
OxygenFlow         → Parameter / Parameter
ElectrodeCurrent   → Parameter / Parameter
FurnacePressure    → Parameter / Parameter
```

Avoid ambiguous names for command/status/interlock/weight tags.

Poor examples:

```text
Start
Running
Done
Fault
Wt
Temp1
Signal01
```

Better examples:

```text
IsStartHeat
IsRunHeat
IsDoneHeat
ErrorCode
TapWeight
TapTemperature
```

---

#### 6. Agent Decision Rules

##### Use `XStudio_Equipment_Mst_Vw` when:

```text
screen needs all equipment
SP accepts equipment from multiple equipment types
mapping or diagnostic screen spans all plants
agent does not know equipment type at design time
```

##### Use typed equipment tables when:

```text
transaction belongs to a fixed equipment type
plant-specific logic needs domain clarity
workflow depends on a specific asset class
```

Examples:

```text
EAF heat execution        → EAF_Mst_Tbl
BF tap execution          → BlastFurnace_Mst_Tbl
RM stand event            → RollingMillStand_Mst_Tbl
CPP turbine generation    → Turbine_Mst_Tbl
Generic delay event       → XStudio_Equipment_Mst_Vw + EquipmentID
```

##### Do not:

```text
Do not create a separate XMES_Equipment_Mst_Tbl as the source of truth.
Do not manually edit generated view definitions unless explicitly repairing platform generation.
Do not rename EquipmetTypeID to EquipmentTypeID in SQL bound to generated views.
Do not update tag mappings through XStudio_Equipment_Tag_Mst_Vw.
Do not assume every equipment type has identical columns outside the consolidated view contract.
```

---

#### 7. Postflight Checks

Use these checks after equipment type generation or after regenerating runtime views.

##### 7.1 Check views exist

```sql
SELECT name
FROM XStudio_XMES.sys.views
WHERE name IN
(
    'XStudio_Equipment_Mst_Vw',
    'XStudio_Equipment_Template_Mst_Vw',
    'XStudio_Equipment_Tag_Mst_Vw'
);
```

Expected:

```text
3 rows
```

##### 7.2 Check `EquipmetTypeID` spelling exists

```sql
SELECT COLUMN_NAME
FROM XStudio_XMES.INFORMATION_SCHEMA.COLUMNS
WHERE TABLE_NAME = 'XStudio_Equipment_Mst_Vw'
  AND COLUMN_NAME = 'EquipmetTypeID';
```

Expected:

```text
1 row
```

##### 7.3 Check equipment view returns active equipment

```sql
SELECT TOP 20
    ID,
    EquipmentName,
    EquipmetTypeID,
    EquipmentTypeName,
    TemplateID,
    TemplateName,
    AreaID
FROM XStudio_XMES.dbo.XStudio_Equipment_Mst_Vw
ORDER BY EquipmentTypeName, EquipmentName;
```

##### 7.4 Check tag classification

```sql
SELECT TOP 50
    EquipmentName,
    Attribute,
    Name,
    TagName,
    TagType,
    FunctionType
FROM XStudio_XMES.dbo.XStudio_Equipment_Tag_Mst_Vw
WHERE ISNULL(IsDeleted, 0) = 0
ORDER BY EquipmentName, Attribute;
```

Review:

```text
IsStart*      → Command / Write
IsRun*        → Status / Read
*Weight*      → Weight / Read
*Error*       → Interlock / Read
normal tags   → Parameter / Parameter
```

##### 7.5 Check generic equipment selector safety

```sql
SELECT
    EquipmentTypeName,
    COUNT(*) AS EquipmentCount
FROM XStudio_XMES.dbo.XStudio_Equipment_Mst_Vw
GROUP BY EquipmentTypeName
ORDER BY EquipmentTypeName;
```

Use this to confirm all generated equipment types are included in the consolidated selector.

---

#### 8. Failure Modes

| Symptom | Likely Cause | Agent Fix |
|---|---|---|
| Equipment type exists but not visible in global selector | Runtime view not regenerated | Run/trigger `XStudio_Create_Equipment_View_Usp` through supported platform flow |
| Template exists but not visible globally | Template view not regenerated | Regenerate `XStudio_Equipment_Template_Mst_Vw` through supported platform flow |
| Tag mappings missing from global tag browser | Mapping rows missing or tag view stale | Confirm instance/template triggers ran; regenerate tag view |
| SQL error: `EquipmentTypeID` invalid column | Agent used corrected spelling | Use generated spelling `EquipmetTypeID` |
| Tag classified as Parameter instead of Command | Attribute name lacks `IsStart`/`IsHold`/etc. | Rename or define attribute using classification-aware naming before mapping |
| Generic dropdown shows only one equipment type | Bound to typed table instead of consolidated view | Bind to `XStudio_Equipment_Mst_Vw` |

---

#### 9. Minimal Agent Contract

When an agent designs a screen, SP, or lookup that uses Asset Structure runtime views, it must specify:

```text
1. Is the lookup generic or typed?
2. If generic, use XStudio_Equipment_Mst_Vw.
3. If typed, name the exact <EquipmentType>_Mst_Tbl.
4. If using runtime view, preserve EquipmetTypeID spelling.
5. If using tag view, treat it as read-only.
6. If creating tag attributes, apply classification-aware naming.
7. Add postflight checks for view existence and expected columns.
```

> End of imported document.

## 9. Asset Structure Triggers And Mapping

**Source file:** `01_CANONICAL_REFERENCE/asset_structure_triggers_and_mapping.md`  
**File type:** `.md`  
**Size:** `13857` bytes  
**Modified on:** `2026-05-04 19:02:53`  
**SHA256:** `958f6d03bc03`  
**Encoding used:** `utf-8`

> Start of imported document.

### Asset Structure Triggers and Mapping

#### Purpose

This document explains how XStudio keeps Asset Structure templates, equipment instances, and tag mapping tables synchronized.

Agent use this document when generating or validating:

```text
asset templates
equipment instance loads
normal tag mappings
event tag mappings
postflight checks for generated mapping rows
```

Do not treat tag mapping as an isolated table load. In XStudio Asset Structure, mapping rows are normally created and maintained by triggers generated for each equipment type.

Batch table behavior is intentionally out of scope.

---

#### 1. Trigger Generator

Stored procedure:

```text
XStudio_Create_Equipment_Mst_And_Template_Dtl_Trigger_Usp
```

This procedure creates three triggers for every equipment type:

```text
XStudio_<EquipmentType>_Mst_Tbl_TRG
XStudio_<EquipmentType>_Attribute_Tag_Template_Mst_Tbl_TRG
XStudio_<EquipmentType>_Attribute_Tag_Template_Dtl_Tbl_TRG
```

Example for equipment type `EAF`:

```text
XStudio_EAF_Mst_Tbl_TRG
XStudio_EAF_Attribute_Tag_Template_Mst_Tbl_TRG
XStudio_EAF_Attribute_Tag_Template_Dtl_Tbl_TRG
```

These triggers are part of the generated equipment module. Agents must assume they exist after successful equipment type generation and must validate them during postflight.

---

#### 2. Trigger Dependency Model

Correct sequence:

```text
Equipment Type
→ generated equipment tables
→ trigger generator
→ template header
→ template detail rows
→ equipment instance
→ mapping rows
→ tag names assigned
```

Do not reverse the sequence. Mapping rows depend on templates, template details, and equipment instances.

---

#### 3. Equipment Instance Trigger

Trigger pattern:

```text
XStudio_<EquipmentType>_Mst_Tbl_TRG
```

Source table:

```text
<EquipmentType>_Mst_Tbl
```

##### 3.1 Active Insert or Update

When a non-deleted row is inserted or updated in the equipment instance table:

```text
<EquipmentType>_Mst_Tbl active row
→ XStudio_Add_Attribute_Equipment_Mapping_Usp
→ creates or updates tag mapping rows for that equipment instance
```

Meaning:

```text
Creating an equipment instance is not just master-data insertion.
It also creates mapping records for the template attributes used by that instance.
```

Example:

```text
EAF-1 inserted into EAF_Mst_Tbl with TemplateID = EAF_100T_Standard
→ trigger creates EAF_Tag_Mapping_Tbl rows for attributes in EAF_100T_Standard
```

##### 3.2 Soft Delete

When an equipment instance is soft-deleted:

```text
<EquipmentType>_Mst_Tbl.IsDeleted = 1
→ XStudio_Delete_Attribute_Equipment_Mapping_Usp
→ related mapping rows removed or soft-deleted
```

##### 3.3 Source Exclusions

The trigger skips rows whose source is:

```text
Synchronizer
HandHeld
```

Agent rule:

```text
Do not rely on mapping-row auto-generation when records are inserted with Source = Synchronizer or Source = HandHeld.
```

For scripted configuration, use the source value expected by the target environment and validate mapping rows after insert.

---

#### 4. Template Master Trigger

Trigger pattern:

```text
XStudio_<EquipmentType>_Attribute_Tag_Template_Mst_Tbl_TRG
```

Source table:

```text
<EquipmentType>_Attribute_Tag_Template_Mst_Tbl
```

##### 4.1 Behavior

When a template header is created or updated:

```text
new template header
→ trigger reads XStudio metadata
→ finds equipment/tag attributes linked to the equipment type
→ inserts default rows into <EquipmentType>_Attribute_Tag_Template_Dtl_Tbl
→ applies default alarm/color values
```

##### 4.2 Required Attribute Datatype

Only attributes with this datatype are auto-added into template details:

```text
EC8BE677-39DB-4945-A62F-70D4A4F9CE78
```

Current understanding:

```text
This GUID represents XStudio equipment/historian tag attributes.
```

Agent rule:

```text
If an attribute must become a template detail and participate in tag mapping,
do not create it as ordinary Text, Decimal, or Number.
Create it as the equipment/tag datatype used by Asset Structure.
```

Example:

```text
Correct for tag-mapped attributes:
TapTemperature
PowerKWh
OxygenFlow
ElectrodeCurrent
ChargeWeight
IsStartHeat
ErrorCode

Incorrect if tag mapping is required:
ordinary decimal column named TapTemperature
ordinary text column named PowerKWh
```

---

#### 5. Template Detail Trigger

Trigger pattern:

```text
XStudio_<EquipmentType>_Attribute_Tag_Template_Dtl_Tbl_TRG
```

Source table:

```text
<EquipmentType>_Attribute_Tag_Template_Dtl_Tbl
```

This is the synchronization trigger between template configuration and equipment tag mappings.

##### 5.1 Updates Existing Mapping Rows

When template detail values change, the trigger pushes the changed template values into existing tag mapping rows for equipment instances using that template.

Values propagated include:

```text
HHRange
HRange
LRange
LLRange
ColorGTHH
ColorBWHHH
ColorBWHL
ColorBWLLL
ColorLTLL
DocumentGTHH
DocumentBWHHH
DocumentBWHL
DocumentBWLLL
DocumentLTLL
```

Join logic conceptually:

```text
TemplateDetail.ParentID = Equipment.TemplateID
TemplateDetail.Attribute = TagMapping.Attribute
TagMapping.EquipmentID = Equipment.ID
```

Meaning:

```text
Changing EAF_100T_Standard.TapTemperature.HHRange
updates EAF_Tag_Mapping_Tbl rows for all EAF instances using EAF_100T_Standard.
```

##### 5.2 Inserts Missing Mapping Rows

When a new template detail attribute is added:

```text
new template attribute
→ trigger finds existing equipment using that template
→ inserts missing rows into <EquipmentType>_Tag_Mapping_Tbl
```

Example:

```text
Template: EAF_100T_Standard
New attribute: ElectrodeCurrent
Existing equipment: EAF-1, EAF-2

Trigger inserts:
EAF-1 + ElectrodeCurrent
EAF-2 + ElectrodeCurrent
into EAF_Tag_Mapping_Tbl
```

Agent rule:

```text
After adding template detail attributes, validate mapping rows for all existing equipment instances using that template.
```

---

#### 6. Normal Equipment Tag Mapping Update

Stored procedure:

```text
XStudio_AssetStructure_Update_Equipment_And_Attribute_Mapping_Usp
```

Purpose:

```text
Write actual historian/source tag names into <EquipmentType>_Tag_Mapping_Tbl.
```

Expected input concept:

```text
Equipment_TagData TVP:
  Attribute
  Equipment
  TagName

Additional input:
  DatasourceID
```

Behavior:

```text
resolve Equipment name → <EquipmentType>_Mst_Tbl.ID
resolve Attribute → existing mapping row
write TagName
write DataSourceID
write ModifiedBy / ModifiedOn / HostAddress
write Source information
blank TagName becomes NULL
```

Correct use:

```text
Use this SP after mapping rows already exist.
```

Incorrect use:

```text
Do not use this SP as a substitute for creating templates or equipment instances.
It updates generated mapping rows; it does not define the asset model.
```

---

#### 7. Event Tag Mapping Update

Stored procedure:

```text
XStudio_AssetStructure_Update_Event_Tag_Mapping_Usp
```

Purpose:

```text
Write actual event-detection tag names into <EquipmentType>_Event_Tag_Mapping_Tbl.
```

Expected input concept:

```text
Equipment_Event_TagData TVP:
  Attribute
  Equipment
  TagName
```

Behavior:

```text
resolve Equipment name → <EquipmentType>_Mst_Tbl.ID
resolve event Attribute → event mapping row
resolve TagName through XStudio_Tag_Mst_Tbl
write event tag mapping data
```

Use only when event schema is enabled for the equipment type.

---

#### 8. Agent Build Sequence

When generating scripts or data-load plans, use this order:

```text
1. Confirm equipment type exists and generated tables exist.
2. Confirm equipment/tag attributes exist with the Asset Structure equipment/tag datatype.
3. Create template header.
4. Let template master trigger auto-create template detail rows.
5. Insert or update template detail ranges/colors/documents where required.
6. Create equipment instances with TemplateID and AreaID.
7. Let equipment instance trigger create mapping rows.
8. Validate mapping rows exist.
9. Call mapping update SP to assign TagName and DataSourceID.
10. If event schema is enabled, configure event attributes/states/actions.
11. Call event tag mapping update SP to assign event tags.
12. Regenerate or validate consolidated views if needed.
```

---

#### 9. Do / Do Not

##### Do

```text
Use generated triggers as part of the normal Asset Structure lifecycle.
Create proper equipment/tag attributes before creating templates.
Create templates before equipment instances where possible.
Validate template detail rows after template creation.
Validate mapping rows after equipment instance creation.
Use mapping update SPs to assign actual historian tags.
Use generated consolidated views for cross-equipment selectors.
```

##### Do Not

```text
Do not hand-create <EquipmentType>_Tag_Mapping_Tbl rows as the normal approach.
Do not create process tag attributes as ordinary decimal/text columns if they must be tag-mapped.
Do not bypass template detail rows.
Do not update generated view column names such as EquipmetTypeID.
Do not assume rows inserted from Synchronizer or HandHeld will trigger normal mapping behavior.
Do not enable or document batch table behavior in this KB section.
```

---

#### 10. Postflight Checks

Agents must include validation checks after asset template, instance, or mapping work.

##### 10.1 Trigger Existence

For equipment type `EAF`, confirm:

```sql
SELECT name
FROM sys.triggers
WHERE name IN
(
    'XStudio_EAF_Mst_Tbl_TRG',
    'XStudio_EAF_Attribute_Tag_Template_Mst_Tbl_TRG',
    'XStudio_EAF_Attribute_Tag_Template_Dtl_Tbl_TRG'
);
```

Expected:

```text
3 rows
```

##### 10.2 Template Detail Rows Exist

```sql
SELECT COUNT(*) AS TemplateDetailCount
FROM dbo.EAF_Attribute_Tag_Template_Dtl_Tbl
WHERE ParentID = @TemplateID
  AND ISNULL(IsDeleted, 0) = 0;
```

Expected:

```text
TemplateDetailCount > 0 for any active tag-mapped template.
```

##### 10.3 Mapping Rows Exist for Equipment Instance

```sql
SELECT COUNT(*) AS MappingRowCount
FROM dbo.EAF_Tag_Mapping_Tbl
WHERE EquipmentID = @EquipmentID
  AND ISNULL(IsDeleted, 0) = 0;
```

Expected:

```text
MappingRowCount >= number of active template detail attributes for that equipment template.
```

##### 10.4 Mapping Rows Have Datasource and Tags After Mapping Load

```sql
SELECT Attribute, EquipmentID, DataSourceID, TagName
FROM dbo.EAF_Tag_Mapping_Tbl
WHERE EquipmentID = @EquipmentID
  AND ISNULL(IsDeleted, 0) = 0
ORDER BY Attribute;
```

Expected:

```text
DataSourceID populated for mapped rows.
TagName populated where tag mapping is required.
Blank/unmapped tags are allowed only when intentionally left unmapped.
```

##### 10.5 Consolidated Tag View Contains Mapping

```sql
SELECT TOP 50
    EquipmentName,
    Attribute,
    DataSourceID,
    TagName,
    TagType,
    FunctionType
FROM dbo.XStudio_Equipment_Tag_Mst_Vw
WHERE EquipmentName = 'EAF-1'
ORDER BY Attribute;
```

Expected:

```text
Rows exist for mapped EAF-1 attributes.
TagType and FunctionType classify correctly based on attribute/mapping row name.
```

---

#### 11. LMEL Example

Target:

```text
Equipment Type: EAF
Template: EAF_100T_Standard
Equipment: EAF-1
Datasource: OSI PI
```

Correct lifecycle:

```text
1. EAF equipment type exists.
2. EAF tag attributes exist:
   TapTemperature
   PowerKWh
   OxygenFlow
   ChargeWeight
   IsStartHeat
   IsRunHeat
   IsDoneHeat
   ErrorCode

3. Create EAF_100T_Standard template.
4. Template trigger creates detail rows.
5. Set HH/H/L/LL ranges and color/document values.
6. Create EAF-1 with TemplateID = EAF_100T_Standard.
7. Equipment trigger creates EAF_Tag_Mapping_Tbl rows.
8. Use mapping update SP to assign:
   TapTemperature → HIST.SMS.EAF1.TAP_TEMP
   PowerKWh       → HIST.SMS.EAF1.POWER_KWH
   OxygenFlow     → HIST.SMS.EAF1.OXY_FLOW
   ChargeWeight   → HIST.SMS.EAF1.CHARGE_WEIGHT
   IsStartHeat    → HIST.SMS.EAF1.HEAT_START_CMD
   IsRunHeat      → HIST.SMS.EAF1.HEAT_RUN_STS
   IsDoneHeat     → HIST.SMS.EAF1.HEAT_DONE_STS
   ErrorCode      → HIST.SMS.EAF1.ERROR_CODE
```

Expected classification in `XStudio_Equipment_Tag_Mst_Vw`:

```text
ChargeWeight → Weight / Read
IsStartHeat  → Command / Write
IsRunHeat    → Status / Read
IsDoneHeat   → Status / Read
ErrorCode    → Interlock / Read
TapTemperature → Parameter / Parameter
PowerKWh → Parameter / Parameter
OxygenFlow → Parameter / Parameter
```

---

#### 12. Failure Modes

| Symptom | Likely Cause | Fix |
|---|---|---|
| Template created but no detail rows | Attributes are not using equipment/tag datatype | Recreate/add attributes with required datatype and reprocess template |
| Equipment created but no mapping rows | Trigger missing, wrong Source, no template details, or TemplateID missing | Validate trigger, source, TemplateID, template details |
| Mapping update SP does nothing | Mapping rows do not exist or equipment name does not resolve | Validate `<Type>_Mst_Tbl` and `<Type>_Tag_Mapping_Tbl` rows |
| Tags visible in type table but not global view | Consolidated tag view not regenerated or generated view failed | Run/validate `XStudio_Create_Equipment_Tag_View_Usp` |
| TagType classification wrong | Attribute/mapping row name does not match pattern | Rename/standardize attribute naming before production use |
| Event tag mapping missing | Event schema not enabled or event attributes not configured | Validate event tables and event attribute rows |

---

#### 13. Agent Output Contract

When an agent creates or modifies Asset Structure templates, instances, or mappings, output must include:

```text
1. Equipment type name.
2. Generated table names used.
3. Template names created/updated.
4. Equipment instances created/updated.
5. Attributes expected in template details.
6. Mapping update source and datasource ID.
7. Event mapping scope, if applicable.
8. Postflight SQL checks.
9. Any unmapped tags intentionally left blank.
10. Any trigger/view validation failures.
```

> End of imported document.

## 10. Database Architecture

**Source file:** `01_CANONICAL_REFERENCE/database_architecture.md`  
**File type:** `.md`  
**Size:** `3860` bytes  
**Modified on:** `2026-05-09 10:09:44`  
**SHA256:** `2fc609f98671`  
**Encoding used:** `utf-8`

> Start of imported document.

### Database Architecture

#### The 4-database layout (XMES, trace-confirmed 2026-04-14)

| Database | Role |
|---|---|
| `XStudio_Configuration` | Shared across ALL systems: users, roles, data types, global params, SMTP, schedulers, tag masters |
| `XStudio_Configuration_XMES` | XMES-specific config metadata: entities, attributes, pages, menus, LVs, edit forms, workflows |
| `XStudio_XMES` | XMES operational data: generated tables, custom SPs, LV views, session state |
| `XStudio_XMES_Audit` | Change history for audited entities |

One IIS app pool + one scheduler service handles all systems on the host. There is no per-system process isolation. Other systems (`XBatch`, `Helpdesk`) share the same host.

#### How entity creation works

1. Engineer creates an Entity via the front-end or SQL config insert
2. XStudio generates a real `CREATE TABLE` SQL and runs it against `XStudio_XMES`
3. The config DB stores metadata; the data DB stores the generated table and its indexes
4. When attributes are added, `ALTER TABLE ADD COLUMN` is issued against the data table

For runtime-recognized business objects, raw `CREATE TABLE` is not enough. The platform must see the entity, attributes, dropdown properties, relations, UI binding, and any workflow metadata in the configuration database.

#### LV views

Every List View (LV) generates a SQL view in the data DB:
```
XStudio_XMES.dbo.XStudio_[LVName]_Vw
```
The view is compiled by `XStudio_CreateViewForLV_Usp` whenever the LV is saved. It must be regenerated manually after config changes.

The current shared-config SP signature takes eight parameters:

```text
@LvID
@LvName
@DataDatabase
@ColumnQuery
@RelationQuery
@GroupByQuery
@WhereCondition
@IsCalculatedView
```

Do not assume that `@ColumnQuery = NULL` triggers recompilation. Some generated scripts must build the select/from/where fragments explicitly and then verify the resulting data DB view.

#### Workflow and action layers

XStudio workflow is a metadata graph, not one table:

| Layer | Role |
|---|---|
| `XStudio_WorkFlow_Mst_Tbl` | Workflow/state-machine header |
| `XStudio_Approval_Status_Mst_Tbl` | Status nodes |
| `XStudio_Approval_Actor_Mst_Tbl` | Actors/approver definitions |
| `XStudio_Approval_Mst_Tbl` | Transition/action edges in current XMES schema |
| `XStudio_WorkFlow_Action_Mst_Tbl` | Side-effect action definition |
| `XStudio_WorkFlow_Action_Assignment_Mst_Tbl` | Link from workflow/transition to side-effect action |

Always inspect live schema before deciding where stored procedure hooks belong.

#### Product-on-XStudio layering

For products such as XMES:

```text
Asset Structure = plant equipment, tags, events, historian/block capture
Data DB transaction tables = operational truth
Business SPs = interpretation, validation, posting, reconciliation
Workflow = lifecycle and controlled transitions
Summary/scheduler = periodic aggregation and rollups
Views/LVs/pages = operator and analytics surfaces
```

#### Session state

IIS session state is stored in `XStudio_XMES.dbo.XStudio_IIS_SessionData_Trn_Tbl` (~3,000 UPDATEs per 6 minutes — hottest table in the system). The SPA also uses a separate `@ClientToken` GUID refreshed via `XStudio_Extend_Token_ExpiryTime_Usp`.

#### Scheduler

A single background scheduler polls all config DBs every 2–5 seconds:
```sql
SELECT * FROM [XStudio_Configuration_XMES].[dbo].[XStudio_ScheduleTask_Trn_Tbl] WITH(NOLOCK)
WHERE IsDeleted = 0 AND Status = 'Waiting';
```
Set `XStudio_ScheduleParameter_Tbl.Name='IsReset'` to `1` to hot-reload the schedule for a system.

#### XMES system identifiers

| Key | Value |
|---|---|
| System ID | `6C8C0070-60B5-4474-BEC8-5C02197B32E6` |
| Application ID (DEFAULT) | `29123B0B-3872-41CF-B5E1-67C4A8F0D4BC` |
| SQL Server | `10.2.6.204` (instance: SSLDATABASE) |
| Config DB | `XStudio_Configuration_XMES` |
| Data DB | `XStudio_XMES` |

> End of imported document.

## 11. Dow Logsheet Feature

**Source file:** `01_CANONICAL_REFERENCE/dow_logsheet_feature.md`  
**File type:** `.md`  
**Size:** `6109` bytes  
**Modified on:** `2026-05-05 09:13:38`  
**SHA256:** `7f6510fb9876`  
**Encoding used:** `utf-8`

> Start of imported document.

### DOW — Digital Operator Workbench / Logsheet Feature

#### Purpose

DOW is the XStudio built-in logsheet (digital round sheet) feature for operator data capture.

It provides versioned logsheet definitions, section-based layouts, and parameter capture tied to equipment/area hierarchy.

Use this document when an agent needs to:

```text
understand DOW logsheet data model
inspect or configure logsheet definitions
understand version lifecycle behavior
understand section and parameter structure
write scripts that interact with Logsheet_Mst_Tbl or section tables
```

---

#### Data Location

DOW data tables live in the **data DB** (`XStudio_XMES.dbo`), not the config DB.

The SP layer resolves the target database dynamically:

```sql
SELECT @DataDatabaseName = DataDatabase
FROM [dbo].[XStudio_GetDataSourceInfoFromSystemId_Fun](@SystemId);
```

All DOW DML targets `[@DataDatabaseName].[dbo].[Logsheet_*]` or `[@DataDatabaseName].[dbo].[Logsheet_Section_*]` tables.

---

#### Object Model

```text
Logsheet_Mst_Tbl
  → master logsheet record (Name, DisplayName, ViewType, CurrentVersionID)

Logsheet_Version_Mst_Tbl
  → versioned configurations for one logsheet
  → LogsheetID → Logsheet_Mst_Tbl.ID
  → VersionCode, IsMobile, VersionReason

Section tables (per logsheet version)
  → section layout definitions
  → parameter/attribute definitions per section
```

##### Key fields in `Logsheet_Mst_Tbl`

| Field | Purpose |
|---|---|
| `Name` | Physical logsheet name |
| `DisplayName` | UI display label |
| `CurrentVersionID` | Active version |
| `ViewType` | View mode configuration |
| `IsConfigurationChanged` | Flag set when configuration changes |

##### Key fields in `Logsheet_Version_Mst_Tbl`

| Field | Purpose |
|---|---|
| `LogsheetID` | Parent logsheet |
| `VersionCode` | Version label |
| `IsMobile` | Whether version is mobile-enabled |
| `VersionReason` | Reason for this version |

---

#### Stored Procedure Reference

| SP | Parameters | Purpose |
|---|---:|---|
| `DOW_Get_Asset_Hierarchy_Usp` | 2 | Returns asset hierarchy for logsheet context |
| `DOW_Get_Logsheet_Details_Usp` | 2 | Reads logsheet master definitions |
| `DOW_Get_Logsheet_Version_Details_Usp` | 2 | Reads versioned logsheet configurations |
| `DOW_Get_Section_Details_Usp` | 2 | Reads sections within a logsheet version |
| `DOW_Get_Section_Parameters_Details_Usp` | 2 | Reads parameters/attributes for each section |
| `DOW_Save_Logsheet_Details_Usp` | 9 | Creates or updates logsheet master |
| `DOW_Save_Logsheet_Version_Details_Usp` | 10 | Creates or updates a logsheet version |
| `DOW_Save_Section_Details_Usp` | 16 | Saves section definitions |
| `DOW_Save_Section_Parameters_Details_Usp` | 13 | Saves section parameter/attribute definitions |
| `DOW_Update_Section_Details_Usp` | 5 | Updates section-level properties |
| `DOW_Delete_Section_Details_Usp` | 5 | Soft-deletes a section |
| `DOW_Delete_Section_Parameters_Details_Usp` | 5 | Soft-deletes a section parameter |
| `DOW_Create_Logsheet_Module_Table_Usp` | 1 | Creates the logsheet physical module table in data DB |
| `DOW_Create_Logsheet_Frequency_Module_Table_Usp` | 1 | Creates frequency-based logsheet module table |
| `DOW_Add_ModifiedOn_AND_Source_Column_In_Section_Lv_Usp` | 2 | Adds ModifiedOn and Source columns to section LV |
| `DOW_Add_Row_Template_In_Section_LV_USP` | 2 | Adds row template to a section LV |

---

#### Version Lifecycle

`DOW_Save_Logsheet_Version_Details_Usp` uses `@Intent` to control behavior:

```text
@Intent != 'current'
→ INSERT new version row

@Intent = 'current'
→ UPDATE current/active version row
```

This means logsheet evolution is tracked as versioned configurations. The active version is linked back to `Logsheet_Mst_Tbl.CurrentVersionID`.

---

#### Logsheet Save Pattern

```sql
-- Step 1: Create or update logsheet master
EXEC [dbo].[DOW_Save_Logsheet_Details_Usp]
    @SystemId    = '<system-guid>',
    @ID          = '<existing-id-or-null>',
    @Name        = 'OperatorRoundSheet',
    @CurrentVersionID = '<version-guid>',
    @UserId      = '<user-guid>',
    @HostAddress = '10.x.x.x',
    @ViewType    = 'Standard',
    @DisplayName = 'Operator Round Sheet',
    @IsConfigurationChanged = 0;

-- Step 2: Create or update logsheet version
EXEC [dbo].[DOW_Save_Logsheet_Version_Details_Usp]
    @SystemId    = '<system-guid>',
    @ID          = '<version-guid-or-null>',
    @Name        = 'v1.0',
    @LogsheetID  = '<logsheet-guid>',
    @UserId      = '<user-guid>',
    @HostAddress = '10.x.x.x',
    @VersionCode = 'v1.0',
    @IsMobile    = 0,
    @VersionReason = 'Initial version',
    @Intent      = 'new';  -- 'new' = INSERT

-- Step 3: Save sections via DOW_Save_Section_Details_Usp
-- Step 4: Save parameters via DOW_Save_Section_Parameters_Details_Usp
```

---

#### Integration with Asset Hierarchy

`DOW_Get_Asset_Hierarchy_Usp` integrates logsheets with the Asset Structure hierarchy.

Logsheets can be scoped to plant areas/equipment in the same hierarchy used by `Asset_Hierarchy_Mst_Tbl`.

---

#### Validation Checks

After logsheet configuration:

```sql
-- 1. Confirm logsheet master exists in data DB
SELECT ID, Name, DisplayName, CurrentVersionID, ViewType
FROM XStudio_XMES.dbo.Logsheet_Mst_Tbl
WHERE IsDeleted = 0;

-- 2. Confirm logsheet version exists
SELECT ID, Name, LogsheetID, VersionCode, IsMobile
FROM XStudio_XMES.dbo.Logsheet_Version_Mst_Tbl
WHERE IsDeleted = 0
ORDER BY CreatedOn DESC;

-- 3. Confirm sections exist
SELECT *
FROM XStudio_XMES.dbo.Logsheet_Section_Mst_Tbl
WHERE IsDeleted = 0;
```

---

#### Agent Rules

```text
1. DOW data lives in the data DB (XStudio_XMES), not config DB.
2. Always pass @SystemId to DOW SPs — they resolve the data DB dynamically.
3. Use @Intent = 'new' to create a version; 'current' to update the active version.
4. Section and parameter saves follow logsheet → version → section → parameter order.
5. DOW_Create_Logsheet_Module_Table_Usp must be called to create the physical capture table before data entry.
6. Do not directly INSERT into Logsheet_Mst_Tbl — use DOW_Save_Logsheet_Details_Usp.
```

> End of imported document.

## 12. Downtime Feature

**Source file:** `01_CANONICAL_REFERENCE/downtime_feature.md`  
**File type:** `.md`  
**Size:** `5741` bytes  
**Modified on:** `2026-05-05 09:14:12`  
**SHA256:** `ee5fe29eff3e`  
**Encoding used:** `utf-8`

> Start of imported document.

### DownTime Feature

#### Purpose

XStudio DownTime tracks equipment downtime events: configuration, tag-based detection, condition-based rules, and event log recording.

Use this document when an agent needs to:

```text
configure downtime monitoring for equipment
understand downtime data model
write scripts that interact with Downtime_* tables
understand tag and condition configuration for downtime detection
```

---

#### Data Location

Downtime tables live in the **data DB** (`XStudio_XMES.dbo`), not the config DB.

The SP layer resolves both data and config databases:

```sql
SELECT @DataDatabaseName = DataDatabase,
       @ConfigurationDatabaseName = ConfigurationDatabase 
FROM [dbo].[XStudio_GetDataSourceInfoFromSystemId_Fun](@SystemId);
```

---

#### Object Model

```text
Downtime_Configuration_Mst_Tbl
  → one row per downtime monitoring config (Name, EquipmentID, DataSourceID, CollectorID, ChannelID)

Downtime_Tag_Mst_Tbl
  → tags associated with a downtime config (ParentID → Configuration)
  → defines which tags indicate downtime state

Downtime_Condition_Mst_Tbl
  → conditions for classifying downtime state
  → ParentID → Configuration

Downtime log table
  → individual downtime event records (StartTime, EndTime, ReasonID, Description, EquipmentID)
```

##### Key fields in `Downtime_Configuration_Mst_Tbl`

| Field | Purpose |
|---|---|
| `Name` | Config name |
| `EquipmentID` | Linked equipment instance |
| `DataSourceID` | Historian/OPC data source |
| `CollectorID` | Data collector |
| `ChannelID` | Tag channel |

---

#### Stored Procedure Reference

| SP | Parameters | Purpose |
|---|---:|---|
| `XStudio_DownTime_Get_Equipment_Usp` | 1 | Returns equipment list for downtime configuration |
| `XStudio_DownTime_Get_Configuration_Usp` | 2 | Reads downtime configurations |
| `XStudio_DownTime_Get_Tag_Configuration_Usp` | 2 | Reads tag configs for a downtime config |
| `XStudio_DownTime_Get_Condition_Configuration_Usp` | 2 | Reads conditions for a downtime config |
| `XStudio_DownTime_Get_Data_Log_Usp` | 2 | Reads downtime event log records |
| `XStudio_DownTime_Get_Reason_Group_State_Usp` | 1 | Returns reason/group/state lookup |
| `XStudio_DownTime_Save_Configuration_Usp` | 7 | Creates/updates downtime config |
| `XStudio_DownTime_Save_Tag_Configuration_Usp` | 6 | Saves tag mapping for a downtime config |
| `XStudio_DownTime_Save_Condition_Configuration_Usp` | 8 | Saves conditions for a downtime config |
| `XStudio_DownTime_Save_Data_Log_Usp` | 7 | Creates/updates a downtime log record |
| `XStudio_Merge_DownTime_Save_Data_Log_Usp` | — | MERGE-based save for downtime log |

---

#### Save Behavior

##### Configuration Save (`XStudio_DownTime_Save_Configuration_Usp`)

When `@Id = '0'`:
- INSERT new `Downtime_Configuration_Mst_Tbl` row
- Return the new ID (TOP 1, ORDER BY CreatedOn DESC)

When `@Id != '0'` (existing config):
- UPDATE `Downtime_Configuration_Mst_Tbl` row
- **DELETE all existing** `Downtime_Tag_Mst_Tbl` rows for this `ParentID`
- **DELETE all existing** `Downtime_Condition_Mst_Tbl` rows for this `ParentID`

This is a **full-replace pattern**: after updating the config, the caller must re-save tags and conditions through separate SP calls.

##### Data Log Save (`XStudio_DownTime_Save_Data_Log_Usp`)

Parameters:
- `@SystemId` — system GUID
- `@ParentID` — config ID
- `@EquipmentId` — equipment instance
- `@StartTime` — downtime start
- `@EndTime` — downtime end
- `@ReasonID` — reason lookup ID
- `@Description` — freetext description

##### Merge Save (`XStudio_Merge_DownTime_Save_Data_Log_Usp`)

Uses SQL MERGE semantics — handles insert-or-update in one call without requiring the caller to check for existing records.

---

#### Configuration Pattern

```text
Step 1: Save downtime configuration
  → XStudio_DownTime_Save_Configuration_Usp
  → returns new ID if @Id = '0'

Step 2: Save tag mappings
  → XStudio_DownTime_Save_Tag_Configuration_Usp
  → links datasource tags to this config

Step 3: Save detection conditions
  → XStudio_DownTime_Save_Condition_Configuration_Usp
  → defines tag value conditions that classify downtime

Step 4: During runtime — log downtime events
  → XStudio_DownTime_Save_Data_Log_Usp or
  → XStudio_Merge_DownTime_Save_Data_Log_Usp
```

---

#### Validation Checks

```sql
-- 1. Confirm downtime configuration exists in data DB
SELECT ID, Name, EquipmentID, DataSourceID, CollectorID, ChannelID, CreatedOn
FROM XStudio_XMES.dbo.Downtime_Configuration_Mst_Tbl
WHERE IsDeleted = 0
ORDER BY CreatedOn DESC;

-- 2. Confirm tags linked to config
SELECT ParentID, TagName, DataSourceID
FROM XStudio_XMES.dbo.Downtime_Tag_Mst_Tbl
WHERE IsDeleted = 0;

-- 3. Confirm conditions linked to config
SELECT *
FROM XStudio_XMES.dbo.Downtime_Condition_Mst_Tbl
WHERE IsDeleted = 0;

-- 4. Confirm log records
SELECT EquipmentId, StartTime, EndTime, ReasonID, Description
FROM XStudio_XMES.dbo.Downtime_Log_Tbl   -- actual table name may vary
WHERE IsDeleted = 0
ORDER BY StartTime DESC;
```

---

#### Agent Rules

```text
1. DownTime data lives in the data DB (XStudio_XMES), not config DB.
2. Always pass @SystemId to DownTime SPs — they resolve the data DB dynamically.
3. Configuration save is a full-replace: updating a config deletes its existing tags and conditions.
4. After updating a config, re-save all tags (Save_Tag_Configuration_Usp) and conditions (Save_Condition_Configuration_Usp).
5. Use Get_Reason_Group_State_Usp to obtain valid ReasonID values before saving log records.
6. Use Merge_DownTime_Save_Data_Log_Usp for runtime log saves to avoid duplicate-insert errors.
7. DownTime equipment selection uses XStudio_DownTime_Get_Equipment_Usp, not XStudio_Equipment_Mst_Vw directly.
```

> End of imported document.

## 13. Object Hierarchy

**Source file:** `01_CANONICAL_REFERENCE/object_hierarchy.md`  
**File type:** `.md`  
**Size:** `3724` bytes  
**Modified on:** `2026-04-28 14:30:36`  
**SHA256:** `1b5e2d89685d`  
**Encoding used:** `utf-8`

> Start of imported document.

### Object Hierarchy

Every XStudio config object has a `ParentID`. Missing or wrong `ParentID` creates orphaned objects that exist in tables but don't render in the UI.

#### Full hierarchy with ParentID mapping

```
System (XStudio_Configuration_XMES)
└── Application (XStudio_Application_Mst_Tbl)
    ParentID → SystemID
    │
    ├── Entity (XStudio_Entities_Mst_Tbl)
    │   ParentID → ApplicationID
    │   │
    │   ├── Attribute (XStudio_Attribute_Mst_Tbl)
    │   │   ParentID → EntityID
    │   │   │
    │   │   └── AttributeProperties (XStudio_AttributeProperties_Mst_Tbl)
    │   │       ParentID → AttributeID
    │   │
    │   └── EntityRelation (XStudio_EntityRelations_Mst_Tbl)
    │       Uses explicit EntityID / AttributeID fields (not ParentID)
    │
    ├── LV (XStudio_LV_Mst_Tbl)
    │   ParentID → EntityID
    │   │
    │   ├── LvColumns (XStudio_LvColumns_Mst_Tbl)
    │   │   ParentID → LV ID
    │   │
    │   └── LVControls (XStudio_LVControls_Mst_Tbl)
    │       ParentID → LV ID
    │
    ├── Edit (XStudio_Edit_Mst_Tbl)
    │   ParentID → EntityID
    │   │
    │   └── EditControls (XStudio_EditControls_Mst_Tbl)
    │       ParentID → Edit ID
    │
    ├── Page (XStudio_Page_Mst_Tbl)
    │   ParentID → ApplicationID
    │   │
    │   └── PageControls (XStudio_PageControls_Mst_Tbl)
    │       ParentID → Page ID
    │       ControlID → LV ID or Edit ID (depending on ControlType)
    │
    ├── Menu (XStudio_Menu_Mst_Tbl)
    │   ParentID → ApplicationID
    │   SubMenuOfID → parent Menu ID (for nesting)
    │   │
    │   └── MenuRights (XStudio_MenuRights_Mst_Tbl)
    │       ParentID → Menu ID
    │
    ├── Tab Container (XStudio_Tab_Container_Mst_Tbl)
    │   ParentID → ApplicationID
    │   │
    │   └── Tab (XStudio_Tab_Mst_Tbl)
    │       ParentID → Tab Container ID
    │       │
    │       └── Tab Detail (XStudio_Tab_Dtl_Tbl)
    │           ParentID → Tab ID
    │
    └── Approval (XStudio_Approval_Mst_Tbl)
        EntityID → Entity ID
        │
        ├── Approval Status (XStudio_Approval_Status_Mst_Tbl)
        │   ParentID → Approval ID
        │
        └── Approval Actor (XStudio_Approval_Actor_Mst_Tbl)
            ParentID → Approval ID
```

#### Creation order (mandatory — bottom-up)

1. Physical table + columns (data DB: `XStudio_XMES`)
2. Entity metadata (`XStudio_Entities_Mst_Tbl`)
3. Attributes + attribute properties + entity relations
4. Edit row + edit controls
5. LV row + LV columns
6. Page rows (list page + edit page)
7. Page controls (linking pages to LV/edit)
8. Menu rows + menu rights

#### Deletion order (top-down)

1. Menu rights → Menus
2. Page controls → Pages
3. LV columns + LV controls → LVs
4. Edit controls → Edits
5. Entity relations + attribute properties → Attributes
6. Entity metadata + physical table (only when intentionally deleting)

#### Module per-entity count (postflight verification targets)

| Object | Expected count per entity |
|---|---|
| Entity rows | 1 |
| Attribute rows | N (your attributes + system columns) |
| AttributeProperties rows | 6 per dropdown attribute |
| EntityRelation rows | 1 per dropdown attribute |
| LV rows | 1 (minimum) |
| LvColumns rows | N (one per displayed column) |
| Edit rows | 1 (minimum) |
| EditControls rows | N (one per form field) |
| Page rows | 2 (list + edit) |
| PageControl rows | 2 (one grid, one edit) |
| Menu rows | 1+ |
| MenuRight rows | 1 per role per menu item |

> End of imported document.

## 14. Runtime Tokens

**Source file:** `01_CANONICAL_REFERENCE/runtime_tokens.md`  
**File type:** `.md`  
**Size:** `2981` bytes  
**Modified on:** `2026-04-28 14:31:00`  
**SHA256:** `0995abc8a3a4`  
**Encoding used:** `utf-8`

> Start of imported document.

### Runtime Tokens

These tokens are resolved by XStudio at runtime. Use them in `FilterCondition`, `CustomLink`, and `ControlProperties.DefaultValue`.

#### `<request.PARAM>` — URL query parameter

Replaced with the value of `?PARAM=...` from the current page URL.

**Works in:**
- `LV_Mst_Tbl.FilterCondition` → filters the grid to rows matching the param
- `LvColumns.CustomLink` → passes row values to child page (use `{COLUMN}` for row data, `<request.PARAM>` for page-level params)
- `ControlProperties.DefaultValue` → pre-populates form field on open
- `<query. SQL >` inside DefaultValue → usable inside the SQL

**URL parameters flow through to popup edit forms.** If the parent LV page was opened with `?WOID=some-guid`, the edit popup that opens from that LV's Add button also has `WOID` available.

**Example — optional filter that shows all records from menu but filters from parent:**
```sql
(WorkOrderID='<request.WOID>' OR IIF('<request.WOID>'='',1,0)=1)
```

#### `{COLUMN_NAME}` — row column token in CustomLink

Replaced with the value of the named column from the current LV row at click time.

```
XMES/DEFAULT/List_Page_XMES_Production_Trn_Tbl?WOID={ID}
```

- `{ID}` = the row's `ID` column (primary key)
- Any other LV result column works the same: `{WorkOrderNumber}`, `{CampaignID}`, etc.

#### `<query. SQL >` — SQL-derived default value

Executes a SQL SELECT returning a single value. Supported in `ControlProperties.DefaultValue`.

**Inner tokens available inside the query:**

| Token | Resolves to |
|---|---|
| `<dbtype.data>` | Data DB name (e.g. `XStudio_XMES`) |
| `<request.PARAM>` | URL query parameter |
| `<RECORD.control_name>` | Current value of another control in the same form |
| `<session.USERID>` | Current logged-in user ID |

**Example — auto-generate next work order number:**
```
<query. SELECT FORMAT(GETDATE(),'yyyyMM') + RIGHT('0000' + CAST(MAX(CAST(RIGHT(WorkOrderNumber,4) AS INT))+1 AS VARCHAR),4) FROM [<dbtype.data>].[dbo].[XMES_Work_Order_Mst_Tbl] >
```

**Example — cascade-fill Grade from selected Production Order:**
```
<query. SELECT Grade FROM [<dbtype.data>].[dbo].[XMES_Production_Order_Mst_Tbl] WHERE ID='<RECORD.cmb_ProductionOrderNo_12513909>' >
```

#### Static literal default value

```json
{ "DefaultValue": "Entered" }
{ "DefaultValue": "#000000" }
```

#### `<request.PARAM>` in FilterCondition

```sql
ParentID='<request.PID>'
(WorkOrderID='<request.WOID>' OR IIF('<request.WOID>'='',1,0)=1)
```

#### `CustomLinkTargetMode` values

| Value | Behavior |
|---|---|
| `Inside` | Opens in the main content frame |
| `Popup` | Opens as a modal overlay |

#### URL format for CustomLink

```
{SystemName}/DEFAULT/{PageName}?{Param}={Token}
```

Example:
```
XMES/DEFAULT/List_Page_XMES_Production_Trn_Tbl?WOID={ID}
```
- `XMES` = system name
- `DEFAULT` = application name
- `List_Page_XMES_Production_Trn_Tbl` = exact `Page_Mst_Tbl.Name`
- `WOID={ID}` = passes the row's ID column as the WOID parameter

> End of imported document.

### XStudio Configuration SP Catalog — Complete Reference

**Source:** `XStudio_Configuration` database SP dump, generated 2026-05-08 19:11:31 IST.  
**Total SPs:** 646 across all feature families.  
**All SPs live in `XStudio_Configuration.dbo` (shared) or `XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo` (tenant-specific) unless noted.**

---

#### SP Family Index

Use this index to navigate to the right SP family for a task.

| Family | Prefix / Pattern | Task |
|---|---|---|
| Asset Structure | `XStudio_AssetStructure_*` | Equipment hierarchy, templates, tag mapping |
| Equipment type generator | `XStudio_Create_Equipment_*`, `XStudio_Add_Equipment_*` | Generate equipment modules |
| Block / historian | `XStudio_Create_Block_*`, `XStudio_Generate_Block_*`, `XStudio_Find_Block_*`, `XStudio_Historian_*` | Block tables, historian SPs, recalculation |
| Summary (standard) | `XStudio_Create_Summary_*`, `XStudio_Historian_Create_Summary_*` | Summary SP generation (minute/hour/shift/day/week/month/quarter/year/financial) |
| Summary (AIMS) | `XStudio_AIMS_Create_Summary_*` | AIMS-path summary SPs |
| Summary (IIOT) | `XStudio_Create_IIOT_Summary_*` | IIoT-path summary SPs |
| Entity / attribute schema | `XStudio_SaveEntity_Usp`, `XStudio_AddAttribute_Usp`, `XStudio_Add_Or_Alter_*_Schema_Usp` | Create/alter entity schema |
| LV management | `XStudio_CreateViewForLV_Usp`, `XStudio_Get_LvColumns_List_Usp`, `XStudio_LV_Copy_Usp` | List view operations |
| Edit management | `XStudio_Delete_Edit_Usp`, `XStudio_Edit_Copy_Usp`, `XStudio_Save_Edit_Logic_And_Validation_USP`, `Xstudio_Insert_Edit_Columns_Info_Usp` | Edit form operations |
| Page / controls / menu | `XStudio_Save_Page_Control_*`, `XStudio_Save_Menu_*`, `XStudio_Save_Tab_*`, `XStudio_Save_HTMLControl_*`, `XStudio_Page_Copy_Usp`, `XStudio_Menu_Copy_Usp` | Generic page containers, page-control binding, menu items, menu rights, nested controls |
| Module duplication | `XStudio_DuplicateModule_Usp`, `XStudio_Create_Duplicate_*_Usp` | Copy LV/Edit/Page/Tab/Report/Dashboard/AI/API/WorkFlow |
| Workflow | `XStudio_Workflow_*`, `XStudio_WorkFlow_Execute_SP_Action_Usp` | Workflow/approval business logic |
| DOW (Logsheet) | `DOW_*` | Digital operator workbench, logsheets, sections |
| DownTime | `XStudio_DownTime_*` | Downtime configuration, tags, conditions, log |
| AI Agent | `XStudio_AI_Agent_*` | AI agent config, data sources, document templates |
| OSI PI / tag | `XStudio_Get_OSI_*`, `XStudio_Save_OSIPI_*`, `XStudio_Get_Tag_*` | Tag master, OSI PI datasource/tag |
| Scheduler | `XStudio_Schedule_*`, `XStudio_Summary_*_Schedule_*` | Scheduled tasks, summary schedules |
| RBAC | `XStudio_Get_Role_Base_*`, `Xstudio_Get_Approval_*`, `Xstudio_Get_Common_Roles_Usp` | Role-based access, approval config |
| Reporting | `XStudio_Get_Reports_Usp`, `XStudio_Report_*`, `XStudio_Get_Report_*` | Reports, report rights, templates |
| Mobile sync | `XStudio_Get_Mobile_*`, `XStudio_Save_Mobile_*`, `XStudio_Reset_Mobile_*` | Mobile device/sync management |
| Notification | `XStudio_Send_Mail_*`, `XStudio_Get_Notification_*`, `XStudio_Schedule_Mail_*` | Email/SMS/notification |
| System / DB | `XStudio_Get_System_*`, `XStudio_Create_Database_Usp`, `XStudio_Get_DatabaseList_Usp` | System, database, and tenant management |
| Audit | `XStudio_Create_Audit_Tbl_Usp`, `XStudio_Get_AuditLog_*` | Audit log tables and reads |
| Archive / purge | `XStudio_Create_Archive_Tbl_Usp`, `XStudio_Data_Archive_Usp`, `XStudio_Data_Purge_Usp` | Data archiving and purging |
| Hotpath (every request) | `XStudio_Get_System_By_ID_Usp`, `XStudio_GetAttributesByCondition_Usp`, `XStudio_GetEntites_Usp`, etc. | Core runtime SPs called on every/most requests |
| Cleanup | `XStudio_DeleteAutoGeneratedEntryByEntityId_Usp`, `XStudio_Remove_Unused_Records_Usp` | Remove orphaned config rows |
| DB sync | `Xstudio_DB_Synch_Status_Update_usp`, `XStudio_Synch_Status_Check_Usp` | Cross-DB sync status management |

---

#### Configuration Action Rule

Use XStudio stored procedures for all configuration actions where a stored procedure exists.

Direct inserts are allowed only when the current stored-procedure catalog does not provide an action SP for that object.

For page/control/menu work:

| Object/action | Correct path |
|---|---|
| Brand-new page header | Guarded insert into `XStudio_Page_Mst_Tbl` only because no generic `XStudio_Save_Page_Configuration_Usp` / `XStudio_Create_Page_Usp` is present in the current SP catalog |
| Bind any control to a page | `XStudio_Save_Page_Control_Configuration_Usp` only |
| Delete page-control binding | `XStudio_Delete_Page_Control_Configuration_Usp` only |
| Create/update menu item | `XStudio_Save_Menu_Configuration_Usp` only |
| Add menu rights | `XStudio_Save_Menu_Rights_Usp` only |
| Delete menu rights | `XStudio_Delete_Menu_Rights_Usp` only |
| Save menu order | `XStudio_Save_Menu_Sequence_Usp` only |
| Save tabs / tab controls | `XStudio_Save_Tab_Configuration_Usp` and `XStudio_Save_Tab_Control_Configuration_Usp` only |
| Save HTML controls | `XStudio_Save_HTMLControl_Usp` and `XStudio_Save_HTMLControl_DataSource_Usp` only |
| Copy existing page/screen | `XStudio_Page_Copy_Usp`, `XStudio_Screen_Copy_Usp`, or `XStudio_Create_Duplicate_Screen_Usp` |
| Page collections | `XStudio_Add_Page_Collection_Usp` / `XStudio_Add_Page_To_Page_Collection_Usp`; these are collections/bookmarks, not runtime page creation |

#### Hotpath SPs (performance-critical, called on every/most requests)

| SP | Calls/6min | Purpose |
|---|---:|---|
| `XStudio_Get_System_By_ID_Usp` | 2,136 | Tenant resolver — on virtually every request |
| `XStudio_GetAttributesByCondition_Usp` | 562 | Fetch attributes by entity or attribute ID |
| `XStudio_GetEntites_Usp` | 277 | List/describe entities |
| `XStudio_Get_Master_Entity_List_Usp` | 217 | Global master-entity catalog |
| `XStudio_Get_DatabaseList_Usp` | 216 | Enumerate the 4 DBs for a system |
| `XStudio_GetRelationEntites_Usp` | 210 | Entity relation tree |
| `XStudio_Extend_Token_ExpiryTime_Usp` | 151 | API-token keep-alive |
| `XStudio_GetModuleDetails_Usp` | 142 | Fetch any module (Page, LV, Edit, Dashboard, …) |

---

#### DOW (Digital Operator Workbench / Logsheet) SPs

DOW is the XStudio logsheet/digital workbench module. Data tables are in the **data DB** (`XStudio_XMES.dbo`).

| SP | Parameters | Purpose |
|---|---:|---|
| `DOW_Get_Asset_Hierarchy_Usp` | 2 | Returns asset hierarchy for logsheet context |
| `DOW_Get_Logsheet_Details_Usp` | 2 | Reads logsheet master definitions |
| `DOW_Get_Logsheet_Version_Details_Usp` | 2 | Reads versioned logsheet configurations |
| `DOW_Get_Section_Details_Usp` | 2 | Reads sections within a logsheet version |
| `DOW_Get_Section_Parameters_Details_Usp` | 2 | Reads parameters/attributes for each section |
| `DOW_Save_Logsheet_Details_Usp` | 9 | Creates or updates logsheet master (`Logsheet_Mst_Tbl` in data DB) |
| `DOW_Save_Logsheet_Version_Details_Usp` | 10 | Creates or updates a logsheet version (`Intent` controls insert vs current-update) |
| `DOW_Save_Section_Details_Usp` | 16 | Saves section definitions |
| `DOW_Save_Section_Parameters_Details_Usp` | 13 | Saves section parameter/attribute definitions |
| `DOW_Update_Section_Details_Usp` | 5 | Updates section-level properties |
| `DOW_Delete_Section_Details_Usp` | 5 | Soft-deletes a section |
| `DOW_Delete_Section_Parameters_Details_Usp` | 5 | Soft-deletes a section parameter |
| `DOW_Create_Logsheet_Module_Table_Usp` | 1 | Creates the logsheet physical module table in data DB |
| `DOW_Create_Logsheet_Frequency_Module_Table_Usp` | 1 | Creates frequency-based logsheet module table |
| `DOW_Add_ModifiedOn_AND_Source_Column_In_Section_Lv_Usp` | 2 | Adds ModifiedOn and Source columns to section LV |
| `DOW_Add_Row_Template_In_Section_LV_USP` | 2 | Adds row template to a section LV |

##### DOW Key Objects

```text
Logsheet_Mst_Tbl          — data DB, logsheet master
Logsheet_Version_Mst_Tbl  — data DB, versioned logsheet configs
Section tables             — data DB, per-logsheet section structure
```

`DOW_Save_Logsheet_Details_Usp` targets `Logsheet_Mst_Tbl` in the data DB (`@DataDatabaseName` resolved via `XStudio_GetDataSourceInfoFromSystemId_Fun`).

`DOW_Save_Logsheet_Version_Details_Usp` uses `@Intent` to distinguish:
- `Intent != 'current'` → INSERT new version
- `Intent = 'current'` → UPDATE current version

---

#### DownTime SPs

Downtime tracking lives in `Downtime_*` tables in the **data DB**.

| SP | Parameters | Purpose |
|---|---:|---|
| `XStudio_DownTime_Get_Equipment_Usp` | 1 | Returns equipment list for downtime configuration |
| `XStudio_DownTime_Get_Configuration_Usp` | 2 | Reads downtime configurations |
| `XStudio_DownTime_Get_Tag_Configuration_Usp` | 2 | Reads tag configurations for a downtime config |
| `XStudio_DownTime_Get_Condition_Configuration_Usp` | 2 | Reads conditions for a downtime config |
| `XStudio_DownTime_Get_Data_Log_Usp` | 2 | Reads downtime event log records |
| `XStudio_DownTime_Get_Reason_Group_State_Usp` | 1 | Returns reason/group/state lookup for downtime |
| `XStudio_DownTime_Save_Configuration_Usp` | 7 | Creates/updates downtime config (`Downtime_Configuration_Mst_Tbl`) |
| `XStudio_DownTime_Save_Tag_Configuration_Usp` | 6 | Saves tag mapping for a downtime config |
| `XStudio_DownTime_Save_Condition_Configuration_Usp` | 8 | Saves conditions for a downtime config |
| `XStudio_DownTime_Save_Data_Log_Usp` | 7 | Creates/updates a downtime log record |
| `XStudio_Merge_DownTime_Save_Data_Log_Usp` | — | MERGE-based save for downtime log (handles insert/update in one call) |

##### DownTime Key Objects

```text
Downtime_Configuration_Mst_Tbl   — data DB, per-equipment downtime config
Downtime_Tag_Mst_Tbl              — data DB, tags linked to a downtime config
Downtime_Condition_Mst_Tbl        — data DB, conditions for downtime detection
Downtime log table                 — data DB, actual downtime events
```

`XStudio_DownTime_Save_Configuration_Usp` (on UPDATE) deletes `Downtime_Tag_Mst_Tbl` and `Downtime_Condition_Mst_Tbl` rows for the `ParentID` before re-saving — full replace pattern.

---

#### AI Agent SPs

XStudio includes a built-in AI Agent configuration layer.

| SP | Parameters | Purpose |
|---|---:|---|
| `XStudio_AI_Agent_Chat_History_Usp` | 7 | Get/save AI agent chat history |
| `XStudio_AI_Agent_Create_Entity_Prompt_Usp` | 4 | Generate entity prompt for AI agent |
| `XStudio_AI_Agent_Save_Configuration_Usp` | 11 | Save AI agent configuration |
| `XStudio_AI_Agent_Save_DataSource_Usp` | 9 | Save AI agent data source |
| `XStudio_AI_Agent_Save_Document_Template_Usp` | 11 | Save document template for AI agent |
| `XStudio_Create_Duplicate_AI_Agent_Usp` | 3 | Duplicate an AI agent configuration |

---

#### Summary SP Families

Three parallel SP families exist for different historian/summary paths. Each family covers the same time granularities.

##### Standard Summary (`XStudio_Create_Summary_*` + `XStudio_Historian_Create_Summary_*`)

| Time Granularity | Create SP | Historian SP |
|---|---|---|
| Minute | `XStudio_Create_Summary_Minute_SP_Usp` | `XStudio_Historian_Create_Summary_Minute_SP_Usp` |
| Hour | `XStudio_Create_Summary_Hour_SP_Usp` | `XStudio_Historian_Create_Summary_Hour_SP_Usp` |
| Shift | `XStudio_Create_Summary_Shift_SP_Usp` | `XStudio_Historian_Create_Summary_Shift_SP_Usp` |
| Day | `XStudio_Create_Summary_Day_SP_Usp` | `XStudio_Historian_Create_Summary_Day_SP_Usp` |
| Week | `XStudio_Create_Summary_Week_SP_Usp` | — |
| Month | `XStudio_Create_Summary_Month_SP_Usp` | `XStudio_Historian_Create_Summary_Month_SP_Usp` |
| Quarter | `XStudio_Create_Summary_Quarter_SP_Usp` | `XStudio_Historian_Create_Summary_Quarter_SP_Usp` |
| Year | `XStudio_Create_Summary_Year_SP_Usp` | `XStudio_Historian_Create_Summary_Year_SP_Usp` |
| Financial Day | `XStudio_Create_Summary_FinancialDay_SP_Usp` | `XStudio_Historian_Create_Summary_FinancialDay_SP_Usp` |
| Financial Month | `XStudio_Create_Summary_FinancialMonth_SP_Usp` | `XStudio_Historian_Create_Summary_FinancialMonth_SP_Usp` |
| Financial Quarter | `XStudio_Create_Summary_FinancialQuarter_SP_Usp` | `XStudio_Historian_Create_Summary_FinancialQuarter_SP_Usp` |
| Financial Year | `XStudio_Create_Summary_FinancialYear_SP_Usp` | `XStudio_Historian_Create_Summary_FinancialYear_SP_Usp` |
| Half Year | `XStudio_Create_Summary_HalfYear_SP_Usp` | `XStudio_Historian_Create_Summary_HalfYear_SP_Usp` |
| Half Financial Year | `XStudio_Create_Summary_HalfFinancialYear_SP_Usp` | `XStudio_Historian_Create_Summary_HalfFinancialYear_SP_Usp` |
| Typewise | `XStudio_Create_Summary_Typewise_SP_USP` | `Xstudio_Historian_Create_Summary_Typewise_SP_USP` |

Master orchestrators:
- `XStudio_Historian_Create_Summary_SP_USP` — dispatches to the correct time-granularity SP
- `XStudio_Create_Summary_Schema_Usp` — creates summary schema structure
- `XStudio_Create_Summary_Entity_SP_USP` — creates summary entity SP
- `XStudio_Create_Summary_IIOT_SP_USP` — creates IIOT-path summary SP

Schedule orchestrators:
- `XStudio_Summary_Historian_Schedule_Task_Usp` — schedules historian summary recalculation
- `XStudio_Summary_Schedule_Task_Usp` — schedules standard summary
- `XStudio_Summary_IIOT_Schedule_Task_Usp` — schedules IIOT summary
- `XStudio_Summary_Entity_Historian_Schedule_Task_Usp` — schedules entity-level historian summary
- `XStudio_Summary_Schedule_Usp` — general summary schedule

##### AIMS Summary (`XStudio_AIMS_Create_Summary_*`)

Parallel family for AIMS (Advanced Industrial Manufacturing System or similar) path. Same granularities as standard summary.

##### IIOT Summary (`XStudio_Create_IIOT_Summary_*`)

Parallel family for IIoT-path historian data. Same granularities as standard summary.

#### Page / Controls / Menu Management SPs

This family covers generic XStudio page composition:

```text
Page header
-> PageControl binding
-> optional nested container controls
-> Menu item
-> Menu rights
```

A page is only a container/screen. It does not render useful UI until one or more controls are bound to it through `XStudio_PageControls_Mst_Tbl`.

The control bound to a page can be a List View/grid, edit form, inline grid, HTML control, dashboard, tab container, tree/treelist/timeline, or another supported XStudio control. A tab container is only one possible control type. Do not create tabs unless the screen explicitly requires a multi-tab layout.

##### Page header SP status

The 2026-05-08 SP dump does not show a general-purpose `XStudio_Save_Page_Configuration_Usp` or `XStudio_Create_Page_Usp` for creating a brand-new page header in `XStudio_Page_Mst_Tbl`.

Therefore, the only allowed direct-write exception in this family is:

```text
For brand-new page headers:
    guarded insert into XStudio_Page_Mst_Tbl

For page-control bindings:
    XStudio_Save_Page_Control_Configuration_Usp only

For menu rows:
    XStudio_Save_Menu_Configuration_Usp only

For menu rights:
    XStudio_Save_Menu_Rights_Usp only
```

Do not confuse page-copy or page-collection SPs with brand-new runtime page creation.

##### Generic page-control binding SPs

| SP | Parameters | Purpose |
|---|---:|---|
| `XStudio_Save_Page_Control_Configuration_Usp` | 15 | Create/update `XStudio_PageControls_Mst_Tbl` rows. Binds a control to a page using `@ParentId`, `@ControlType`, `@ControlId`, layout fields, URL/CSS fields, and `@ControlProperties`. |
| `XStudio_Delete_Page_Control_Configuration_Usp` | 4 | Delete/soft-delete page-control configuration. |
| `XStudio_Get_Page_Control_By_ControlId_Usp` | 2 | Read page-control rows by the underlying control ID. |
| `XStudio_Get_Page_Controls_By_PageId_Usp` | 2 | Read controls bound to a page. |
| `XStudio_Update_Page_Control_Position_Usp` | — | Update page-control position/layout. |
| `XStudio_Save_Dependent_Page_Control_Configuration_USP` | — | Save dependency rules between page controls. |
| `XStudio_Delete_Dependent_Page_Control_Configuration_Usp` | 4 | Delete dependent page-control configuration. |
| `XStudio_Get_Dependent_Page_Control_List_USP` | 4 | Read dependent page-control rules/list. |

##### Supported page-control model

`XStudio_Save_Page_Control_Configuration_Usp` is generic. Use it for every Page → Control binding.

| Desired page content | PageControl `ControlType` | `ControlID` points to | Extra configuration |
|---|---|---|---|
| List View / grid | `grid` | `XStudio_LV_Mst_Tbl.ID` | Ensure LV view exists through `XStudio_CreateViewForLV_Usp` |
| Edit form | `edit` | `XStudio_Edit_Mst_Tbl.ID` | Ensure edit controls and renderer-safe JSON are configured |
| Inline grid | `inlinegrid` | Inline grid / LV configuration ID | Validate parent-child/inline-grid configuration |
| HTML control | platform HTML control type | HTML control ID | Use HTML control SPs |
| Dashboard | `dashboard` | Dashboard ID | Validate dashboard configuration |
| Tab container | `tab` | `XStudio_Tab_Container_Mst_Tbl.ID` | Then configure tabs and tab controls |
| Tree / treelist / timeline | platform-specific value | corresponding control ID | Use matching control SP family |

##### Correct page/menu build sequence

```text
1. Resolve SystemID, ApplicationID, ConfigDB, user ID, role IDs.
2. Create or resolve the page header.
3. Choose the control type to host on the page.
4. Bind the control using XStudio_Save_Page_Control_Configuration_Usp.
5. If the bound control is a container, configure its children through that control family's SPs.
6. Create/update menu using XStudio_Save_Menu_Configuration_Usp.
7. Add menu rights using XStudio_Save_Menu_Rights_Usp.
8. Optionally save menu sequence using XStudio_Save_Menu_Sequence_Usp.
9. Run postflight:
   - page exists
   - page has at least one active PageControl
   - each PageControl resolves to the target LV/Edit/HTML/Tab/etc.
   - menu points to the page
   - menu has active rights for intended roles
```

##### Common page/menu failure modes

| Symptom | Cause | Correct fix |
|---|---|---|
| Page exists but renders blank | Missing or wrong PageControl row | Run `XStudio_Save_Page_Control_Configuration_Usp` |
| LV/edit does not render | Wrong `ControlType` or `ControlID` | Re-run `XStudio_Save_Page_Control_Configuration_Usp` with correct values |
| Menu item not visible | Missing menu rights | Run `XStudio_Save_Menu_Rights_Usp` |
| Child menu visible path broken | Parent menu lacks rights | Use `XStudio_AddRoleToParentMenu_USP` after validating live behavior |
| Page collection exists but no page appears in menu | Page collection was confused with runtime page/menu creation | Create/resolve a real page header, bind controls, then create menu and rights |
| Tab page appears empty | Tab container not bound as page control or tab has no child controls | Bind tab container through PageControl, then save tabs and tab controls |
| Duplicate menu-right rows | Rights SP called without an existence guard | Use read-only guard before `XStudio_Save_Menu_Rights_Usp` |

#### Notes

- Page/menu playbooks must use SPs for PageControls, Menu, MenuRights, Tab, TabControl, and HTML-control actions. Direct inserts are allowed only for brand-new page headers because no generic page-save SP is present in the current SP catalog.
- Page collections are not page creation. Use page collection SPs only for collection/bookmark behavior.
- Tabs are optional container controls, not a mandatory part of page creation.
- All DOW SPs prefix with `DOW_` (distinct from `XStudio_` prefix).
- `@Intent` in `DOW_Save_Logsheet_Version_Details_Usp` controls insert vs update behavior.
- DownTime data tables are in the **data DB**, not config DB.
- Summary SP families (standard / AIMS / IIOT) are structurally parallel — same granularities, different runtime paths.
- `XStudio_Merge_DownTime_Save_Data_Log_Usp` uses MERGE semantics (insert or update in one call).
- Financial period SPs follow Indian fiscal year conventions (April–March).

> End of imported document.

[Back to Table of Contents](#table-of-contents)

### Stored Procedure Runtime Surface

All procs live in `XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo` unless noted. Call counts from SQL Profiler trace, 2026-04-14.

#### Hot path procs (called on every/most requests)

| USP | Calls/6min | Purpose |
|---|---:|---|
| `XStudio_Get_System_By_ID_Usp` | 2,136 | Tenant resolver — on virtually every request |
| `XStudio_GetAttributesByCondition_Usp` | 562 | Fetch attributes by entity or attribute ID |
| `XStudio_GetEntites_Usp` | 277 | List/describe entities |
| `XStudio_Get_Master_Entity_List_Usp` | 217 | Global master-entity catalog |
| `XStudio_Get_DatabaseList_Usp` | 216 | Enumerate the 4 DBs for a system |
| `XStudio_GetRelationEntites_Usp` | 210 | Entity relation tree |
| `XStudio_Extend_Token_ExpiryTime_Usp` | 151 | API-token keep-alive |
| `XStudio_GetModuleDetails_Usp` | 142 | Fetch any module (Page, LV, Edit, Dashboard, …) |

#### Schema management procs

| USP | Purpose | Key params |
|---|---|---|
| `XStudio_SaveEntity_Usp` | Create/update entity | `@EntityName`, `@SystemId`, `@ApplicationId`, `@EntityID='0'` for new |
| `XStudio_AddAttribute_Usp` | Add column to entity (issues ALTER TABLE) | `@ID=''` for new, `@EntityID`, `@Name`, `@DataTypeID` |
| `XStudio_Add_Or_Alter_Entity_Schema_Usp` | Bulk entity schema update | — |
| `XStudio_Add_Or_Alter_Attribute_Schema_Usp` | Bulk attribute schema update | — |

#### LV management procs

| USP | Purpose |
|---|---|
| `XStudio_CreateViewForLV_Usp` | Compile CREATE VIEW in data DB — **must call after any LV change** |
| `XStudio_Get_LvColumns_List_Usp` | Read LV column metadata |
| `XStudio_Get_Role_Base_Lv_Columns_Usp` | Per-role column visibility |
| `XStudio_LV_Copy_Usp` | Duplicate an LV |

#### Edit management procs

| USP | Purpose |
|---|---|
| `XStudio_Delete_Edit_Usp` | Delete an edit form |
| `XStudio_Edit_Copy_Usp` | Duplicate an edit form |
| `XStudio_Save_Edit_Logic_And_Validation_USP` | Save AfterSave SP / validation SP config |
| `Xstudio_Insert_Edit_Columns_Info_Usp` | Insert edit controls |

#### Page / Menu procs

| USP | Purpose |
|---|---|
| `XStudio_Save_Page_Control_Configuration_Usp` | Save page control placements |
| `XStudio_Page_Copy_Usp` | Duplicate a page |
| `XStudio_Save_Menu_Configuration_Usp` | Save menu item |
| `XStudio_Save_Menu_Rights_Usp` | Save role rights for a menu |
| `XStudio_Menu_Copy_Usp` | Duplicate a menu |
| `XStudio_AddRoleToParentMenu_USP` | Add role to parent menu |

#### Module duplication ("Save As")

```sql
EXEC XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_DuplicateModule_Usp
    @SystemId   = '6C8C0070-60B5-4474-BEC8-5C02197B32E6',
    @ModuleId   = '<source_guid>',
    @ModuleType = 'LV',   -- also 'Page', 'Edit', 'Menu'
    @ModuleName = 'SourceName',
    @NewName    = 'TargetName'
```

#### Workflow procs

| USP | Purpose |
|---|---|
| `XStudio_Workflow_Create_Business_Logic_Stored_Procedure_USP` | Generate SP for a workflow transition |
| `XStudio_WorkFlow_Execute_SP_Action_Usp` | Execute a workflow SP action |

#### Cleanup procs

| USP | Purpose |
|---|---|
| `XStudio_DeleteAutoGeneratedEntryByEntityId_Usp` | Remove all generated controls/LVs for an entity |
| `XStudio_Remove_Unused_Records_Usp` | Clean orphaned config rows |

#### RBAC procs

| USP | Purpose |
|---|---|
| `Xstudio_Get_FunctionalRoleRights_Configuration_By_LvId_Usp` | CRUD rights per role/LV |
| `Xstudio_Get_Common_Roles_Usp` | Common roles for an LV |
| `Xstudio_Get_Approval_Configuration_By_AttributeId_Usp` | Approval config for an attribute |

#### DOW (Logsheet) procs

All prefix with `DOW_` (not `XStudio_`). Data targets the **data DB**, not config DB.

| USP | Purpose |
|---|---|
| `DOW_Save_Logsheet_Details_Usp` | Create/update logsheet master in data DB |
| `DOW_Save_Logsheet_Version_Details_Usp` | Create/update logsheet version (`@Intent='new'` = INSERT, `'current'` = UPDATE) |
| `DOW_Save_Section_Details_Usp` | Save section definitions |
| `DOW_Save_Section_Parameters_Details_Usp` | Save section parameters |
| `DOW_Get_Logsheet_Details_Usp` | Read logsheet master |
| `DOW_Get_Section_Details_Usp` | Read sections |
| `DOW_Create_Logsheet_Module_Table_Usp` | Create physical logsheet table in data DB |

See `01_CANONICAL_REFERENCE/dow_logsheet_feature.md` for full reference.

#### DownTime procs

Data targets `Downtime_*` tables in the **data DB**.

| USP | Purpose |
|---|---|
| `XStudio_DownTime_Save_Configuration_Usp` | Create/update downtime config (UPDATE = full-replace of tags/conditions) |
| `XStudio_DownTime_Save_Tag_Configuration_Usp` | Save tags for a config (call after Save_Configuration_Usp on update) |
| `XStudio_DownTime_Save_Condition_Configuration_Usp` | Save conditions (call after Save_Configuration_Usp on update) |
| `XStudio_DownTime_Save_Data_Log_Usp` | Save individual downtime event log record |
| `XStudio_Merge_DownTime_Save_Data_Log_Usp` | MERGE-based save (insert-or-update in one call) |
| `XStudio_DownTime_Get_Reason_Group_State_Usp` | Lookup table for reason/group/state values |

See `01_CANONICAL_REFERENCE/downtime_feature.md` for full reference.

#### Summary SP families

Three parallel families — standard, AIMS, IIOT — each covering same time granularities:

```text
Granularities: Minute, Hour, Shift, Day, Week, Month, Quarter, Year,
               FinancialDay, FinancialMonth, FinancialQuarter, FinancialYear,
               HalfYear, HalfFinancialYear, Typewise
```

| Family | SP prefix | Orchestrator |
|---|---|---|
| Standard | `XStudio_Create_Summary_*` | `XStudio_Historian_Create_Summary_SP_USP` |
| Standard (historian) | `XStudio_Historian_Create_Summary_*` | same |
| AIMS | `XStudio_AIMS_Create_Summary_*` | — |
| IIOT | `XStudio_Create_IIOT_Summary_*` | `XStudio_Create_Summary_IIOT_SP_USP` |

Schedule orchestrators: `XStudio_Summary_Historian_Schedule_Task_Usp`, `XStudio_Summary_Schedule_Task_Usp`, `XStudio_Summary_IIOT_Schedule_Task_Usp`.

#### AI Agent procs

| USP | Purpose |
|---|---|
| `XStudio_AI_Agent_Save_Configuration_Usp` | Save AI agent configuration |
| `XStudio_AI_Agent_Save_DataSource_Usp` | Save AI agent data source |
| `XStudio_AI_Agent_Save_Document_Template_Usp` | Save document template |
| `XStudio_AI_Agent_Chat_History_Usp` | Get/save chat history |
| `XStudio_AI_Agent_Create_Entity_Prompt_Usp` | Generate entity prompt |
| `XStudio_Create_Duplicate_AI_Agent_Usp` | Duplicate AI agent |

#### Full SP catalog

For the complete list of all ~370 SPs organized by feature family, see:

```
01_CANONICAL_REFERENCE/sp_catalog_complete.md
```

#### Notes

- `XStudio_Add_DropDown_Attribute_Properties_And_Relation_Usp` — **broken**: only inserts 3 of 6 required dropdown properties. Always verify all 6 are present after calling it.
- `XStudio_Add_Or_Alter_Attribute_Schema_Usp` — **bug observed**: may use `@DisplayName` for the physical column name instead of `@Name`. Verify physical column names after bulk use.
- Procs use `_Usp` suffix by convention. Two RBAC helpers use lowercase `Xstudio_` prefix — treat as case-insensitive in SQL Server.
- DOW procs use `DOW_` prefix (not `XStudio_`) and target data DB, not config DB.
- DownTime update pattern: `Save_Configuration_Usp` deletes child tags/conditions before update — always re-save them after.
