## 17. Table Reference Core

**Source file:** `01_CANONICAL_REFERENCE/table_reference_core.md`  
**File type:** `.md`  
**Size:** `18844` bytes  
**Modified on:** `2026-05-12 10:47:27`  
**SHA256:** `4404a204946f`  
**Encoding used:** `utf-8`

> Start of imported document.

### Core Table Reference

All tables are in `XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo` unless noted.

#### Global rules

Use XStudio stored procedures for all configuration actions where an XStudio stored procedure exists.

Direct writes are allowed only when the current XStudio stored-procedure reference does not expose a suitable action SP for that object. In the page/control/menu family, the only confirmed direct-write exception is a guarded insert into `XStudio_Page_Mst_Tbl` for a brand-new page header, because no generic `XStudio_Save_Page_Configuration_Usp` / `XStudio_Create_Page_Usp` is confirmed.

For page/control/menu work:

| Object/action | Required method |
|---|---|
| Create brand-new page header | Guarded insert into `XStudio_Page_Mst_Tbl` only |
| Bind any control to a page | `XStudio_Configuration.dbo.XStudio_Save_Page_Control_Configuration_Usp` |
| Delete page-control binding | `XStudio_Configuration.dbo.XStudio_Delete_Page_Control_Configuration_Usp` |
| Create/update menu item | `XStudio_Configuration.dbo.XStudio_Save_Menu_Configuration_Usp` |
| Add menu rights | `XStudio_Configuration.dbo.XStudio_Save_Menu_Rights_Usp` |
| Delete menu rights | `XStudio_Configuration.dbo.XStudio_Delete_Menu_Rights_Usp` |
| Save menu sequence | `XStudio_Configuration.dbo.XStudio_Save_Menu_Sequence_Usp` |
| Create/update tab | `XStudio_Configuration.dbo.XStudio_Save_Tab_Configuration_Usp` |
| Bind controls inside a tab | `XStudio_Configuration.dbo.XStudio_Save_Tab_Control_Configuration_Usp` |
| Save HTML control/config datasource | `XStudio_Configuration.dbo.XStudio_Save_HTMLControl_Usp` / `XStudio_Configuration.dbo.XStudio_Save_HTMLControl_DataSource_Usp` |

---

#### ParentID contract summary

| Table | Required parent |
|---|---|
| `XStudio_Entities_Mst_Tbl.ParentID` | Application ID |
| `XStudio_Attribute_Mst_Tbl.ParentID` | Entity ID |
| `XStudio_AttributeProperties_Mst_Tbl.ParentID` | Attribute ID |
| `XStudio_LV_Mst_Tbl.ParentID` | Entity ID |
| `XStudio_LvColumns_Mst_Tbl.ParentID` | LV ID |
| `XStudio_Edit_Mst_Tbl.ParentID` | Entity ID |
| `XStudio_EditControls_Mst_Tbl.ParentID` | Edit ID |
| `XStudio_Page_Mst_Tbl.ParentID` | Application ID |
| `XStudio_PageControls_Mst_Tbl.ParentID` | Page ID |
| `XStudio_Menu_Mst_Tbl.ParentID` | Application ID |
| `XStudio_MenuRights_Mst_Tbl.ParentID` | Menu ID |
| `XStudio_Tab_Container_Mst_Tbl.ParentID` | Application ID |
| `XStudio_Tab_Mst_Tbl.ParentID` | Tab container ID |
| `XStudio_Tab_Dtl_Tbl.ParentID` | Tab ID |

---

#### XStudio_Entities_Mst_Tbl

| Column | Type | Notes |
|---|---|---|
| `ID` | uniqueidentifier | PK |
| `Name` | varchar | **Physical SQL table name — never change on existing entities** |
| `DisplayName` | varchar | UI label |
| `TableType` | varchar | `Master` or `Transaction` |
| `IsChild` | bit | `1` = child/detail entity |
| `ParentEntityID` | uniqueidentifier | FK to parent entity when `IsChild=1` |
| `IsAuditLog` | bit | Enables audit trail |
| `IsTimeLine` | bit | Timeline tracking |
| `WorkFlowId` | uniqueidentifier | Links to Approval workflow |
| `WorkFlowStatusId` | uniqueidentifier | Attribute that holds the status |
| `IsAutoRefresh` | bit | Auto-refresh LV |
| `RefreshInterval` | int | Seconds between refreshes |
| `ParentID` | uniqueidentifier | **Must be ApplicationID** |

##### Contract

- Entity configuration should be created/updated through entity/schema SPs such as `XStudio_SaveEntity_Usp`.
- `Name` is the physical table name. Do not rename it casually after data exists.
- `ParentID` controls application ownership. If it does not resolve to an active application, the entity can exist but not appear in expected module lists.

---

#### XStudio_Attribute_Mst_Tbl

| Column | Type | Notes |
|---|---|---|
| `ID` | uniqueidentifier | PK |
| `Name` | varchar | **Physical SQL column name — changing auto-renames physical column** |
| `DisplayName` | varchar | UI label (safe to change) |
| `DataType` | uniqueidentifier | FK to `XStudio_DataType_Mst_Tbl` |
| `IsRequired` | bit | NOT NULL constraint |
| `IsUnique` | bit | UNIQUE constraint |
| `ParentID` | uniqueidentifier | **Must be EntityID** |
| `Priority` / `SerialNumber` | int | Display order |

##### Contract

- Attribute creation/update should use attribute/schema SPs such as `XStudio_AddAttribute_Usp`.
- `Name` is the physical SQL column name.
- Dropdown attributes also require complete attribute properties and entity relation metadata.

---

#### XStudio_AttributeProperties_Mst_Tbl

| Column | Type | Notes |
|---|---|---|
| `ID` | uniqueidentifier | PK |
| `ParentID` | uniqueidentifier | **Must be AttributeID** |
| `Name` | varchar | Property key (e.g. `DropdownEntity`) |
| `Value` | varchar | Property value |

##### Contract

- Dropdown attributes require a complete property set. A common failure mode is only some dropdown properties being created.
- Validate property count after any dropdown-generation SP.

---

#### XStudio_EntityRelations_Mst_Tbl

| Column | Type | Notes |
|---|---|---|
| `ID` | uniqueidentifier | PK |
| `EntityID` | uniqueidentifier | Owning entity (the one with the FK attribute) |
| `AttributeID` | uniqueidentifier | The FK attribute |
| `Cardinality` | varchar | `Many` (FK to parent) or `One` (parent owns children) |
| `RelationEntityID` | uniqueidentifier | Target entity |
| `RelationAttributeID` | uniqueidentifier | Target attribute (usually ID) |
| `RelationName` | varchar | Format: `AttributeName-TargetEntity` |
| `RelationEntity` | varchar | Denormalized table name |
| `RelationAttribute` | varchar | Denormalized column name |

##### Contract

- Dropdowns and parent-child relationships depend on this table resolving correctly.
- Missing relations can cause LV dropdown display text and edit dropdown controls to fail even when attributes exist.

---

#### XStudio_LV_Mst_Tbl

| Column | Type | Notes |
|---|---|---|
| `ID` | uniqueidentifier | PK |
| `Name` | varchar | Internal name |
| `DisplayName` | varchar | Grid header label |
| `ParentID` | uniqueidentifier | **Must be EntityID** |
| `SPName` | varchar | Custom SP (NULL = use default entity query) |
| `FilterCondition` | varchar | SQL WHERE fragment; supports `<request.PARAM>` tokens |
| `IsDefaultLoad` | bit | Auto-load on page open |

##### Contract

- LV belongs to an entity.
- A page does not render an LV by referencing the LV directly. It renders an LV only through a PageControl binding where `ControlType` is the platform LV/grid value and `ControlID = XStudio_LV_Mst_Tbl.ID`.
- After LV definition or LV columns change, compile/regenerate the LV view through `XStudio_CreateViewForLV_Usp`.

---

#### XStudio_LvColumns_Mst_Tbl

| Column | Type | Notes |
|---|---|---|
| `ID` | uniqueidentifier | PK |
| `ParentID` | uniqueidentifier | **Must be LV ID** |
| `Name` | varchar | Internal identifier (no spaces) |
| `Header` | varchar | Column header label |
| `Type` | varchar | `''`=data, `link`=navigation, `bool`, `date`, `number` |
| `SerialNumber` | int | Column order |
| `AttributeID` | uniqueidentifier | FK attribute (blank for link columns) |
| `RelationID` | uniqueidentifier | FK relation (for dropdown display text) |
| `IsVisible` | bit | Show/hide |
| `IsCustomField` | bit | `1` for navigation link columns |
| `LinkType` | varchar | `fixedtext` for text link columns |
| `LinkValue` | varchar | Clickable text (e.g. `Open`) |
| `CustomLink` | varchar | URL with `{COLUMN}` tokens |
| `CustomLinkTargetMode` | varchar | `Inside` or `Popup` |
| `RoleIDList` | varchar | Comma-separated role GUIDs |
| `ColumnProperties` | nvarchar | Required JSON object (see template) |

##### Contract

- LV columns belong to the LV, not the entity directly.
- Pure navigation/action link columns should not be linked to physical attributes or relations unless the renderer expects that.
- Unsafe link-column metadata can break navigation even when the LV itself exists.

---

#### XStudio_Edit_Mst_Tbl

| Column | Type | Notes |
|---|---|---|
| `ID` | uniqueidentifier | PK |
| `ParentID` | uniqueidentifier | **Must be EntityID** |
| `AfterSavePageID` | uniqueidentifier | Page opened after Save |
| `AfterCancelPageID` | uniqueidentifier | Page opened after Cancel |
| `AfterSaveAndContinuePageID` | uniqueidentifier | Page opened after Save & Continue |
| `IsSaveAndContinueButtonVisible` | bit | Show "Save & Add Another" button |
| `SaveAndContinueButtonName` | varchar | Label override |

##### Contract

- Edit belongs to an entity.
- A page does not render an edit form by referencing the edit directly. It renders an edit form only through a PageControl binding where `ControlType = 'edit'` and `ControlID = XStudio_Edit_Mst_Tbl.ID`.
- `AfterSavePageID` and `AfterCancelPageID` must resolve to active pages when configured.

---

#### XStudio_EditControls_Mst_Tbl

| Column | Type | Notes |
|---|---|---|
| `ID` | uniqueidentifier | PK |
| `ParentID` | uniqueidentifier | **Must be Edit ID** |
| `AttributeID` | uniqueidentifier | Which attribute this control edits |
| `ControlType` | varchar | `textbox`, `number`, `selectbox`, `checkbox`, `datetimepicker`, etc. |
| `ControlName` | varchar | Internal name (convention: `cmb_` for dropdowns, `dtp_` for datetimes) |
| `SerialNumber` | int | Field order |
| `ControlProperties` | nvarchar | **Full renderer-safe JSON required — never NULL** |

##### Contract

- Edit controls belong to an edit form.
- `ControlProperties` must be renderer-safe JSON and should never be NULL.
- Dropdown attributes should usually render through `selectbox` / platform dropdown control values.

---

#### XStudio_Page_Mst_Tbl

| Column | Type | Notes |
|---|---|---|
| `ID` | uniqueidentifier | PK |
| `Name` | varchar | Used in menu/navigation and CustomLink URLs |
| `DisplayName` | varchar | User-facing page label |
| `ParentID` | uniqueidentifier | **Must be ApplicationID** |
| `IsActive` | bit | Live flag |
| `IsPopup` | bit | `1` for popup/edit pages; `0` for normal navigation pages |
| `Roles` | varchar | Legacy/metadata role field; do not rely on this instead of MenuRights |
| `IsDeleted` | bit | Soft-delete flag |
| `IsSystem` | bit | System/generated flag |

##### Contract

- Page is only a container/header. It does not render useful UI by itself.
- Visible page content comes from `XStudio_PageControls_Mst_Tbl`.
- For brand-new page headers, guarded insert is currently the allowed exception because no generic page-create SP is confirmed.
- For copying an existing page/screen, use page/screen duplicate SPs such as `XStudio_Page_Copy_Usp`, `XStudio_Screen_Copy_Usp`, or `XStudio_Create_Duplicate_Screen_Usp`.
- Do not confuse PageCollection tables/SPs with runtime pages.

##### Failure modes

| Symptom | Likely cause |
|---|---|
| Page exists but opens blank | No active PageControl rows |
| Page exists but menu cannot open it | Menu `PageID` wrong or menu missing |
| CustomLink cannot find page | `Name` mismatch or page deleted/inactive |
| Role cannot see page from menu | MenuRights missing; `Roles` field alone is not enough |

---

#### XStudio_PageControls_Mst_Tbl

| Column | Type | Notes |
|---|---|---|
| `ID` | uniqueidentifier | PK |
| `ParentID` | uniqueidentifier | **Must be Page ID** |
| `Name` | varchar | Page-control display/internal name |
| `ControlID` | uniqueidentifier | ID of the bound control: LV, Edit, Tab container, HTML control, Dashboard, InlineGrid, etc. |
| `ControlType` | varchar | `grid`, `edit`, `tab`, `inlinegrid`, `dashboard`, HTML/platform-specific control values, etc. |
| `RowID` | varchar/int | Layout row |
| `DivID` | varchar/int | Layout/div position |
| `ControlUrl` | varchar | URL for URL/HTML/IFrame-style controls when applicable |
| `CustomCss` | varchar | Optional CSS |
| `LoadUrlAsIFrame` | bit | IFrame flag for URL controls |
| `ControlProperties` | nvarchar | JSON config (at minimum `{"IpFiltering":"False"}` unless a specific control requires more) |

##### Contract

- This is the actual Page → Control binding layer.
- Create/update PageControls only through `XStudio_Save_Page_Control_Configuration_Usp`.
- Delete/remove PageControls only through `XStudio_Delete_Page_Control_Configuration_Usp`.
- Do not directly insert into or update this table when the SP is available.
- Each visible control on a page needs its own active PageControl row.
- If the control is a container, such as a tab container, configure the child controls through that control family’s SPs after binding the container to the page.

##### Common control bindings

| Desired page content | `ControlType` | `ControlID` points to | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| List View / grid | `grid` | `XStudio_LV_Mst_Tbl.ID` | LV and generated LV view must exist |
| Edit form | `edit` | `XStudio_Edit_Mst_Tbl.ID` | Edit controls must be renderer-safe |
| Inline grid | `inlinegrid` | Inline grid / LV-related control ID | Validate parent-child/inline-grid configuration |
| HTML control | platform HTML control value | HTML control ID | Use HTML control SPs |
| Dashboard | `dashboard` | Dashboard ID | Dashboard config must exist |
| Tab container | `tab` | `XStudio_Tab_Container_Mst_Tbl.ID` | Then configure tabs and tab controls |
| Tree / treelist / timeline | platform-specific value | corresponding control ID | Use matching control SPs |

---

#### XStudio_Menu_Mst_Tbl

| Column | Type | Notes |
|---|---|---|
| `ID` | uniqueidentifier | PK |
| `ParentID` | uniqueidentifier | **Must be ApplicationID** |
| `Name` | varchar | Menu label |
| `PageID` | uniqueidentifier | Page this menu item opens; can be NULL for parent/header menus |
| `SubMenuOfID` | uniqueidentifier | Parent menu item (for nesting) |
| `Sn` | int | Sort order |
| `IsVisible` | bit | Shown in navigation |
| `MenuTooltip` | varchar | Tooltip/label metadata |
| `MenuType` | varchar | Platform menu type metadata |
| `ControlType` | varchar | Platform menu control type metadata |
| `IconID` | uniqueidentifier | Optional icon |
| `ImageID` | uniqueidentifier | Optional image |
| `LinkTarget` | varchar | Optional link target |
| `FilterCondition` | varchar | Optional filter condition |

##### Contract

- Create/update menu rows only through `XStudio_Save_Menu_Configuration_Usp`.
- Delete menu rows only through `XStudio_Delete_Menu_Configuration_Usp`.
- Change menu order only through `XStudio_Save_Menu_Sequence_Usp`.
- `ParentID` is the application ID, not the page ID.
- `PageID` points to the page opened by the menu.
- `SubMenuOfID` points to the parent menu when nested.
- Parent/header menus can have `PageID = NULL`.
- A menu row alone is not enough for visibility; it needs MenuRights.

##### Failure modes

| Symptom | Likely cause |
|---|---|
| Menu missing | No active MenuRights, `IsVisible = 0`, wrong `ParentID`, or parent menu missing rights |
| Menu opens wrong page | Wrong `PageID` |
| Child menu invisible | Parent menu does not have role access |
| Menu order wrong | Sequence not saved through menu sequence SP |

---

#### XStudio_MenuRights_Mst_Tbl

| Column | Type | Notes |
|---|---|---|
| `ID` | uniqueidentifier | PK |
| `ParentID` | uniqueidentifier | **Must be Menu ID** |
| `RoleID` | uniqueidentifier | Role that can see this menu |
| `IsDeleted` | bit | Soft-delete flag |

##### Contract

- MenuRights are required for menu visibility.
- Add rights only through `XStudio_Save_Menu_Rights_Usp`.
- Remove rights only through `XStudio_Delete_Menu_Rights_Usp`.
- The `ParentID` is the menu ID, not the application ID or page ID.
- When adding rights to a child menu, verify the parent menu also has rights for the same role.

---

#### XStudio_Tab_Container_Mst_Tbl

| Column | Type | Notes |
|---|---|---|
| `ID` | uniqueidentifier | PK |
| `Name` | varchar | Tab container name |
| `ParentID` | uniqueidentifier | **Must be ApplicationID** |

##### Contract

- A tab container is a control that can be bound to a page using `XStudio_Save_Page_Control_Configuration_Usp` with `ControlType = 'tab'`.
- Tab containers are optional. Do not create them for ordinary single-control pages.
- The page only hosts the tab container; tabs and tab child controls are configured separately.

---

#### XStudio_Tab_Mst_Tbl

| Column | Type | Notes |
|---|---|---|
| `ID` | uniqueidentifier | PK |
| `ParentID` | uniqueidentifier | **Must be Tab container ID** |
| `Name` | varchar | Tab label/name |
| `Icon` | varchar | Optional icon |
| `ImageID` | uniqueidentifier | Optional image |
| `RoleIDList` | varchar | Roles allowed for the tab |

##### Contract

- Create/update tabs through `XStudio_Save_Tab_Configuration_Usp`.
- Delete tabs through tab delete SPs.
- Tabs are children of a tab container, not of a page directly.

---

#### XStudio_Tab_Dtl_Tbl

| Column | Type | Notes |
|---|---|---|
| `ID` | uniqueidentifier | PK |
| `ParentID` | uniqueidentifier | **Must be Tab ID** |
| `Name` | varchar | Tab-control name |
| `ControlID` | uniqueidentifier | Bound child control ID |
| `ControlType` | varchar | Child control type such as `grid`, `edit`, `html`, etc. |
| `RowID` | varchar/int | Layout row |
| `DivID` | varchar/int | Layout/div |
| `ControlUrl` | varchar | URL for URL/HTML/IFrame-style controls when applicable |
| `CustomCss` | varchar | Optional CSS |
| `AdditionalParameter` | varchar | Additional control parameters |
| `LoadUrlAsIFrame` | bit | IFrame flag |

##### Contract

- Bind controls inside tabs through `XStudio_Save_Tab_Control_Configuration_Usp`.
- Do not directly insert tab-control rows when the SP is available.
- A tab with no active tab controls renders as empty content.

---

#### Page collection tables

Page collection tables are not runtime page/menu creation tables.

They are collection/bookmark/grouping metadata used by page-collection SPs such as:

- `XStudio_Add_Page_Collection_Usp`
- `XStudio_Add_Page_To_Page_Collection_Usp`
- `XStudio_Get_Page_Collection_Usp`
- `XStudio_Get_Page_Collection_Details_Usp`
- `XStudio_Update_Page_Collection_Usp`
- `XStudio_Update_Page_Collection_Dtl_Usp`
- `XStudio_Remove_Page_Collection_Usp`
- `XStudio_Remove_Page_From_Page_Collection`

Do not use page-collection SPs or tables to create screens, bind controls, or expose pages in menus.

---

#### Runtime UI composition summary

```text
Create/resolve page header
-> bind one or more controls through PageControls
-> if a bound control is a container, configure its children through that control family's SPs
-> create/update menu
-> add menu rights
-> run postflight checks
```

| Step | Required mechanism |
|---|---|
| Page header | Guarded `XStudio_Page_Mst_Tbl` insert only if no page-create SP exists |
| Page → Control | `XStudio_Save_Page_Control_Configuration_Usp` |
| Menu | `XStudio_Save_Menu_Configuration_Usp` |
| MenuRights | `XStudio_Save_Menu_Rights_Usp` |
| Menu order | `XStudio_Save_Menu_Sequence_Usp` |
| Tab | `XStudio_Save_Tab_Configuration_Usp` |
| Tab → Control | `XStudio_Save_Tab_Control_Configuration_Usp` |

> End of imported document.

## 18. Configure Asset Structure Events

**Source file:** `02_TASK_PLAYBOOKS/configure_asset_structure_events.md`  
**File type:** `.md`  
**Size:** `1465` bytes  
**Modified on:** `2026-05-04 17:49:24`  
**SHA256:** `f845655f9259`  
**Encoding used:** `utf-8`

> Start of imported document.

### Playbook — Configure Asset Structure Events

#### Goal

Configure event detection/state/action behavior for equipment types where `IsEventCheck = 1`.

#### Generated event tables

For equipment type `EAF`:

```text
EAF_Event_Mst_Tbl
EAF_Event_Attribute_Mst_Tbl
EAF_Event_Tag_Mapping_Tbl
EAF_Event_Configuration_Mst_Tbl
EAF_Event_State_Mst_Tbl
EAF_Event_Error_Condition_Mst_Tbl
EAF_Event_Error_Configuration_Mst_Tbl
EAF_Event_Action_Mst_Tbl
```

#### Event model

```text
Event master
→ event attributes
→ event tag mappings
→ event configuration
→ event states
→ error conditions/configuration
→ event actions
```

#### Example events

##### EAF

```text
Charging Start
Power On
Melting Start
Tapping Start
Tapping End
Power Off
```

##### Blast Furnace

```text
Tap Start
Tap End
Slag Start
Slag End
```

##### Rolling Mill

```text
Mill Running
Cobble
Stand Change
Delay Start
Delay End
```

#### State fields

Important state columns include:

```text
StateName
StateSequence
StateCondition
IsErrorState
StateOnWorkFlow
StateOffWorkFlow
IsActive
WorkFlowAttribute
IsWorkFlowEnable
StateOnDelay
StateOffDelay
IsDifferentOffCondition
StateOffCondition
```

#### Mapping event tags

Use:

```text
XStudio_AssetStructure_Update_Event_Tag_Mapping_Usp
```

or API endpoints related to AssetStructure event configuration.

#### Design rule

Enable event schema only for assets that have meaningful automated state transitions. Avoid event schema for passive/static assets.

> End of imported document.

## 19. Configure Save And Continue

**Source file:** `02_TASK_PLAYBOOKS/configure_save_and_continue.md`  
**File type:** `.md`  
**Size:** `8318` bytes  
**Modified on:** `2026-05-12 15:19:59`  
**SHA256:** `7332b3f2c094`  
**Encoding used:** `utf-8`

> Start of imported document.

### Playbook: Configure Save / Cancel / Save & Continue Navigation

#### Purpose

Configure where a user lands after clicking:

```text
Save
Cancel
Save & Continue
```

on an XStudio edit form.

#### Correct rule

```text
Use XStudio SPs wherever a suitable SP exists.
If no suitable SP exists, preserve the table operation as a documented no-SP exception with live schema inspection, guards, real user audit values, and postflight.
```

For edit navigation fields:

```text
No generic edit-navigation save SP is currently confirmed.
```

Therefore this playbook preserves the original `XStudio_Edit_Mst_Tbl` update knowledge as a **documented no-SP exception**.

#### Table touched

| Table | Purpose | Write path |
|---|---|---|
| `XStudio_Edit_Mst_Tbl` | Save/cancel/save-and-continue navigation and button labels | No confirmed edit-navigation SP; guarded no-SP exception |

#### Key columns

| Column | Purpose |
|---|---|
| `AfterSavePageID` | Page opened after Save |
| `AfterCancelPageID` | Page opened after Cancel |
| `AfterSaveAndContinuePageID` | Page opened after Save & Continue |
| `AfterSaveUrl` | URL override, alternative to PageID where supported |
| `IsOpenSaveContinuePageAsEditMode` | Opens next record/page in edit mode where supported |
| `SaveButtonName` | Save button label |
| `CancelButtonName` | Cancel button label |
| `SaveAndContinueButtonName` | Save & Continue button label |
| `IsSaveButtonVisible` | Show/hide Save |
| `IsSaveAndContinueButtonVisible` | Show/hide Save & Continue |
| `IsCancelButtonVisible` | Show/hide Cancel |

#### Critical correction

```text
AfterSaveAndContinuePageID is a Page ID, not an Edit ID.
```

The page may contain the same edit form, but the value stored is the **page** that should open after Save & Continue.

#### Preflight

Inspect live edit table columns:

```sql
SELECT
    c.name,
    TYPE_NAME(c.user_type_id) AS DataType,
    c.max_length,
    c.is_nullable
FROM XStudio_Configuration_XMES.sys.columns c
JOIN XStudio_Configuration_XMES.sys.tables t
  ON t.object_id = c.object_id
JOIN XStudio_Configuration_XMES.sys.schemas s
  ON s.schema_id = t.schema_id
WHERE s.name = 'dbo'
  AND t.name = 'XStudio_Edit_Mst_Tbl'
ORDER BY c.column_id;
```

Resolve the edit:

```sql
SELECT
    ID,
    Name,
    ParentID,
    AfterSavePageID,
    AfterCancelPageID,
    AfterSaveAndContinuePageID
FROM XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_Edit_Mst_Tbl
WHERE ID = '<edit_guid>'
  AND IsDeleted = 0;
```

Validate target pages:

```sql
SELECT
    ID,
    Name,
    DisplayName,
    IsPopup,
    IsDeleted
FROM XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_Page_Mst_Tbl
WHERE ID IN
(
    '<list_page_guid>',
    '<edit_page_guid>',
    '<next_step_page_guid>'
)
AND IsDeleted = 0;
```

Expected:

```text
Every configured PageID resolves to one active page.
```

#### Pattern 1 — Standard: Save and Cancel return to list

Use when a normal edit form returns to the list page after Save or Cancel.

Preserved operation as no-SP exception:

```sql
DECLARE @EditID uniqueidentifier = '<edit_guid>';
DECLARE @ListPageID uniqueidentifier = '<list_page_guid>';
DECLARE @UserID varchar(36) = '<deployment_user_guid>';

UPDATE XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_Edit_Mst_Tbl
SET AfterSavePageID   = @ListPageID,
    AfterCancelPageID = @ListPageID,
    ModifiedBy        = @UserID,
    ModifiedOn        = GETDATE()
WHERE ID = @EditID
  AND IsDeleted = 0;
```

If the table has `Source` / `HostAddress`, set them as well.

#### Pattern 2 — Rapid data entry: Save returns to list, Save & Continue opens a fresh form

Use when users add multiple child/detail records in sequence, such as consumption records against a WO.

```sql
DECLARE @EditID uniqueidentifier = '<edit_guid>';
DECLARE @ListPageID uniqueidentifier = '<list_page_guid>';
DECLARE @EditPageID uniqueidentifier = '<edit_page_guid>';
DECLARE @UserID varchar(36) = '<deployment_user_guid>';

UPDATE XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_Edit_Mst_Tbl
SET AfterSavePageID                = @ListPageID,
    AfterCancelPageID              = @ListPageID,
    AfterSaveAndContinuePageID     = @EditPageID,
    IsSaveAndContinueButtonVisible = 1,
    SaveAndContinueButtonName      = 'Save & Add Another',
    ModifiedBy                     = @UserID,
    ModifiedOn                     = GETDATE()
WHERE ID = @EditID
  AND IsDeleted = 0;
```

Important:

```text
@EditPageID is the page containing the edit control, not the edit-control ID.
```

#### URL/request parameters carry through

When the list page has a URL like:

```text
?WOID=<guid>
```

and the edit popup/page opens from that list, the edit form can use:

```text
<request.WOID>
```

After Save & Continue, if the target edit page keeps the same URL context, a new blank form can still use:

```text
DefaultValue = <request.WOID>
```

This is why Save & Continue is commonly paired with hidden/default FK fields in child entry forms.

#### Pattern 3 — Wizard/sequential flow: Save advances to next step

```sql
DECLARE @EditID uniqueidentifier = '<edit_guid>';
DECLARE @NextStepPageID uniqueidentifier = '<next_step_page_guid>';
DECLARE @UserID varchar(36) = '<deployment_user_guid>';

UPDATE XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_Edit_Mst_Tbl
SET AfterSavePageID                  = @NextStepPageID,
    AfterSaveAndContinuePageID       = NULL,
    IsSaveAndContinueButtonVisible   = 0,
    IsSaveButtonVisible              = 1,
    SaveButtonName                   = 'Next',
    ModifiedBy                       = @UserID,
    ModifiedOn                       = GETDATE()
WHERE ID = @EditID
  AND IsDeleted = 0;
```

#### Pattern 4 — Cancel hidden / save-only form

Use only when the UX intentionally blocks cancel.

```sql
UPDATE XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_Edit_Mst_Tbl
SET IsCancelButtonVisible = 0,
    IsSaveButtonVisible   = 1,
    ModifiedBy            = '<deployment_user_guid>',
    ModifiedOn            = GETDATE()
WHERE ID = '<edit_guid>'
  AND IsDeleted = 0;
```

#### Pattern 5 — URL override

Where the platform uses `AfterSaveUrl`, preserve the URL override mechanics.

```sql
UPDATE XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_Edit_Mst_Tbl
SET AfterSaveUrl = '<relative_or_platform_url>',
    ModifiedBy   = '<deployment_user_guid>',
    ModifiedOn   = GETDATE()
WHERE ID = '<edit_guid>'
  AND IsDeleted = 0;
```

Use only when the live renderer supports URL navigation for the target scenario.

#### Postflight

```sql
DECLARE @EditID uniqueidentifier = '<edit_guid>';

SELECT
    ID,
    Name,
    AfterSavePageID,
    AfterCancelPageID,
    AfterSaveAndContinuePageID,
    AfterSaveUrl,
    IsOpenSaveContinuePageAsEditMode,
    SaveButtonName,
    CancelButtonName,
    SaveAndContinueButtonName,
    IsSaveButtonVisible,
    IsSaveAndContinueButtonVisible,
    IsCancelButtonVisible
FROM XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_Edit_Mst_Tbl
WHERE ID = @EditID
  AND IsDeleted = 0;
```

Verify configured pages resolve:

```sql
SELECT
    p.ID,
    p.Name,
    p.DisplayName
FROM XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_Page_Mst_Tbl p
WHERE p.ID IN
(
    SELECT AfterSavePageID
    FROM XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_Edit_Mst_Tbl
    WHERE ID = @EditID

    UNION

    SELECT AfterCancelPageID
    FROM XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_Edit_Mst_Tbl
    WHERE ID = @EditID

    UNION

    SELECT AfterSaveAndContinuePageID
    FROM XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_Edit_Mst_Tbl
    WHERE ID = @EditID
)
AND p.IsDeleted = 0;
```

Expected:

```text
Every non-null configured PageID resolves to an active page.
```

#### Common mistakes

| Mistake | Effect |
|---|---|
| `AfterSaveAndContinuePageID = <edit_guid>` | Navigation fails or opens wrong target; it must be a page ID |
| Target page deleted/inactive | Save/cancel appears broken |
| URL context not preserved | `<request.WOID>` default no longer works |
| `ModifiedBy = 'sa'` | Unsafe audit data |
| Updating every edit by name only | Can change multiple forms unintentionally |
| Hiding Cancel without UX approval | Users can get trapped in a form |

#### See also

- [create_edit_form.md](create_edit_form.md)
- [create_parent_child_flow.md](create_parent_child_flow.md)
- [create_inline_grid.md](create_inline_grid.md)
- [page_menu_template.sql](../03_SQL_TEMPLATES/page_menu_template.sql)
- [postflight_checks.sql](../03_SQL_TEMPLATES/postflight_checks.sql)

> End of imported document.

## 20. Configure Wo Execution

**Source file:** `02_TASK_PLAYBOOKS/configure_wo_execution.md`  
**File type:** `.md`  
**Size:** `10898` bytes  
**Modified on:** `2026-05-12 15:20:09`  
**SHA256:** `de8b1d8de173`  
**Encoding used:** `utf-8`

> Start of imported document.

### Playbook: Configure WO Execution

#### Purpose

Set up the WO Execution module: Production, Consumption, and WO Events linked to Work Orders through row-level navigation, request-token filtering, edit-form defaults, optional tabs, and postflight validation.

#### Context

WO Execution is the shop-floor data-entry layer. From a Work Order, operators record:

| Area | Purpose |
|---|---|
| Production | What was made: output, quantity, heat/lot, grade |
| Consumption | What was consumed: materials, charged quantities |
| WO Events | Process events, timestamps, status changes, operator notes |
| Plan vs Actual | Comparison of planned requirements vs actual consumption/production |

#### Correct rule

```text
Use XStudio SPs wherever a suitable SP exists.
If no suitable SP exists, preserve the operation as a documented no-SP exception with live schema inspection, guards, real user audit values, and postflight.
```

This playbook is domain orchestration. It intentionally preserves XMES-specific mechanics.

#### Entities involved

| Entity | Table | Purpose |
|---|---|---|
| Work Order | `XMES_Work_Order_Mst_Tbl` | Parent WO being executed |
| Production | `XMES_Production_Trn_Tbl` | What was produced |
| Consumption | `XMES_Consumption_Trn_Tbl` | What was consumed |
| WO Events | `XMES_WO_Event_Trn_Tbl` | Process events |
| Requirements / Plan | `XMES_Work_Order_Requirement_Trn_Tbl` | Planned materials/quantities |
| Plan vs Actual | custom SP-backed LV | Compares plan vs actual |

#### Navigation architecture

```text
Work Order LV
  -> WOProduction link column
  -> WOConsumption link column
  -> WOEvents link column
  -> WO Execution tab/list pages

Execution pages:
  Production LV + edit, filtered by WOID
  Consumption LV + edit, filtered by WOID
  WO Events LV + edit, filtered by WOID
  Plan vs Actual SP-backed LV
```

Tabs are optional. Use tabs only if the actual page design requires a tab container.

#### Step 1 — Add link columns to Work Order LVs

Use:

```text
create_lv_link_column.md
lv_link_column_template.sql
```

Preserved WO link-column contract:

| Link column | Target URL | Serial |
|---|---|---:|
| `WOProduction` | `XMES/DEFAULT/List_Page_XMES_Production_Trn_Tbl?WOID={ID}` | 45 |
| `WOConsumption` | `XMES/DEFAULT/List_Page_XMES_Consumption_Trn_Tbl?WOID={ID}` | 46 |
| `WOEvents` | `XMES/DEFAULT/List_Page_XMES_WO_Event_Trn_Tbl?WOID={ID}` | 47 |

Common fields:

```text
Type = link
LinkType = fixedtext
LinkValue = Open
CustomLinkTargetMode = Inside
AttributeID blank/null
RelationID blank/null
RelationIDList blank/null
IsVisible = 1
IsCustomField = 1
Alignment = center
ColumnProperties = valid JSON
```

No generic save-LV-column SP is confirmed, so link-column metadata remains a guarded no-SP exception inside the link-column template.

After adding each column, regenerate the Work Order LV view through:

```text
XStudio_CreateViewForLV_Usp
```

#### Step 2 — Filter execution LVs by WOID

Preserve the confirmed filter pattern:

```text
(WorkOrderID='<request.WOID>' OR IIF('<request.WOID>'='',1,0)=1)
```

This means:

| Entry path | Result |
|---|---|
| Opened from WO row with `?WOID=<guid>` | shows only records for that WO |
| Opened from menu with no `WOID` | shows all records |

No generic LV-header save SP is confirmed for `FilterCondition`, so this remains a guarded no-SP exception.

```sql
DECLARE @UserID varchar(36) = '<deployment_user_guid>';

UPDATE XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_LV_Mst_Tbl
SET FilterCondition = '(WorkOrderID=''<request.WOID>'' OR IIF(''<request.WOID>''='''',1,0)=1)',
    ModifiedBy      = @UserID,
    ModifiedOn      = GETDATE()
WHERE Name IN
(
    'List_XMES_Production_Trn_Tbl',
    'List_XMES_Consumption_Trn_Tbl',
    'List_XMES_WO_Event_Trn_Tbl'
)
AND IsDeleted = 0;
```

Regenerate/verify the affected LV views if required by the local LV implementation.

#### Step 3 — Edit form controls

The Consumption edit form is the canonical working pattern.

| Field | ControlType | ControlName | DefaultValue |
|---|---|---|---|
| `WorkOrderID` | `selectbox` or hidden field | `cmb_WorkOrderID` | `<request.WOID>` |
| `MaterialID` | `selectbox` | `cmb_MaterialID` | — |
| `UOMID` | `selectbox` | `cmb_UOMID` | — |
| `ConsumedQty` | `number` | `num_ConsumedQty` | — |
| `RecordedOn` | `datetimepicker` | `dtp_RecordedOn` | — |

Required setup:

1. dropdown attributes must have six properties + relation;
2. edit controls generated through `Xstudio_Insert_Edit_Columns_Info_Usp`;
3. renderer-safe JSON completed through `edit_control_safe_json_template.sql`;
4. WorkOrderID default set to `<request.WOID>`.

Preferred sequence:

```text
dropdown_properties_template.sql
edit_control_safe_json_template.sql
configure parent FK DefaultValue
postflight
```

Preserved default-value operation as no-SP exception if not covered by the template:

```sql
DECLARE @ConsumptionEditID uniqueidentifier = '<consumption_edit_id>';
DECLARE @UserID varchar(36) = '<deployment_user_guid>';

UPDATE XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_EditControls_Mst_Tbl
SET ControlProperties = JSON_MODIFY(
        CASE
            WHEN ControlProperties IS NULL
              OR ISJSON(CONVERT(nvarchar(max), ControlProperties)) <> 1
            THEN '{}'
            ELSE CONVERT(nvarchar(max), ControlProperties)
        END,
        '$.DefaultValue',
        '<request.WOID>'
    ),
    ModifiedBy = @UserID,
    ModifiedOn = GETDATE()
WHERE ParentID = @ConsumptionEditID
  AND ControlName LIKE 'cmb_WorkOrderID%'
  AND IsDeleted = 0;
```

#### Step 4 — Tab container / WO Execution page

Known live object from earlier work:

| Object | ID |
|---|---|
| Application DEFAULT | `29123B0B-3872-41CF-B5E1-67C4A8F0D4BC` |
| Tab Container `WO Execution` | `590CBC0D-CCA7-4AC8-A0AF-B4A3C086A480` |

Tabs inside container:

```text
Consumption
Production
WO Events
Plan vs Actual
```

Use tab SPs only if tabs are part of the page design:

```text
XStudio_Save_Tab_Configuration_Usp
XStudio_Save_Tab_Control_Configuration_Usp
```

Bind the tab container or direct LV/edit controls to the page through:

```text
XStudio_Save_Page_Control_Configuration_Usp
```

via:

```text
page_menu_template.sql
```

Do not directly insert PageControls when the SP exists.

#### Step 5 — Save & Continue on execution edit forms

No generic edit-navigation SP is confirmed, so preserve as guarded no-SP exception.

```sql
DECLARE @ExecutionEditID uniqueidentifier = '<execution_edit_guid>';
DECLARE @ExecutionListPageID uniqueidentifier = '<execution_list_page_guid>';
DECLARE @ExecutionEditPageID uniqueidentifier = '<execution_edit_page_guid>';
DECLARE @UserID varchar(36) = '<deployment_user_guid>';

UPDATE XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_Edit_Mst_Tbl
SET AfterSavePageID                = @ExecutionListPageID,
    AfterCancelPageID              = @ExecutionListPageID,
    AfterSaveAndContinuePageID     = @ExecutionEditPageID,
    IsSaveAndContinueButtonVisible = 1,
    SaveAndContinueButtonName      = 'Save & Add Another',
    ModifiedBy                     = @UserID,
    ModifiedOn                     = GETDATE()
WHERE ID = @ExecutionEditID
  AND IsDeleted = 0;
```

Correction:

```text
AfterSaveAndContinuePageID is the edit page ID, not the edit control ID.
```

The edit page must preserve URL/request context so `<request.WOID>` continues to populate WorkOrderID.

#### Step 6 — Regenerate LV views

After LV column or query-fragment changes, regenerate each affected LV using:

```text
XStudio_CreateViewForLV_Usp
```

Use explicit non-null fragments:

```text
@ColumnQuery
@RelationQuery
@GroupByQuery
@WhereCondition
@IsCalculatedView
```

Do not assume `@LvID`-only calls are valid.

#### Step 7 — Plan vs Actual LV

The Plan vs Actual tab can be custom-SP-backed.

Preserved specification:

```text
Plan source:
  XMES_Work_Order_Requirement_Trn_Tbl

Actual source:
  SUM(XMES_Consumption_Trn_Tbl.ConsumedQty)
  grouped by RequirementID or matching material/requirement key
```

Custom SP:

```text
usp_XMES_WO_Plan_Vs_Actual
```

No generic LV-header save SP is confirmed for `SPName`, so setting `SPName` is a guarded no-SP exception.

```sql
UPDATE XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_LV_Mst_Tbl
SET SPName     = 'usp_XMES_WO_Plan_Vs_Actual',
    ModifiedBy = '<deployment_user_guid>',
    ModifiedOn = GETDATE()
WHERE Name = 'List_XMES_Plan_vs_Actual'
  AND IsDeleted = 0;
```

Validate the SP exists in the data DB:

```sql
SELECT OBJECT_ID('XStudio_XMES.dbo.usp_XMES_WO_Plan_Vs_Actual') AS ObjectId;
```

#### Postflight

```sql
-- Link columns on WO LV
SELECT
    Name,
    Type,
    CustomLink,
    CustomLinkTargetMode,
    IsCustomField,
    AttributeID
FROM XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_LvColumns_Mst_Tbl
WHERE ParentID = '<WO_LV_GUID>'
  AND Type = 'link'
  AND IsDeleted = 0;

-- FilterCondition on execution LVs
SELECT
    Name,
    FilterCondition
FROM XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_LV_Mst_Tbl
WHERE Name IN
(
    'List_XMES_Production_Trn_Tbl',
    'List_XMES_Consumption_Trn_Tbl',
    'List_XMES_WO_Event_Trn_Tbl'
)
AND IsDeleted = 0;

-- Verify execution views exist and return data
SELECT TOP 1 *
FROM XStudio_XMES.dbo.XStudio_List_XMES_Consumption_Trn_Tbl_Vw;

SELECT TOP 1 *
FROM XStudio_XMES.dbo.XStudio_List_XMES_Production_Trn_Tbl_Vw;

-- Verify WorkOrderID default in execution edit
SELECT
    ControlName,
    JSON_VALUE(CONVERT(nvarchar(max), ControlProperties), '$.DefaultValue') AS DefaultValue
FROM XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_EditControls_Mst_Tbl
WHERE ParentID = '<execution_edit_guid>'
  AND ControlName LIKE 'cmb_WorkOrderID%'
  AND IsDeleted = 0;
```

Expected:

```text
WO link columns exist and are custom link columns
execution LV filters use <request.WOID>
execution views query successfully
edit WorkOrderID defaults to <request.WOID>
Save & Continue target resolves to active page
```

#### Common mistakes

| Mistake | Effect |
|---|---|
| Direct link column with real AttributeID | Generated view tries to select physical action column |
| Missing WOID filter | Execution lists show all records |
| WorkOrderID dropdown missing default | Records save without parent WO context |
| Dropdown properties incomplete | Consumption edit crashes or shows no options |
| Save & Continue target set to edit ID | Navigation fails |
| Tab assumed when design is direct page | Overcomplicates page binding |
| Plan vs Actual SP missing | Tab opens but shows no data |

#### See also

- [create_lv_link_column.md](create_lv_link_column.md)
- [create_parent_child_flow.md](create_parent_child_flow.md)
- [configure_save_and_continue.md](configure_save_and_continue.md)
- [create_edit_form.md](create_edit_form.md)
- [create_lv_grid.md](create_lv_grid.md)
- [plan_vs_actual.md](../05_XMES_DOMAIN/plan_vs_actual.md)
- [page_menu_template.sql](../03_SQL_TEMPLATES/page_menu_template.sql)

## 21. Create Asset Structure Equipment Instances

**Source file:** `02_TASK_PLAYBOOKS/create_asset_structure_equipment_instances.md`  
**File type:** `.md`  
**Size:** `1479` bytes  
**Modified on:** `2026-05-04 17:49:24`  
**SHA256:** `697e275055df`  
**Encoding used:** `utf-8`

> Start of imported document.

### Playbook — Create Asset Structure Equipment Instances

#### Goal

Create physical equipment records under a generated equipment type table.

Example:

```text
Equipment Type: EAF
Instances: EAF-1, EAF-2
Table: EAF_Mst_Tbl
```

#### Required inputs

```text
Equipment instance name
TemplateID
AreaID
RoleIDList if needed
AssetIdentificationProperties if needed
PhysicalTag if used
```

#### Flow

```text
Create equipment type
→ create template
→ insert equipment instance into <EquipmentType>_Mst_Tbl or use AssetStructure Equipment/Nodes API
→ equipment instance trigger creates mapping rows
→ consolidated equipment view is regenerated or should already include instance
```

#### Trigger behavior

When an active equipment instance is inserted/updated:

```text
XStudio_<EquipmentType>_Mst_Tbl_TRG
→ XStudio_Add_Attribute_Equipment_Mapping_Usp
→ mapping rows created for template attributes
```

When soft-deleted:

```text
XStudio_Delete_Attribute_Equipment_Mapping_Usp
```

#### Generic selector

After creation, check:

```sql
SELECT *
FROM XStudio_XMES.dbo.XStudio_Equipment_Mst_Vw
WHERE EquipmentName = '<InstanceName>';
```

Remember column spelling:

```text
EquipmetTypeID
```

#### MES linking pattern

For generic MES event tables:

```text
EquipmentTypeID + EquipmentID
```

For plant-specific execution tables:

```text
SMSHeat.EAFID → EAF_Mst_Tbl.ID
BFTap.BlastFurnaceID → BlastFurnace_Mst_Tbl.ID
RMRolling.MillStandID → RollingMillStand_Mst_Tbl.ID
```

> End of imported document.

## 22. Create Asset Structure Equipment Type

**Source file:** `02_TASK_PLAYBOOKS/create_asset_structure_equipment_type.md`  
**File type:** `.md`  
**Size:** `17977` bytes  
**Modified on:** `2026-05-04 19:19:26`  
**SHA256:** `028034c96474`  
**Encoding used:** `utf-8`

> Start of imported document.

### Playbook — Create Asset Structure Equipment Type

#### Purpose

Use this playbook when an agent must create or validate a new XStudio Asset Structure equipment class such as `EAF`, `LRF`, `BlastFurnace`, `RollingMillStand`, `Boiler`, or `Turbine`.

An Equipment Type is not an equipment instance. It is a structural class that causes XStudio to generate a family of physical SQL tables, XStudio entities, attributes, triggers, runtime views, optional block tables, and optional event tables.

Batch table behavior is intentionally out of scope for this playbook. Do not enable or design around `IsBatchingEntity` unless a separate batch-specific analysis has been completed.

---

#### Agent Rules

Agents must follow these rules before producing SQL, API calls, seed data, or validation scripts.

```text
1. Do not manually create generated <EquipmentType> tables in production.
2. Use the Asset Structure UI/API or controlled insert into Equipment_Type_Mst_Tbl only when trigger behavior is expected.
3. Treat equipment type creation as a heavy generator operation.
4. Define hierarchy before equipment types.
5. Use equipment class names, not physical instance names.
6. Choose block frequency before creation; it is structural.
7. Choose event schema before creation; event tables are generated only if IsEventCheck = 1.
8. Do not include batch table creation in this playbook.
9. Always run postflight checks after creation.
10. If postflight fails, do not patch randomly; inspect the generator failure point first.
```

---

#### Decision Gate — Asset Structure vs Custom Entity

Before creating an Equipment Type, answer these questions.

| Question | If YES | If NO |
|---|---|---|
| Is this a physical plant asset? | Use Asset Structure | Consider a normal Master entity |
| Does it have historian/SCADA/OPC tags? | Use Asset Structure | Consider normal Master entity if static |
| Will multiple similar units exist? | Use Equipment Type + Templates | Still use Asset Structure if it has tags/events |
| Does it produce interval data? | Use block/frequency | No block required |
| Does it have operating states/events? | Use `IsEventCheck = 1` | Leave event schema disabled |
| Is it only a classification list? | Do not use Asset Structure | Use custom Master entity |

Examples that should use Asset Structure:

```text
EAF
LRF
VD
CCM
BlastFurnace
DRIKiln
PelletIndurationFurnace
RollingMillStand
Boiler
Turbine
Generator
Compressor
Pump
```

Examples that usually should not be Equipment Types:

```text
Shift
Crew
Grade
Material
ReasonCode
QualityParameter
StorageCategory
```

---

#### Required Decisions Before Creation

| Decision | Required? | Why it matters |
|---|---:|---|
| Equipment Type Name | Yes | Becomes physical table prefix |
| Hierarchy / Area model | Yes | Instances require placement context |
| Block Type | Yes, if historian/block capture needed | Controls block behavior |
| Frequency | Yes, if block enabled | Creates physical table name like `EAF_5` |
| Summary Hierarchy | Recommended | Used for summary/hierarchy grouping |
| Data Source | Recommended if tags are mapped | Used during historian/tag mapping |
| Is Event Check | Yes/No decision | Generates event tables only when enabled |
| Is Batching Entity | Out of scope | Do not design here |

---

#### Naming Rules

Use equipment class names, not physical asset names.

Correct:

```text
EAF
LRF
BlastFurnace
DRIKiln
PelletBallingDisc
PelletIndurationFurnace
CCM
CCMStrand
ReheatingFurnace
RollingMillStand
Boiler
Turbine
Generator
```

Incorrect:

```text
EAF1
EAF-1
BF1
TG1
CCM1Strand1
RollingStand7
```

Reason:

```text
Equipment Type = class
Equipment Instance = physical unit
```

Example:

```text
Equipment Type: EAF
Generated table: EAF_Mst_Tbl
Instances: EAF-1, EAF-2
```

SQL-safe naming rules:

```text
Use PascalCase or approved short uppercase acronyms.
No spaces.
No hyphens.
No slashes.
No plant-specific instance suffixes.
Do not rename after creation.
```

---

#### Pre-Work — Confirm Hierarchy Exists

Run this before creating equipment types.

```sql
SELECT ID, Name, SerialNumber
FROM XStudio_XMES.dbo.Asset_Hierarchy_Mst_Tbl
WHERE IsDeleted = 0
ORDER BY SerialNumber ASC;
```

Expected LMEL-style hierarchy:

```text
1 Enterprise
2 Site
3 Plant / Area
4 Shop / Process Line
5 Work Center
6 Equipment
7 Sub-equipment / Instrumented Unit
```

If hierarchy is missing or incomplete, stop. Configure hierarchy first through Asset Structure hierarchy flow.

Relevant procedures:

```text
XStudio_AssetStructure_Get_Hierarchy_Usp
XStudio_Save_Hierarchy_Configuration_Usp
XStudio_AssetStructure_Create_Hierarchy_Entity_Usp
XStudio_AssetStructure_Update_Hierarchy_Menu_Usp
```

---

#### Creation Pattern

Supported pattern:

```text
Save Equipment_Type_Mst_Tbl row through UI/API
→ Equipment_Type_TRG fires
→ XStudio_Create_Equipment_Type_Table_Usp runs
→ generated table family is created
→ generated triggers are created
→ consolidated views are regenerated
```

Do not manually create the generated tables except during controlled repair of failed generation.

Main generator trigger behavior:

```text
Equipment_Type_Mst_Tbl INSERT/UPDATE
→ if Source not in ('Synchronizer', 'HandHeld')
   → if IsDeleted = 0:
        XStudio_Create_Equipment_Type_Table_Usp
     else:
        XStudio_Delete_Equipment_Type_Related_Table_Usp
   → XStudio_Create_Equipment_View_Usp
   → XStudio_Create_Equipment_Template_View_Usp
   → XStudio_Create_Equipment_Tag_View_Usp
```

---

#### UI/API Creation Flow

Preferred human/UI path:

```text
System Settings
→ Asset Structure
→ Equipment Types
→ Add New
→ Save and Process
```

Fields:

| UI Field | Agent interpretation |
|---|---|
| Equipment Type | Class name and physical table prefix |
| Block Type | Block behavior if interval capture is required |
| Summary Hierarchy | Hierarchy grouping used by summary/aggregation |
| Icon | UI display only; optional |
| Is Event Check | Enables generated event schema |
| Is Batching Entity | Out of scope here |
| Data Source | Historian/OPC/IIoT source context |

Known API direction:

```text
GET  /api/Rest/{systemId}/AssetStructure/EquipmentType
POST /api/Rest/{systemId}/AssetStructure/EquipmentType/Save
```

Agents must verify the exact Swagger path before generating API automation.

---

#### Block / Frequency Decision

Frequency is structural. It affects physical table naming, block triggers, historian SP generation, and summary configuration.

Common table naming:

```text
<EquipmentType>_<Frequency>
```

Examples:

```text
EAF_5
BlastFurnace_5
RollingMillStand_1
Turbine_15
```

Recommended LMEL starting points:

| Frequency | Use |
|---:|---|
| 1 minute | Critical high-frequency process equipment only |
| 5 minutes | Normal process equipment historian blocks |
| 15 minutes | CPP/utilities/slower process balances |
| 60 minutes | Low-frequency balances |

Recommended Day-1 guidance:

| Plant / equipment | Suggested frequency |
|---|---:|
| SMS EAF/LRF/VD/CCM | 1 or 5 min |
| Blast Furnace | 5 or 15 min |
| DRI / Pellet | 5 or 15 min |
| Rolling Mill critical stands | 1 or 5 min |
| CPP boiler/turbine/generator | 5 or 15 min |
| Yard/static assets | No block unless required |

Do not create unnecessary frequency variants. Every frequency creates additional physical objects.

---

#### Event Check Decision

Set `IsEventCheck = 1` only when the equipment has meaningful states/events.

Good candidates:

```text
EAF
LRF
VD
CCM
BlastFurnace
DRIKiln
PelletIndurationFurnace
ReheatingFurnace
RollingMillStand
Boiler
Turbine
Generator
```

Usually avoid event schema for passive or highly numerous assets unless specifically required:

```text
Yard
Storage bay
Minor pump
Static weighbridge location
Non-instrumented sub-equipment
```

If `IsEventCheck = 1`, expected generated event tables include:

```text
<EquipmentType>_Event_Mst_Tbl
<EquipmentType>_Event_Attribute_Mst_Tbl
<EquipmentType>_Event_Tag_Mapping_Tbl
<EquipmentType>_Event_Configuration_Mst_Tbl
<EquipmentType>_Event_State_Mst_Tbl
<EquipmentType>_Event_Error_Condition_Mst_Tbl
<EquipmentType>_Event_Error_Configuration_Mst_Tbl
<EquipmentType>_Event_Action_Mst_Tbl
```

---

#### Expected Generated Objects

For equipment type `EAF`, expect:

```text
EAF_Mst_Tbl
EAF_Tag_Mapping_Tbl
EAF_Attribute_Tag_Template_Mst_Tbl
EAF_Attribute_Tag_Template_Dtl_Tbl
EAF_Data
EAF_<Frequency> or EAF_Block, if block configured
EAF_Event_* tables, if IsEventCheck = 1
```

Expected generated/updated platform objects:

```text
XStudio entities for generated tables
XStudio attributes for generated columns
dropdown properties for references such as TemplateID and AreaID
entity relations for generated dropdowns
equipment/template/template-detail triggers
block trigger if block table exists
historian SPs where generated
LV/list views where generated
XStudio_Equipment_Mst_Vw regenerated
XStudio_Equipment_Template_Mst_Vw regenerated
XStudio_Equipment_Tag_Mst_Vw regenerated
```

Do not assume the generated objects exist until postflight confirms them.

---

#### After Equipment Type Creation

Creating the equipment type only creates the structural family. It does not complete asset modeling.

Required next steps:

```text
1. Create template headers.
2. Ensure tag/equipment attributes exist using the correct equipment/tag datatype.
3. Confirm template detail rows are created.
4. Create equipment instances.
5. Let triggers create tag mapping rows.
6. Update tag mapping rows with datasource and tag names.
7. Configure event definitions and event tag mappings if IsEventCheck = 1.
8. Validate block generation if block/frequency is configured.
```

---

#### Template Follow-Up

Templates are created in:

```text
<EquipmentType>_Attribute_Tag_Template_Mst_Tbl
```

Template detail rows live in:

```text
<EquipmentType>_Attribute_Tag_Template_Dtl_Tbl
```

Template detail rows are auto-populated only from attributes with the equipment/tag datatype:

```text
EC8BE677-39DB-4945-A62F-70D4A4F9CE78
```

Agent rule:

```text
If a process parameter must appear in template detail and tag mapping,
do not create it as an ordinary Text/Decimal attribute.
Create it using the platform equipment/tag datatype.
```

Example EAF template attributes:

```text
TapTemperature
PowerKWh
OxygenFlow
ElectrodeCurrent
FurnacePressure
ChargeWeight
TapWeight
IsStartHeat
IsRunHeat
IsDoneHeat
ErrorCode
```

---

#### Equipment Instance Follow-Up

Instances are created in:

```text
<EquipmentType>_Mst_Tbl
```

Example:

```text
EAF_Mst_Tbl
  EAF-1
  EAF-2
```

Required instance fields typically include:

```text
Name
TemplateID
AreaID
```

Instance trigger effect:

```text
Insert/update active equipment instance
→ XStudio_<EquipmentType>_Mst_Tbl_TRG
→ XStudio_Add_Attribute_Equipment_Mapping_Usp
→ mapping rows created/updated
```

Do not manually create mapping rows unless repairing failed generation.

---

#### Tag Mapping Follow-Up

Normal sequence:

```text
create equipment type
→ create tag/equipment attributes
→ create template
→ create equipment instance
→ let triggers create mapping rows
→ update mapping with tag names
```

Mapping update procedure:

```text
XStudio_AssetStructure_Update_Equipment_And_Attribute_Mapping_Usp
```

Input TVP shape:

```text
Attribute
Equipment
TagName
```

Plus datasource ID.

Mapping target:

```text
<EquipmentType>_Tag_Mapping_Tbl
```

Do not expect historian/current/block values until tag mappings are valid.

---

#### LMEL Recommended Equipment Types

| Equipment Type | Example instances | Event Check | Block Data | Notes |
|---|---|---:|---:|---|
| `CokeOvenBattery` | Battery-1, Battery-2 | Yes | Yes | Battery-level operating events and process data |
| `CokeOvenChamber` | Chamber-01..72 | Yes | Optional | High instance count; avoid unnecessary block volume |
| `BlastFurnace` | BF-1 | Yes | Yes | Tap events, burden/process values |
| `DRIKiln` | DRI-Kiln-1, DRI-Kiln-2 | Yes | Yes | Kiln run/feed/discharge/temp zones |
| `PelletBallingDisc` | Disc-1, Disc-2 | Yes | Yes | Balling process values |
| `PelletIndurationFurnace` | Induration-1 | Yes | Yes | Furnace/zone process data |
| `EAF` | EAF-1, EAF-2 | Yes | Yes | Heat execution and energy/process values |
| `LRF` | LRF-1, LRF-2 | Yes | Yes | Ladle treatment/heating/alloying |
| `VD` | VD-1 | Yes | Yes | Vacuum process data |
| `CCM` | CCM-1 | Yes | Yes | Cast/sequence state |
| `CCMStrand` | CCM-1-S1, CCM-1-S2 | Yes | Optional | Strand-specific values/events |
| `Ladle` | Ladle-001..030 | Optional | No | May be MES lifecycle object more than live asset |
| `ReheatingFurnace` | RHF-1, RHF-2 | Yes | Yes | Charging/discharging, zones, fuel |
| `RollingMillStand` | WRM-S1..S20 | Yes | Yes | Stand speed/load/status |
| `CoolingBed` | CB-1 | Optional | Optional | Depends on automation scope |
| `Boiler` | Boiler-1, Boiler-2 | Yes | Yes | CPP steam/fuel/process values |
| `Turbine` | TG-1, TG-2 | Yes | Yes | CPP generation/load/trip events |
| `Generator` | Gen-1, Gen-2 | Yes | Yes | Electrical generation values |
| `Compressor` | AC-1, AC-2 | Yes | Yes | Utility equipment |
| `Pump` | Pump-1..N | Optional | Optional | Model critical pumps first |
| `Weighbridge` | WB-1, WB-2 | No | Optional | Mostly transactional integration |

---

#### Postflight Checks

Replace `<Name>` and `<EquipmentTypeID>` before running.

##### 1. Equipment type row exists

```sql
SELECT ID, Name, IsDeleted, Source
FROM XStudio_XMES.dbo.Equipment_Type_Mst_Tbl
WHERE Name = '<Name>';
```

Expected:

```text
One active row, IsDeleted = 0
```

##### 2. Generated physical tables exist

```sql
SELECT TABLE_NAME
FROM XStudio_XMES.INFORMATION_SCHEMA.TABLES
WHERE TABLE_NAME LIKE '<Name>%'
ORDER BY TABLE_NAME;
```

Expected minimum:

```text
<Name>_Mst_Tbl
<Name>_Tag_Mapping_Tbl
<Name>_Attribute_Tag_Template_Mst_Tbl
<Name>_Attribute_Tag_Template_Dtl_Tbl
<Name>_Data
```

If block configured, expect:

```text
<Name>_<Frequency> or <Name>_Block
```

If event enabled, expect event tables.

##### 3. XStudio entities exist

```sql
SELECT ID, Name, TableType, IsEquipmentEntity, EquipmentType, IsDeleted
FROM XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_Entities_Mst_Tbl
WHERE EquipmentType = '<EquipmentTypeID>'
  AND IsDeleted = 0
ORDER BY Name;
```

Expected:

```text
Generated entities exist for the generated table family.
```

##### 4. Generated triggers exist

```sql
SELECT name
FROM XStudio_XMES.sys.triggers
WHERE name IN
(
    'XStudio_<Name>_Mst_Tbl_TRG',
    'XStudio_<Name>_Attribute_Tag_Template_Mst_Tbl_TRG',
    'XStudio_<Name>_Attribute_Tag_Template_Dtl_Tbl_TRG'
)
ORDER BY name;
```

Expected:

```text
Three rows.
```

##### 5. Consolidated views compile

```sql
SELECT TOP 1 * FROM XStudio_XMES.dbo.XStudio_Equipment_Mst_Vw;
SELECT TOP 1 * FROM XStudio_XMES.dbo.XStudio_Equipment_Template_Mst_Vw;
SELECT TOP 1 * FROM XStudio_XMES.dbo.XStudio_Equipment_Tag_Mst_Vw;
```

Important:

```text
Use generated column name EquipmetTypeID in these views.
Do not use EquipmentTypeID unless the actual schema confirms it.
```

##### 6. Event tables exist when event check enabled

```sql
SELECT TABLE_NAME
FROM XStudio_XMES.INFORMATION_SCHEMA.TABLES
WHERE TABLE_NAME IN
(
    '<Name>_Event_Mst_Tbl',
    '<Name>_Event_Attribute_Mst_Tbl',
    '<Name>_Event_Tag_Mapping_Tbl',
    '<Name>_Event_Configuration_Mst_Tbl',
    '<Name>_Event_State_Mst_Tbl',
    '<Name>_Event_Error_Condition_Mst_Tbl',
    '<Name>_Event_Error_Configuration_Mst_Tbl',
    '<Name>_Event_Action_Mst_Tbl'
)
ORDER BY TABLE_NAME;
```

Expected when `IsEventCheck = 1`:

```text
Eight rows.
```

##### 7. Template and instance readiness

After creating templates and instances:

```sql
SELECT *
FROM XStudio_XMES.dbo.<Name>_Attribute_Tag_Template_Mst_Tbl
WHERE IsDeleted = 0;

SELECT *
FROM XStudio_XMES.dbo.<Name>_Attribute_Tag_Template_Dtl_Tbl
WHERE IsDeleted = 0;

SELECT *
FROM XStudio_XMES.dbo.<Name>_Mst_Tbl
WHERE IsDeleted = 0;

SELECT *
FROM XStudio_XMES.dbo.<Name>_Tag_Mapping_Tbl
WHERE IsDeleted = 0;
```

Expected:

```text
Template exists.
Template detail rows exist.
Equipment instance exists.
Tag mapping rows exist for template attributes.
```

---

#### Common Mistakes and Agent Corrections

| Mistake | Consequence | Correction |
|---|---|---|
| Using instance name as equipment type, e.g. `EAF1` | Wrong generated schema and no reusable template model | Use `EAF`; create `EAF-1` as instance |
| Using spaces/special characters in type name | Table generation failure or bad SQL identifiers | Use PascalCase / approved acronym |
| Manually creating `<Type>_Mst_Tbl` | Missing metadata, triggers, relations, views | Use Asset Structure generator |
| Enabling frequency without volume planning | Unnecessary block table volume | Choose minimal frequency set |
| Expecting tag mapping before instance creation | No mapping rows exist | Create template and instance first |
| Creating process parameters as ordinary Decimal/Text | Template details not auto-created | Use equipment/tag datatype |
| Expecting current data before tags are mapped | `<Type>_Data` remains empty | Update tag mappings with valid datasource/tag names |
| Using `EquipmentTypeID` in generated views | SQL failure if view uses `EquipmetTypeID` | Use actual generated spelling |
| Enabling events for passive assets | Extra tables/config with little value | Enable `IsEventCheck` only for stateful assets |
| Deleting type with live data | Drops generated tables/views/SPs | Never delete live equipment types |

---

#### Agent Output Contract

When an agent completes this task, it must output:

```text
1. Equipment type name.
2. Creation method used: UI/API/controlled SQL.
3. Block/frequency decision.
4. Event-check decision.
5. Generated table list from postflight.
6. Generated trigger list from postflight.
7. Consolidated view validation result.
8. Remaining required next steps:
   - templates
   - tag attributes
   - instances
   - tag mappings
   - event configuration
   - block validation
9. Any failed postflight checks.
```

> End of imported document.

## 23. Create Asset Structure Template

**Source file:** `02_TASK_PLAYBOOKS/create_asset_structure_template.md`  
**File type:** `.md`  
**Size:** `1699` bytes  
**Modified on:** `2026-05-04 17:49:24`  
**SHA256:** `e7282307c786`  
**Encoding used:** `utf-8`

> Start of imported document.

### Playbook — Create Asset Structure Template

#### Goal

Create reusable template headers and details for an equipment type.

Example:

```text
Equipment Type: EAF
Template: EAF_Standard_Template
```

#### Flow

```text
Create equipment type first
→ create equipment/tag attributes on generated/current data entity where required
→ create template header
→ template master trigger auto-adds template detail rows
→ edit template detail ranges/colors/documents/formulas
```

#### Template header table

Pattern:

```text
<EquipmentType>_Attribute_Tag_Template_Mst_Tbl
```

Example:

```text
EAF_Attribute_Tag_Template_Mst_Tbl
```

#### Template detail table

Pattern:

```text
<EquipmentType>_Attribute_Tag_Template_Dtl_Tbl
```

Important fields:

```text
Attribute
HHRange
HRange
LRange
LLRange
ColorGTHH
ColorBWHHH
ColorBWHL
ColorBWLLL
ColorLTLL
DocumentGTHH
DocumentBWHHH
DocumentBWHL
DocumentBWLLL
DocumentLTLL
ExtendRetrievalType
ExtendEquationType
ExtendEquation
TransactionRetrievalType
Procedure
PWherecolumn
POperator
PConditionType
PMSTAttribute
PWhereValue
IType
TransactionEquationType
TransactionEquation
```

#### Auto-population rule

Template detail rows are created automatically only for attributes with the equipment/tag datatype:

```text
EC8BE677-39DB-4945-A62F-70D4A4F9CE78
```

#### Postflight

```sql
SELECT *
FROM XStudio_XMES.dbo.<EquipmentType>_Attribute_Tag_Template_Mst_Tbl
WHERE IsDeleted = 0;

SELECT *
FROM XStudio_XMES.dbo.<EquipmentType>_Attribute_Tag_Template_Dtl_Tbl
WHERE IsDeleted = 0;
```

#### Design rule

Templates should be created before equipment instances wherever possible. This allows instance creation triggers to create complete mapping rows immediately.
