## 24. Create Dropdown Attribute

**Source file:** `02_TASK_PLAYBOOKS/create_dropdown_attribute.md`  
**File type:** `.md`  
**Size:** `14114` bytes  
**Modified on:** `2026-05-12 11:52:41`  
**SHA256:** `3a1b48900416`  
**Encoding used:** `utf-8`

> Start of imported document.

### Playbook: Create Dropdown Attribute

#### Purpose

Add a dropdown/FK-style attribute to an existing XStudio entity using the platform-correct flow:

```text
base attribute
→ physical column through XStudio attribute SP
→ dropdown AttributeProperties
→ entity relation metadata
→ edit-control JSON refresh, if the field appears on an edit form
→ LV/edit/page postflight
```

A dropdown attribute stores the selected record key, normally a GUID, and displays text from another entity.

#### Use when

Use this when an existing entity needs a field that shows a list of values from another entity, such as:

```text
MaterialID → XMES_Material_Mst_Tbl.Name
WorkCenterID → XMES_WorkCenter_Mst_Tbl.Name
StatusID → status master display text
```

Do not use this playbook for simple fixed-value UI choices unless XStudio requires those values to be modeled as a relation-backed dropdown.

#### Hard rules

1. Do not directly insert into `XStudio_Attribute_Mst_Tbl`.
2. Do not manually `ALTER TABLE` to add the physical dropdown column.
3. Do not directly delete/insert dropdown property rows as the normal path.
4. Do not directly insert `XStudio_EntityRelations_Mst_Tbl` as the normal path.
5. Use `XStudio_AddAttribute_Usp` for the base attribute.
6. Use `dropdown_properties_template.sql` to call the XStudio dropdown SP and complete the six-property contract.
7. Use a real XStudio deployment user GUID. Do not use `sa`.
8. Run postflight checks before using the dropdown in an edit form or LV.

#### Tables involved

| Table | Role | Correct write path |
|---|---|---|
| `XStudio_Attribute_Mst_Tbl` | Base dropdown attribute metadata | `XStudio_AddAttribute_Usp` |
| Physical entity table | Physical FK/GUID column | Created by `XStudio_AddAttribute_Usp` |
| `XStudio_AttributeProperties_Mst_Tbl` | Dropdown lookup settings | `dropdown_properties_template.sql` |
| `XStudio_EntityRelations_Mst_Tbl` | Entity relation metadata | `dropdown_properties_template.sql` |
| `XStudio_EditControls_Mst_Tbl` | Dropdown edit control and renderer JSON | `edit_control_safe_json_template.sql` |
| `XStudio_LvColumns_Mst_Tbl` | LV display column, if needed | LV grid/template flow |

#### Required dropdown property contract

A complete dropdown has exactly one active row for each property below:

| Property | Purpose |
|---|---|
| `DropdownDatabase` | Database containing the dropdown source table |
| `DropdownEntity` | Target entity GUID |
| `DropdownEntryMode` | Usually `single`; use `multi` only when supported by the design |
| `DropdownTextField` | Display field/expression, such as `[XMES_Material_Mst_Tbl].[Name]` |
| `DropdownValueField` | Target value attribute GUID, usually the target entity `ID` attribute |
| `TextCharacterLength` | Usually `36` for GUID-backed dropdown values |

Important: `XStudio_Add_DropDown_Attribute_Properties_And_Relation_Usp` is the platform SP for dropdown property/relation creation, but the corrected SQL template still postflights the full six-property contract because some SP builds do not produce every required row.

#### Correct sequence

| Step | Action | File/SP |
|---:|---|---|
| 1 | Confirm owning entity and target entity exist | Read-only SQL |
| 2 | Add the base dropdown attribute | `XStudio_AddAttribute_Usp` via `entity_create_template.sql` or dedicated call |
| 3 | Add dropdown properties and relation | `../03_SQL_TEMPLATES/dropdown_properties_template.sql` |
| 4 | Refresh edit controls if the dropdown appears on an edit form | `../03_SQL_TEMPLATES/edit_control_safe_json_template.sql` |
| 5 | Refresh LV columns/view if the dropdown is displayed in a grid | `create_lv_grid.md` / LV template |
| 6 | Run postflight | `../03_SQL_TEMPLATES/postflight_checks.sql` |

#### Pre-flight

##### 1. Resolve system databases

```sql
DECLARE @SystemID varchar(36) = '6C8C0070-60B5-4474-BEC8-5C02197B32E6';

SELECT
    ConfigurationDatabase,
    DataDatabase
FROM XStudio_Configuration.dbo.XStudio_GetDataSourceInfoFromSystemId_Fun(@SystemID);
```

Use the resolved configuration database for metadata and the resolved data database for physical table validation.

##### 2. Resolve Dropdown data type

```sql
SELECT ID, Name, ActualDataType
FROM XStudio_Configuration.dbo.XStudio_DataType_Mst_Tbl
WHERE IsDeleted = 0
  AND Name = 'Dropdown';
```

Do not hardcode the dropdown datatype GUID without verifying it in the target environment.

##### 3. Resolve owning entity and target entity

```sql
DECLARE @OwningEntityName varchar(200) = '<OwningEntityTableName>';
DECLARE @TargetEntityName varchar(200) = '<TargetEntityTableName>';

SELECT
    'OwningEntity' AS ObjectType,
    e.ID,
    e.Name,
    e.ParentID
FROM XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_Entities_Mst_Tbl e
WHERE e.Name = @OwningEntityName
  AND e.IsDeleted = 0;

SELECT
    'TargetEntity' AS ObjectType,
    e.ID,
    e.Name,
    e.ParentID
FROM XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_Entities_Mst_Tbl e
WHERE e.Name = @TargetEntityName
  AND e.IsDeleted = 0;
```

Expected:

```text
1 active owning entity
1 active target entity
```

##### 4. Resolve target value and display attributes

The target value attribute is normally `ID`.

```sql
DECLARE @TargetEntityID uniqueidentifier = '<target_entity_guid>';

SELECT
    a.ID,
    a.Name,
    a.DisplayName,
    dt.Name AS DataType
FROM XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_Attribute_Mst_Tbl a
LEFT JOIN XStudio_Configuration.dbo.XStudio_DataType_Mst_Tbl dt
  ON dt.ID = a.DataType
WHERE a.ParentID = @TargetEntityID
  AND a.IsDeleted = 0
ORDER BY a.Name;
```

Confirm:

```text
value attribute = ID or another intended key
display column = Name, Code, Description, or intended display field
```

##### 5. Confirm the dropdown attribute does not already exist

```sql
DECLARE @OwningEntityID uniqueidentifier = '<owning_entity_guid>';
DECLARE @AttributeName varchar(100) = '<DropdownAttributeName>';

SELECT
    ID,
    Name,
    DisplayName,
    DataType,
    ParentID,
    IsDeleted
FROM XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_Attribute_Mst_Tbl
WHERE ParentID = @OwningEntityID
  AND Name = @AttributeName;
```

Expected for a new dropdown attribute:

```text
0 active rows
```

If an active row already exists, do not create a duplicate. Validate/fix properties and relation instead.

#### Step 1 — Add the base dropdown attribute

Preferred path: add the dropdown attribute through the same entity/attribute template used for base attributes:

```text
../03_SQL_TEMPLATES/entity_create_template.sql
```

In `@AttributeDef`, define the dropdown attribute with `DataTypeName = 'Dropdown'` or the verified dropdown `DataTypeID`.

Example conceptual row:

```sql
INSERT INTO @AttributeDef
(
    Name,
    DisplayName,
    DataTypeName,
    DataTypeID,
    IsRequired,
    IsUnique,
    IsSearchable,
    IsCalculatedColumn,
    Description
)
VALUES
(
    'MaterialID',
    'Material',
    'Dropdown',
    NULL,
    0,
    0,
    1,
    0,
    'Selected material'
);
```

The template calls:

```sql
EXEC XStudio_Configuration.dbo.XStudio_AddAttribute_Usp
    @ID                 = '0',
    @EntityID           = '<owning_entity_guid>',
    @Name               = 'MaterialID',
    @DataTypeID         = '<dropdown_datatype_guid>',
    @IsRequired         = 0,
    @IsUnique           = 0,
    @IsSearchable       = 1,
    @IsCalculatedColumn = 0,
    @DisplayName        = 'Material',
    @SystemID           = '<system_guid>',
    @Description        = 'Selected material';
```

Do not manually `ALTER TABLE`. `XStudio_AddAttribute_Usp` owns the physical column creation/update path.

#### Step 2 — Complete dropdown properties and relation

Use:

```text
../03_SQL_TEMPLATES/dropdown_properties_template.sql
```

Set:

| Template variable | Example |
|---|---|
| `@SystemID` | `6C8C0070-60B5-4474-BEC8-5C02197B32E6` |
| `@MainEntityID` | owning entity GUID |
| `@MainTableName` | owning entity table name |
| `@MainAttributeName` | `MaterialID` |
| `@RelationTable` | `XMES_Material_Mst_Tbl` |
| `@RelationAttributeName` | `ID` |
| `@RelationDisplayColumnName` | `Name` |
| `@DropdownEntryMode` | `single` |
| `@TextCharacterLength` | `36` |
| `@UserID` | real deployment user GUID |

The template calls:

```text
XStudio_Add_DropDown_Attribute_Properties_And_Relation_Usp
```

Then it validates and, only if needed, completes:

```text
DropdownDatabase
DropdownEntity
DropdownEntryMode
DropdownTextField
DropdownValueField
TextCharacterLength
XStudio_EntityRelations_Mst_Tbl
```

#### Step 3 — Refresh edit controls if this dropdown appears on an edit form

If the entity already has an edit form, the edit controls must be regenerated or made renderer-safe after adding the dropdown.

Use:

```text
../03_SQL_TEMPLATES/edit_control_safe_json_template.sql
```

This is required because dropdown edit controls must have:

| Field | Expected value |
|---|---|
| `ControlType` | usually `selectbox` |
| `ControlName` | usually `cmb_<AttributeName>` |
| `ControlProperties` | full valid JSON |
| `DropdownEntityID` JSON key | non-empty target entity GUID |
| `DropdownValueField` JSON key | non-empty target value attribute GUID |
| `DropdownTextField` JSON key | non-empty display field |
| `DropdownDatabase` JSON key | non-empty dropdown database |

This avoids renderer failures such as:

```text
BindControls.js: Cannot read properties of null (reading 'toLowerCase')
```

#### Step 4 — Refresh LV/grid if this dropdown is displayed

If the dropdown needs to display text in an LV/grid, update the LV flow, not this dropdown playbook.

Use:

```text
create_lv_grid.md
```

or the corrected LV grid SQL template when available.

The LV must use relation metadata correctly so the grid displays the text value rather than an unresolved GUID.

After LV changes, regenerate the LV view through:

```text
XStudio_CreateViewForLV_Usp
```

Do not add LV relation/display columns by guessing table joins in this playbook.

#### Step 5 — Run postflight

Use:

```text
../03_SQL_TEMPLATES/postflight_checks.sql
```

Minimum dropdown-specific postflight:

```sql
DECLARE @AttrID uniqueidentifier = '<dropdown_attribute_guid>';

-- Must return exactly the six required active property names.
SELECT
    Name,
    Value
FROM XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_AttributeProperties_Mst_Tbl
WHERE ParentID = @AttrID
  AND IsDeleted = 0
ORDER BY Name;

-- Must return one active relation.
SELECT
    ID,
    EntityID,
    AttributeID,
    Cardinality,
    RelationEntityID,
    RelationAttributeID,
    RelationName,
    RelationEntity,
    RelationAttribute
FROM XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_EntityRelations_Mst_Tbl
WHERE AttributeID = @AttrID
  AND IsDeleted = 0;
```

Expected property names:

```text
DropdownDatabase
DropdownEntity
DropdownEntryMode
DropdownTextField
DropdownValueField
TextCharacterLength
```

If the dropdown appears on an edit form, also verify:

```sql
SELECT
    ec.ID,
    ec.ControlName,
    ec.ControlType,
    JSON_VALUE(CONVERT(nvarchar(max), ec.ControlProperties), '$.DropdownEntityID') AS DropdownEntityID,
    JSON_VALUE(CONVERT(nvarchar(max), ec.ControlProperties), '$.DropdownValueField') AS DropdownValueField,
    JSON_VALUE(CONVERT(nvarchar(max), ec.ControlProperties), '$.DropdownTextField') AS DropdownTextField,
    JSON_VALUE(CONVERT(nvarchar(max), ec.ControlProperties), '$.DropdownDatabase') AS DropdownDatabase
FROM XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_EditControls_Mst_Tbl ec
WHERE ec.AttributeID = @AttrID
  AND ec.IsDeleted = 0;
```

Expected:

```text
ControlType = selectbox
ControlName = cmb_<AttributeName>
all dropdown JSON fields non-empty
ControlProperties is valid JSON
```

#### Common errors and correct fixes

| Symptom | Likely cause | Correct fix |
|---|---|---|
| `BindControls.js ... toLowerCase` | Dropdown JSON/property value is null or missing | Run `dropdown_properties_template.sql`, then `edit_control_safe_json_template.sql` |
| Dropdown shows no options | `DropdownTextField`, `DropdownDatabase`, or relation target is wrong | Fix through `dropdown_properties_template.sql`; then refresh edit controls |
| Saves empty GUID | `DropdownValueField` is missing/wrong | Fix through `dropdown_properties_template.sql`; verify target value attribute |
| Physical column missing | Attribute was not created through `XStudio_AddAttribute_Usp` or SP failed | Rerun/fix `entity_create_template.sql` / `XStudio_AddAttribute_Usp`; do not manually `ALTER TABLE` |
| Grid shows GUID instead of display text | LV relation/display column not configured | Fix LV grid flow and regenerate view with `XStudio_CreateViewForLV_Usp` |
| Duplicate dropdown properties | Old direct insert/delete pattern was used | Run `dropdown_properties_template.sql`; it deactivates/rebuilds only through documented fallback after SP call |
| Relation missing | Dropdown relation was not created or points to wrong target | Run `dropdown_properties_template.sql` and postflight relation |

#### Anti-patterns removed from this playbook

Do not use these as the normal dropdown workflow:

```text
INSERT INTO XStudio_Attribute_Mst_Tbl
ALTER TABLE dbo.<EntityTable> ADD <DropdownAttribute> varchar(36)
DELETE FROM XStudio_AttributeProperties_Mst_Tbl WHERE ParentID = ...
INSERT INTO XStudio_AttributeProperties_Mst_Tbl
INSERT INTO XStudio_EntityRelations_Mst_Tbl
UPDATE XStudio_EditControls_Mst_Tbl SET ControlType = 'selectbox'
ModifiedBy = 'sa'
CreatedBy = 'sa'
```

If a no-SP exception is unavoidable, it must live in the relevant corrected SQL template with live schema inspection and postflight validation.

#### See also

- [create_entity_module.md](create_entity_module.md)
- [create_edit_form.md](create_edit_form.md)
- [create_lv_grid.md](create_lv_grid.md)
- [dropdown_failures.md](../04_DEBUGGING/dropdown_failures.md)
- [renderer_crashes.md](../04_DEBUGGING/renderer_crashes.md)
- [entity_create_template.sql](../03_SQL_TEMPLATES/entity_create_template.sql)
- [dropdown_properties_template.sql](../03_SQL_TEMPLATES/dropdown_properties_template.sql)
- [edit_control_safe_json_template.sql](../03_SQL_TEMPLATES/edit_control_safe_json_template.sql)
- [postflight_checks.sql](../03_SQL_TEMPLATES/postflight_checks.sql)

## 25. Create Edit Form

**Source file:** `02_TASK_PLAYBOOKS/create_edit_form.md`  
**File type:** `.md`  
**Size:** `22771` bytes  
**Modified on:** `2026-05-12 12:08:00`  
**SHA256:** `476214271345`  
**Encoding used:** `utf-8`

> Start of imported document.

### Playbook: Create Edit Form

#### Purpose

Create or repair an XStudio edit form with:

```text
edit header
edit controls
renderer-safe ControlProperties
dropdown selectbox controls
date/time controls
hidden/request-value fields
save/cancel/save-and-continue navigation
optional edit logic/validation SPs
optional page binding
postflight checks
```

This playbook preserves the operational edit-form knowledge from the original file, but updates it to the current XStudio rule:

```text
Use XStudio SPs wherever a suitable SP exists.
If no suitable SP exists, keep the operation as a documented no-SP exception with live schema inspection, guarded writes, real user audit values, and postflight.
```

#### Use when

Use this when:

- you need an add/edit screen for an entity;
- existing edit controls are missing;
- existing edit controls are crashing `BindControls.js`;
- dropdown edit controls render incorrectly;
- save/cancel/save-and-continue navigation needs to be configured.

#### Relevant SP review

The current SP reference confirms the following edit-related SPs.

| SP | Confirmed use | Limitation |
|---|---|---|
| `Xstudio_Insert_Edit_Columns_Info_Usp` | Generates edit controls from entity attributes | Some branches do not populate full renderer-safe `ControlProperties`; must run safe JSON template afterward |
| `XStudio_Save_Edit_Logic_And_Validation_USP` | Saves edit-level logic/validation SP names | Does not create edit header, edit controls, or save/cancel page navigation |
| `XStudio_Delete_Edit_Usp` | Deletes edit form and related edit controls/dependent controls | Not for creation |
| `XStudio_Edit_Copy_Usp` | Copies an edit across system/module context | Not a generic from-scratch create SP |
| `XStudio_Create_Duplicate_Edit_Usp` | Duplicates an existing edit by name | Requires a source edit |

Current conclusion:

```text
No generic XStudio_Save_Edit_Configuration_Usp / XStudio_Create_Edit_Usp is confirmed.
No generic edit-navigation save SP is confirmed for AfterSavePageID / AfterCancelPageID / SaveAndContinue fields.
```

Therefore the original edit-header and edit-navigation operations are **not removed**. They remain documented no-SP exceptions, but must be done safely.

#### Tables touched

| Table | Role | Write method |
|---|---|---|
| `XStudio_Edit_Mst_Tbl` | Edit header and save/cancel navigation | Copy/duplicate SP if cloning; otherwise guarded schema-inspected no-SP exception |
| `XStudio_EditControls_Mst_Tbl` | Field controls | `Xstudio_Insert_Edit_Columns_Info_Usp` first; safe JSON template/fallback after |
| `XStudio_Attribute_Mst_Tbl` | Source attributes for controls | Read here |
| `XStudio_AttributeProperties_Mst_Tbl` | Dropdown metadata for selectbox controls | Read here; created by dropdown template |
| `XStudio_Page_Mst_Tbl` + `XStudio_PageControls_Mst_Tbl` | Page that hosts the form | Use page/menu template and `XStudio_Save_Page_Control_Configuration_Usp`; do not insert PageControls here |

#### Critical rule

Never leave active edit controls with invalid `ControlProperties`.

Invalid means:

```text
SQL NULL
blank string
{}
invalid JSON
missing dropdown JSON keys for dropdown controls
```

The renderer reads values from this JSON and can call string methods on them. Missing or null values can cause errors such as:

```text
Cannot read properties of null (reading 'toLowerCase')
```

#### Correct sequence

| Step | Action | Method |
|---:|---|---|
| 1 | Confirm entity and attributes exist | Read-only SQL |
| 2 | Confirm dropdown metadata if dropdown controls are expected | `dropdown_properties_template.sql` |
| 3 | Create/copy/resolve edit header | copy/duplicate SP if cloning; otherwise guarded no-SP exception |
| 4 | Generate edit controls | `Xstudio_Insert_Edit_Columns_Info_Usp` |
| 5 | Complete renderer-safe JSON and dropdown control metadata | `edit_control_safe_json_template.sql` |
| 6 | Configure date/time/hidden/request-value specifics where required | guarded no-SP exception if not covered by template |
| 7 | Configure save/cancel/save-and-continue navigation | guarded no-SP exception until a save-navigation SP is found |
| 8 | Configure edit logic/validation SP names, if needed | `XStudio_Save_Edit_Logic_And_Validation_USP` |
| 9 | Bind edit to page | `page_menu_template.sql` / `XStudio_Save_Page_Control_Configuration_Usp` |
| 10 | Run postflight | `postflight_checks.sql` |

#### Pre-flight

##### 1. Resolve system databases

```sql
DECLARE @SystemID varchar(36) = '6C8C0070-60B5-4474-BEC8-5C02197B32E6';

SELECT
    ConfigurationDatabase,
    DataDatabase
FROM XStudio_Configuration.dbo.XStudio_GetDataSourceInfoFromSystemId_Fun(@SystemID);
```

Set:

```sql
DECLARE @ConfigDB sysname = 'XStudio_Configuration_XMES';
DECLARE @DataDB   sysname = 'XStudio_XMES';
```

Use resolved values. Do not hardcode unless verified.

##### 2. Confirm entity exists

```sql
DECLARE @EntityName varchar(100) = '<EntityTableName>';

SELECT
    ID,
    Name,
    ParentID,
    TableType,
    IsDeleted
FROM XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_Entities_Mst_Tbl
WHERE Name = @EntityName
  AND IsDeleted = 0;
```

Expected:

```text
1 active entity row
```

If missing, run:

```text
entity_create_template.sql
```

##### 3. Confirm attributes and datatypes

```sql
DECLARE @EntityID uniqueidentifier = '<entity_guid>';

SELECT
    a.ID,
    a.Name,
    a.DisplayName,
    dt.Name AS DataType,
    a.IsRequired,
    a.IsUnique,
    a.IsCalculatedColumn
FROM XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_Attribute_Mst_Tbl a
LEFT JOIN XStudio_Configuration.dbo.XStudio_DataType_Mst_Tbl dt
  ON dt.ID = a.DataType
WHERE a.ParentID = @EntityID
  AND a.IsDeleted = 0
ORDER BY a.Name;
```

##### 4. Confirm dropdown metadata before generating dropdown edit controls

```sql
DECLARE @EntityID uniqueidentifier = '<entity_guid>';

SELECT
    a.ID AS AttributeID,
    a.Name AS AttributeName,
    COUNT(p.ID) AS ActivePropertyCount
FROM XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_Attribute_Mst_Tbl a
JOIN XStudio_Configuration.dbo.XStudio_DataType_Mst_Tbl dt
  ON dt.ID = a.DataType
LEFT JOIN XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_AttributeProperties_Mst_Tbl p
  ON p.ParentID = a.ID
 AND p.IsDeleted = 0
WHERE a.ParentID = @EntityID
  AND a.IsDeleted = 0
  AND dt.Name = 'Dropdown'
GROUP BY a.ID, a.Name
HAVING COUNT(p.ID) <> 6;
```

Expected:

```text
0 rows
```

If rows return, run:

```text
dropdown_properties_template.sql
```

before creating/repairing edit controls.

#### Step 1 — Create edit row

##### Preferred path when copying an existing edit

If a known-good edit form exists, use:

```text
XStudio_Create_Duplicate_Edit_Usp
XStudio_Edit_Copy_Usp
```

After copy/duplicate:

```text
inspect copied edit header
inspect copied edit controls
run edit_control_safe_json_template.sql
run postflight
```

##### Brand-new edit row: documented no-SP exception

No generic edit-header save/create SP is confirmed. Preserve the original operation, but make it schema-inspected and guarded.

Original knowledge preserved:

| Field | Rule |
|---|---|
| `ID` | new edit GUID, unless identity |
| `Name` | `Edit_<EntityTableName>` |
| `DisplayName` | user-facing entity/edit name |
| `ParentID` | entity ID |
| `IsActive` | `1`, if column exists |
| `IsDeleted` | `0` |
| `IsSystem` | `0`, if column exists |
| `CreatedBy` / `ModifiedBy` | real `@UserID`, not `sa` |
| `CreatedOn` / `ModifiedOn` | `GETDATE()`, if columns exist |
| `Source` | `System`, if column exists |
| `HostAddress` | `@IPAddress`, if column exists |

Required inputs:

```sql
DECLARE @SystemID varchar(36) = '6C8C0070-60B5-4474-BEC8-5C02197B32E6';
DECLARE @ConfigDB sysname = 'XStudio_Configuration_XMES';

DECLARE @EditID uniqueidentifier = NEWID();
DECLARE @EntityID uniqueidentifier = '<entity_guid>';
DECLARE @EditName varchar(100) = 'Edit_<EntityTableName>';
DECLARE @EditDisplayName varchar(200) = '<Entity Display Name>';
DECLARE @UserID varchar(36) = '<deployment_user_guid>';
DECLARE @IPAddress varchar(100) = 'T-SQL';
```

Before writing, inspect live columns:

```sql
SELECT
    c.name,
    TYPE_NAME(c.user_type_id) AS DataType,
    c.max_length,
    c.is_nullable,
    c.is_identity
FROM XStudio_Configuration_XMES.sys.columns c
JOIN XStudio_Configuration_XMES.sys.tables t
  ON t.object_id = c.object_id
JOIN XStudio_Configuration_XMES.sys.schemas s
  ON s.schema_id = t.schema_id
WHERE s.name = 'dbo'
  AND t.name = 'XStudio_Edit_Mst_Tbl'
ORDER BY c.column_id;
```

Guard against duplicate edit rows:

```sql
SELECT
    ID,
    Name,
    ParentID,
    IsDeleted
FROM XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_Edit_Mst_Tbl
WHERE ParentID = @EntityID
  AND Name = @EditName
  AND IsDeleted = 0;
```

Expected before create:

```text
0 active rows
```

If the edit exists, reuse it. Do not create a duplicate.

The executable version belongs in a future corrected template:

```text
../03_SQL_TEMPLATES/edit_form_template.sql
```

Until that exists, write the one-off edit-header insert as a no-SP exception using the live `sys.columns` result.

#### Step 2 — Add edit controls

Use the platform SP first:

```sql
EXEC XStudio_Configuration.dbo.Xstudio_Insert_Edit_Columns_Info_Usp
    @EntityID     = '<entity_guid>',
    @EditID       = '<edit_guid>',
    @PkColumn     = 'ID',
    @DatabaseName = '<ConfigDB>';
```

This preserves the original goal of adding controls, but avoids manually inserting `XStudio_EditControls_Mst_Tbl` rows where the SP can generate them.

Important:

```text
This SP is necessary but not sufficient.
```

Because the SP may not fill complete renderer-safe `ControlProperties`, immediately run:

```text
../03_SQL_TEMPLATES/edit_control_safe_json_template.sql
```

#### Control type mapping

The original datatype-to-control knowledge is preserved.

| Attribute datatype | ControlType | ControlName prefix |
|---|---|---|
| Text / varchar / nvarchar | `textbox` | `txt_` |
| Number / Decimal / int | `number` | `num_` |
| Bool / bit | `checkbox` | `chk_` |
| DateTime | `datetimepicker` | `dtp_` |
| Date | `datepicker` or platform date control | `dtp_` |
| Dropdown | `selectbox` | `cmb_` |
| TextArea / large text | `textarea` | `ta_` |
| Hidden/request value | `hiddenfield` | platform convention |

Do not default every field to `textbox`.

#### Textbox control contract

The original textbox control knowledge is preserved as a field contract.

A textbox edit control needs:

| Field | Rule |
|---|---|
| `ParentID` | edit ID |
| `AttributeID` | source attribute GUID |
| `ControlType` | `textbox` |
| `ControlName` | `txt_<AttributeName>` |
| `SerialNumber` | field order |
| `ControlProperties` | full valid JSON |
| audit fields | real `@UserID`, not `sa` |

The full JSON should include keys used by the renderer, including:

```text
DefaultValue
IsRequired
IsUnique
IsVisible
IsReadOnly
IsReadOnlyOnEdit
IsSaveOrNot
IsSaveAsXml
IsSaveAsJson
LabelFormat
IsLabelTitleVisible
TextBoxType
TextBoxPasswordType
NumericMinValue
NumericMaxValue
NumericStepValue
NumericInputMask
TextAreaRowNumber
IsTextEditor
DropdownType
DropdownDatabase
DropdownEntityID
DropdownTextField
DropdownValueField
DropdownEntryMode
DropdownFilterCondition
DateTimePickerFormat
DateTimePickerType
FileBrowseType
CheckboxThemeType
IsSwitch
Rating
Slider
AutoIncrementPrefix
AutoIncrementSuffix
AutoIncrementLength
MapCenterPoint
MapZoomLevel
MapLayer
InstrumentTagPosition
```

Do not insert this manually as the normal path. Use the SP + safe JSON template.

#### Dropdown/selectbox control contract

The original canonical dropdown control knowledge is preserved.

Both physical dropdown fields and JSON must align where the physical fields exist.

Expected dropdown edit-control contract:

| Field | Rule |
|---|---|
| `ControlType` | `selectbox` |
| `ControlName` | `cmb_<AttributeName>` |
| `DropdownType` | `dynamic`, if column exists |
| `DropDownEntityID` | target entity GUID, if column exists |
| `DropdownTextField` | display field expression |
| `DropdownValueField` | target value attribute GUID |
| `DropdownDatabase` | dropdown database |
| `ControlProperties.DropdownType` | `dynamic` |
| `ControlProperties.DropdownDatabase` | non-empty |
| `ControlProperties.DropdownEntityID` | target entity GUID |
| `ControlProperties.DropdownTextField` | display field |
| `ControlProperties.DropdownValueField` | value attribute GUID |
| `ControlProperties.DropdownEntryMode` | `single` or intended mode |

The safe JSON template gets these values from `XStudio_AttributeProperties_Mst_Tbl`, so the dropdown property template must run first.

If a dropdown edit control is wrong:

```text
1. Run dropdown_properties_template.sql.
2. Run edit_control_safe_json_template.sql.
3. Run postflight_checks.sql.
```

#### DateTime control contract

The original DateTime guidance is preserved.

Typical rule:

```text
ControlName = dtp_<FieldName>
ControlType = datetimepicker
```

Common JSON overrides:

```text
DateTimePickerFormat
DateTimePickerOutputFormat
DateTimePickerType
IsDateTimePickerBlockPastTime
IsDateTimePickerBlockFutureTime
IsDateTimePickerSelectCurrentTime
```

Example output-format rule from the original:

```text
DateTimePickerOutputFormat = DD-MM-yyyy HH:mm:ss
```

If the safe JSON template does not set the exact business-required format, handle the override as a guarded edit-control metadata exception after live schema inspection and postflight.

#### Hidden field / request parameter contract

The original hidden-field guidance is preserved.

Use hidden fields when a value should be pre-populated from the URL/request context.

Example:

```text
ControlType = hiddenfield
ControlProperties.DefaultValue = <request.WOID>
```

Use case:

```text
auto-populate WorkOrderID from parent page URL
```

If `Xstudio_Insert_Edit_Columns_Info_Usp` does not infer this field type automatically, handle the control override as a guarded edit-control metadata exception or add the override to `edit_control_safe_json_template.sql`.

#### Step 3 — Configure navigation after save

The original save/cancel/save-and-continue knowledge is preserved.

Fields:

| Field | Meaning |
|---|---|
| `AfterSavePageID` | page opened after Save |
| `AfterCancelPageID` | page opened after Cancel |
| `AfterSaveAndContinuePageID` | page opened after Save & Continue |
| `IsSaveAndContinueButtonVisible` | shows/hides Save & Continue |
| `SaveAndContinueButtonName` | label override |

Correction:

```text
AfterSaveAndContinuePageID must be a page ID, not the edit ID.
```

No generic edit-navigation save SP is confirmed, so this is a no-SP exception.

Before updating, inspect live columns:

```sql
SELECT
    c.name,
    TYPE_NAME(c.user_type_id) AS DataType,
    c.max_length,
    c.is_nullable
FROM XStudio_Configuration_XMES.sys.columns c
JOIN XStudio_Configuration_XMES.sys.tables t
  ON t.object_id = c.object_id
JOIN XStudio_Configuration_XMES.sys.schemas s
  ON s.schema_id = t.schema_id
WHERE s.name = 'dbo'
  AND t.name = 'XStudio_Edit_Mst_Tbl'
ORDER BY c.column_id;
```

Validate target pages:

```sql
SELECT
    ID,
    Name,
    DisplayName,
    IsPopup,
    IsDeleted
FROM XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_Page_Mst_Tbl
WHERE ID IN
(
    '<list_page_guid>',
    '<edit_page_guid_or_save_continue_page_guid>'
)
AND IsDeleted = 0;
```

Required guard:

```text
Every configured navigation value must resolve to an active page.
```

Safe update contract:

| Column | Rule |
|---|---|
| `AfterSavePageID` | list/return page ID |
| `AfterCancelPageID` | list/return page ID |
| `AfterSaveAndContinuePageID` | page ID for fresh/continue form |
| `IsSaveAndContinueButtonVisible` | `1` only when required |
| `SaveAndContinueButtonName` | e.g. `Save & Add Another` |
| `ModifiedBy` | real `@UserID`, if column exists |
| `ModifiedOn` | `GETDATE()`, if column exists |
| `Source` / `HostAddress` | set if columns exist |

This should be moved into the corrected:

```text
configure_save_and_continue.md
```

#### Step 4 — Configure edit logic and validation SPs, if needed

Use the confirmed SP:

```sql
EXEC XStudio_Configuration.dbo.XStudio_Save_Edit_Logic_And_Validation_USP
    @SystemID             = '<system_guid>',
    @EditID               = '<edit_guid>',
    @BeforeSaveLogic      = '<before_save_sp_or_empty>',
    @AfterSaveLogic       = '<after_save_sp_or_empty>',
    @SPValidation         = '<validation_sp_or_empty>',
    @MobileAfterSaveLogic = '<mobile_after_save_sp_or_empty>',
    @MobileSPValidation   = '<mobile_validation_sp_or_empty>',
    @UserID               = '<deployment_user_guid>',
    @IPAddress            = 'T-SQL';
```

Use this SP only for edit logic and validation fields. It does not replace:

```text
edit header creation
edit control generation
ControlProperties repair
save/cancel page navigation
PageControl binding
```

#### Step 5 — Bind the edit form to a page

Do not insert into `XStudio_PageControls_Mst_Tbl`.

Use:

```text
page_menu_template.sql
```

Required SP:

```text
XStudio_Save_Page_Control_Configuration_Usp
```

Edit PageControl contract:

| Field | Rule |
|---|---|
| `ParentID` | page ID |
| `ControlType` | `edit` |
| `ControlID` | edit ID |
| `ControlProperties` | at least `{"IpFiltering":"False"}` unless more is required |

#### Postflight

##### Check control count

```sql
DECLARE @EditID uniqueidentifier = '<edit_guid>';

SELECT COUNT(*) AS ActiveControlCount
FROM XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_EditControls_Mst_Tbl
WHERE ParentID = @EditID
  AND IsDeleted = 0;
```

Expected:

```text
> 0
```

##### Check no null/blank/invalid ControlProperties

```sql
SELECT
    ControlName,
    ControlType,
    ControlProperties
FROM XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_EditControls_Mst_Tbl
WHERE ParentID = @EditID
  AND IsDeleted = 0
  AND
  (
       ControlProperties IS NULL
    OR LTRIM(RTRIM(CONVERT(nvarchar(max), ControlProperties))) = ''
    OR ISJSON(CONVERT(nvarchar(max), ControlProperties)) <> 1
  );
```

Expected:

```text
0 rows
```

##### Check dropdown controls have selectbox type, not textbox

```sql
SELECT
    ec.ControlName,
    ec.ControlType,
    a.Name AS AttributeName,
    dt.Name AS DataType
FROM XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_EditControls_Mst_Tbl ec
JOIN XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_Attribute_Mst_Tbl a
  ON a.ID = ec.AttributeID
JOIN XStudio_Configuration.dbo.XStudio_DataType_Mst_Tbl dt
  ON dt.ID = a.DataType
WHERE ec.ParentID = @EditID
  AND ec.IsDeleted = 0
  AND dt.Name = 'Dropdown'
  AND ec.ControlType <> 'selectbox';
```

Expected:

```text
0 rows
```

##### Check dropdown JSON values

```sql
SELECT
    ec.ControlName,
    ec.ControlType,
    JSON_VALUE(CONVERT(nvarchar(max), ec.ControlProperties), '$.DropdownDatabase') AS DropdownDatabase,
    JSON_VALUE(CONVERT(nvarchar(max), ec.ControlProperties), '$.DropdownEntityID') AS DropdownEntityID,
    JSON_VALUE(CONVERT(nvarchar(max), ec.ControlProperties), '$.DropdownTextField') AS DropdownTextField,
    JSON_VALUE(CONVERT(nvarchar(max), ec.ControlProperties), '$.DropdownValueField') AS DropdownValueField
FROM XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_EditControls_Mst_Tbl ec
JOIN XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_Attribute_Mst_Tbl a
  ON a.ID = ec.AttributeID
JOIN XStudio_Configuration.dbo.XStudio_DataType_Mst_Tbl dt
  ON dt.ID = a.DataType
WHERE ec.ParentID = @EditID
  AND ec.IsDeleted = 0
  AND dt.Name = 'Dropdown';
```

Expected:

```text
all dropdown JSON fields non-empty
```

##### Check navigation pages resolve

```sql
SELECT
    e.ID AS EditID,
    e.Name AS EditName,
    e.AfterSavePageID,
    save_page.Name AS AfterSavePageName,
    e.AfterCancelPageID,
    cancel_page.Name AS AfterCancelPageName,
    e.AfterSaveAndContinuePageID,
    continue_page.Name AS AfterSaveAndContinuePageName
FROM XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_Edit_Mst_Tbl e
LEFT JOIN XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_Page_Mst_Tbl save_page
  ON save_page.ID = e.AfterSavePageID
 AND save_page.IsDeleted = 0
LEFT JOIN XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_Page_Mst_Tbl cancel_page
  ON cancel_page.ID = e.AfterCancelPageID
 AND cancel_page.IsDeleted = 0
LEFT JOIN XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_Page_Mst_Tbl continue_page
  ON continue_page.ID = e.AfterSaveAndContinuePageID
 AND continue_page.IsDeleted = 0
WHERE e.ID = @EditID
  AND e.IsDeleted = 0;
```

Expected:

```text
each configured navigation page ID resolves to an active page
```

##### Check page binding, if expected

```sql
SELECT
    p.Name AS PageName,
    pc.ControlType,
    pc.ControlID
FROM XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_PageControls_Mst_Tbl pc
JOIN XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_Page_Mst_Tbl p
  ON p.ID = pc.ParentID
WHERE pc.ControlType = 'edit'
  AND pc.ControlID = @EditID
  AND pc.IsDeleted = 0
  AND p.IsDeleted = 0;
```

Expected:

```text
1 active PageControl binding if this edit form should be accessible through a page
```

#### Common errors

| Symptom | Cause | Correct fix |
|---|---|---|
| `BindControls.js ... toLowerCase` | null/missing JSON key or null `ControlProperties` | Run dropdown template, then edit safe JSON template |
| Edit form opens blank | edit has no controls or page has no PageControl | Run edit-control SP/template and page menu template |
| Dropdown renders as textbox | dropdown metadata was incomplete before edit controls were generated | Run dropdown template, then safe JSON template |
| Dropdown shows no options | dropdown property/relation JSON mismatch | Fix dropdown properties, rerun safe JSON template |
| Hidden field not populated | `DefaultValue` does not match request parameter | Set `ControlProperties.DefaultValue = <request.PARAM>` through guarded control override |
| DateTime saves in wrong format | output format missing/wrong | Override date-time JSON safely and postflight |
| Save & Continue goes wrong | `AfterSaveAndContinuePageID` is wrong or set to edit ID | Set it to the intended page ID through guarded navigation exception |
| Save/cancel navigation broken | page IDs do not resolve to active pages | Fix navigation and rerun postflight |
| Edit page opens but no form appears | missing/wrong PageControl | Use `XStudio_Save_Page_Control_Configuration_Usp` through page/menu template |

## 26. Create Entity Module

**Source file:** `02_TASK_PLAYBOOKS/create_entity_module.md`  
**File type:** `.md`  
**Size:** `17890` bytes  
**Modified on:** `2026-05-12 11:47:32`  
**SHA256:** `0f4fcfcb4031`  
**Encoding used:** `utf-8`

> Start of imported document.

### Playbook: Create Entity Module

#### Purpose

Create a complete XStudio module using the platform-correct sequence:

```text
Entity + attributes
→ dropdown properties/relations, if needed
→ LV/grid
→ edit form
→ pages and PageControls
→ menu and MenuRights
→ postflight validation
```

This playbook is an orchestration guide. It does not inline direct table-write scripts. Use the SQL templates in `03_SQL_TEMPLATES/` for executable work.

#### Use when

Use this when you need a brand-new data entity with a list screen and an add/edit screen.

Do not use this playbook for physical plant equipment if Asset Structure should own the equipment family. For plant equipment, use Asset Structure equipment-type/template/instance flows instead of creating a disconnected custom equipment master.

#### Hard rules

1. Use XStudio stored procedures wherever an XStudio SP exists.
2. Do not manually create the physical data table for a normal XStudio entity.
3. Do not directly insert entity or attribute metadata into `XStudio_Entities_Mst_Tbl` or `XStudio_Attribute_Mst_Tbl`.
4. Do not directly insert PageControls, menus, or menu rights.
5. Do not use `sa` as the audit user in generated scripts. Use a real XStudio deployment user GUID.
6. Treat direct DML as a documented exception only when no suitable SP exists and the template explicitly performs live schema inspection.
7. Run `postflight_checks.sql` before declaring the module complete.

#### Module completeness contract

A usable XStudio module is not only a physical table.

A production-ready module normally needs:

```text
entity metadata
physical table created through XStudio entity SP
attributes created through XStudio attribute SP
dropdown AttributeProperties and EntityRelations, if dropdowns exist
LV header and LV columns
regenerated LV view
edit header and edit controls with renderer-safe JSON
pages
PageControls binding LV/edit/other controls to pages
menu
MenuRights
optional workflow/action hooks
postflight checks
```

#### Correct template sequence

| Step | File/template | What it owns |
|---:|---|---|
| 1 | `../03_SQL_TEMPLATES/entity_create_template.sql` | Entity metadata, physical table creation through `XStudio_SaveEntity_Usp`, base attributes through `XStudio_AddAttribute_Usp` |
| 2 | `../03_SQL_TEMPLATES/dropdown_properties_template.sql` | Dropdown properties and `XStudio_EntityRelations_Mst_Tbl` metadata after base dropdown attribute exists |
| 3 | LV grid template / `create_lv_grid.md` | LV header, normal LV columns, LV view regeneration |
| 4 | `../03_SQL_TEMPLATES/lv_link_column_template.sql` | LV action/navigation link columns and LV view regeneration |
| 5 | edit form template / `create_edit_form.md` | Edit header and edit-control generation |
| 6 | `../03_SQL_TEMPLATES/edit_control_safe_json_template.sql` | Renderer-safe edit-control `ControlProperties`, especially dropdown edit controls |
| 7 | `../03_SQL_TEMPLATES/page_menu_template.sql` | Page headers, PageControls, menu, MenuRights, menu sequence |
| 8 | `../03_SQL_TEMPLATES/postflight_checks.sql` | Final validation |

Current known template gap:

```text
A dedicated generic LV grid template and a dedicated edit-header template still need to be corrected/created.
Until then, use create_lv_grid.md and create_edit_form.md only after correcting them to the same SP-first standard.
```

#### Work moved out of `entity_create_template.sql`

The entity template was deliberately narrowed. This playbook tracks where the removed work now belongs.

| Removed from old entity/module scripts | Correct owner now |
|---|---|
| Physical `CREATE TABLE` | `entity_create_template.sql` via `XStudio_SaveEntity_Usp` |
| Direct entity metadata insert | `entity_create_template.sql` via `XStudio_SaveEntity_Usp` |
| Direct attribute metadata insert | `entity_create_template.sql` via `XStudio_AddAttribute_Usp` |
| Dropdown property rows | `dropdown_properties_template.sql` |
| Dropdown entity relation | `dropdown_properties_template.sql` |
| LV header and normal LV columns | `create_lv_grid.md` / future `lv_grid_template.sql` |
| LV action/link columns | `lv_link_column_template.sql` |
| LV view regeneration | `XStudio_CreateViewForLV_Usp` through LV templates |
| Edit header | `create_edit_form.md` / future `edit_form_template.sql` |
| Edit controls | `Xstudio_Insert_Edit_Columns_Info_Usp` through edit templates |
| Edit-control JSON safety | `edit_control_safe_json_template.sql` |
| Page headers | `page_menu_template.sql` guarded page-header path |
| PageControls | `page_menu_template.sql` via `XStudio_Save_Page_Control_Configuration_Usp` |
| Menu | `page_menu_template.sql` via `XStudio_Save_Menu_Configuration_Usp` |
| MenuRights | `page_menu_template.sql` via `XStudio_Save_Menu_Rights_Usp` |
| Menu sequence | `page_menu_template.sql` via `XStudio_Save_Menu_Sequence_Usp` |
| Workflow/action hooks | `create_workflow_action.md` / future workflow template |

#### Pre-flight

Before writing any configuration:

##### 1. Resolve system/application context

```sql
DECLARE @SystemID varchar(36) = '6C8C0070-60B5-4474-BEC8-5C02197B32E6';

SELECT
    ConfigurationDatabase,
    DataDatabase
FROM XStudio_Configuration.dbo.XStudio_GetDataSourceInfoFromSystemId_Fun(@SystemID);
```

Resolve the target application:

```sql
SELECT ID, Name
FROM XStudio_Configuration.dbo.XStudio_Application_Mst_Tbl
WHERE IsDeleted = 0
ORDER BY Name;
```

For XMES DEFAULT application, the known application ID is:

```text
29123B0B-3872-41CF-B5E1-67C4A8F0D4BC
```

##### 2. Confirm that the entity does not already exist

Use the resolved configuration database.

```sql
SELECT ID, Name, ParentID, TableType, IsDeleted
FROM XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_Entities_Mst_Tbl
WHERE Name = '<EntityTableName>';
```

Expected for a new entity:

```text
0 active rows
```

If the physical table exists but entity metadata does not, stop. That is exceptional recovery, not normal module creation.

##### 3. Resolve data types

```sql
SELECT ID, Name, ActualDataType
FROM XStudio_Configuration.dbo.XStudio_DataType_Mst_Tbl
WHERE IsDeleted = 0
ORDER BY Name;
```

Use data type names or GUIDs in `entity_create_template.sql`. Do not guess datatype IDs.

##### 4. Confirm target table schemas before exceptions

Most normal work should go through SPs/templates. If a documented exception requires direct DML, inspect live columns first:

```sql
SELECT COLUMN_NAME, DATA_TYPE, CHARACTER_MAXIMUM_LENGTH
FROM XStudio_Configuration_XMES.INFORMATION_SCHEMA.COLUMNS
WHERE TABLE_NAME = '<target_config_table>'
ORDER BY ORDINAL_POSITION;
```

#### Step 1 — Create entity and base attributes

Use:

```text
../03_SQL_TEMPLATES/entity_create_template.sql
```

This template is responsible for:

```text
XStudio_SaveEntity_Usp
XStudio_AddAttribute_Usp
physical table creation through platform path
physical column creation through platform path
entity/attribute postflight
```

Do not manually run `CREATE TABLE` for the entity.

Do not directly insert rows into:

```text
XStudio_Entities_Mst_Tbl
XStudio_Attribute_Mst_Tbl
```

Base dropdown attributes can be created in this step, but their dropdown metadata is not complete until Step 2.

#### Step 2 — Complete dropdown attributes, if any

For each dropdown attribute, use:

```text
../03_SQL_TEMPLATES/dropdown_properties_template.sql
```

This template is responsible for:

```text
XStudio_Add_DropDown_Attribute_Properties_And_Relation_Usp
six-property dropdown contract
entity relation metadata
dropdown postflight
```

The required dropdown property contract is:

| Property | Required purpose |
|---|---|
| `DropdownDatabase` | Source DB for dropdown lookup |
| `DropdownEntity` | Target entity GUID |
| `DropdownEntryMode` | Single/multi selection behavior |
| `DropdownTextField` | Display field/expression |
| `DropdownValueField` | Stored value field |
| `TextCharacterLength` | Usually `36` for GUID FK |

Do not treat `XStudio_Add_DropDown_Attribute_Properties_And_Relation_Usp` alone as sufficient unless postflight proves all six properties and the relation exist.

#### Step 3 — Create the LV/grid

Use the LV playbook/template:

```text
create_lv_grid.md
```

Current gap:

```text
A corrected generic SQL template for LV header + normal LV columns is still pending.
```

Until that is corrected, any LV-grid script must follow these rules:

1. Inspect the live schema before writing LV/LV-column rows.
2. Use any confirmed LV save/copy/delete SPs where applicable.
3. Treat direct LV/LV-column DML as an exception only if no complete save SP exists.
4. Regenerate the LV view through `XStudio_CreateViewForLV_Usp`.
5. Pass explicit non-null query fragments to `XStudio_CreateViewForLV_Usp`.

Confirmed LV view SP surface:

```sql
EXEC XStudio_Configuration.dbo.XStudio_CreateViewForLV_Usp
    @LvID             = '<LV_GUID>',
    @LvName           = '<LV_NAME>',
    @DataDatabase     = '<DATA_DB>',
    @ColumnQuery      = '<SELECT ...>',
    @RelationQuery    = '<FROM/JOIN ...>',
    @GroupByQuery     = '',
    @WhereCondition   = '<WHERE ...>',
    @IsCalculatedView = 0;
```

Do not pass `NULL` query fragments.

#### Step 4 — Add LV action/navigation link columns, if needed

Use:

```text
../03_SQL_TEMPLATES/lv_link_column_template.sql
```

This is for link/action columns such as:

```text
Edit
Delete
Open
Drilldown
WOProduction
WOConsumption
```

A pure action/navigation LV column must not be treated as a physical attribute.

Required pattern:

| Field | Rule |
|---|---|
| `Type` | `link` |
| `IsCustomField` | `1` |
| `AttributeID` | null/blank |
| `RelationID` | null/blank |
| `RelationIDList` | null/blank |
| `ColumnProperties` | valid JSON |
| LV view | regenerated after change |

For `Edit` and `Delete` action columns, use the target page ID as the custom link when the renderer expects page-ID navigation.

#### Step 5 — Create edit form

Use:

```text
create_edit_form.md
```

Current gap:

```text
A corrected generic SQL template for edit header creation is still pending.
```

The edit form flow must follow these rules:

1. Create or resolve the edit header using the corrected edit playbook/template.
2. Generate edit controls through `Xstudio_Insert_Edit_Columns_Info_Usp`.
3. Do not directly insert edit controls as the normal path.
4. Do not leave active edit controls with null/blank/minimal `ControlProperties`.

Known edit-control generation SP:

```sql
EXEC XStudio_Configuration.dbo.Xstudio_Insert_Edit_Columns_Info_Usp
    @EntityID     = '<ENTITY_GUID>',
    @EditID       = '<EDIT_GUID>',
    @PkColumn     = 'ID',
    @DatabaseName = '<CONFIG_DB>';
```

Important: this SP can generate controls but may not fully populate renderer-safe `ControlProperties`. Always run Step 6.

#### Step 6 — Make edit controls renderer-safe

Use:

```text
../03_SQL_TEMPLATES/edit_control_safe_json_template.sql
```

This template is responsible for:

```text
edit-control generation through Xstudio_Insert_Edit_Columns_Info_Usp
ControlProperties validation
full renderer-safe JSON completion
dropdown edit-control JSON completion
dropdown ControlType/ControlName consistency
```

This is required because `BindControls.js` reads many JSON keys and can crash when expected keys are missing, null, or non-string.

#### Step 7 — Create pages, bind controls, create menu, add rights

Use:

```text
../03_SQL_TEMPLATES/page_menu_template.sql
```

This template is responsible for:

```text
page header creation
PageControl binding
menu creation/update
menu rights
menu sequence
```

Correct SP surface:

| Task | Required method |
|---|---|
| Bind LV/edit/inlinegrid/HTML/dashboard/tab/etc. to a page | `XStudio_Save_Page_Control_Configuration_Usp` |
| Remove PageControl binding | `XStudio_Delete_Page_Control_Configuration_Usp` |
| Create/update menu item | `XStudio_Save_Menu_Configuration_Usp` |
| Add menu rights | `XStudio_Save_Menu_Rights_Usp` |
| Remove menu rights | `XStudio_Delete_Menu_Rights_Usp` |
| Save menu sequence | `XStudio_Save_Menu_Sequence_Usp` |
| Configure tabs if tab container is used | tab SPs only when tabs are actually used |

Important page/menu model:

```text
Page = container/header
PageControls = actual Page → Control bindings
Menu = navigation row pointing to a page
MenuRights = role visibility
```

Do not directly insert into:

```text
XStudio_PageControls_Mst_Tbl
XStudio_Menu_Mst_Tbl
XStudio_MenuRights_Mst_Tbl
```

Do not assume every page needs tabs. Tabs are optional container controls.

#### Step 8 — Configure edit navigation

If the edit form should return to the list page after save/cancel, configure:

```text
AfterSavePageID
AfterCancelPageID
AfterSaveAndContinuePageID, if needed
```

Current status:

```text
No generic edit-navigation save SP has been confirmed in the current KB.
```

Therefore:

1. Prefer a corrected edit-form template/playbook if it establishes edit navigation safely.
2. If no SP exists in the live environment, treat edit-navigation updates as a documented exception.
3. Inspect live `XStudio_Edit_Mst_Tbl` columns before writing.
4. Use a real `@UserID`, not `sa`.
5. Run `postflight_checks.sql`.

Do not bury a direct edit-navigation update inside a generic module creation script without explicitly marking it as a no-SP exception.

#### Step 9 — Optional workflow/action hooks

Use:

```text
create_workflow_action.md
```

Current gap:

```text
This playbook still needs SP-first correction.
```

Do not directly insert approval/workflow/action metadata as the default pattern until the relevant workflow SP definitions have been reviewed.

Known workflow/action areas to review before use:

```text
approval status SPs
workflow business-logic SP creation
workflow SP action execution
workflow action assignment
workflow node-position/configuration SPs
```

If workflow is not needed, do not add workflow objects.

#### Step 10 — Postflight checks

Use:

```text
../03_SQL_TEMPLATES/postflight_checks.sql
```

Postflight must prove:

| Area | Required proof |
|---|---|
| Entity | Active entity metadata exists and ParentID resolves to application |
| Physical table | Data table exists |
| Attributes | Expected attributes exist |
| Dropdowns | Every dropdown has all six active property rows and relation metadata |
| LV | LV exists, has columns, generated view exists |
| LV links | Action columns are link/custom columns and not physical attributes |
| Edit | Edit exists and has active controls |
| Edit controls | No active control has null/blank/invalid `ControlProperties` |
| Pages | Page headers exist |
| PageControls | Each page has active PageControl bindings |
| PageControl target | ControlID resolves to LV/edit/tab/control target |
| Menu | Menu points to intended page |
| MenuRights | Intended roles can see the menu |
| Tabs | Only validated when a tab container is actually used |

Example final sanity checks:

```sql
-- Entity
SELECT ID, Name, ParentID
FROM XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_Entities_Mst_Tbl
WHERE Name = '<EntityTableName>'
  AND IsDeleted = 0;

-- LV view
SELECT TOP 3 *
FROM XStudio_XMES.dbo.XStudio_<LVName>_Vw;

-- Menu rights
SELECT m.Name, mr.RoleID
FROM XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_Menu_Mst_Tbl m
JOIN XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_MenuRights_Mst_Tbl mr
  ON mr.ParentID = m.ID
 AND mr.IsDeleted = 0
WHERE m.Name = '<MenuName>'
  AND m.IsDeleted = 0;
```

#### Correct end-to-end build checklist

Use this checklist before calling a module complete.

```text
[ ] Entity does not already exist as a conflicting metadata/physical split-brain state
[ ] entity_create_template.sql ran successfully
[ ] All base attributes exist
[ ] dropdown_properties_template.sql ran for every dropdown attribute
[ ] LV header and normal LV columns exist
[ ] LV action/link columns added through lv_link_column_template.sql, if needed
[ ] LV view regenerated with XStudio_CreateViewForLV_Usp
[ ] Edit header exists
[ ] Xstudio_Insert_Edit_Columns_Info_Usp ran for edit controls
[ ] edit_control_safe_json_template.sql ran successfully
[ ] List page exists
[ ] Edit/popup page exists, if required
[ ] LV is bound to list page through XStudio_Save_Page_Control_Configuration_Usp
[ ] Edit is bound to edit page through XStudio_Save_Page_Control_Configuration_Usp
[ ] Menu created/updated through XStudio_Save_Menu_Configuration_Usp
[ ] MenuRights added through XStudio_Save_Menu_Rights_Usp
[ ] Menu sequence saved if needed
[ ] postflight_checks.sql passes
[ ] UI tested with intended user role
```

#### Common anti-patterns removed from this playbook

Do not use these patterns as normal module creation:

```text
CREATE TABLE dbo.<EntityTableName> manually
INSERT INTO XStudio_Entities_Mst_Tbl
INSERT INTO XStudio_Attribute_Mst_Tbl
INSERT INTO XStudio_PageControls_Mst_Tbl
INSERT INTO XStudio_Menu_Mst_Tbl
INSERT INTO XStudio_MenuRights_Mst_Tbl
UPDATE XStudio_Edit_Mst_Tbl SET AfterSavePageID = ...
ModifiedBy = 'sa'
```

If a no-SP exception is required, it belongs in the specific corrected template or a one-off exceptional recovery script, not in this orchestration playbook.

## 27. Create Inline Grid

**Source file:** `02_TASK_PLAYBOOKS/create_inline_grid.md`  
**File type:** `.md`  
**Size:** `8647` bytes  
**Modified on:** `2026-05-12 15:20:02`  
**SHA256:** `acffcdacf8f5`  
**Encoding used:** `utf-8`

> Start of imported document.

### Playbook: Create Inline Grid (Child Records Inside Parent Edit Form)

#### Purpose

Embed a child entity’s grid directly inside a parent edit/page so child records are visible and editable without navigating away.

Use this for:

```text
Campaign -> campaign detail lines
Sales Order -> order lines
Work Order -> consumption/production/detail rows
Any master-detail child table that should appear inline
```

#### Correct rule

```text
Use XStudio SPs wherever a suitable SP exists.
If no suitable SP exists, preserve the table operation as a documented no-SP exception with live schema inspection, guards, real user audit values, and postflight.
```

#### Objects touched

| Object/table | Purpose | Write path |
|---|---|---|
| `XStudio_Entities_Mst_Tbl` | Child entity flags `IsChild`, `ParentEntityID` | `XStudio_SaveEntity_Usp` where possible; guarded no-SP update only if SP cannot update child flags |
| Child FK attribute | Parent reference on child entity | `XStudio_AddAttribute_Usp` and dropdown template if relation-backed |
| Child LV | Grid shown inline | `create_lv_grid.md` / LV templates |
| `XStudio_PageControls_Mst_Tbl` | Places inline grid on parent edit page | `XStudio_Save_Page_Control_Configuration_Usp` |
| Child LV `FilterCondition` | Filters child rows by parent record | No confirmed LV-header save SP; guarded no-SP exception |
| Child edit navigation | Returns to parent edit/page | No confirmed edit-navigation SP; guarded no-SP exception |

#### Pre-requisites

The child entity must be properly defined:

```sql
SELECT
    ID,
    Name,
    IsChild,
    ParentEntityID
FROM XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_Entities_Mst_Tbl
WHERE Name = 'XMES_Campaign_Dtl_tbl'
  AND IsDeleted = 0;
```

Expected:

```text
IsChild = 1
ParentEntityID = parent entity ID
```

The child entity must have a FK attribute pointing to the parent, typically:

```text
ParentID
CampaignID
SalesOrderID
WorkOrderID
```

The child entity also needs an LV because inlinegrid `ControlID` points to the child entity’s LV ID.

#### Step 1 — Verify or set child entity relationship

Preferred path:

```text
Use XStudio_SaveEntity_Usp when it can set IsChild and ParentEntityID in the target environment.
```

If the SP cannot update this safely in the local build, preserve the original operation as a no-SP exception.

Before update:

```sql
SELECT
    c.name,
    TYPE_NAME(c.user_type_id) AS DataType,
    c.is_nullable
FROM XStudio_Configuration_XMES.sys.columns c
JOIN XStudio_Configuration_XMES.sys.tables t
  ON t.object_id = c.object_id
JOIN XStudio_Configuration_XMES.sys.schemas s
  ON s.schema_id = t.schema_id
WHERE s.name = 'dbo'
  AND t.name = 'XStudio_Entities_Mst_Tbl'
ORDER BY c.column_id;
```

Guarded no-SP exception:

```sql
DECLARE @ChildEntityName varchar(100) = 'MyChild_Dtl_Tbl';
DECLARE @ParentEntityID uniqueidentifier = '<parent_entity_guid>';
DECLARE @UserID varchar(36) = '<deployment_user_guid>';

UPDATE XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_Entities_Mst_Tbl
SET IsChild        = 1,
    ParentEntityID = @ParentEntityID,
    ModifiedBy     = @UserID,
    ModifiedOn     = GETDATE()
WHERE Name = @ChildEntityName
  AND IsDeleted = 0;
```

Do not use `ModifiedBy = 'sa'`.

#### Step 2 — Ensure child FK attribute exists

If the child FK attribute is missing, create it through:

```text
XStudio_AddAttribute_Usp
```

If the FK should be dropdown-backed, also run:

```text
dropdown_properties_template.sql
```

Typical child FK examples:

```text
CampaignID
SalesOrderID
WorkOrderID
ParentID
```

#### Step 3 — Create child LV

The inline grid uses the child LV as its control target.

Use:

```text
create_lv_grid.md
```

Make sure the child LV has a filter compatible with the parent edit/page context.

#### Step 4 — Add inlinegrid PageControl to the parent edit page

Critical preserved knowledge:

```text
ControlID points to the child entity’s LV ID, not the child edit ID.
ControlType = inlinegrid.
ParentID = parent edit page ID.
```

Use the SP:

```text
XStudio_Save_Page_Control_Configuration_Usp
```

Example:

```sql
DECLARE @SystemID varchar(36) = '6C8C0070-60B5-4474-BEC8-5C02197B32E6';
DECLARE @PageControlID varchar(36) = CONVERT(varchar(36), NEWID());
DECLARE @ParentEditPageID varchar(36) = '<parent_edit_page_id>';
DECLARE @ChildLVID varchar(36) = '<child_lv_id>';
DECLARE @UserID varchar(36) = '<deployment_user_guid>';

EXEC XStudio_Configuration.dbo.XStudio_Save_Page_Control_Configuration_Usp
    @SystemId          = @SystemID,
    @Id                = @PageControlID,
    @Name              = 'Inline Grid - Child Records',
    @ParentId          = @ParentEditPageID,
    @RowId             = '2',
    @DivId             = '1',
    @ControlType       = 'inlinegrid',
    @ControlId         = @ChildLVID,
    @ControlUrl        = '',
    @CustomCss         = '',
    @LoadUrlAsIFrame   = 0,
    @Description       = '',
    @ControlProperties = '{"IpFiltering":"False"}',
    @UserId            = @UserID,
    @IPAddress         = 'T-SQL';
```

Do not directly insert into `XStudio_PageControls_Mst_Tbl` when this SP exists.

#### Step 5 — Child LV FilterCondition

Preserve the original filter mechanic:

```text
Parent record ID is available as <request.ID> when the parent edit form is open.
```

Typical filter:

```text
ParentID='<request.ID>'
```

or:

```text
CampaignID='<request.ID>'
```

No generic LV-header save SP is confirmed for `FilterCondition`, so this remains a no-SP exception.

```sql
DECLARE @ChildLVID uniqueidentifier = '<child_lv_guid>';
DECLARE @UserID varchar(36) = '<deployment_user_guid>';

UPDATE XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_LV_Mst_Tbl
SET FilterCondition = 'ParentID=''<request.ID>''',
    ModifiedBy      = @UserID,
    ModifiedOn      = GETDATE()
WHERE ID = @ChildLVID
  AND IsDeleted = 0;
```

Regenerate the LV view if the view/query behavior depends on the changed filter.

#### Step 6 — Child edit navigation

If the child edit form opens separately, it should return to the parent edit page.

No generic edit-navigation SP is confirmed, so preserve this as a guarded no-SP exception.

```sql
DECLARE @ChildEditID uniqueidentifier = '<child_edit_guid>';
DECLARE @ParentEditPageID uniqueidentifier = '<parent_edit_page_guid>';
DECLARE @UserID varchar(36) = '<deployment_user_guid>';

UPDATE XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_Edit_Mst_Tbl
SET AfterSavePageID   = @ParentEditPageID,
    AfterCancelPageID = @ParentEditPageID,
    ModifiedBy        = @UserID,
    ModifiedOn        = GETDATE()
WHERE ID = @ChildEditID
  AND IsDeleted = 0;
```

#### XMES examples

| Parent entity | Child entity | Inline grid page |
|---|---|---|
| `XMES_Campaign_Mst_Tbl` | `XMES_Campaign_Dtl_tbl` | `ILG_Page_XMES_Campaign_Dtl_tbl` |
| `XMES_Sales_Order_Mst_Tbl` | `XMES_Sales_Order_Dtl_Tbl` | `ILG_Page_XMES_Sales_Order_Dtl_Tbl` |

XStudio may auto-generate `ILG_Page_*` pages for inline grid views. Verify live config instead of assuming the page exists.

#### Postflight

```sql
-- Child entity flags
SELECT
    IsChild,
    ParentEntityID
FROM XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_Entities_Mst_Tbl
WHERE Name = 'MyChild_Dtl_Tbl'
  AND IsDeleted = 0;

-- Inlinegrid control on parent edit page
SELECT
    ParentID,
    ControlType,
    ControlID,
    RowID,
    DivID,
    ControlProperties
FROM XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_PageControls_Mst_Tbl
WHERE ParentID = '<parent_edit_page_id>'
  AND ControlType = 'inlinegrid'
  AND IsDeleted = 0;

-- Child LV has FilterCondition
SELECT
    Name,
    FilterCondition
FROM XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_LV_Mst_Tbl
WHERE ID = '<child_lv_guid>'
  AND IsDeleted = 0;
```

Expected:

```text
child entity is flagged as child
inlinegrid PageControl exists
inlinegrid ControlID = child LV ID
child LV has correct filter
```

#### Common mistakes

| Mistake | Effect |
|---|---|
| `ControlID = child edit ID` | Inline grid will not render correctly; it needs child LV ID |
| Missing `IsChild` / `ParentEntityID` | Child behavior and generation may be incomplete |
| Missing child FK attribute | Filter cannot connect child to parent |
| Wrong request token | Inline grid shows no rows or all rows |
| Direct PageControls insert | Bypasses SP behavior; use PageControl SP |
| Using `sa` audit values | Unsafe and non-traceable |

#### See also

- [create_parent_child_flow.md](create_parent_child_flow.md)
- [create_lv_grid.md](create_lv_grid.md)
- [create_edit_form.md](create_edit_form.md)
- [configure_save_and_continue.md](configure_save_and_continue.md)
- [page_menu_template.sql](../03_SQL_TEMPLATES/page_menu_template.sql)
- [postflight_checks.sql](../03_SQL_TEMPLATES/postflight_checks.sql)

> End of imported document.
