## 28. Create Lv Grid

**Source file:** `02_TASK_PLAYBOOKS/create_lv_grid.md`  
**File type:** `.md`  
**Size:** `21851` bytes  
**Modified on:** `2026-05-12 12:03:44`  
**SHA256:** `2da8d4665225`  
**Encoding used:** `utf-8`

> Start of imported document.

### Playbook: Create LV Grid

#### Purpose

Configure a List View (LV/grid) for an entity with:

```text
LV header
normal data columns
dropdown display-text columns
optional filter condition
optional action/link columns
generated SQL view
optional page binding
postflight checks
```

This playbook preserves the operational LV knowledge from the original file, but updates it to the current XStudio rule:

```text
Use XStudio SPs wherever a suitable SP exists.
If no suitable SP exists, keep the operation as a documented no-SP exception with live schema inspection, guarded writes, real user audit values, and postflight.
```

#### Use when

Use this when you need a grid that shows rows from an entity table.

For navigation/action link columns, use:

```text
create_lv_link_column.md
../03_SQL_TEMPLATES/lv_link_column_template.sql
```

#### Relevant SP review

The currently confirmed LV SP surface does **not** include a generic “save LV header” or “save LV column” SP.

| SP | Confirmed use |
|---|---|
| `XStudio_CreateViewForLV_Usp` | Regenerates the LV view from explicit query fragments |
| `XStudio_Get_LvColumns_List_Usp` | Reads LV column configuration |
| `XStudio_Get_Role_Base_Lv_Columns_Usp` and patch variants | Reads role-based LV column visibility |
| `XStudio_LV_Copy_Usp` | Copies an existing LV |
| `XStudio_Create_Duplicate_Lv_Usp` | Duplicates an existing LV |
| `XStudio_Delete_Lv_Usp` | Deletes an LV |
| `XStudio_Drop_List_View_By_Entity_Usp` | Drops generated list views for an entity |

Therefore, for **brand-new LV header** and **normal LV data columns**, this playbook keeps the original operational work as a no-confirmed-SP exception.

The exception is allowed only with:

```text
live sys.columns inspection
single target LV/entity guards
real @UserID
transaction
no guessed columns
postflight checks
view regeneration through XStudio_CreateViewForLV_Usp
```

#### Tables touched

| Table/object | Role | Write method |
|---|---|---|
| `XStudio_LV_Mst_Tbl` | Grid/LV header | No confirmed save SP; guarded schema-inspected exception |
| `XStudio_LvColumns_Mst_Tbl` | Column definitions | No confirmed save SP; guarded schema-inspected exception |
| `XStudio_EntityRelations_Mst_Tbl` | Relation metadata for dropdown display text | Read here; created by dropdown playbook/template |
| `XStudio_XMES.dbo.XStudio_<LVName>_Vw` | Generated view | `XStudio_CreateViewForLV_Usp` |
| `XStudio_PageControls_Mst_Tbl` | Page → LV binding | Not here; use `XStudio_Save_Page_Control_Configuration_Usp` via page/menu template |

#### Hard rules

1. Do not create the entity or attributes in this playbook.
2. Do not create dropdown properties or relations here.
3. Do not create page controls here.
4. Do not create menu or menu rights here.
5. Do not use `sa`; use a real XStudio deployment user GUID.
6. Do not guess config-table columns. Inspect `sys.columns` before no-SP exception writes.
7. Do not pass `NULL` to `@ColumnQuery`, `@RelationQuery`, or `@WhereCondition` in `XStudio_CreateViewForLV_Usp`.
8. Do not create action/link columns as normal data columns.
9. Always run postflight after LV changes.

#### Correct LV build sequence

| Step | Action | Method |
|---:|---|---|
| 1 | Confirm entity and attributes exist | Read-only SQL |
| 2 | Confirm dropdown relation metadata if dropdown display columns are needed | `dropdown_properties_template.sql` |
| 3 | Create or resolve LV header | `XStudio_LV_Copy_Usp` / duplicate SP if cloning; otherwise guarded no-SP exception |
| 4 | Add normal data columns | Guarded no-SP exception |
| 5 | Add dropdown display-text columns | Guarded no-SP exception using verified `RelationID` |
| 6 | Add action/link columns, if needed | `lv_link_column_template.sql` |
| 7 | Configure `FilterCondition`, if needed | Guarded no-SP exception |
| 8 | Regenerate view | `XStudio_CreateViewForLV_Usp` |
| 9 | Bind LV to page | `page_menu_template.sql` / `XStudio_Save_Page_Control_Configuration_Usp` |
| 10 | Run postflight | `postflight_checks.sql` |

#### Pre-flight

##### 1. Resolve system databases

```sql
DECLARE @SystemID varchar(36) = '6C8C0070-60B5-4474-BEC8-5C02197B32E6';

SELECT
    ConfigurationDatabase,
    DataDatabase
FROM XStudio_Configuration.dbo.XStudio_GetDataSourceInfoFromSystemId_Fun(@SystemID);
```

Set:

```sql
DECLARE @ConfigDB sysname = 'XStudio_Configuration_XMES';
DECLARE @DataDB   sysname = 'XStudio_XMES';
```

Use the resolved values. Do not hardcode them unless you have verified the system context.

##### 2. Confirm entity exists

```sql
DECLARE @EntityName varchar(100) = '<EntityTableName>';

SELECT
    ID,
    Name,
    ParentID,
    TableType,
    IsDeleted
FROM XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_Entities_Mst_Tbl
WHERE Name = @EntityName
  AND IsDeleted = 0;
```

Expected:

```text
1 active entity row
```

If missing, run:

```text
entity_create_template.sql
```

##### 3. Confirm attributes exist

```sql
DECLARE @EntityID uniqueidentifier = '<entity_guid>';

SELECT
    a.ID,
    a.Name,
    a.DisplayName,
    dt.Name AS DataType,
    a.IsRequired,
    a.IsUnique
FROM XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_Attribute_Mst_Tbl a
LEFT JOIN XStudio_Configuration.dbo.XStudio_DataType_Mst_Tbl dt
  ON dt.ID = a.DataType
WHERE a.ParentID = @EntityID
  AND a.IsDeleted = 0
ORDER BY a.Name;
```

##### 4. Confirm physical columns exist

```sql
DECLARE @EntityTableName varchar(100) = '<EntityTableName>';

SELECT
    COLUMN_NAME,
    DATA_TYPE,
    CHARACTER_MAXIMUM_LENGTH
FROM XStudio_XMES.INFORMATION_SCHEMA.COLUMNS
WHERE TABLE_NAME = @EntityTableName
ORDER BY ORDINAL_POSITION;
```

A normal data LV column must map to an active XStudio attribute and a physical data-table column.

##### 5. Confirm relation metadata for dropdown display columns

For dropdown columns that should display text instead of GUID:

```sql
SELECT
    er.ID AS RelationID,
    er.EntityID,
    er.AttributeID,
    a.Name AS AttributeName,
    er.RelationEntityID,
    er.RelationAttributeID,
    er.RelationName,
    er.RelationEntity,
    er.RelationAttribute
FROM XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_EntityRelations_Mst_Tbl er
JOIN XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_Attribute_Mst_Tbl a
  ON a.ID = er.AttributeID
WHERE er.EntityID = '<owning_entity_guid>'
  AND er.IsDeleted = 0
ORDER BY a.Name;
```

If missing, run:

```text
dropdown_properties_template.sql
```

before creating the dropdown LV display column.

#### Step 1 — Create the LV row

##### Preferred path when copying an existing LV

If a working LV already exists and should be cloned, use a copy/duplicate SP:

```text
XStudio_LV_Copy_Usp
XStudio_Create_Duplicate_Lv_Usp
```

After copy:

```text
inspect copied LV
inspect copied columns
adjust columns as needed
regenerate view
run postflight
```

##### Brand-new LV row: documented no-SP exception

No generic save-LV-header SP is confirmed. Keep the original LV-header creation operation, but make it guarded and schema-inspected.

Required values:

```sql
DECLARE @SystemID varchar(36) = '6C8C0070-60B5-4474-BEC8-5C02197B32E6';
DECLARE @ConfigDB sysname = 'XStudio_Configuration_XMES';

DECLARE @LVID uniqueidentifier = NEWID();
DECLARE @EntityID uniqueidentifier = '<entity_guid>';
DECLARE @LVName varchar(100) = 'List_<EntityTableName>';
DECLARE @LVDisplayName varchar(200) = '<Entity Display Name>';
DECLARE @UserID varchar(36) = '<deployment_user_guid>';
DECLARE @IPAddress varchar(100) = 'T-SQL';
```

Before writing, verify the table columns:

```sql
SELECT
    c.name,
    TYPE_NAME(c.user_type_id) AS DataType,
    c.max_length,
    c.is_nullable,
    c.is_identity
FROM XStudio_Configuration_XMES.sys.columns c
JOIN XStudio_Configuration_XMES.sys.tables t
  ON t.object_id = c.object_id
JOIN XStudio_Configuration_XMES.sys.schemas s
  ON s.schema_id = t.schema_id
WHERE s.name = 'dbo'
  AND t.name = 'XStudio_LV_Mst_Tbl'
ORDER BY c.column_id;
```

Minimum required LV header fields:

| Column | Value |
|---|---|
| `ID` | new GUID, unless identity |
| `Name` | `List_<EntityTableName>` |
| `DisplayName` | user-facing grid name |
| `ParentID` | entity ID |
| `IsDefaultLoad` | usually `1` |
| `IsActive` | `1`, if column exists |
| `SPName` | `NULL` unless custom-SP-backed LV |
| `FilterCondition` | `NULL` or intended filter |
| `IsDeleted` | `0` |
| `IsSystem` | `0`, if column exists |
| `CreatedBy` / `ModifiedBy` | real `@UserID`, if columns exist |
| `CreatedOn` / `ModifiedOn` | `GETDATE()`, if columns exist |
| `Source` | `System`, if column exists |
| `HostAddress` | `@IPAddress`, if column exists |

Guard conditions:

```sql
-- Must return 0 active rows before creating a brand-new LV header.
SELECT
    ID,
    Name,
    ParentID,
    IsDeleted
FROM XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_LV_Mst_Tbl
WHERE Name = @LVName
  AND ParentID = @EntityID
  AND IsDeleted = 0;
```

If the row exists, reuse it. Do not create a duplicate.

The executable version of this schema-inspected insert belongs in the future corrected SQL template:

```text
../03_SQL_TEMPLATES/lv_grid_template.sql
```

Until that template exists, write the LV header script as a one-off no-SP exception using the columns shown by `sys.columns`.

#### Step 2 — Add normal data columns

A normal LV data column maps to an entity attribute.

The original column knowledge is still valid as a contract:

| Column | Rule |
|---|---|
| `ID` | new GUID, unless identity |
| `Name` | internal identifier; usually attribute name |
| `Header` | grid header |
| `Type` | empty string or platform normal-data value |
| `Width` | pixel width, if column exists |
| `SerialNumber` | column order |
| `ParentID` | LV ID |
| `AttributeID` | active attribute GUID |
| `RelationID` | null/blank unless dropdown display-text column |
| `IsVisible` | `1` unless hidden |
| `IsSearchable` | `1` if searchable |
| `IsEditable` | `0` unless inline editing is intended |
| `RoleIDList` | optional comma-separated roles |
| `IsRestricted` | `1` only if role restriction applies |
| `IsCustomField` | `0` for normal data columns |
| `ColumnProperties` | valid JSON |
| audit fields | real `@UserID`, not `sa` |

Before writing, inspect `XStudio_LvColumns_Mst_Tbl`:

```sql
SELECT
    c.name,
    TYPE_NAME(c.user_type_id) AS DataType,
    c.max_length,
    c.is_nullable,
    c.is_identity
FROM XStudio_Configuration_XMES.sys.columns c
JOIN XStudio_Configuration_XMES.sys.tables t
  ON t.object_id = c.object_id
JOIN XStudio_Configuration_XMES.sys.schemas s
  ON s.schema_id = t.schema_id
WHERE s.name = 'dbo'
  AND t.name = 'XStudio_LvColumns_Mst_Tbl'
ORDER BY c.column_id;
```

Column guard:

```sql
DECLARE @LVID uniqueidentifier = '<lv_guid>';
DECLARE @ColumnName varchar(100) = '<AttributeName>';

-- Must return 0 active rows before adding a new column with this name.
SELECT
    ID,
    Name,
    ParentID,
    IsDeleted
FROM XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_LvColumns_Mst_Tbl
WHERE ParentID = @LVID
  AND Name = @ColumnName
  AND IsDeleted = 0;
```

Attribute guard:

```sql
DECLARE @EntityID uniqueidentifier = '<entity_guid>';
DECLARE @AttributeName varchar(100) = '<AttributeName>';

SELECT
    ID,
    Name,
    DisplayName
FROM XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_Attribute_Mst_Tbl
WHERE ParentID = @EntityID
  AND Name = @AttributeName
  AND IsDeleted = 0;
```

Required `ColumnProperties` JSON:

```json
{
  "IsConditionalFormatting": false,
  "IsContextMenuEnableForApproval": false,
  "IsDefaultSearch": false,
  "Groupable": true,
  "IsPivotColumn": false,
  "ColumnUnit": "",
  "Title": "",
  "TitleAlignment": "left"
}
```

Do not leave `ColumnProperties` null.

The executable version of this schema-inspected insert belongs in:

```text
../03_SQL_TEMPLATES/lv_grid_template.sql
```

Until that template exists, preserve these field rules and write the one-off script with `sys.columns` inspection.

#### Step 3 — Column for a Dropdown attribute

A dropdown LV column should display related text, not the stored GUID.

The original operational concept is preserved:

```text
AttributeID = owning FK/dropdown attribute GUID
RelationID  = XStudio_EntityRelations_Mst_Tbl.ID for that FK attribute
```

Example:

| Field | Example |
|---|---|
| LV column `Name` | `MaterialName` |
| LV column `Header` | `Material` |
| `AttributeID` | `MaterialID` attribute GUID |
| `RelationID` | relation row for `MaterialID-XMES_Material_Mst_Tbl` |
| `IsCustomField` | `0` |
| `ColumnProperties` | valid JSON |

Resolve the relation instead of guessing it:

```sql
DECLARE @EntityID uniqueidentifier = '<owning_entity_guid>';
DECLARE @AttributeID uniqueidentifier = '<dropdown_attribute_guid>';

SELECT
    er.ID AS RelationID,
    er.RelationName,
    er.RelationEntity,
    er.RelationAttribute,
    er.RelationEntityID,
    er.RelationAttributeID
FROM XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_EntityRelations_Mst_Tbl er
WHERE er.EntityID = @EntityID
  AND er.AttributeID = @AttributeID
  AND er.IsDeleted = 0;
```

Expected:

```text
1 active relation row
```

If missing, run:

```text
dropdown_properties_template.sql
```

Then add the LV column as a normal data column with `RelationID` populated.

#### Step 4 — Action/navigation link columns

Do not create action/navigation columns as normal data columns.

Use:

```text
create_lv_link_column.md
../03_SQL_TEMPLATES/lv_link_column_template.sql
```

Action/link columns have a different contract:

| Field | Rule |
|---|---|
| `Type` | `link` |
| `IsCustomField` | `1` |
| `AttributeID` | null/blank |
| `RelationID` | null/blank |
| `RelationIDList` | null/blank |
| `LinkType` | usually `fixedtext` |
| `LinkValue` | label such as `Open`, `Edit`, `Delete` |
| `CustomLink` / `ColumnCustomLink` | page ID or URL pattern |
| `CustomLinkTargetMode` | `Inside` or `Popup` |
| `ColumnProperties` | valid JSON |

For `Edit` and `Delete` action columns, use the target page ID as the custom link when the renderer expects page-reference semantics.

#### Step 5 — Set `FilterCondition` manually when needed

The original filter-condition operation is preserved.

Typical drilldown filter:

```text
(WorkOrderID='<request.WOID>' OR IIF('<request.WOID>'='',1,0)=1)
```

No generic LV save SP is confirmed for updating `FilterCondition`, so this is a no-SP exception.

Before updating:

```sql
DECLARE @LVID uniqueidentifier = '<lv_guid>';

SELECT
    ID,
    Name,
    FilterCondition
FROM XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_LV_Mst_Tbl
WHERE ID = @LVID
  AND IsDeleted = 0;
```

Write only a guarded one-off update after `sys.columns` inspection:

| Field | Rule |
|---|---|
| `FilterCondition` | intended filter only |
| `ModifiedBy` | real `@UserID`, if column exists |
| `ModifiedOn` | `GETDATE()`, if column exists |
| `Source` / `HostAddress` | set if columns exist |

Do not use `ModifiedBy = 'sa'`.

After changing a filter that affects generated view behavior, regenerate the LV view and run postflight.

#### Step 6 — Regenerate the SQL view

This is mandatory after LV column changes unless the LV is explicitly custom-SP-backed.

Use the confirmed SP:

```sql
EXEC XStudio_Configuration.dbo.XStudio_CreateViewForLV_Usp
    @LvID             = '<lv_guid>',
    @LvName           = '<lv_name>',
    @DataDatabase     = '<data_db>',
    @ColumnQuery      = '<SELECT ...>',
    @RelationQuery    = '<FROM/JOIN ...>',
    @GroupByQuery     = '',
    @WhereCondition   = '<WHERE ...>',
    @IsCalculatedView = 0;
```

Do not pass null or placeholder fragments.

##### Query fragment contract

| Parameter | Required content |
|---|---|
| `@ColumnQuery` | full `SELECT ...` list; aliases must match LV column `Name` values |
| `@RelationQuery` | full `FROM ... JOIN ...` fragment |
| `@GroupByQuery` | group-by fragment if needed; otherwise empty string |
| `@WhereCondition` | valid `WHERE ...`; use `WHERE 1=1` if no filter is needed |
| `@IsCalculatedView` | `0` for ordinary LV |

##### Example conceptual fragments

For a simple entity table:

```sql
DECLARE @ColumnQuery varchar(max) =
'SELECT
    t.[ID],
    t.[Code] AS [Code],
    t.[Name] AS [Name]';

DECLARE @RelationQuery varchar(max) =
' FROM [XStudio_XMES].dbo.[MyEntity_Mst_Tbl] t';

DECLARE @WhereCondition varchar(max) =
' WHERE ISNULL(t.IsDeleted,0)=0';
```

For a dropdown display column, the fragments must join the relation target table and alias the display text to the LV column name:

```sql
DECLARE @ColumnQuery varchar(max) =
'SELECT
    t.[ID],
    mat.[Name] AS [MaterialName]';

DECLARE @RelationQuery varchar(max) =
' FROM [XStudio_XMES].dbo.[MyEntity_Mst_Tbl] t
  LEFT JOIN [XStudio_XMES].dbo.[XMES_Material_Mst_Tbl] mat
    ON mat.[ID] = t.[MaterialID]
   AND ISNULL(mat.IsDeleted,0)=0';

DECLARE @WhereCondition varchar(max) =
' WHERE ISNULL(t.IsDeleted,0)=0';
```

#### Step 7 — Custom-SP-backed LV

If `XStudio_LV_Mst_Tbl.SPName` is set, the LV may use a custom stored procedure instead of the generated view.

Diagnostic:

```sql
SELECT
    ID,
    Name,
    SPName,
    FilterCondition
FROM XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_LV_Mst_Tbl
WHERE ID = '<lv_guid>'
  AND IsDeleted = 0;
```

If `SPName` is present:

1. verify the stored procedure exists;
2. verify it returns columns matching LV column `Name` values;
3. verify it handles runtime/request parameters;
4. do not rely only on generated view validation.

#### Step 8 — Bind LV to a page

Do not insert into `XStudio_PageControls_Mst_Tbl`.

Use:

```text
page_menu_template.sql
```

Required SP:

```text
XStudio_Save_Page_Control_Configuration_Usp
```

LV PageControl contract:

| Field | Rule |
|---|---|
| `ParentID` | page ID |
| `ControlType` | `grid` / platform LV control value |
| `ControlID` | LV ID |
| `ControlProperties` | at least `{"IpFiltering":"False"}` unless more is required |

#### Postflight

##### LV and column count

```sql
DECLARE @LVID uniqueidentifier = '<lv_guid>';

SELECT
    ID,
    Name,
    DisplayName,
    ParentID,
    SPName,
    FilterCondition
FROM XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_LV_Mst_Tbl
WHERE ID = @LVID
  AND IsDeleted = 0;

SELECT COUNT(*) AS ActiveColumnCount
FROM XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_LvColumns_Mst_Tbl
WHERE ParentID = @LVID
  AND IsDeleted = 0;
```

##### Column details

```sql
SELECT
    ID,
    Name,
    Header,
    [Type],
    AttributeID,
    RelationID,
    IsVisible,
    IsSearchable,
    IsEditable,
    IsCustomField,
    RoleIDList,
    IsRestricted,
    ColumnProperties
FROM XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_LvColumns_Mst_Tbl
WHERE ParentID = @LVID
  AND IsDeleted = 0
ORDER BY SerialNumber, Name;
```

##### Normal data columns must resolve to active attributes

```sql
SELECT
    lc.ID,
    lc.Name,
    lc.AttributeID
FROM XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_LvColumns_Mst_Tbl lc
LEFT JOIN XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_Attribute_Mst_Tbl a
  ON a.ID = lc.AttributeID
 AND a.IsDeleted = 0
WHERE lc.ParentID = @LVID
  AND lc.IsDeleted = 0
  AND ISNULL(lc.IsCustomField,0) = 0
  AND lc.AttributeID IS NOT NULL
  AND a.ID IS NULL;
```

Expected:

```text
0 rows
```

##### Dropdown columns must have valid relation

```sql
SELECT
    lc.ID,
    lc.Name,
    lc.AttributeID,
    lc.RelationID
FROM XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_LvColumns_Mst_Tbl lc
LEFT JOIN XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_EntityRelations_Mst_Tbl er
  ON er.ID = lc.RelationID
 AND er.IsDeleted = 0
WHERE lc.ParentID = @LVID
  AND lc.IsDeleted = 0
  AND lc.RelationID IS NOT NULL
  AND er.ID IS NULL;
```

Expected:

```text
0 rows
```

##### ColumnProperties must be valid JSON where present

```sql
SELECT
    ID,
    Name,
    ColumnProperties
FROM XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_LvColumns_Mst_Tbl
WHERE ParentID = @LVID
  AND IsDeleted = 0
  AND ColumnProperties IS NOT NULL
  AND ISJSON(CONVERT(nvarchar(max), ColumnProperties)) <> 1;
```

Expected:

```text
0 rows
```

##### Generated view must exist and expose the expected columns

```sql
SELECT TOP 3 *
FROM XStudio_XMES.dbo.XStudio_<LVName>_Vw;
```

If the LV is custom-SP-backed, validate the custom SP output instead.

#### Common errors

| Symptom | Likely cause | Correct fix |
|---|---|---|
| Grid does not show | LV is not bound to a page or PageControl is wrong | Use `page_menu_template.sql` / `XStudio_Save_Page_Control_Configuration_Usp` |
| Grid exists but has no columns | No active rows in `XStudio_LvColumns_Mst_Tbl` | Add columns through guarded LV-column exception or corrected LV template |
| Grid shows stale columns | View was not regenerated | Run `XStudio_CreateViewForLV_Usp` with explicit fragments |
| `Invalid column name` in LV view | LV column points to missing/renamed physical column | Fix entity/attribute or remove stale LV column through corrected LV flow |
| Dropdown shows GUID | Dropdown LV column missing `RelationID`, or view fragment does not join target table | Run dropdown template, set correct `RelationID`, regenerate view |
| Link column behaves like data column | Action column was created as a normal LV column | Use `lv_link_column_template.sql` |
| View SP creates invalid output | Null/placeholder query fragments | Provide full `@ColumnQuery`, `@RelationQuery`, and `@WhereCondition` |
| LV ignores generated view | `SPName` is set | Validate custom SP output |

#### See also

- [create_entity_module.md](create_entity_module.md)
- [create_dropdown_attribute.md](create_dropdown_attribute.md)
- [create_lv_link_column.md](create_lv_link_column.md)
- [create_page_bind_controls_menu_playbook.md](create_page_bind_controls_menu_playbook.md)
- [lv_view_failures.md](../04_DEBUGGING/lv_view_failures.md)
- [broken_link_columns.md](../04_DEBUGGING/broken_link_columns.md)
- [dropdown_properties_template.sql](../03_SQL_TEMPLATES/dropdown_properties_template.sql)
- [lv_link_column_template.sql](../03_SQL_TEMPLATES/lv_link_column_template.sql)
- [page_menu_template.sql](../03_SQL_TEMPLATES/page_menu_template.sql)
- [postflight_checks.sql](../03_SQL_TEMPLATES/postflight_checks.sql)

## 29. Create Lv Link Column

**Source file:** `02_TASK_PLAYBOOKS/create_lv_link_column.md`  
**File type:** `.md`  
**Size:** `8593` bytes  
**Modified on:** `2026-05-12 15:19:58`  
**SHA256:** `d43e1b6e0b60`  
**Encoding used:** `utf-8`

> Start of imported document.

### Playbook: Create LV Link Column

#### Purpose

Create a row-level navigation/action link in an LV grid that opens a target page or URL and passes row context.

Use this for parent → child drill-down links such as:

```text
Work Order → Production
Work Order → Consumption
Work Order → WO Events
Sales Order → Detail lines
Parent record → Filtered child list
```

#### Correct rule

```text
Use XStudio SPs wherever a suitable SP exists.
If no suitable SP exists, preserve the table operation as a documented no-SP exception with live schema inspection, guards, real user audit values, and postflight.
```

For LV link columns:

```text
No generic XStudio save-LV-column SP is currently confirmed.
```

Therefore the LV-column metadata write is preserved as a **documented no-SP exception**. The LV view regeneration must use:

```text
XStudio_CreateViewForLV_Usp
```

#### Tables / objects touched

| Object | Purpose | Write path |
|---|---|---|
| `XStudio_LvColumns_Mst_Tbl` | Link/action column metadata | No confirmed save-column SP; guarded no-SP exception |
| `XStudio_XMES.dbo.XStudio_<LVName>_Vw` | Generated LV view | `XStudio_CreateViewForLV_Usp` |
| Target page | Destination page opened by link | Must already exist |
| Parent/child page flow | URL/request-token context | Preserve token mechanics |

#### Required fields

| Field | Required value | Notes |
|---|---|---|
| `Name` | No spaces, e.g. `WOProduction` | Internal identifier and view alias |
| `Header` | Column header text | Shown in grid header |
| `Type` | `link` | Marks as link, not data column |
| `LinkType` | `fixedtext` | Text link using `LinkValue` |
| `LinkValue` | `Open`, `Edit`, `Delete`, etc. | The clickable text shown in every row |
| `CustomLink` / `ColumnCustomLink` | Page ID or URL/token target | Column name varies by schema |
| `CustomLinkTargetMode` | `Inside` or `Popup` | Match live platform examples |
| `RoleIDList` | Comma-separated role GUIDs | Optional visibility restriction |
| `IsVisible` | `1` | Show column |
| `IsCustomField` | `1` | Required so XStudio does not treat it as a physical column |
| `AttributeID` | `NULL` or blank | Must not point to a real physical attribute |
| `RelationID` | `NULL` or blank | Must be blank for pure action links |
| `RelationIDList` | `NULL` or blank | Must be blank for pure action links |
| `Alignment` | `center` | Common working pattern |
| `IsRestricted` | `1` only when `RoleIDList` is used | Otherwise `0` |
| `SerialNumber` | Column order | Place after normal data columns |
| `ParentID` | LV ID | Owning LV |
| `ColumnProperties` | Valid JSON | Required for grid features |
| audit fields | real deployment user | Do not use `sa` |

#### ColumnProperties minimum JSON

```json
{
  "IsConditionalFormatting": false,
  "IsContextMenuEnableForApproval": false,
  "IsDefaultSearch": false,
  "Groupable": true,
  "IsPivotColumn": true,
  "ColumnUnit": "",
  "Title": "",
  "TitleAlignment": "left"
}
```

#### URL/token target format

```text
{SystemName}/DEFAULT/{PageName}?{Param}={RowToken}
```

Examples:

```text
XMES/DEFAULT/List_Page_XMES_Production_Trn_Tbl?WOID={ID}
XMES/DEFAULT/List_Page_XMES_Consumption_Trn_Tbl?WOID={ID}
XMES/DEFAULT/List_Page_XMES_WO_Event_Trn_Tbl?WOID={ID}
```

Token rules:

| Token | Meaning |
|---|---|
| `{ID}` | Replaced with current row primary key |
| `{WorkOrderNumber}` | Replaced with row’s `WorkOrderNumber` value |
| `{AnyColumn}` | Any column exposed by the LV result set can be used if the renderer supports it |

#### Page-ID action target rule

Some platform action columns, especially Edit/Delete, may expect a page-reference target rather than URL-only behavior.

For Edit/Delete action columns:

```text
Use the target page ID as the custom link when the live renderer expects page-reference semantics.
Validate generated view JSON carries PageId / record metadata where required.
```

For drill-down URL columns:

```text
Use URL/token target such as XMES/DEFAULT/List_Page_...?WOID={ID}.
```

#### No-SP exception implementation pattern

Before writing to `XStudio_LvColumns_Mst_Tbl`, inspect live columns:

```sql
SELECT
    c.name,
    TYPE_NAME(c.user_type_id) AS DataType,
    c.max_length,
    c.is_nullable,
    c.is_identity
FROM XStudio_Configuration_XMES.sys.columns c
JOIN XStudio_Configuration_XMES.sys.tables t
  ON t.object_id = c.object_id
JOIN XStudio_Configuration_XMES.sys.schemas s
  ON s.schema_id = t.schema_id
WHERE s.name = 'dbo'
  AND t.name = 'XStudio_LvColumns_Mst_Tbl'
ORDER BY c.column_id;
```

Guard against duplicates:

```sql
DECLARE @LVID uniqueidentifier = '<lv_guid>';
DECLARE @ColumnName varchar(100) = 'WOProduction';

SELECT
    ID,
    Name,
    ParentID,
    IsDeleted
FROM XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_LvColumns_Mst_Tbl
WHERE ParentID = @LVID
  AND Name = @ColumnName
  AND IsDeleted = 0;
```

Expected before insert:

```text
0 active rows
```

If the row exists, update only the intended row and preserve its identity.

#### Preserved insert field contract

The original insert knowledge is preserved, but it must be implemented with live schema inspection and guards:

```text
ID
Name
Header
Type
LinkType
LinkValue
CustomLink / ColumnCustomLink
CustomLinkTargetMode
AttributeID
RelationID
RelationIDList
RoleIDList
IsVisible
IsCustomField
Alignment
IsRestricted
SerialNumber
ParentID
ColumnProperties
CreatedBy
ModifiedBy
CreatedOn
ModifiedOn
IsDeleted
IsSystem
Source / HostAddress, where present
```

Use:

```text
@UserID = '<deployment_user_guid>'
@IPAddress = 'T-SQL'
```

Do not use:

```text
CreatedBy = 'sa'
ModifiedBy = 'sa'
```

#### Regenerate LV view

After inserting/updating the LV link column, regenerate the view:

```sql
EXEC XStudio_Configuration.dbo.XStudio_CreateViewForLV_Usp
    @LvID             = '<LV_GUID>',
    @LvName           = '<LV_NAME>',
    @DataDatabase     = 'XStudio_XMES',
    @ColumnQuery      = '<SELECT with JSON alias for link column>',
    @RelationQuery    = '<FROM/JOIN ...>',
    @GroupByQuery     = '',
    @WhereCondition   = '<WHERE ...>',
    @IsCalculatedView = 0;
```

Do not pass `NULL` query fragments.

#### Generated view alias contract

The generated view must expose the link column alias.

For URL/token links, the alias may be a JSON string that carries target metadata. Example shape:

```json
{
  "Link": "XMES/DEFAULT/List_Page_XMES_Production_Trn_Tbl?WOID={ID}",
  "LinkTarget": "inside",
  "LinkValue": "Open"
}
```

For platform Edit/Delete action columns, validate against live generated examples. Some environments expect generated view JSON to include:

```text
PageId
RecordId
Action
LinkTarget
```

Treat the metadata row and generated view alias as one contract.

#### Target modes

| `CustomLinkTargetMode` | Behavior |
|---|---|
| `Inside` | Opens in the main content frame |
| `Popup` | Opens as a modal overlay |

Match casing to live examples if the renderer is case-sensitive.

#### Postflight

```sql
-- Metadata row exists and is safe
SELECT
    Name,
    Header,
    Type,
    LinkType,
    LinkValue,
    CustomLink,
    CustomLinkTargetMode,
    AttributeID,
    RelationID,
    RelationIDList,
    RoleIDList,
    IsVisible,
    IsCustomField,
    Alignment,
    IsRestricted,
    SerialNumber,
    ColumnProperties
FROM XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_LvColumns_Mst_Tbl
WHERE ParentID = '<LV_GUID>'
  AND Name = 'WOProduction'
  AND IsDeleted = 0;

-- Link column appears in generated view
SELECT TOP 1
    WOProduction
FROM XStudio_XMES.dbo.XStudio_<LVName>_Vw;
```

Expected:

```text
Type = link
IsCustomField = 1
AttributeID blank/null
RelationID blank/null
ColumnProperties valid JSON
generated view exposes the link column alias
```

#### Common mistakes

| Mistake | Effect |
|---|---|
| `AttributeID` points to a real attribute | XStudio may try to read a physical column named like the link column |
| `IsCustomField = 0` | Column treated as bound data column |
| `Type` not `link` | Grid renders as plain text/data |
| `ColumnProperties` is NULL | Grid features break |
| View not regenerated | Link column invisible or stale |
| URL target used where page-ID target is required | Edit/Delete may fail or open wrong page |
| `CustomLinkTargetMode` wrong casing for environment | Link may open in wrong mode |

#### See also

- [create_lv_grid.md](create_lv_grid.md)
- [create_parent_child_flow.md](create_parent_child_flow.md)
- [broken_link_columns.md](../04_DEBUGGING/broken_link_columns.md)
- [lv_link_column_template.sql](../03_SQL_TEMPLATES/lv_link_column_template.sql)
- [working_lv_link_example.md](../06_EXAMPLES/working_lv_link_example.md)

> End of imported document.

## 30. Create Page Bind Controls Menu Playbook

**Source file:** `02_TASK_PLAYBOOKS/create_page_bind_controls_menu_playbook.md`  
**File type:** `.md`  
**Size:** `21933` bytes  
**Modified on:** `2026-05-12 08:34:13`  
**SHA256:** `fa8bcd44a115`  
**Encoding used:** `utf-8`

> Start of imported document.

### Playbook: Create Page, Bind Controls, and Add Menu

#### Purpose

Create a generic XStudio page, bind one or more controls to that page, and expose the page through a menu item with role rights.

A page is only a container. It does not render useful UI by itself. The visible UI comes from page-control bindings in `XStudio_PageControls_Mst_Tbl`, created or updated through `XStudio_Configuration.dbo.XStudio_Save_Page_Control_Configuration_Usp`.

The control bound to a page can be a List View, edit form, inline grid, HTML control, dashboard, tab container, or another supported XStudio control. A tab container is only one possible control type. Do not create tabs unless the screen explicitly needs a multi-tab layout.

#### Core pattern

```text
Page header
-> PageControl binding
-> Menu item
-> Menu rights
```

#### Hard rule

Use XStudio stored procedures for all actions where an XStudio stored procedure exists.

Direct inserts are allowed only where the current XStudio stored procedure reference proves that no suitable platform SP exists.

In this playbook:

| Action | Required method |
|---|---|
| Create or update PageControl binding | `XStudio_Save_Page_Control_Configuration_Usp` only |
| Create or update menu item | `XStudio_Save_Menu_Configuration_Usp` only |
| Add menu role visibility | `XStudio_Save_Menu_Rights_Usp` only |
| Delete page control binding | `XStudio_Delete_Page_Control_Configuration_Usp` only |
| Delete menu item | `XStudio_Delete_Menu_Configuration_Usp` only |
| Delete menu rights | `XStudio_Delete_Menu_Rights_Usp` only |
| Create/update tabs | Tab SPs only, and only for tab-container pages |
| Create brand-new page header | Guarded insert into `XStudio_Page_Mst_Tbl` only because no confirmed generic page-save SP exists |

#### Mental model

```text
XStudio_Page_Mst_Tbl
  = screen/page header only

XStudio_PageControls_Mst_Tbl
  = binds an actual control to the page

XStudio_Menu_Mst_Tbl
  = navigation item pointing to a page

XStudio_MenuRights_Mst_Tbl
  = role visibility for the menu item
```

A page without PageControls is a blank page.

A menu without MenuRights is usually invisible to users.

A tab container is a control. It is not required for ordinary pages.

#### Supported page control patterns

| Desired page content | PageControl `ControlType` | `ControlID` points to | Extra configuration |
|---|---|---|---|
| List View / grid | `grid` | `XStudio_LV_Mst_Tbl.ID` | LV and generated LV view must exist |
| Edit form | `edit` | `XStudio_Edit_Mst_Tbl.ID` | Edit controls must be renderer-safe |
| Inline grid | `inlinegrid` | Inline grid / LV-related control ID | Parent-child configuration must be valid |
| HTML control | platform HTML control value | HTML control ID | Use HTML control SPs |
| Dashboard | `dashboard` | Dashboard ID | Dashboard configuration must exist |
| Tab container | `tab` | `XStudio_Tab_Container_Mst_Tbl.ID` | Then configure tabs and tab controls |
| Tree / treelist / timeline | platform-specific value | corresponding control ID | Use matching control SPs |

Always confirm the exact `ControlType` value from existing working XStudio rows before using less-common controls.

#### Stored procedures used

| SP | Purpose |
|---|---|
| `XStudio_Save_Page_Control_Configuration_Usp` | Create/update Page -> Control binding |
| `XStudio_Delete_Page_Control_Configuration_Usp` | Delete/soft-delete PageControl binding |
| `XStudio_Get_Page_Controls_By_PageId_Usp` | Read controls bound to a page |
| `XStudio_Get_Page_Control_By_ControlId_Usp` | Read page-control rows by target control |
| `XStudio_Update_Page_Control_Position_Usp` | Update page-control layout/position |
| `XStudio_Save_Dependent_Page_Control_Configuration_USP` | Save dependency rules between page controls |
| `XStudio_Get_Dependent_Page_Control_List_USP` | Read dependent page-control configuration |
| `XStudio_Delete_Dependent_Page_Control_Configuration_Usp` | Delete dependent page-control configuration |
| `XStudio_Save_Menu_Configuration_Usp` | Create/update menu item |
| `XStudio_Save_Menu_Rights_Usp` | Add menu role visibility |
| `XStudio_Delete_Menu_Configuration_Usp` | Delete/soft-delete menu item |
| `XStudio_Delete_Menu_Rights_Usp` | Delete menu rights |
| `XStudio_Save_Menu_Sequence_Usp` | Save menu ordering |
| `XStudio_AddRoleToParentMenu_USP` | Add/propagate role access to a parent menu, after live behavior is confirmed |
| `XStudio_Page_Copy_Usp` | Copy existing page only |
| `XStudio_Create_Duplicate_Screen_Usp` | Duplicate existing screen/page only |
| `XStudio_Menu_Copy_Usp` | Copy existing menu only |
| `XStudio_Save_Tab_Configuration_Usp` | Create/update tab, only for tab-container pages |
| `XStudio_Save_Tab_Control_Configuration_Usp` | Bind controls inside a tab, only for tab-container pages |

#### Do not confuse page collections with pages

These SPs manage user page collections/bookmarks. They do not create runtime pages in `XStudio_Page_Mst_Tbl`:

| SP | Meaning |
|---|---|
| `XStudio_Add_Page_Collection_Usp` | Creates a page collection header |
| `XStudio_Add_Page_To_Page_Collection_Usp` | Adds a page/link to a page collection |
| `XStudio_Get_Page_Collection_Usp` | Reads page collections |
| `XStudio_Get_Page_Collection_Details_Usp` | Reads page collection contents |
| `XStudio_Update_Page_Collection_Usp` | Updates collection header |
| `XStudio_Update_Page_Collection_Dtl_Usp` | Updates collection detail |
| `XStudio_Remove_Page_Collection_Usp` | Removes collection |
| `XStudio_Remove_Page_From_Page_Collection` | Removes one item from collection |

Do not use page-collection SPs to create screens.

#### Required inputs

```sql
DECLARE @SystemID     varchar(36)  = '6C8C0070-60B5-4474-BEC8-5C02197B32E6';
DECLARE @ConfigDB     sysname      = 'XStudio_Configuration_XMES';
DECLARE @AppID        varchar(36)  = '29123B0B-3872-41CF-B5E1-67C4A8F0D4BC';
DECLARE @UserID       varchar(36)  = '<deployment_user_guid>';
DECLARE @IPAddress    varchar(20)  = 'T-SQL';

DECLARE @PageName     varchar(200) = 'Page_MyFeature';
DECLARE @PageTitle    varchar(200) = 'My Feature';
DECLARE @IsPopup      bit          = 0;

DECLARE @ControlID    varchar(36)  = '<control_guid>';
DECLARE @ControlType  varchar(50)  = 'grid'; -- grid, edit, inlinegrid, tab, dashboard, etc.
DECLARE @ControlName  varchar(200) = 'My Feature Control';

DECLARE @MenuName     varchar(200) = 'My Feature';
DECLARE @RoleID       varchar(36)  = '<role_guid>';
DECLARE @ParentMenuID varchar(36)  = NULL; -- set only for submenu items
```

#### Preflight

##### 1. Confirm deployment user

```sql
SELECT ID, UserName, Name
FROM XStudio_Configuration.dbo.XStudio_User_Mst_Tbl
WHERE ID = @UserID
  AND IsDeleted = 0;
```

Stop if this returns no row.

##### 2. Confirm application

```sql
SELECT ID, Name
FROM XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_Application_Mst_Tbl
WHERE ID = @AppID
  AND IsDeleted = 0;
```

##### 3. Confirm target role

```sql
SELECT ID, Name
FROM XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_Role_Mst_Tbl
WHERE ID = @RoleID
  AND IsDeleted = 0;
```

##### 4. Confirm required SPs exist

```sql
SELECT name
FROM XStudio_Configuration.sys.procedures
WHERE name IN
(
    'XStudio_Save_Page_Control_Configuration_Usp',
    'XStudio_Save_Menu_Configuration_Usp',
    'XStudio_Save_Menu_Rights_Usp',
    'XStudio_Save_Menu_Sequence_Usp',
    'XStudio_Get_Page_Controls_By_PageId_Usp',
    'XStudio_Get_Page_Control_By_ControlId_Usp'
)
ORDER BY name;
```

Stop if any required SP is missing.

##### 5. Confirm the menu TVP shape

`XStudio_Save_Menu_Configuration_Usp` uses a `dbo.Menu` table-valued parameter. Always inspect the live type before constructing the TVP.

```sql
SELECT
    c.column_id,
    c.name AS ColumnName,
    TYPE_NAME(c.user_type_id) AS DataType,
    c.max_length,
    c.is_nullable
FROM XStudio_Configuration.sys.table_types tt
JOIN XStudio_Configuration.sys.columns c
  ON c.object_id = tt.type_table_object_id
WHERE tt.name = 'Menu'
ORDER BY c.column_id;
```

Adjust the `INSERT INTO @MenuTable (...)` column list to match the live `dbo.Menu` type exactly.

##### 6. Confirm target control exists

Use the control-specific check based on `@ControlType`.

###### LV / grid

```sql
IF @ControlType = 'grid'
BEGIN
    SELECT ID, Name
    FROM XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_LV_Mst_Tbl
    WHERE ID = @ControlID
      AND IsDeleted = 0;
END;
```

###### Edit form

```sql
IF @ControlType = 'edit'
BEGIN
    SELECT ID, Name
    FROM XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_Edit_Mst_Tbl
    WHERE ID = @ControlID
      AND IsDeleted = 0;
END;
```

###### Tab container

```sql
IF @ControlType = 'tab'
BEGIN
    SELECT ID, Name
    FROM XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_Tab_Container_Mst_Tbl
    WHERE ID = @ControlID
      AND IsDeleted = 0;
END;
```

###### HTML control

Confirm the live HTML control table and SP surface before use:

```sql
SELECT name
FROM XStudio_Configuration.sys.procedures
WHERE name IN
(
    'XStudio_Save_HTMLControl_Usp',
    'XStudio_Save_HTMLControl_DataSource_Usp',
    'XStudio_Get_HTMLControl_Details_Usp',
    'XStudio_Get_HTMLControl_DataSouce_Usp'
)
ORDER BY name;
```

For less-common controls, inspect a known working page-control row and the relevant control table before creating a new binding.

#### Step 1 - Create or resolve page header

No general-purpose `XStudio_Save_Page_Configuration_Usp` / `XStudio_Create_Page_Usp` is confirmed in the current SP reference. Therefore a guarded direct insert into `XStudio_Page_Mst_Tbl` is the allowed exception.

```sql
DECLARE @PageID varchar(36);

SELECT @PageID = CAST(ID AS varchar(36))
FROM XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_Page_Mst_Tbl
WHERE Name = @PageName
  AND ParentID = @AppID
  AND IsDeleted = 0;

IF @PageID IS NULL
BEGIN
    SET @PageID = CAST(NEWID() AS varchar(36));

    INSERT INTO XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_Page_Mst_Tbl
    (
        ID,
        Name,
        DisplayName,
        Description,
        IsActive,
        IsPopup,
        ParentID,
        CreatedBy,
        ModifiedBy,
        CreatedOn,
        ModifiedOn,
        IsDeleted,
        IsSystem,
        Source,
        HostAddress
    )
    VALUES
    (
        @PageID,
        @PageName,
        @PageTitle,
        '',
        1,
        @IsPopup,
        @AppID,
        @UserID,
        @UserID,
        GETDATE(),
        GETDATE(),
        0,
        0,
        'System',
        @IPAddress
    );
END;
```

#### Step 2 - Bind a control to the page

Use `XStudio_Save_Page_Control_Configuration_Usp` for every page-control binding.

Do not insert into `XStudio_PageControls_Mst_Tbl` directly.

```sql
DECLARE @PageControlID varchar(36);

SELECT @PageControlID = CAST(ID AS varchar(36))
FROM XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_PageControls_Mst_Tbl
WHERE ParentID = @PageID
  AND ControlID = @ControlID
  AND ControlType = @ControlType
  AND IsDeleted = 0;

IF @PageControlID IS NULL
    SET @PageControlID = CAST(NEWID() AS varchar(36));

EXEC XStudio_Configuration.dbo.XStudio_Save_Page_Control_Configuration_Usp
    @SystemId          = @SystemID,
    @Id                = @PageControlID,
    @Name              = @ControlName,
    @ParentId          = @PageID,
    @RowId             = '1',
    @DivId             = '1',
    @ControlType       = @ControlType,
    @ControlId         = @ControlID,
    @ControlUrl        = '',
    @CustomCss         = '',
    @LoadUrlAsIFrame   = 0,
    @Description       = '',
    @ControlProperties = '{"IpFiltering":"False"}',
    @UserId            = @UserID,
    @IPAddress         = @IPAddress;
```

##### Multiple controls on one page

Repeat Step 2 for each control, changing:

```text
@ControlID
@ControlType
@ControlName
@RowId
@DivId
@ControlProperties
```

Each control needs a separate PageControl binding.

#### Step 3 - If the control is a tab container

Use this section only when `@ControlType = 'tab'`.

A tab container is itself a page control. After the tab container is attached to the page, configure tabs and bind controls inside those tabs.

Do not create tabs for ordinary single-control pages.

##### 3a. Save tab

Use `XStudio_Save_Tab_Configuration_Usp`. Do not insert directly into `XStudio_Tab_Mst_Tbl`.

```sql
DECLARE @TabName varchar(200) = 'Production';
DECLARE @RoleIDList varchar(max) = @RoleID;

EXEC XStudio_Configuration.dbo.XStudio_Save_Tab_Configuration_Usp
    @SystemId    = @SystemID,
    @Id          = NULL,
    @ParentID    = @ControlID,       -- tab container ID
    @Name        = @TabName,
    @Icon        = '',
    @ImageID     = NULL,
    @RoleIDList  = @RoleIDList;
```

Resolve the created tab:

```sql
DECLARE @TabID varchar(36);

SELECT @TabID = CAST(ID AS varchar(36))
FROM XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_Tab_Mst_Tbl
WHERE ParentID = @ControlID
  AND Name = @TabName
  AND IsDeleted = 0;
```

##### 3b. Bind a control inside the tab

Use `XStudio_Save_Tab_Control_Configuration_Usp`. Do not insert directly into `XStudio_Tab_Dtl_Tbl`.

```sql
DECLARE @TabControlID varchar(36) = CAST(NEWID() AS varchar(36));
DECLARE @TabChildControlID varchar(36) = '<lv_or_edit_or_other_control_guid>';
DECLARE @TabChildControlType varchar(50) = 'grid';

EXEC XStudio_Configuration.dbo.XStudio_Save_Tab_Control_Configuration_Usp
    @SystemId             = @SystemID,
    @Id                   = @TabControlID,
    @Name                 = 'Production Grid',
    @ParentID             = @TabID,
    @DivID                = '1',
    @ControlType          = @TabChildControlType,
    @RowID                = '1',
    @ControlID            = @TabChildControlID,
    @ControlUrl           = '',
    @CustomCss            = '',
    @AdditionalParameter  = '',
    @LoadUrlAsIFrame      = 0,
    @UserId               = @UserID,
    @IPAddress            = @IPAddress;
```

#### Step 4 - Create or update menu item

Use `XStudio_Save_Menu_Configuration_Usp`.

Do not insert into `XStudio_Menu_Mst_Tbl` directly.

```sql
DECLARE @MenuID varchar(36);
DECLARE @MenuMode char(1);

SELECT @MenuID = CAST(ID AS varchar(36))
FROM XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_Menu_Mst_Tbl
WHERE Name = @MenuName
  AND ParentID = @AppID
  AND ISNULL(SubMenuOfID, '') = ISNULL(@ParentMenuID, '')
  AND IsDeleted = 0;

IF @MenuID IS NULL
BEGIN
    SET @MenuID = CAST(NEWID() AS varchar(36));
    SET @MenuMode = 'I';
END
ELSE
BEGIN
    SET @MenuMode = 'U';
END;

DECLARE @MenuTable XStudio_Configuration.dbo.Menu;

-- IMPORTANT: adjust this column list to match the live dbo.Menu table type.
INSERT INTO @MenuTable
(
    ID,
    Name,
    ParentID,
    PageID,
    SubMenuOfID,
    MenuTooltip,
    MenuType,
    ControlType,
    IsVisible,
    IconID,
    ImageID,
    LinkTarget,
    FilterCondition,
    CreatedBy,
    ModifiedBy
)
VALUES
(
    @MenuID,
    @MenuName,
    @AppID,
    @PageID,
    @ParentMenuID,
    @MenuName,
    '',
    '',
    1,
    NULL,
    NULL,
    '',
    '',
    @UserID,
    @UserID
);

EXEC XStudio_Configuration.dbo.XStudio_Save_Menu_Configuration_Usp
    @SystemId   = @SystemID,
    @Id         = @MenuID,
    @IPAddress  = @IPAddress,
    @Mode       = @MenuMode,
    @TempTable  = @MenuTable;
```

#### Step 5 - Add menu rights

Use `XStudio_Save_Menu_Rights_Usp`.

Do not insert into `XStudio_MenuRights_Mst_Tbl` directly.

The SP should be called once for each role that should see the menu. Guard the call because the SP may not de-duplicate existing rights.

```sql
IF NOT EXISTS
(
    SELECT 1
    FROM XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_MenuRights_Mst_Tbl
    WHERE ParentID = @MenuID
      AND RoleID = @RoleID
      AND IsDeleted = 0
)
BEGIN
    EXEC XStudio_Configuration.dbo.XStudio_Save_Menu_Rights_Usp
        @SystemId   = @SystemID,
        @Id         = @MenuID,
        @RoleID     = @RoleID,
        @UserId     = @UserID,
        @IPAddress  = @IPAddress;
END;
```

If the menu is a child menu, verify the parent menu has access for the same role. Use `XStudio_AddRoleToParentMenu_USP` only after confirming its live behavior.

#### Step 6 - Optional menu ordering

Use `XStudio_Save_Menu_Sequence_Usp`.

Do not update `XStudio_Menu_Mst_Tbl.Sn` directly.

```sql
EXEC XStudio_Configuration.dbo.XStudio_Save_Menu_Sequence_Usp
    @SystemId     = @SystemID,
    @Id           = @MenuID,
    @Sn           = 100,
    @SubMenuOfID  = @ParentMenuID,
    @UserId       = @UserID,
    @IPAddress    = @IPAddress;
```

#### Postflight

##### 1. Page exists

```sql
SELECT ID, Name, DisplayName, IsActive, IsPopup, ParentID
FROM XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_Page_Mst_Tbl
WHERE ID = @PageID
  AND IsDeleted = 0;
```

##### 2. Page has active controls

```sql
SELECT
    pc.ID,
    pc.Name,
    pc.ParentID,
    p.Name AS PageName,
    pc.ControlType,
    pc.ControlID,
    pc.RowID,
    pc.DivID,
    pc.ControlProperties
FROM XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_PageControls_Mst_Tbl pc
JOIN XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_Page_Mst_Tbl p
  ON p.ID = pc.ParentID
WHERE pc.ParentID = @PageID
  AND pc.IsDeleted = 0;
```

Fail if this returns zero rows.

##### 3. Page controls resolve to real controls

###### LV/grid controls

```sql
SELECT pc.ID AS PageControlID, pc.ControlID, lv.Name AS LVName
FROM XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_PageControls_Mst_Tbl pc
LEFT JOIN XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_LV_Mst_Tbl lv
  ON lv.ID = pc.ControlID
 AND lv.IsDeleted = 0
WHERE pc.ParentID = @PageID
  AND pc.ControlType = 'grid'
  AND pc.IsDeleted = 0;
```

###### Edit controls

```sql
SELECT pc.ID AS PageControlID, pc.ControlID, e.Name AS EditName
FROM XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_PageControls_Mst_Tbl pc
LEFT JOIN XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_Edit_Mst_Tbl e
  ON e.ID = pc.ControlID
 AND e.IsDeleted = 0
WHERE pc.ParentID = @PageID
  AND pc.ControlType = 'edit'
  AND pc.IsDeleted = 0;
```

###### Tab container controls

```sql
SELECT pc.ID AS PageControlID, pc.ControlID, tc.Name AS TabContainerName
FROM XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_PageControls_Mst_Tbl pc
LEFT JOIN XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_Tab_Container_Mst_Tbl tc
  ON tc.ID = pc.ControlID
 AND tc.IsDeleted = 0
WHERE pc.ParentID = @PageID
  AND pc.ControlType = 'tab'
  AND pc.IsDeleted = 0;
```

##### 4. No blank active pages

```sql
SELECT
    p.ID,
    p.Name,
    COUNT(pc.ID) AS ActiveControlCount
FROM XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_Page_Mst_Tbl p
LEFT JOIN XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_PageControls_Mst_Tbl pc
  ON pc.ParentID = p.ID
 AND pc.IsDeleted = 0
WHERE p.ID = @PageID
  AND p.IsDeleted = 0
GROUP BY p.ID, p.Name
HAVING COUNT(pc.ID) = 0;
```

This must return zero rows.

##### 5. Menu exists and points to the page

```sql
SELECT ID, Name, PageID, ParentID, SubMenuOfID, Sn, IsVisible
FROM XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_Menu_Mst_Tbl
WHERE ID = @MenuID
  AND PageID = @PageID
  AND IsDeleted = 0;
```

##### 6. Menu has rights

```sql
SELECT
    m.Name AS MenuName,
    mr.RoleID,
    r.Name AS RoleName
FROM XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_Menu_Mst_Tbl m
JOIN XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_MenuRights_Mst_Tbl mr
  ON mr.ParentID = m.ID
 AND mr.IsDeleted = 0
JOIN XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_Role_Mst_Tbl r
  ON r.ID = mr.RoleID
 AND r.IsDeleted = 0
WHERE m.ID = @MenuID
  AND m.IsDeleted = 0;
```

Fail if this returns zero rows.

##### 7. Tab-container postflight, only if used

```sql
SELECT
    t.ID AS TabID,
    t.Name AS TabName,
    td.ID AS TabControlID,
    td.ControlType,
    td.ControlID
FROM XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_Tab_Mst_Tbl t
LEFT JOIN XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_Tab_Dtl_Tbl td
  ON td.ParentID = t.ID
 AND td.IsDeleted = 0
WHERE t.ParentID = @ControlID
  AND t.IsDeleted = 0;
```

Run only when the page control is a tab container.

#### Common issues

| Symptom | Cause | Correct fix |
|---|---|---|
| Page exists but renders blank | No active PageControl row | Run `XStudio_Save_Page_Control_Configuration_Usp` |
| LV/edit does not render | Wrong `ControlType` or `ControlID` | Re-run `XStudio_Save_Page_Control_Configuration_Usp` with correct values |
| Inline grid not working | Parent-child/inline-grid config missing | Validate inline grid config and then bind via PageControl SP |
| HTML control not rendering | HTML control config missing or wrong control type | Use HTML control SPs, then bind via PageControl SP |
| Menu item not visible | Missing menu rights | Run `XStudio_Save_Menu_Rights_Usp` for every required role |
| Menu visible but opens wrong page | Wrong `PageID` in menu config | Re-run `XStudio_Save_Menu_Configuration_Usp` in update mode |
| Tab page blank | Tab container not attached as a PageControl | Run `XStudio_Save_Page_Control_Configuration_Usp` with `ControlType = 'tab'` |
| Tab exists but tab content blank | No tab-control row | Run `XStudio_Save_Tab_Control_Configuration_Usp` |
| Page collection created but screen not visible | Page-collection SP confused with page creation | Create a page header, bind controls, then create menu and rights |

#### Forbidden direct-write patterns

```sql
-- Forbidden when SP exists:
INSERT INTO XStudio_PageControls_Mst_Tbl (...);

-- Forbidden when SP exists:
INSERT INTO XStudio_Menu_Mst_Tbl (...);

-- Forbidden when SP exists:
INSERT INTO XStudio_MenuRights_Mst_Tbl (...);

-- Forbidden when SP exists:
INSERT INTO XStudio_Tab_Mst_Tbl (...);

-- Forbidden when SP exists:
INSERT INTO XStudio_Tab_Dtl_Tbl (...);
```

#### Correct build order

```text
1. Resolve SystemID, ConfigDB, ApplicationID, UserID, RoleID.
2. Confirm target control exists.
3. Create or resolve page header.
4. Bind the desired control to the page through XStudio_Save_Page_Control_Configuration_Usp.
5. If and only if the control is a tab container, configure tabs and tab controls through tab SPs.
6. Create/update menu through XStudio_Save_Menu_Configuration_Usp.
7. Add menu rights through XStudio_Save_Menu_Rights_Usp.
8. Optionally set menu order through XStudio_Save_Menu_Sequence_Usp.
9. Run postflight and fail if the page has no controls or the menu has no rights.
```

> End of imported document.

## 31. Create Parent Child Flow

**Source file:** `02_TASK_PLAYBOOKS/create_parent_child_flow.md`  
**File type:** `.md`  
**Size:** `8434` bytes  
**Modified on:** `2026-05-12 15:20:03`  
**SHA256:** `53d94b45f4dd`  
**Encoding used:** `utf-8`

> Start of imported document.

### Playbook: Create Parent-Child Navigation Flow

#### Purpose

Build a parent grid → child page drill-down:

```text
parent LV row
→ link column
→ child list page URL with parent row token
→ child LV FilterCondition using request token
→ child edit form defaults parent FK from request token
→ child edit navigation returns to child list / edit flow
```

#### Correct rule

```text
Use XStudio SPs wherever a suitable SP exists.
If no suitable SP exists, preserve the table operation as a documented no-SP exception with live schema inspection, guards, real user audit values, and postflight.
```

#### Pattern overview

```text
Parent LV page
  -> parent LV link column
  -> child list page URL: /ChildPage?ParentParam={ID}
  -> child LV FilterCondition:
       (ParentColumn='<request.ParentParam>' OR IIF('<request.ParentParam>'='',1,0)=1)
  -> child edit form:
       DefaultValue on parent FK field = <request.ParentParam>
```

The `OR IIF` pattern is intentional:

| Context | Behavior |
|---|---|
| Opened from parent link with parameter | Shows only child rows for that parent |
| Opened from menu with no parameter | Shows all child rows |

#### Step 1 — Add link column to parent LV

Use:

```text
create_lv_link_column.md
lv_link_column_template.sql
```

Preserve the original link mechanics:

```text
CustomLink = 'XMES/DEFAULT/List_Page_XMES_Consumption_Trn_Tbl?WOID={ID}'
CustomLinkTargetMode = 'Inside'
IsCustomField = 1
AttributeID blank/null
Type = link
```

There is no confirmed generic save-LV-column SP, so the template uses a guarded no-SP exception and then regenerates the LV view through:

```text
XStudio_CreateViewForLV_Usp
```

#### Step 2 — Create child entity and LV

The child entity must have a FK column pointing to the parent.

Examples:

```text
WorkOrderID
CampaignID
SalesOrderID
ParentID
```

Create the FK attribute through:

```text
XStudio_AddAttribute_Usp
```

If it is dropdown-backed, also run:

```text
dropdown_properties_template.sql
```

Create the child LV through:

```text
create_lv_grid.md
```

#### Step 3 — Set child LV FilterCondition

No generic LV-header save SP is confirmed for `FilterCondition`, so preserve this as a no-SP exception.

Example for WO child lists:

```sql
DECLARE @ChildLVID uniqueidentifier = '<child_lv_guid>';
DECLARE @UserID varchar(36) = '<deployment_user_guid>';

UPDATE XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_LV_Mst_Tbl
SET FilterCondition = '(WorkOrderID=''<request.WOID>'' OR IIF(''<request.WOID>''='''',1,0)=1)',
    ModifiedBy      = @UserID,
    ModifiedOn      = GETDATE()
WHERE ID = @ChildLVID
  AND IsDeleted = 0;
```

Do not use `ModifiedBy = 'sa'`.

Regenerate/verify the child LV view where needed.

#### Step 4 — Child edit form: pre-populate parent FK

Preserve the original edit-control defaulting mechanic:

```text
ControlProperties.DefaultValue = <request.WOID>
```

For visible dropdown parent FK:

```sql
DECLARE @ChildEditID uniqueidentifier = '<child_edit_id>';
DECLARE @WorkOrderAttrID uniqueidentifier = '<WorkOrderID_attr_guid>';
DECLARE @UserID varchar(36) = '<deployment_user_guid>';

UPDATE XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_EditControls_Mst_Tbl
SET ControlProperties = JSON_MODIFY(
        CASE
            WHEN ControlProperties IS NULL
              OR ISJSON(CONVERT(nvarchar(max), ControlProperties)) <> 1
            THEN '{}'
            ELSE CONVERT(nvarchar(max), ControlProperties)
        END,
        '$.DefaultValue',
        '<request.WOID>'
    ),
    ModifiedBy = @UserID,
    ModifiedOn = GETDATE()
WHERE ParentID = @ChildEditID
  AND AttributeID = @WorkOrderAttrID
  AND IsDeleted = 0;
```

Because edit-control JSON safety is critical, preferred route is:

```text
edit_control_safe_json_template.sql
```

then apply the specific default-value override if the template does not already cover it.

#### Hidden parent FK option

Use a hidden field if the user should not see/change the parent FK.

Required preserved contract:

```text
ControlType = hiddenfield
ControlProperties.DefaultValue = <request.WOID>
ControlProperties.IsVisible = False
ControlProperties.IsSaveOrNot = True
```

If no SP covers this control override, preserve it as a guarded edit-control no-SP exception.

#### Step 5 — Configure child edit navigation

No generic edit-navigation SP is confirmed, so preserve this as a no-SP exception.

```sql
DECLARE @ChildEditID uniqueidentifier = '<child_edit_guid>';
DECLARE @ChildListPageID uniqueidentifier = '<child_list_page_guid>';
DECLARE @ChildEditPageID uniqueidentifier = '<child_edit_page_guid>';
DECLARE @UserID varchar(36) = '<deployment_user_guid>';

UPDATE XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_Edit_Mst_Tbl
SET AfterSavePageID                = @ChildListPageID,
    AfterCancelPageID              = @ChildListPageID,
    AfterSaveAndContinuePageID     = @ChildEditPageID,
    IsSaveAndContinueButtonVisible = 1,
    SaveAndContinueButtonName      = 'Save & Add Another',
    ModifiedBy                     = @UserID,
    ModifiedOn                     = GETDATE()
WHERE ID = @ChildEditID
  AND IsDeleted = 0;
```

Correction:

```text
AfterSaveAndContinuePageID is a page ID, not the edit ID.
```

The child list page should preserve the request parameter context where the platform supports it.

#### Step 6 — Inline grid alternative

If children should appear inside the parent edit form instead of a separate child list page, use:

```text
create_inline_grid.md
```

Preserved inline grid contract:

```text
PageControl.ControlType = inlinegrid
PageControl.ControlID = child LV ID
Parent page = parent edit page
Child entity IsChild = 1
Child entity ParentEntityID = parent entity ID
```

Because PageControl SP exists, use:

```text
XStudio_Save_Page_Control_Configuration_Usp
```

Do not directly insert into `XStudio_PageControls_Mst_Tbl`.

#### WO Execution confirmed pattern

Three execution LVs hang off Work Order via link columns.

| Link column | Target URL | FilterCondition on child LV |
|---|---|---|
| `WOProduction` | `XMES/DEFAULT/List_Page_XMES_Production_Trn_Tbl?WOID={ID}` | `(WorkOrderID='<request.WOID>' OR IIF('<request.WOID>'='',1,0)=1)` |
| `WOConsumption` | `XMES/DEFAULT/List_Page_XMES_Consumption_Trn_Tbl?WOID={ID}` | same pattern |
| `WOEvents` | `XMES/DEFAULT/List_Page_XMES_WO_Event_Trn_Tbl?WOID={ID}` | same pattern |

All three use:

```text
CustomLinkTargetMode = Inside
```

and open in the WO Execution navigation context.

#### Postflight

```sql
-- Link column in parent LV
SELECT
    Name,
    Type,
    CustomLink,
    CustomLinkTargetMode,
    IsCustomField,
    AttributeID
FROM XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_LvColumns_Mst_Tbl
WHERE ParentID = '<parent_lv_guid>'
  AND Type = 'link'
  AND IsDeleted = 0;

-- FilterCondition on child LV
SELECT
    Name,
    FilterCondition
FROM XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_LV_Mst_Tbl
WHERE ID = '<child_lv_guid>'
  AND IsDeleted = 0;

-- Verify child LV view exposes FK column
SELECT TOP 1
    WorkOrderID
FROM XStudio_XMES.dbo.XStudio_List_XMES_Consumption_Trn_Tbl_Vw;

-- Verify DefaultValue on child edit FK control
SELECT
    JSON_VALUE(CONVERT(nvarchar(max), ControlProperties), '$.DefaultValue') AS DefaultValue
FROM XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_EditControls_Mst_Tbl
WHERE ParentID = '<child_edit_id>'
  AND ControlName LIKE 'cmb_WorkOrderID%'
  AND IsDeleted = 0;
```

Expected:

```text
link column exists and is custom link
child LV has correct request-token filter
child edit FK default is <request.WOID>
navigation page IDs resolve to active pages
```

#### Common mistakes

| Mistake | Effect |
|---|---|
| Child link column bound to real AttributeID | Generated view may try to select non-existent physical link column |
| Missing child LV FilterCondition | Child list shows all records |
| Wrong request token name | Child list/edit defaults do not filter |
| DefaultValue set before safe JSON | Renderer may still crash |
| AfterSaveAndContinuePageID set to edit ID | Navigation fails |
| Direct PageControl insert for inlinegrid | Bypasses PageControl SP |

#### See also

- [create_lv_link_column.md](create_lv_link_column.md)
- [create_inline_grid.md](create_inline_grid.md)
- [configure_save_and_continue.md](configure_save_and_continue.md)
- [create_lv_grid.md](create_lv_grid.md)
- [edit_control_safe_json_template.sql](../03_SQL_TEMPLATES/edit_control_safe_json_template.sql)
- [page_menu_template.sql](../03_SQL_TEMPLATES/page_menu_template.sql)

## 32. Create Workflow Action

**Source file:** `02_TASK_PLAYBOOKS/create_workflow_action.md`  
**File type:** `.md`  
**Size:** `10326` bytes  
**Modified on:** `2026-05-12 15:20:08`  
**SHA256:** `3e18f3c7178a`  
**Encoding used:** `utf-8`

> Start of imported document.

### Playbook: Create Workflow / Approval Action

#### Purpose

Configure an approval/state-machine lifecycle on an entity:

```text
workflow / approval header
statuses
actors / transitions
optional workflow actions and action assignments
approval/status attribute on the entity
entity workflow links
generated workflow SPs / business side effects
postflight validation
```

#### Correct rule

```text
Use XStudio SPs wherever a suitable SP exists.
If no suitable SP exists, preserve the table operation as a documented no-SP exception with live schema inspection, guards, real user audit values, and postflight.
```

Workflow is an area where schema and responsibility split can vary across XStudio builds. Do not delete original workflow field knowledge. Validate live columns and SP behavior before replacing table operations.

#### Tables / objects involved

| Object/table | Purpose |
|---|---|
| `XStudio_WorkFlow_Mst_Tbl` | Workflow/state-machine header where present |
| `XStudio_Approval_Mst_Tbl` | Approval/state-machine header in current XMES-style schema |
| `XStudio_Approval_Status_Mst_Tbl` | Individual states |
| `XStudio_Approval_Actor_Mst_Tbl` | Transition actors/buttons |
| `XStudio_WorkFlow_Action_Mst_Tbl` | Side-effect action definition |
| `XStudio_WorkFlow_Action_Assignment_Mst_Tbl` | Links transitions to side effects |
| `XStudio_Attribute_Mst_Tbl` | Approval/status attribute on the entity |
| `XStudio_Entities_Mst_Tbl` | Links entity to workflow through `WorkFlowId` and `WorkFlowStatusId` |
| data DB workflow SPs | Business side effects triggered by workflow |

#### Separation rule

Do not assume actor rows are the only place for stored-procedure side effects.

Correct model:

```text
workflow/status/actor/transition metadata
+
workflow action/action-assignment metadata
+
data DB stored procedure with validated signature
```

Use actor/approval rows for lifecycle and UI transitions. Use workflow action/action-assignment rows for side effects when the live schema supports them.

#### SP usage

Known workflow-related SP families exist, but their exact coverage must be verified before replacing metadata operations.

Potential SP-backed areas include:

```text
approval status save SPs
workflow business logic SP creation
workflow SP action execution
workflow action assignment
workflow node-position/configuration SPs
```

Before using a workflow SP:

```sql
SELECT
    p.name AS ProcedureName,
    prm.parameter_id,
    prm.name AS ParameterName,
    TYPE_NAME(prm.user_type_id) AS ParameterType,
    prm.max_length
FROM XStudio_Configuration.sys.procedures p
LEFT JOIN XStudio_Configuration.sys.parameters prm
  ON prm.object_id = p.object_id
WHERE p.name LIKE '%Workflow%'
   OR p.name LIKE '%WorkFlow%'
   OR p.name LIKE '%Approval%'
ORDER BY p.name, prm.parameter_id;
```

If no suitable SP covers a metadata operation, preserve the original table operation as a guarded no-SP exception.

#### Step 1 — Inspect live workflow tables

Always inspect first.

```sql
SELECT TABLE_NAME
FROM XStudio_Configuration_XMES.INFORMATION_SCHEMA.TABLES
WHERE TABLE_NAME LIKE '%Workflow%'
   OR TABLE_NAME LIKE '%WorkFlow%'
   OR TABLE_NAME LIKE '%Approval%'
ORDER BY TABLE_NAME;
```

Inspect columns before writing:

```sql
SELECT
    TABLE_NAME,
    COLUMN_NAME,
    DATA_TYPE,
    CHARACTER_MAXIMUM_LENGTH
FROM XStudio_Configuration_XMES.INFORMATION_SCHEMA.COLUMNS
WHERE TABLE_NAME IN
(
    'XStudio_Approval_Mst_Tbl',
    'XStudio_Approval_Status_Mst_Tbl',
    'XStudio_Approval_Actor_Mst_Tbl',
    'XStudio_WorkFlow_Mst_Tbl',
    'XStudio_WorkFlow_Action_Mst_Tbl',
    'XStudio_WorkFlow_Action_Assignment_Mst_Tbl'
)
ORDER BY TABLE_NAME, ORDINAL_POSITION;
```

#### Step 2 — Create approval/workflow header

If no suitable save-header SP is confirmed, preserve original operation as a no-SP exception.

Field contract:

| Field | Rule |
|---|---|
| `ID` | new GUID |
| `Name` | workflow/approval name, e.g. `WO Status` |
| `EntityID` / `ParentID` | owning entity, depending on schema |
| audit fields | real `@UserID`, not `sa` |
| `IsDeleted` | `0` |
| `IsSystem` | `0` |

Guard:

```sql
SELECT
    ID,
    Name,
    EntityID,
    IsDeleted
FROM XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_Approval_Mst_Tbl
WHERE EntityID = '<entity_guid>'
  AND Name = 'WO Status'
  AND IsDeleted = 0;
```

Only create if no active header exists.

#### Step 3 — Create status rows

Preserve status-row field knowledge.

Typical statuses:

```text
Draft
Released
In Progress
Completed
Closed
Cancelled
```

Field contract:

| Field | Rule |
|---|---|
| `ID` | new GUID |
| `Name` | status name |
| `Description` | status description |
| `ParentID` | approval/workflow header ID |
| `SrNo` / sequence | display/order |
| audit fields | real `@UserID` |
| `IsDeleted` | `0` |
| `IsSystem` | `0` |

Use save-status SP if the live SP text confirms one. Otherwise use guarded no-SP exception with `NOT EXISTS`.

#### Step 4 — Create transition actors

Each actor can represent one transition button.

Preserved actor/transition contract:

| Field | Meaning |
|---|---|
| `Name` | internal actor/transition name |
| `ActionName` | button/action label |
| `ParentID` | workflow/approval header |
| `SourceStatusID` | from-state |
| `DestinationStatusID` | to-state |
| `RuntimeCondition` | optional role/context condition |
| `StoreProcedure` | optional transition SP where live schema supports it |
| `IsNeedConfirmation` | require confirmation dialog |
| `ConfirmationMessage` | confirmation text |
| `SuccessMessage` | success text |
| `Priority` | ordering |

Do not put side-effect SPs into actor rows unless the live schema and runtime behavior prove this is how the current build executes them.

If workflow action/action-assignment tables are present, prefer the action-assignment layer for side effects.

#### Step 5 — Add approval/status attribute to entity

Create the approval/status attribute through:

```text
XStudio_AddAttribute_Usp
```

Do not directly insert into `XStudio_Attribute_Mst_Tbl` or manually `ALTER TABLE` when adding the status attribute.

Use the Approval datatype resolved from:

```sql
SELECT
    ID,
    Name,
    ActualDataType
FROM XStudio_Configuration.dbo.XStudio_DataType_Mst_Tbl
WHERE IsDeleted = 0
  AND (Name LIKE '%Approval%' OR Name LIKE '%Workflow%' OR Name LIKE '%Status%');
```

Conceptual SP call:

```sql
EXEC XStudio_Configuration.dbo.XStudio_AddAttribute_Usp
    @ID                 = '0',
    @EntityID           = '<entity_guid>',
    @Name               = 'Status',
    @DataTypeID         = '<approval_datatype_guid>',
    @IsRequired         = 0,
    @IsUnique           = 0,
    @IsSearchable       = 1,
    @IsCalculatedColumn = 0,
    @DisplayName        = 'Status',
    @SystemID           = '<system_guid>',
    @Description        = 'Workflow status';
```

#### Step 6 — Link workflow to entity

`XStudio_Entities_Mst_Tbl` commonly stores:

```text
WorkFlowId
WorkFlowStatusId
```

No generic entity-workflow-link save SP is confirmed here beyond entity save/update behavior. If `XStudio_SaveEntity_Usp` can safely set these fields in the local build, use it. Otherwise preserve the original update as a guarded no-SP exception.

Field contract:

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| `WorkFlowId` | approval/workflow header ID |
| `WorkFlowStatusId` | status attribute ID |
| `ModifiedBy` | real `@UserID` |
| `ModifiedOn` | `GETDATE()` |

Guard:

```sql
SELECT
    ID,
    Name,
    WorkFlowId,
    WorkFlowStatusId
FROM XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_Entities_Mst_Tbl
WHERE ID = '<entity_guid>'
  AND IsDeleted = 0;
```

#### Step 7 — Workflow actions and side effects

Use workflow action/action-assignment metadata where live schema supports it.

Preserve this distinction:

| Layer | Use for |
|---|---|
| Approval/status/actor rows | User-visible state lifecycle and transition buttons |
| Workflow action rows | SP/API/email/business side effects |
| Data DB stored procedure | Actual domain business logic |

Examples of business logic:

```text
work order release
WO requirement sync
SAP posting
sample evaluation
lot release
genealogy update
inventory movement
```

Validate the target data DB SP exists and has the expected signature before linking it.

```sql
SELECT
    OBJECT_ID('XStudio_XMES.dbo.<sp_name>') AS ObjectId;
```

#### XStudio-generated workflow SPs

When configured, XStudio may generate one SP in the data DB per transition:

```text
XStudio_XMES.dbo.XSTUDIO_WORKFLOW_{GUID}_SP
```

Do not rename or delete generated workflow SPs unless a deliberate regeneration plan exists.

#### Postflight

```sql
-- Approval/workflow header
SELECT *
FROM XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_Approval_Mst_Tbl
WHERE EntityID = '<entity_guid>'
  AND IsDeleted = 0;

-- Statuses
SELECT
    Name,
    SrNo
FROM XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_Approval_Status_Mst_Tbl
WHERE ParentID = '<approval_id>'
  AND IsDeleted = 0
ORDER BY SrNo;

-- Transitions
SELECT
    Name,
    ActionName,
    SourceStatusID,
    DestinationStatusID,
    StoreProcedure
FROM XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_Approval_Actor_Mst_Tbl
WHERE ParentID = '<approval_id>'
  AND IsDeleted = 0;

-- Entity workflow links
SELECT
    WorkFlowId,
    WorkFlowStatusId
FROM XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_Entities_Mst_Tbl
WHERE ID = '<entity_guid>'
  AND IsDeleted = 0;

-- Side-effect action assignments, if used
SELECT *
FROM XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_WorkFlow_Action_Assignment_Mst_Tbl
WHERE IsDeleted = 0;
```

#### Common mistakes

| Mistake | Effect |
|---|---|
| Putting all side effects in actor rows without schema proof | Transition may not execute business logic |
| Direct attribute insert for Status | Physical column/metadata drift |
| Missing entity workflow link | UI does not know workflow/status attribute |
| Missing status attribute | Workflow cannot persist current status |
| Using `sa` audit values | Unsafe and non-traceable |
| Not validating generated workflow SPs | Runtime transition fails |
| Confusing approval header with workflow action | Lifecycle and side effects become mixed |

#### See also

- [create_entity_module.md](create_entity_module.md)
- [configure_wo_execution.md](configure_wo_execution.md)
- [sp_catalog_complete.md](../sp_catalog_complete.md)
- [postflight_checks.sql](../03_SQL_TEMPLATES/postflight_checks.sql)

> End of imported document.
