## 33. Explore Database

**Source file:** `02_TASK_PLAYBOOKS/explore_database.md`  
**File type:** `.md`  
**Size:** `8477` bytes  
**Modified on:** `2026-04-29 13:10:54`  
**SHA256:** `21103656871c`  
**Encoding used:** `utf-8`

> Start of imported document.

### Database Exploration Workflow

Use this playbook when you need to understand what already exists in the config or data DB before writing SQL — for debugging, designing a new module, or checking the state of an existing one.

---

#### When to use this

- Before writing any INSERT: confirm object GUIDs, column names, existing rows
- When debugging: check what's actually there vs. what you think is there
- When designing a new module: survey existing entities, roles, pages to avoid duplicates
- When handed a vague requirement: map it to existing entities before configuring new ones

---

#### Phase 1 — Orient (run first, every session)

These give you the lay of the land in under 2 minutes.

##### 1a. List all entities
```sql
-- Run in: XStudio_Configuration_XMES
SELECT Name, DisplayName, TableType, IsChild, ParentEntityID
FROM XStudio_Entities_Mst_Tbl
WHERE IsDeleted = 0
ORDER BY TableType, Name;
```
**What you learn:** What modules exist, which are masters vs transactions, which are child entities.

##### 1b. List all pages
```sql
SELECT Name, IsActive, IsPopup
FROM XStudio_Page_Mst_Tbl
WHERE IsDeleted = 0
ORDER BY Name;
```
**What you learn:** Existing page names — copy exact spelling for CustomLink URLs.

##### 1c. List all menus (with page names)
```sql
SELECT m.Name as MenuName, p.Name as PageName, m.SubMenuOfID, m.Sn
FROM XStudio_Menu_Mst_Tbl m
JOIN XStudio_Page_Mst_Tbl p ON p.ID = m.PageID
WHERE m.IsDeleted = 0
ORDER BY m.Sn;
```
**What you learn:** Navigation structure and where new menu items should hang.

##### 1d. Confirm system/application IDs
```sql
SELECT ID, Name FROM XStudio_Application_Mst_Tbl WHERE IsDeleted = 0;
-- Expected: 29123B0B-3872-41CF-B5E1-67C4A8F0D4BC for XMES DEFAULT
```

---

#### Phase 2 — Drill into a specific entity

Once you know which entity you're working with, pull its full config.

##### 2a. Get entity ID
```sql
DECLARE @EntityName varchar(100) = 'XMES_Work_Order_Mst_Tbl';
SELECT ID, Name, DisplayName, TableType, IsChild, WorkFlowId
FROM XStudio_Entities_Mst_Tbl
WHERE Name = @EntityName AND IsDeleted = 0;
-- Copy the ID — you'll need it for every subsequent query
```

##### 2b. List attributes (columns)
```sql
SELECT a.Name, a.DisplayName, dt.Name as DataType, a.IsRequired, a.SrNo
FROM XStudio_Attribute_Mst_Tbl a
JOIN XStudio_Configuration.dbo.XStudio_DataType_Mst_Tbl dt ON dt.ID = a.DataType
WHERE a.ParentID = '<EntityID>' AND a.IsDeleted = 0
ORDER BY a.SrNo;
```
**What you learn:** Exact column names (for FK references, FilterCondition, DefaultValue expressions), data types, order.

##### 2c. List LVs and their columns
```sql
-- LVs for entity
SELECT ID, Name, FilterCondition, SPName
FROM XStudio_LV_Mst_Tbl
WHERE ParentID = '<EntityID>' AND IsDeleted = 0;

-- Columns for an LV
SELECT Name, Header, SerialNumber, Type, IsVisible, CustomLink, LinkValue, IsCustomField
FROM XStudio_LvColumns_Mst_Tbl
WHERE ParentID = '<LVID>' AND IsDeleted = 0
ORDER BY SerialNumber;
```

##### 2d. List edit forms and controls
```sql
-- Edit forms
SELECT ID, Name, AfterSavePageID, IsSaveAndContinueButtonVisible
FROM XStudio_Edit_Mst_Tbl
WHERE ParentID = '<EntityID>' AND IsDeleted = 0;

-- Controls for an edit form
SELECT ec.ControlName, ec.ControlType, ec.SerialNumber,
       a.Name as AttributeName,
       LEN(ec.ControlProperties) as PropLen,
       CASE WHEN ec.ControlProperties IS NULL THEN 'NULL - CRASH RISK' ELSE 'OK' END as PropStatus
FROM XStudio_EditControls_Mst_Tbl ec
LEFT JOIN XStudio_Attribute_Mst_Tbl a ON a.ID = ec.AttributeID
WHERE ec.ParentID = '<EditID>' AND ec.IsDeleted = 0
ORDER BY ec.SerialNumber;
```
**What you learn:** Which controls exist, whether ControlProperties is populated (NULL = crash), control naming.

##### 2e. Dropdown attribute properties
```sql
SELECT a.Name as AttributeName, p.Name as PropKey, p.Value as PropValue
FROM XStudio_Attribute_Mst_Tbl a
JOIN XStudio_AttributeProperties_Mst_Tbl p ON p.ParentID = a.ID AND p.IsDeleted = 0
WHERE a.ParentID = '<EntityID>' AND a.IsDeleted = 0
ORDER BY a.Name, p.Name;
-- Must see 6 rows per dropdown attribute
```

##### 2f. Entity relations (FK wiring)
```sql
SELECT er.RelationName, er.Cardinality,
       e2.Name as RelatedEntity, a2.Name as RelatedAttribute
FROM XStudio_EntityRelations_Mst_Tbl er
JOIN XStudio_Entities_Mst_Tbl e2 ON e2.ID = er.RelationEntityID
JOIN XStudio_Attribute_Mst_Tbl a2 ON a2.ID = er.RelationAttributeID
WHERE er.EntityID = '<EntityID>' AND er.IsDeleted = 0;
```

---

#### Phase 3 — Inspect physical tables (data DB)

Use these to see what data actually looks like, and to verify the config matches reality.

##### 3a. List all physical tables for XMES entities
```sql
-- Run in: XStudio_XMES
SELECT TABLE_NAME
FROM INFORMATION_SCHEMA.TABLES
WHERE TABLE_TYPE = 'BASE TABLE'
  AND TABLE_NAME NOT LIKE 'XStudio_%'   -- exclude platform tables
ORDER BY TABLE_NAME;
```

##### 3b. Physical columns of a table
```sql
SELECT COLUMN_NAME, DATA_TYPE, CHARACTER_MAXIMUM_LENGTH, IS_NULLABLE
FROM XStudio_XMES.INFORMATION_SCHEMA.COLUMNS
WHERE TABLE_NAME = 'XMES_Work_Order_Mst_Tbl'
ORDER BY ORDINAL_POSITION;
```
**Critical use:** Before writing any INSERT, verify physical column names match the config attribute Names. Discrepancy = broken SP or silent data loss.

##### 3c. Check generated LV views
```sql
SELECT TABLE_NAME, TABLE_TYPE
FROM XStudio_XMES.INFORMATION_SCHEMA.TABLES
WHERE TABLE_NAME LIKE 'XStudio_List_%'
ORDER BY TABLE_NAME;

-- Inspect a view definition
SELECT VIEW_DEFINITION
FROM XStudio_XMES.INFORMATION_SCHEMA.VIEWS
WHERE TABLE_NAME = 'XStudio_List_XMES_Work_Order_Mst_Tbl_Vw';
```

##### 3d. Sample data (verify FK values exist)
```sql
-- Before wiring a dropdown, confirm the target table has data
SELECT TOP 10 ID, Name FROM XStudio_XMES.dbo.XMES_Material_Mst_Tbl
WHERE IsDeleted = 0 ORDER BY Name;
```

---

#### Phase 4 — Confirm column names before any INSERT

**This is non-negotiable.** Run before first INSERT of any table in a session.

```bash
sqlcmd -S "10.2.6.204" -U sa -P '$$Mit@1234' -d XStudio_Configuration_XMES -Q \
"SELECT COLUMN_NAME, ORDINAL_POSITION FROM INFORMATION_SCHEMA.COLUMNS WHERE TABLE_NAME='XStudio_LvColumns_Mst_Tbl' ORDER BY ORDINAL_POSITION"
```

**Confirmed gotchas** (still check — these can change between platform versions):

| Table | Trap | Correct |
|---|---|---|
| `XStudio_LvColumns_Mst_Tbl` | `SrNo` | `SerialNumber` |
| `XStudio_LvColumns_Mst_Tbl` | `DataType` | `Type` |
| `XStudio_PageControls_Mst_Tbl` | `PageID` | `ParentID` |
| `XStudio_PageControls_Mst_Tbl` | `ControlIDRef` | `ControlID` |
| `XStudio_Tab_Mst_Tbl` | `SerialNumber` | `SrNo` |
| `XStudio_EditControls_Mst_Tbl` | `ControlLabel` | not a column — it's inside `ControlProperties` JSON |

---

#### Phase 5 — Lookup reference values

##### Get all role GUIDs
```sql
SELECT ID, Name, Description
FROM XStudio_Configuration.dbo.XStudio_Role_Mst_Tbl
WHERE IsDeleted = 0 ORDER BY Name;
```

##### Get all DataType GUIDs
```sql
SELECT ID, Name
FROM XStudio_Configuration.dbo.XStudio_DataType_Mst_Tbl
ORDER BY Name;
```

##### Get a specific page/entity/LV ID by name
```sql
-- Page
SELECT ID, Name FROM XStudio_Page_Mst_Tbl WHERE Name LIKE '%WO_Execution%' AND IsDeleted = 0;

-- Entity
SELECT ID, Name FROM XStudio_Entities_Mst_Tbl WHERE Name LIKE '%WorkOrder%' AND IsDeleted = 0;

-- LV
SELECT ID, Name, ParentID FROM XStudio_LV_Mst_Tbl WHERE Name LIKE '%WorkOrder%' AND IsDeleted = 0;
```

---

#### Phase 6 — Copy an existing working row (before inserting new)

Before inserting a new link column, edit control, or page control, **always fetch an existing working row first**:

```sql
-- Copy shape of a working link column
SELECT * FROM XStudio_LvColumns_Mst_Tbl
WHERE Type = 'link' AND IsDeleted = 0 AND IsCustomField = 1
ORDER BY CreatedOn DESC;
-- Look at CustomLink pattern, ColumnProperties JSON, field values

-- Copy shape of a working selectbox control
SELECT * FROM XStudio_EditControls_Mst_Tbl
WHERE ControlType = 'selectbox' AND IsDeleted = 0
ORDER BY CreatedOn DESC;
-- Copy ControlProperties JSON, replace entity-specific GUIDs
```

---

#### Exploration order summary

```
Phase 1 (orient)  →  Phase 2 (drill entity)  →  Phase 3 (physical table)
       ↓
Phase 4 (confirm columns for every new table)
       ↓
Phase 5 (resolve reference GUIDs)
       ↓
Phase 6 (copy existing row shape)
       ↓
Write INSERT
```

**Stop and read before you write.** One INFORMATION_SCHEMA query takes 5 seconds and prevents a column-name error that takes 20 minutes to debug.

> End of imported document.

## 34. Map Asset Structure Tags

**Source file:** `02_TASK_PLAYBOOKS/map_asset_structure_tags.md`  
**File type:** `.md`  
**Size:** `1755` bytes  
**Modified on:** `2026-05-04 17:49:24`  
**SHA256:** `8f3e9eb9a9d9`  
**Encoding used:** `utf-8`

> Start of imported document.

### Playbook — Map Asset Structure Tags

#### Goal

Map logical template attributes to real historian/source tags for each equipment instance.

Example:

```text
EAF-1 + TapTemperature → HIST.SMS.EAF1.TAP_TEMP
EAF-1 + PowerKWh → HIST.SMS.EAF1.POWER_KWH
```

#### Pre-requisites

```text
Equipment type exists
Template exists
Template details exist
Equipment instances exist
Mapping rows have been auto-created
Datasource exists
Tags exist in XStudio_Tag_Mst_Tbl or source tag list
```

#### Mapping table

Pattern:

```text
<EquipmentType>_Tag_Mapping_Tbl
```

Important fields:

```text
EquipmentID
Attribute
DataSourceID
TagName
InstrumentTag
HHRange
HRange
LRange
LLRange
Type
IsXBatchTag
```

#### Preferred update path

Use:

```text
XStudio_AssetStructure_Update_Equipment_And_Attribute_Mapping_Usp
```

or the API:

```text
POST /api/Rest/{systemId}/AssetStructure/Equipment/SaveMapping/{euiqmentType}/{mappingType}
```

#### Validation

Use validation endpoints where available:

```text
POST /api/Rest/{systemId}/AssetStructure/Equipment/Tags/Validate/{datasourceId}
POST /api/Rest/{systemId}/AssetStructure/Equipment/Tags/ValidateTagMapping
```

#### Tag naming convention

Because `XStudio_Equipment_Tag_Mst_Vw` classifies tags by name pattern, use names intentionally:

```text
IsStartHeat      → Command / Write
IsRunHeat        → Status / Read
IsDoneHeat       → Status / Read
IsInterlockReady → Status / Read
ErrorCode        → Interlock / Read
ChargeWeight     → Weight / Read
TapTemperature   → Parameter
```

#### Postflight

```sql
SELECT EquipmentName, Attribute, DataSourceID, TagName, TagType, FunctionType
FROM XStudio_XMES.dbo.XStudio_Equipment_Tag_Mst_Vw
WHERE EquipmentName LIKE 'EAF%'
ORDER BY EquipmentName, Attribute;
```

> End of imported document.

## Chapter 4: Sql Templates

**Folder:** `03_SQL_TEMPLATES`  
**Included file count:** `7`

## 35. Dropdown Properties Template

**Source file:** `03_SQL_TEMPLATES/dropdown_properties_template.sql`  
**File type:** `.sql`  
**Size:** `40633` bytes  
**Modified on:** `2026-05-12 11:12:12`  
**SHA256:** `0215fc9eda3d`

> Start of imported document.

```sql
-- ============================================================
-- Dropdown Attribute Properties Template
-- Complete dropdown metadata + entity relation for one attribute
-- ============================================================
--
-- Purpose:
--   Complete a dropdown attribute after the base attribute has been
--   created by entity_create_template.sql / XStudio_AddAttribute_Usp.
--
-- This template covers work intentionally removed from
-- entity_create_template.sql:
--   - dropdown advanced properties
--   - dropdown entity relation metadata
--   - dropdown postflight validation
--
-- Hard rule:
--   Use the XStudio dropdown SP first.
--
-- Primary SP:
--   XStudio_Configuration.dbo.XStudio_Add_DropDown_Attribute_Properties_And_Relation_Usp
--
-- Important finding from the 2026-05-08 SP text:
--   The current SP has a 12-parameter signature and writes:
--     DropdownDatabase
--     DropdownEntity
--     DropdownEntryMode
--     DropdownTextField
--     DropdownValueField
--     XStudio_EntityRelations_Mst_Tbl row
--
--   It does not write TextCharacterLength.
--
-- Therefore this template:
--   1. resolves live configuration/data DBs;
--   2. validates the live SP signature and table columns;
--   3. calls the dropdown SP;
--   4. postflights the full six-property dropdown contract;
--   5. uses a documented fallback only if the SP leaves the contract incomplete.
--
-- Required dropdown property contract:
--   1. DropdownDatabase
--   2. DropdownEntity
--   3. DropdownEntryMode
--   4. DropdownTextField
--   5. DropdownValueField
--   6. TextCharacterLength
--
-- Do not use this template to create the base attribute.
-- Create the base attribute first with XStudio_AddAttribute_Usp.
--
-- ============================================================

SET NOCOUNT ON;
SET XACT_ABORT ON;

BEGIN TRY
    BEGIN TRANSACTION;

    -- ============================================================
    -- 0. INPUTS
    -- ============================================================

    DECLARE @SystemID varchar(36) = '6C8C0070-60B5-4474-BEC8-5C02197B32E6';

    -- Owning entity/table where the dropdown attribute lives.
    DECLARE @MainEntityID varchar(36) = '<owning_entity_guid>';
    DECLARE @MainTableName varchar(200) = '<OwningEntityTableName>';       -- e.g. XMES_WorkOrder_Mst_Tbl
    DECLARE @MainAttributeName varchar(200) = '<DropdownAttributeName>';   -- e.g. MaterialID

    -- Target dropdown entity/table.
    DECLARE @RelationTable varchar(200) = '<TargetEntityTableName>';       -- e.g. XMES_Material_Mst_Tbl
    DECLARE @RelationAttributeName varchar(200) = 'ID';                    -- usually ID
    DECLARE @RelationDisplayColumnName varchar(200) = 'Name';              -- usually Name

    -- Override only when the target entity metadata lives outside the tenant config DB.
    -- Examples:
    --   tenant entity target: NULL -> resolved ConfigDB
    --   global config target: 'XStudio_Configuration'
    DECLARE @RelationMetadataDB sysname = NULL;

    -- Override only when the target data table lives outside the tenant data DB.
    -- Examples:
    --   tenant data target: NULL -> resolved DataDB
    --   global config target: 'XStudio_Configuration'
    DECLARE @RelationDataDB sysname = NULL;

    DECLARE @DropdownEntryMode varchar(200) = 'single';
    DECLARE @TextCharacterLength varchar(50) = '36';

    -- Optional display text override. If NULL/blank, template builds:
    --   [@RelationTable].[@RelationDisplayColumnName]
    DECLARE @DropdownTextField varchar(200) = NULL;

    -- Optional cross-system relation override. If NULL, @SystemID is used.
    DECLARE @DropdownRelationSystemID varchar(36) = NULL;

    -- Required only for documented fallback audit columns when those columns exist.
    -- Use a real XStudio user ID; do not use 'sa'.
    DECLARE @UserID varchar(36) = '<deployment_user_guid>';
    DECLARE @IPAddress varchar(50) = 'T-SQL';

    -- Strict mode:
    --   0 = call SP and fail if the six-property contract is incomplete.
    --   1 = call SP, then use documented fallback to complete/prove the contract.
    DECLARE @AllowDocumentedFallback bit = 1;

    -- ============================================================
    -- 1. RESOLVE LIVE DATABASES
    -- ============================================================

    DECLARE @ConfigDB sysname;
    DECLARE @DataDB sysname;
    DECLARE @XStudioMetadataDB sysname;
    DECLARE @DropdownDataDB sysname;

    SELECT
        @ConfigDB = ConfigurationDatabase,
        @DataDB = DataDatabase
    FROM XStudio_Configuration.dbo.XStudio_GetDataSourceInfoFromSystemId_Fun(@SystemID);

    IF @ConfigDB IS NULL OR @DataDB IS NULL
        THROW 54000, 'Could not resolve ConfigDB/DataDB from @SystemID.', 1;

    SET @XStudioMetadataDB = COALESCE(@RelationMetadataDB, @ConfigDB);
    SET @DropdownDataDB = COALESCE(@RelationDataDB, @DataDB);
    SET @DropdownRelationSystemID = COALESCE(@DropdownRelationSystemID, @SystemID);

    IF @DropdownTextField IS NULL OR LTRIM(RTRIM(@DropdownTextField)) = ''
        SET @DropdownTextField = '[' + @RelationTable + '].[' + @RelationDisplayColumnName + ']';

    IF DB_ID(@ConfigDB) IS NULL
        THROW 54001, 'Resolved tenant configuration database does not exist.', 1;

    IF DB_ID(@DataDB) IS NULL
        THROW 54002, 'Resolved tenant data database does not exist.', 1;

    IF DB_ID(@XStudioMetadataDB) IS NULL
        THROW 54003, 'Resolved relation metadata database does not exist.', 1;

    IF DB_ID(@DropdownDataDB) IS NULL
        THROW 54004, 'Resolved relation data database does not exist.', 1;

    -- ============================================================
    -- 2. INPUT VALIDATION
    -- ============================================================

    IF TRY_CONVERT(uniqueidentifier, @SystemID) IS NULL
        THROW 54005, 'Invalid @SystemID.', 1;

    IF TRY_CONVERT(uniqueidentifier, @MainEntityID) IS NULL
        THROW 54006, 'Invalid @MainEntityID.', 1;

    IF TRY_CONVERT(uniqueidentifier, @DropdownRelationSystemID) IS NULL
        THROW 54007, 'Invalid @DropdownRelationSystemID.', 1;

    IF @UserID IS NULL OR @UserID = '<deployment_user_guid>' OR TRY_CONVERT(uniqueidentifier, @UserID) IS NULL
        THROW 54008, 'Set @UserID to a real XStudio user GUID before running this template.', 1;

    IF @MainTableName IS NULL OR @MainTableName LIKE '%[^A-Za-z0-9_]%'
        THROW 54009, '@MainTableName must be a simple table name.', 1;

    IF @MainAttributeName IS NULL OR @MainAttributeName LIKE '%[^A-Za-z0-9_]%'
        THROW 54010, '@MainAttributeName must be a simple attribute name.', 1;

    IF @RelationTable IS NULL OR @RelationTable LIKE '%[^A-Za-z0-9_]%'
        THROW 54011, '@RelationTable must be a simple table name.', 1;

    IF @RelationAttributeName IS NULL OR @RelationAttributeName LIKE '%[^A-Za-z0-9_]%'
        THROW 54012, '@RelationAttributeName must be a simple attribute name.', 1;

    IF @RelationDisplayColumnName IS NULL OR @RelationDisplayColumnName LIKE '%[^A-Za-z0-9_]%'
        THROW 54013, '@RelationDisplayColumnName must be a simple column name.', 1;

    IF @DropdownEntryMode NOT IN ('single', 'multi')
        THROW 54014, '@DropdownEntryMode must be single or multi.', 1;

    -- ============================================================
    -- 3. LIVE SP SIGNATURE PREFLIGHT
    -- ============================================================

    IF OBJECT_ID('XStudio_Configuration.dbo.XStudio_Add_DropDown_Attribute_Properties_And_Relation_Usp', 'P') IS NULL
        THROW 54015, 'XStudio_Add_DropDown_Attribute_Properties_And_Relation_Usp is missing.', 1;

    DECLARE @DropdownSpParamCount int;

    SELECT @DropdownSpParamCount = COUNT(1)
    FROM XStudio_Configuration.sys.parameters p
    WHERE p.object_id = OBJECT_ID('XStudio_Configuration.dbo.XStudio_Add_DropDown_Attribute_Properties_And_Relation_Usp');

    IF @DropdownSpParamCount NOT IN (9, 12)
        THROW 54016, 'Unexpected XStudio_Add_DropDown_Attribute_Properties_And_Relation_Usp parameter count. Expected 9 or 12.', 1;

    IF
    (
        SELECT COUNT(1)
        FROM XStudio_Configuration.sys.parameters p
        WHERE p.object_id = OBJECT_ID('XStudio_Configuration.dbo.XStudio_Add_DropDown_Attribute_Properties_And_Relation_Usp')
          AND p.name IN
          (
              '@SYSTEMID',
              '@XSTUDIO_DATABASE_NAME',
              '@CONFIGURATION_DATABASE_NAME',
              '@DATA_DATABASE_NAME',
              '@MAIN_TABLE_ENTITYID',
              '@MAIN_TABLE_NAME',
              '@MAIN_ATTRIBUTE_NAME',
              '@RELATION_TABLE',
              '@RELATION_ATTRIBUTE_NAME'
          )
    ) <> 9
        THROW 54017, 'Dropdown SP mandatory parameter names do not match expected names.', 1;

    -- ============================================================
    -- 4. LIVE TABLE/COLUMN PREFLIGHT
    -- ============================================================

    DECLARE @SQL nvarchar(max);

    SET @SQL = N'
        IF NOT EXISTS
        (
            SELECT 1
            FROM ' + QUOTENAME(@ConfigDB) + N'.sys.tables t
            JOIN ' + QUOTENAME(@ConfigDB) + N'.sys.schemas s
              ON s.schema_id = t.schema_id
            WHERE s.name = ''dbo''
              AND t.name = ''XStudio_Attribute_Mst_Tbl''
        )
            THROW 54018, ''XStudio_Attribute_Mst_Tbl missing in ConfigDB.'', 1;

        IF NOT EXISTS
        (
            SELECT 1
            FROM ' + QUOTENAME(@ConfigDB) + N'.sys.tables t
            JOIN ' + QUOTENAME(@ConfigDB) + N'.sys.schemas s
              ON s.schema_id = t.schema_id
            WHERE s.name = ''dbo''
              AND t.name = ''XStudio_AttributeProperties_Mst_Tbl''
        )
            THROW 54019, ''XStudio_AttributeProperties_Mst_Tbl missing in ConfigDB.'', 1;

        IF NOT EXISTS
        (
            SELECT 1
            FROM ' + QUOTENAME(@ConfigDB) + N'.sys.tables t
            JOIN ' + QUOTENAME(@ConfigDB) + N'.sys.schemas s
              ON s.schema_id = t.schema_id
            WHERE s.name = ''dbo''
              AND t.name = ''XStudio_EntityRelations_Mst_Tbl''
        )
            THROW 54020, ''XStudio_EntityRelations_Mst_Tbl missing in ConfigDB.'', 1;

        IF
        (
            SELECT COUNT(1)
            FROM ' + QUOTENAME(@ConfigDB) + N'.sys.columns c
            JOIN ' + QUOTENAME(@ConfigDB) + N'.sys.tables t
              ON t.object_id = c.object_id
            JOIN ' + QUOTENAME(@ConfigDB) + N'.sys.schemas s
              ON s.schema_id = t.schema_id
            WHERE s.name = ''dbo''
              AND t.name = ''XStudio_AttributeProperties_Mst_Tbl''
              AND c.name IN (''Name'', ''Value'', ''ParentID'', ''IsDeleted'')
        ) < 4
            THROW 54021, ''AttributeProperties core columns missing or renamed.'', 1;

        IF
        (
            SELECT COUNT(1)
            FROM ' + QUOTENAME(@ConfigDB) + N'.sys.columns c
            JOIN ' + QUOTENAME(@ConfigDB) + N'.sys.tables t
              ON t.object_id = c.object_id
            JOIN ' + QUOTENAME(@ConfigDB) + N'.sys.schemas s
              ON s.schema_id = t.schema_id
            WHERE s.name = ''dbo''
              AND t.name = ''XStudio_EntityRelations_Mst_Tbl''
              AND c.name IN
              (
                  ''EntityID'',
                  ''AttributeID'',
                  ''Cardinality'',
                  ''RelationEntityID'',
                  ''RelationAttributeID'',
                  ''RelationName'',
                  ''RelationEntity'',
                  ''RelationAttribute'',
                  ''IsDeleted''
              )
        ) < 9
            THROW 54022, ''EntityRelations core columns missing or renamed.'', 1;';

    EXEC sys.sp_executesql @SQL;

    -- ============================================================
    -- 5. RESOLVE ATTRIBUTE AND TARGET METADATA
    -- ============================================================

    DECLARE @AttrID varchar(36);
    DECLARE @TargetEntityID varchar(36);
    DECLARE @TargetIDAttrID varchar(36);
    DECLARE @OwningEntityName varchar(200);

    SET @SQL = N'
        SELECT
            @AttrID = CAST(a.ID AS varchar(36)),
            @OwningEntityName = e.Name
        FROM ' + QUOTENAME(@ConfigDB) + N'.dbo.XStudio_Attribute_Mst_Tbl a WITH (NOLOCK)
        JOIN ' + QUOTENAME(@ConfigDB) + N'.dbo.XStudio_Entities_Mst_Tbl e WITH (NOLOCK)
          ON e.ID = a.ParentID
         AND e.IsDeleted = 0
        WHERE a.ParentID = @MainEntityID
          AND a.Name = @MainAttributeName
          AND a.IsDeleted = 0;

        SELECT @TargetEntityID = CAST(e.ID AS varchar(36))
        FROM ' + QUOTENAME(@XStudioMetadataDB) + N'.dbo.XStudio_Entities_Mst_Tbl e WITH (NOLOCK)
        WHERE e.Name = @RelationTable
          AND e.IsDeleted = 0;

        SELECT @TargetIDAttrID = CAST(a.ID AS varchar(36))
        FROM ' + QUOTENAME(@XStudioMetadataDB) + N'.dbo.XStudio_Attribute_Mst_Tbl a WITH (NOLOCK)
        WHERE a.ParentID = TRY_CONVERT(uniqueidentifier, @TargetEntityID)
          AND a.Name = @RelationAttributeName
          AND a.IsDeleted = 0;';

    EXEC sys.sp_executesql
        @SQL,
        N'@MainEntityID varchar(36),
          @MainAttributeName varchar(200),
          @RelationTable varchar(200),
          @RelationAttributeName varchar(200),
          @AttrID varchar(36) OUTPUT,
          @OwningEntityName varchar(200) OUTPUT,
          @TargetEntityID varchar(36) OUTPUT,
          @TargetIDAttrID varchar(36) OUTPUT',
        @MainEntityID = @MainEntityID,
        @MainAttributeName = @MainAttributeName,
        @RelationTable = @RelationTable,
        @RelationAttributeName = @RelationAttributeName,
        @AttrID = @AttrID OUTPUT,
        @OwningEntityName = @OwningEntityName OUTPUT,
        @TargetEntityID = @TargetEntityID OUTPUT,
        @TargetIDAttrID = @TargetIDAttrID OUTPUT;

    IF @AttrID IS NULL
        THROW 54023, 'Dropdown attribute not found under @MainEntityID. Create it first with XStudio_AddAttribute_Usp.', 1;

    IF @TargetEntityID IS NULL
        THROW 54024, 'Target relation entity not found in @XStudioMetadataDB.', 1;

    IF @TargetIDAttrID IS NULL
        THROW 54025, 'Target relation attribute not found under target entity.', 1;

    SET @SQL = N'
        IF OBJECT_ID(QUOTENAME(@DropdownDataDB) + ''.dbo.'' + QUOTENAME(@RelationTable), ''U'') IS NULL
            THROW 54026, ''Target relation data table not found in @DropdownDataDB.'', 1;

        IF COL_LENGTH(QUOTENAME(@DropdownDataDB) + ''.dbo.'' + QUOTENAME(@RelationTable), @RelationDisplayColumnName) IS NULL
            THROW 54027, ''Target display column not found in target relation data table.'', 1;';

    EXEC sys.sp_executesql
        @SQL,
        N'@DropdownDataDB sysname, @RelationTable varchar(200), @RelationDisplayColumnName varchar(200)',
        @DropdownDataDB = @DropdownDataDB,
        @RelationTable = @RelationTable,
        @RelationDisplayColumnName = @RelationDisplayColumnName;

    -- ============================================================
    -- 6. CALL PLATFORM DROPDOWN SP
    -- ============================================================

    IF @DropdownSpParamCount = 12
    BEGIN
        EXEC XStudio_Configuration.dbo.XStudio_Add_DropDown_Attribute_Properties_And_Relation_Usp
            @SYSTEMID = @SystemID,
            @XSTUDIO_DATABASE_NAME = @XStudioMetadataDB,
            @CONFIGURATION_DATABASE_NAME = @ConfigDB,
            @DATA_DATABASE_NAME = @DropdownDataDB,
            @MAIN_TABLE_ENTITYID = @MainEntityID,
            @MAIN_TABLE_NAME = @MainTableName,
            @MAIN_ATTRIBUTE_NAME = @MainAttributeName,
            @RELATION_TABLE = @RelationTable,
            @RELATION_ATTRIBUTE_NAME = @RelationAttributeName,
            @DROPDOWN_ENTRY_MODE = @DropdownEntryMode,
            @DROPDOWN_TEXT_FIELD = @DropdownTextField,
            @DROPDOWN_RELATION_SYSTEMID = @DropdownRelationSystemID;
    END
    ELSE
    BEGIN
        -- Legacy 9-parameter version. It is retained for old environments.
        -- Postflight below still enforces the complete six-property contract.
        EXEC XStudio_Configuration.dbo.XStudio_Add_DropDown_Attribute_Properties_And_Relation_Usp
            @SYSTEMID = @SystemID,
            @XSTUDIO_DATABASE_NAME = @XStudioMetadataDB,
            @CONFIGURATION_DATABASE_NAME = @ConfigDB,
            @DATA_DATABASE_NAME = @DropdownDataDB,
            @MAIN_TABLE_ENTITYID = @MainEntityID,
            @MAIN_TABLE_NAME = @MainTableName,
            @MAIN_ATTRIBUTE_NAME = @MainAttributeName,
            @RELATION_TABLE = @RelationTable,
            @RELATION_ATTRIBUTE_NAME = @RelationAttributeName;
    END;

    -- ============================================================
    -- 7. EXPECTED CONTRACT
    -- ============================================================

    IF OBJECT_ID('tempdb..#ExpectedDropdownProperty') IS NOT NULL
        DROP TABLE #ExpectedDropdownProperty;

    CREATE TABLE #ExpectedDropdownProperty
    (
        Name varchar(200) NOT NULL PRIMARY KEY,
        Value varchar(max) NOT NULL
    );

    INSERT INTO #ExpectedDropdownProperty (Name, Value)
    VALUES
        ('DropdownDatabase', @DropdownDataDB),
        ('DropdownEntity', @TargetEntityID),
        ('DropdownEntryMode', @DropdownEntryMode),
        ('DropdownTextField', @DropdownTextField),
        ('DropdownValueField', @TargetIDAttrID),
        ('TextCharacterLength', @TextCharacterLength);

    DECLARE @MissingOrWrongPropertyCount int;

    SET @SQL = N'
        SELECT @MissingOrWrongPropertyCount = COUNT(1)
        FROM #ExpectedDropdownProperty expected
        WHERE NOT EXISTS
        (
            SELECT 1
            FROM ' + QUOTENAME(@ConfigDB) + N'.dbo.XStudio_AttributeProperties_Mst_Tbl actual WITH (NOLOCK)
            WHERE actual.ParentID = @AttrID
              AND actual.Name = expected.Name
              AND actual.Value = expected.Value
              AND actual.IsDeleted = 0
        );';

    EXEC sys.sp_executesql
        @SQL,
        N'@AttrID varchar(36), @MissingOrWrongPropertyCount int OUTPUT',
        @AttrID = @AttrID,
        @MissingOrWrongPropertyCount = @MissingOrWrongPropertyCount OUTPUT;

    IF @MissingOrWrongPropertyCount > 0 AND @AllowDocumentedFallback = 0
        THROW 54028, 'Dropdown SP completed but six-property contract is incomplete. Set @AllowDocumentedFallback = 1 only if documented fallback is allowed.', 1;

    -- ============================================================
    -- 8. DOCUMENTED FALLBACK FOR INCOMPLETE SP OUTPUT
    -- ============================================================
    -- This fallback is intentionally narrow:
    --   - it runs only after the XStudio dropdown SP;
    --   - it writes only AttributeProperties and EntityRelations;
    --   - it uses live schema inspection for optional audit columns;
    --   - it is needed because the SP does not write TextCharacterLength
    --     and old builds may leave partial/stale dropdown properties.
    --
    -- If XStudio later provides a complete dropdown save SP, remove this
    -- fallback and call that SP instead.

    IF @MissingOrWrongPropertyCount > 0 AND @AllowDocumentedFallback = 1
    BEGIN
        DECLARE
            @APHasID bit = 0,
            @APHasCreatedBy bit = 0,
            @APHasModifiedBy bit = 0,
            @APHasCreatedOn bit = 0,
            @APHasModifiedOn bit = 0,
            @APHasIsDeleted bit = 0,
            @APHasIsSystem bit = 0,
            @APHasSource bit = 0,
            @APHasHostAddress bit = 0,
            @APIDIsIdentity bit = 0;

        SET @SQL = N'
            SELECT
                @APHasID = MAX(CASE WHEN c.name = ''ID'' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END),
                @APHasCreatedBy = MAX(CASE WHEN c.name = ''CreatedBy'' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END),
                @APHasModifiedBy = MAX(CASE WHEN c.name = ''ModifiedBy'' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END),
                @APHasCreatedOn = MAX(CASE WHEN c.name = ''CreatedOn'' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END),
                @APHasModifiedOn = MAX(CASE WHEN c.name = ''ModifiedOn'' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END),
                @APHasIsDeleted = MAX(CASE WHEN c.name = ''IsDeleted'' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END),
                @APHasIsSystem = MAX(CASE WHEN c.name = ''IsSystem'' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END),
                @APHasSource = MAX(CASE WHEN c.name = ''Source'' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END),
                @APHasHostAddress = MAX(CASE WHEN c.name = ''HostAddress'' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END),
                @APIDIsIdentity = MAX(CASE WHEN c.name = ''ID'' AND c.is_identity = 1 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END)
            FROM ' + QUOTENAME(@ConfigDB) + N'.sys.columns c
            JOIN ' + QUOTENAME(@ConfigDB) + N'.sys.tables t
              ON t.object_id = c.object_id
            JOIN ' + QUOTENAME(@ConfigDB) + N'.sys.schemas s
              ON s.schema_id = t.schema_id
            WHERE s.name = ''dbo''
              AND t.name = ''XStudio_AttributeProperties_Mst_Tbl'';';

        EXEC sys.sp_executesql
            @SQL,
            N'@APHasID bit OUTPUT,
              @APHasCreatedBy bit OUTPUT,
              @APHasModifiedBy bit OUTPUT,
              @APHasCreatedOn bit OUTPUT,
              @APHasModifiedOn bit OUTPUT,
              @APHasIsDeleted bit OUTPUT,
              @APHasIsSystem bit OUTPUT,
              @APHasSource bit OUTPUT,
              @APHasHostAddress bit OUTPUT,
              @APIDIsIdentity bit OUTPUT',
            @APHasID = @APHasID OUTPUT,
            @APHasCreatedBy = @APHasCreatedBy OUTPUT,
            @APHasModifiedBy = @APHasModifiedBy OUTPUT,
            @APHasCreatedOn = @APHasCreatedOn OUTPUT,
            @APHasModifiedOn = @APHasModifiedOn OUTPUT,
            @APHasIsDeleted = @APHasIsDeleted OUTPUT,
            @APHasIsSystem = @APHasIsSystem OUTPUT,
            @APHasSource = @APHasSource OUTPUT,
            @APHasHostAddress = @APHasHostAddress OUTPUT,
            @APIDIsIdentity = @APIDIsIdentity OUTPUT;

        DECLARE @APUpdateSet nvarchar(max) = N'';
        IF @APHasModifiedBy = 1 SET @APUpdateSet += N', ModifiedBy = @UserID';
        IF @APHasModifiedOn = 1 SET @APUpdateSet += N', ModifiedOn = GETDATE()';
        IF @APHasSource = 1 SET @APUpdateSet += N', Source = ''System''';
        IF @APHasHostAddress = 1 SET @APUpdateSet += N', HostAddress = @IPAddress';

        SET @SQL = N'
            UPDATE ap
            SET IsDeleted = 1' + @APUpdateSet + N'
            FROM ' + QUOTENAME(@ConfigDB) + N'.dbo.XStudio_AttributeProperties_Mst_Tbl ap
            JOIN #ExpectedDropdownProperty expected
              ON expected.Name = ap.Name
            WHERE ap.ParentID = @AttrID
              AND ap.IsDeleted = 0;';

        EXEC sys.sp_executesql
            @SQL,
            N'@AttrID varchar(36), @UserID varchar(36), @IPAddress varchar(50)',
            @AttrID = @AttrID,
            @UserID = @UserID,
            @IPAddress = @IPAddress;

        DECLARE @APInsertColumns nvarchar(max) = N'';
        DECLARE @APSelectColumns nvarchar(max) = N'';

        IF @APHasID = 1 AND @APIDIsIdentity = 0
        BEGIN
            SET @APInsertColumns += N'[ID],';
            SET @APSelectColumns += N'NEWID(),';
        END;

        SET @APInsertColumns += N'[Name],[Value],[ParentID]';
        SET @APSelectColumns += N'expected.Name, expected.Value, @AttrID';

        IF @APHasCreatedBy = 1
        BEGIN
            SET @APInsertColumns += N',[CreatedBy]';
            SET @APSelectColumns += N',@UserID';
        END;

        IF @APHasModifiedBy = 1
        BEGIN
            SET @APInsertColumns += N',[ModifiedBy]';
            SET @APSelectColumns += N',@UserID';
        END;

        IF @APHasCreatedOn = 1
        BEGIN
            SET @APInsertColumns += N',[CreatedOn]';
            SET @APSelectColumns += N',GETDATE()';
        END;

        IF @APHasModifiedOn = 1
        BEGIN
            SET @APInsertColumns += N',[ModifiedOn]';
            SET @APSelectColumns += N',GETDATE()';
        END;

        IF @APHasIsDeleted = 1
        BEGIN
            SET @APInsertColumns += N',[IsDeleted]';
            SET @APSelectColumns += N',0';
        END;

        IF @APHasIsSystem = 1
        BEGIN
            SET @APInsertColumns += N',[IsSystem]';
            SET @APSelectColumns += N',0';
        END;

        IF @APHasSource = 1
        BEGIN
            SET @APInsertColumns += N',[Source]';
            SET @APSelectColumns += N',''System''';
        END;

        IF @APHasHostAddress = 1
        BEGIN
            SET @APInsertColumns += N',[HostAddress]';
            SET @APSelectColumns += N',@IPAddress';
        END;

        SET @SQL = N'
            INSERT INTO ' + QUOTENAME(@ConfigDB) + N'.dbo.XStudio_AttributeProperties_Mst_Tbl
            (' + @APInsertColumns + N')
            SELECT ' + @APSelectColumns + N'
            FROM #ExpectedDropdownProperty expected;';

        EXEC sys.sp_executesql
            @SQL,
            N'@AttrID varchar(36), @UserID varchar(36), @IPAddress varchar(50)',
            @AttrID = @AttrID,
            @UserID = @UserID,
            @IPAddress = @IPAddress;
    END;

    -- ============================================================
    -- 9. DOCUMENTED FALLBACK FOR MISSING/WRONG ENTITY RELATION
    -- ============================================================

    DECLARE @RelationProblemCount int;

    SET @SQL = N'
        SELECT @RelationProblemCount = CASE WHEN EXISTS
        (
            SELECT 1
            FROM ' + QUOTENAME(@ConfigDB) + N'.dbo.XStudio_EntityRelations_Mst_Tbl er WITH (NOLOCK)
            WHERE er.EntityID = @MainEntityID
              AND er.AttributeID = @AttrID
              AND er.RelationEntityID = @TargetEntityID
              AND er.RelationAttributeID = @TargetIDAttrID
              AND er.RelationEntity = @RelationTable
              AND er.RelationAttribute = @RelationAttributeName
              AND er.IsDeleted = 0
        )
        THEN 0 ELSE 1 END;';

    EXEC sys.sp_executesql
        @SQL,
        N'@MainEntityID varchar(36),
          @AttrID varchar(36),
          @TargetEntityID varchar(36),
          @TargetIDAttrID varchar(36),
          @RelationTable varchar(200),
          @RelationAttributeName varchar(200),
          @RelationProblemCount int OUTPUT',
        @MainEntityID = @MainEntityID,
        @AttrID = @AttrID,
        @TargetEntityID = @TargetEntityID,
        @TargetIDAttrID = @TargetIDAttrID,
        @RelationTable = @RelationTable,
        @RelationAttributeName = @RelationAttributeName,
        @RelationProblemCount = @RelationProblemCount OUTPUT;

    IF @RelationProblemCount > 0 AND @AllowDocumentedFallback = 0
        THROW 54029, 'Dropdown SP completed but entity relation is missing/wrong. Set @AllowDocumentedFallback = 1 only if documented fallback is allowed.', 1;

    IF @RelationProblemCount > 0 AND @AllowDocumentedFallback = 1
    BEGIN
        DECLARE
            @ERHasID bit = 0,
            @ERIDIsIdentity bit = 0,
            @ERHasSource bit = 0,
            @ERHasRelationColumnCardinality bit = 0,
            @ERHasRelationSystemID bit = 0,
            @ERHasRelationEntityDatabase bit = 0,
            @ERHasCreatedBy bit = 0,
            @ERHasModifiedBy bit = 0,
            @ERHasCreatedOn bit = 0,
            @ERHasModifiedOn bit = 0,
            @ERHasIsDeleted bit = 0,
            @ERHasIsSystem bit = 0,
            @ERHasHostAddress bit = 0;

        SET @SQL = N'
            SELECT
                @ERHasID = MAX(CASE WHEN c.name = ''ID'' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END),
                @ERIDIsIdentity = MAX(CASE WHEN c.name = ''ID'' AND c.is_identity = 1 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END),
                @ERHasSource = MAX(CASE WHEN c.name = ''Source'' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END),
                @ERHasRelationColumnCardinality = MAX(CASE WHEN c.name = ''RelationColumnCardinality'' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END),
                @ERHasRelationSystemID = MAX(CASE WHEN c.name = ''RelationSystemID'' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END),
                @ERHasRelationEntityDatabase = MAX(CASE WHEN c.name = ''RelationEntityDatabase'' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END),
                @ERHasCreatedBy = MAX(CASE WHEN c.name = ''CreatedBy'' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END),
                @ERHasModifiedBy = MAX(CASE WHEN c.name = ''ModifiedBy'' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END),
                @ERHasCreatedOn = MAX(CASE WHEN c.name = ''CreatedOn'' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END),
                @ERHasModifiedOn = MAX(CASE WHEN c.name = ''ModifiedOn'' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END),
                @ERHasIsDeleted = MAX(CASE WHEN c.name = ''IsDeleted'' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END),
                @ERHasIsSystem = MAX(CASE WHEN c.name = ''IsSystem'' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END),
                @ERHasHostAddress = MAX(CASE WHEN c.name = ''HostAddress'' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END)
            FROM ' + QUOTENAME(@ConfigDB) + N'.sys.columns c
            JOIN ' + QUOTENAME(@ConfigDB) + N'.sys.tables t
              ON t.object_id = c.object_id
            JOIN ' + QUOTENAME(@ConfigDB) + N'.sys.schemas s
              ON s.schema_id = t.schema_id
            WHERE s.name = ''dbo''
              AND t.name = ''XStudio_EntityRelations_Mst_Tbl'';';

        EXEC sys.sp_executesql
            @SQL,
            N'@ERHasID bit OUTPUT,
              @ERIDIsIdentity bit OUTPUT,
              @ERHasSource bit OUTPUT,
              @ERHasRelationColumnCardinality bit OUTPUT,
              @ERHasRelationSystemID bit OUTPUT,
              @ERHasRelationEntityDatabase bit OUTPUT,
              @ERHasCreatedBy bit OUTPUT,
              @ERHasModifiedBy bit OUTPUT,
              @ERHasCreatedOn bit OUTPUT,
              @ERHasModifiedOn bit OUTPUT,
              @ERHasIsDeleted bit OUTPUT,
              @ERHasIsSystem bit OUTPUT,
              @ERHasHostAddress bit OUTPUT',
            @ERHasID = @ERHasID OUTPUT,
            @ERIDIsIdentity = @ERIDIsIdentity OUTPUT,
            @ERHasSource = @ERHasSource OUTPUT,
            @ERHasRelationColumnCardinality = @ERHasRelationColumnCardinality OUTPUT,
            @ERHasRelationSystemID = @ERHasRelationSystemID OUTPUT,
            @ERHasRelationEntityDatabase = @ERHasRelationEntityDatabase OUTPUT,
            @ERHasCreatedBy = @ERHasCreatedBy OUTPUT,
            @ERHasModifiedBy = @ERHasModifiedBy OUTPUT,
            @ERHasCreatedOn = @ERHasCreatedOn OUTPUT,
            @ERHasModifiedOn = @ERHasModifiedOn OUTPUT,
            @ERHasIsDeleted = @ERHasIsDeleted OUTPUT,
            @ERHasIsSystem = @ERHasIsSystem OUTPUT,
            @ERHasHostAddress = @ERHasHostAddress OUTPUT;

        DECLARE @ERUpdateSet nvarchar(max) = N'';
        IF @ERHasModifiedBy = 1 SET @ERUpdateSet += N', ModifiedBy = @UserID';
        IF @ERHasModifiedOn = 1 SET @ERUpdateSet += N', ModifiedOn = GETDATE()';
        IF @ERHasSource = 1 SET @ERUpdateSet += N', Source = ''System''';
        IF @ERHasHostAddress = 1 SET @ERUpdateSet += N', HostAddress = @IPAddress';

        SET @SQL = N'
            UPDATE er
            SET IsDeleted = 1' + @ERUpdateSet + N'
            FROM ' + QUOTENAME(@ConfigDB) + N'.dbo.XStudio_EntityRelations_Mst_Tbl er
            WHERE er.EntityID = @MainEntityID
              AND er.AttributeID = @AttrID
              AND er.IsDeleted = 0;';

        EXEC sys.sp_executesql
            @SQL,
            N'@MainEntityID varchar(36), @AttrID varchar(36), @UserID varchar(36), @IPAddress varchar(50)',
            @MainEntityID = @MainEntityID,
            @AttrID = @AttrID,
            @UserID = @UserID,
            @IPAddress = @IPAddress;

        DECLARE @ERInsertColumns nvarchar(max) = N'';
        DECLARE @ERSelectColumns nvarchar(max) = N'';

        IF @ERHasID = 1 AND @ERIDIsIdentity = 0
        BEGIN
            SET @ERInsertColumns += N'[ID],';
            SET @ERSelectColumns += N'NEWID(),';
        END;

        IF @ERHasSource = 1
        BEGIN
            SET @ERInsertColumns += N'[Source],';
            SET @ERSelectColumns += N'''System'',';
        END;

        SET @ERInsertColumns += N'[EntityID],[Cardinality],[RelationEntityID],';
        SET @ERSelectColumns += N'@MainEntityID,''Many'',@TargetEntityID,';

        IF @ERHasRelationColumnCardinality = 1
        BEGIN
            SET @ERInsertColumns += N'[RelationColumnCardinality],';
            SET @ERSelectColumns += N'''One'',';
        END;

        SET @ERInsertColumns += N'[RelationAttributeID],[AttributeID],';
        SET @ERSelectColumns += N'@TargetIDAttrID,@AttrID,';

        IF @ERHasRelationSystemID = 1
        BEGIN
            SET @ERInsertColumns += N'[RelationSystemID],';
            SET @ERSelectColumns += N'@DropdownRelationSystemID,';
        END;

        SET @ERInsertColumns += N'[RelationName],';
        SET @ERSelectColumns += N'@MainAttributeName + ''-'' + @RelationTable,';

        IF @ERHasRelationEntityDatabase = 1
        BEGIN
            SET @ERInsertColumns += N'[RelationEntityDatabase],';
            SET @ERSelectColumns += N'@DropdownDataDB,';
        END;

        SET @ERInsertColumns += N'[RelationEntity],[RelationAttribute]';
        SET @ERSelectColumns += N'@RelationTable,@RelationAttributeName';

        IF @ERHasCreatedBy = 1
        BEGIN
            SET @ERInsertColumns += N',[CreatedBy]';
            SET @ERSelectColumns += N',@UserID';
        END;

        IF @ERHasModifiedBy = 1
        BEGIN
            SET @ERInsertColumns += N',[ModifiedBy]';
            SET @ERSelectColumns += N',@UserID';
        END;

        IF @ERHasCreatedOn = 1
        BEGIN
            SET @ERInsertColumns += N',[CreatedOn]';
            SET @ERSelectColumns += N',GETDATE()';
        END;

        IF @ERHasModifiedOn = 1
        BEGIN
            SET @ERInsertColumns += N',[ModifiedOn]';
            SET @ERSelectColumns += N',GETDATE()';
        END;

        IF @ERHasIsDeleted = 1
        BEGIN
            SET @ERInsertColumns += N',[IsDeleted]';
            SET @ERSelectColumns += N',0';
        END;

        IF @ERHasIsSystem = 1
        BEGIN
            SET @ERInsertColumns += N',[IsSystem]';
            SET @ERSelectColumns += N',0';
        END;

        IF @ERHasHostAddress = 1
        BEGIN
            SET @ERInsertColumns += N',[HostAddress]';
            SET @ERSelectColumns += N',@IPAddress';
        END;

        SET @SQL = N'
            INSERT INTO ' + QUOTENAME(@ConfigDB) + N'.dbo.XStudio_EntityRelations_Mst_Tbl
            (' + @ERInsertColumns + N')
            SELECT ' + @ERSelectColumns + N';';

        EXEC sys.sp_executesql
            @SQL,
            N'@MainEntityID varchar(36),
              @TargetEntityID varchar(36),
              @TargetIDAttrID varchar(36),
              @AttrID varchar(36),
              @DropdownRelationSystemID varchar(36),
              @MainAttributeName varchar(200),
              @RelationTable varchar(200),
              @DropdownDataDB sysname,
              @RelationAttributeName varchar(200),
              @UserID varchar(36),
              @IPAddress varchar(50)',
            @MainEntityID = @MainEntityID,
            @TargetEntityID = @TargetEntityID,
            @TargetIDAttrID = @TargetIDAttrID,
            @AttrID = @AttrID,
            @DropdownRelationSystemID = @DropdownRelationSystemID,
            @MainAttributeName = @MainAttributeName,
            @RelationTable = @RelationTable,
            @DropdownDataDB = @DropdownDataDB,
            @RelationAttributeName = @RelationAttributeName,
            @UserID = @UserID,
            @IPAddress = @IPAddress;
    END;

    -- ============================================================
    -- 10. FINAL POSTFLIGHT
    -- ============================================================

    DECLARE @FinalPropertyProblemCount int;
    DECLARE @FinalRelationProblemCount int;
    DECLARE @DuplicatePropertyCount int;

    SET @SQL = N'
        SELECT @FinalPropertyProblemCount = COUNT(1)
        FROM #ExpectedDropdownProperty expected
        WHERE NOT EXISTS
        (
            SELECT 1
            FROM ' + QUOTENAME(@ConfigDB) + N'.dbo.XStudio_AttributeProperties_Mst_Tbl actual WITH (NOLOCK)
            WHERE actual.ParentID = @AttrID
              AND actual.Name = expected.Name
              AND actual.Value = expected.Value
              AND actual.IsDeleted = 0
        );

        SELECT @DuplicatePropertyCount = COUNT(1)
        FROM
        (
            SELECT actual.Name
            FROM ' + QUOTENAME(@ConfigDB) + N'.dbo.XStudio_AttributeProperties_Mst_Tbl actual WITH (NOLOCK)
            JOIN #ExpectedDropdownProperty expected
              ON expected.Name = actual.Name
            WHERE actual.ParentID = @AttrID
              AND actual.IsDeleted = 0
            GROUP BY actual.Name
            HAVING COUNT(1) > 1
        ) duplicate_props;';

    EXEC sys.sp_executesql
        @SQL,
        N'@AttrID varchar(36),
          @FinalPropertyProblemCount int OUTPUT,
          @DuplicatePropertyCount int OUTPUT',
        @AttrID = @AttrID,
        @FinalPropertyProblemCount = @FinalPropertyProblemCount OUTPUT,
        @DuplicatePropertyCount = @DuplicatePropertyCount OUTPUT;

    SET @SQL = N'
        SELECT @FinalRelationProblemCount = CASE WHEN EXISTS
        (
            SELECT 1
            FROM ' + QUOTENAME(@ConfigDB) + N'.dbo.XStudio_EntityRelations_Mst_Tbl er WITH (NOLOCK)
            WHERE er.EntityID = @MainEntityID
              AND er.AttributeID = @AttrID
              AND er.RelationEntityID = @TargetEntityID
              AND er.RelationAttributeID = @TargetIDAttrID
              AND er.RelationEntity = @RelationTable
              AND er.RelationAttribute = @RelationAttributeName
              AND er.IsDeleted = 0
        )
        THEN 0 ELSE 1 END;';

    EXEC sys.sp_executesql
        @SQL,
        N'@MainEntityID varchar(36),
          @AttrID varchar(36),
          @TargetEntityID varchar(36),
          @TargetIDAttrID varchar(36),
          @RelationTable varchar(200),
          @RelationAttributeName varchar(200),
          @FinalRelationProblemCount int OUTPUT',
        @MainEntityID = @MainEntityID,
        @AttrID = @AttrID,
        @TargetEntityID = @TargetEntityID,
        @TargetIDAttrID = @TargetIDAttrID,
        @RelationTable = @RelationTable,
        @RelationAttributeName = @RelationAttributeName,
        @FinalRelationProblemCount = @FinalRelationProblemCount OUTPUT;

    IF @FinalPropertyProblemCount > 0
        THROW 54030, 'Final postflight failed: dropdown does not have all six expected active properties with expected values.', 1;

    IF @DuplicatePropertyCount > 0
        THROW 54031, 'Final postflight failed: dropdown has duplicate active properties.', 1;

    IF @FinalRelationProblemCount > 0
        THROW 54032, 'Final postflight failed: dropdown entity relation is missing or wrong.', 1;

    -- ============================================================
    -- 11. RESULT
    -- ============================================================

    SET @SQL = N'
        SELECT
            ''DropdownProperty'' AS ObjectType,
            CAST(actual.ID AS varchar(36)) AS ID,
            CAST(actual.ParentID AS varchar(36)) AS ParentID,
            actual.Name,
            actual.Value
        FROM ' + QUOTENAME(@ConfigDB) + N'.dbo.XStudio_AttributeProperties_Mst_Tbl actual WITH (NOLOCK)
        JOIN #ExpectedDropdownProperty expected
          ON expected.Name = actual.Name
        WHERE actual.ParentID = @AttrID
          AND actual.IsDeleted = 0

        UNION ALL

        SELECT
            ''EntityRelation'' AS ObjectType,
            CAST(er.ID AS varchar(36)) AS ID,
            CAST(er.AttributeID AS varchar(36)) AS ParentID,
            er.RelationName AS Name,
            er.RelationEntity + ''.'' + er.RelationAttribute AS Value
        FROM ' + QUOTENAME(@ConfigDB) + N'.dbo.XStudio_EntityRelations_Mst_Tbl er WITH (NOLOCK)
        WHERE er.EntityID = @MainEntityID
          AND er.AttributeID = @AttrID
          AND er.IsDeleted = 0
        ORDER BY ObjectType, Name;';

    EXEC sys.sp_executesql
        @SQL,
        N'@AttrID varchar(36), @MainEntityID varchar(36)',
        @AttrID = @AttrID,
        @MainEntityID = @MainEntityID;

    COMMIT TRANSACTION;

    PRINT 'Dropdown properties and entity relation completed successfully. Next: run postflight_checks.sql and then edit_control_safe_json_template.sql if the dropdown appears in an edit form.';

END TRY
BEGIN CATCH

    IF @@TRANCOUNT > 0
        ROLLBACK TRANSACTION;

    DECLARE @ErrorMessage nvarchar(4000) = ERROR_MESSAGE();
    DECLARE @ErrorLine int = ERROR_LINE();
    DECLARE @ErrorNumber int = ERROR_NUMBER();

    RAISERROR('dropdown_properties_template.sql failed. Error %d at line %d: %s', 16, 1, @ErrorNumber, @ErrorLine, @ErrorMessage);

END CATCH;
```

## 36. Edit Control Safe Json Template

**Source file:** `03_SQL_TEMPLATES/edit_control_safe_json_template.sql`  
**File type:** `.sql`
