```sql
-- ============================================================
-- Edit Control Safe JSON Template
-- Generate/complete renderer-safe edit-control configuration
-- ============================================================
--
-- Purpose:
--   Ensure active edit controls have renderer-safe ControlProperties JSON,
--   and ensure dropdown edit controls are consistent across:
--     - ControlType / ControlName
--     - physical dropdown columns when present
--     - ControlProperties JSON
--
-- This template covers work intentionally removed from
-- entity_create_template.sql:
--   - edit-control generation/validation
--   - renderer-safe ControlProperties
--   - dropdown edit-control JSON and physical dropdown metadata
--
-- Hard rule:
--   Use XStudio stored procedures where an XStudio SP exists.
--
-- Primary SP used first:
--   XStudio_Configuration.dbo.Xstudio_Insert_Edit_Columns_Info_Usp
--
-- Important finding from the 2026-05-08 SP text:
--   Xstudio_Insert_Edit_Columns_Info_Usp has this 4-parameter signature:
--     @EntityID varchar(36)
--     @EditID varchar(36)
--     @PkColumn varchar(100)
--     @DatabaseName varchar(200)
--
--   Its definition generates edit-control rows from attributes, but some
--   insert branches do not populate ControlProperties. Therefore this SP is
--   necessary but not sufficient for BindControls.js safety.
--
-- No complete generic "save edit control configuration" SP was found in the
-- current stored-procedure reference. Therefore this template uses a narrow,
-- documented, schema-inspected fallback update for XStudio_EditControls_Mst_Tbl
-- after the platform SP has run.
--
-- Do not use this template to create the entity, base attributes, dropdown
-- AttributeProperties, LV, page, menu, or menu rights.
--
-- Execution sequence:
--   1. Create entity/attributes with entity_create_template.sql.
--   2. Complete dropdown AttributeProperties/relations with
--      dropdown_properties_template.sql when needed.
--   3. Create/resolve Edit header using the edit-form playbook/template.
--   4. Run this template to generate/complete edit controls safely.
--   5. Bind the Edit to a page using page_menu_template.sql.
--
-- ============================================================

SET NOCOUNT ON;
SET XACT_ABORT ON;

BEGIN TRY
    BEGIN TRANSACTION;

    -- ============================================================
    -- 0. INPUTS
    -- ============================================================

    DECLARE @SystemID varchar(36) = '6C8C0070-60B5-4474-BEC8-5C02197B32E6';
    DECLARE @EntityID varchar(36) = '<entity_guid>';
    DECLARE @EditID varchar(36) = '<edit_guid>';
    DECLARE @PkColumn varchar(100) = 'ID';

    -- Use real XStudio deployment user GUID. Do not use 'sa'.
    DECLARE @UserID varchar(36) = '<deployment_user_guid>';
    DECLARE @IPAddress varchar(100) = 'T-SQL';

    -- 1 = call Xstudio_Insert_Edit_Columns_Info_Usp before completing JSON.
    -- 0 = skip generation and only validate/complete existing controls.
    DECLARE @GenerateMissingControls bit = 1;

    -- 1 = allow documented direct update fallback for EditControls safety fields
    -- after XStudio SP execution. Set 0 to fail instead.
    DECLARE @AllowDocumentedFallback bit = 1;

    DECLARE @ConfigDB sysname;
    DECLARE @DataDB sysname;
    DECLARE @SQL nvarchar(max);

    -- ============================================================
    -- 1. RESOLVE LIVE DATABASES
    -- ============================================================

    SELECT
        @ConfigDB = ConfigurationDatabase,
        @DataDB = DataDatabase
    FROM XStudio_Configuration.dbo.XStudio_GetDataSourceInfoFromSystemId_Fun(@SystemID);

    IF @ConfigDB IS NULL OR @DataDB IS NULL
        THROW 55000, 'Could not resolve ConfigDB/DataDB from @SystemID.', 1;

    IF DB_ID(@ConfigDB) IS NULL
        THROW 55001, 'Resolved configuration database does not exist.', 1;

    IF DB_ID(@DataDB) IS NULL
        THROW 55002, 'Resolved data database does not exist.', 1;

    -- ============================================================
    -- 2. INPUT VALIDATION
    -- ============================================================

    IF TRY_CONVERT(uniqueidentifier, @SystemID) IS NULL
        THROW 55003, 'Invalid @SystemID.', 1;

    IF TRY_CONVERT(uniqueidentifier, @EntityID) IS NULL
        THROW 55004, 'Invalid @EntityID.', 1;

    IF TRY_CONVERT(uniqueidentifier, @EditID) IS NULL
        THROW 55005, 'Invalid @EditID.', 1;

    IF @UserID IS NULL OR @UserID = '<deployment_user_guid>' OR TRY_CONVERT(uniqueidentifier, @UserID) IS NULL
        THROW 55006, 'Set @UserID to a real XStudio user GUID before running this template.', 1;

    IF @PkColumn IS NULL OR @PkColumn LIKE '%[^A-Za-z0-9_]%'
        THROW 55007, '@PkColumn must be a simple column name.', 1;

    -- ============================================================
    -- 3. LIVE SP SIGNATURE PREFLIGHT
    -- ============================================================

    IF OBJECT_ID('XStudio_Configuration.dbo.Xstudio_Insert_Edit_Columns_Info_Usp', 'P') IS NULL
        THROW 55008, 'Xstudio_Insert_Edit_Columns_Info_Usp is missing.', 1;

    IF
    (
        SELECT COUNT(1)
        FROM XStudio_Configuration.sys.parameters p
        WHERE p.object_id = OBJECT_ID('XStudio_Configuration.dbo.Xstudio_Insert_Edit_Columns_Info_Usp')
          AND p.name IN ('@EntityID', '@EditID', '@PkColumn', '@DatabaseName')
    ) <> 4
        THROW 55009, 'Xstudio_Insert_Edit_Columns_Info_Usp signature does not match expected 4-parameter surface.', 1;

    -- ============================================================
    -- 4. LIVE TABLE/COLUMN PREFLIGHT
    -- ============================================================

    SET @SQL = N'
        IF NOT EXISTS
        (
            SELECT 1
            FROM ' + QUOTENAME(@ConfigDB) + N'.sys.tables t
            JOIN ' + QUOTENAME(@ConfigDB) + N'.sys.schemas s
              ON s.schema_id = t.schema_id
            WHERE s.name = ''dbo''
              AND t.name = ''XStudio_EditControls_Mst_Tbl''
        )
            THROW 55010, ''XStudio_EditControls_Mst_Tbl missing in ConfigDB.'', 1;

        IF NOT EXISTS
        (
            SELECT 1
            FROM ' + QUOTENAME(@ConfigDB) + N'.sys.tables t
            JOIN ' + QUOTENAME(@ConfigDB) + N'.sys.schemas s
              ON s.schema_id = t.schema_id
            WHERE s.name = ''dbo''
              AND t.name = ''XStudio_Edit_Mst_Tbl''
        )
            THROW 55011, ''XStudio_Edit_Mst_Tbl missing in ConfigDB.'', 1;

        IF NOT EXISTS
        (
            SELECT 1
            FROM ' + QUOTENAME(@ConfigDB) + N'.sys.tables t
            JOIN ' + QUOTENAME(@ConfigDB) + N'.sys.schemas s
              ON s.schema_id = t.schema_id
            WHERE s.name = ''dbo''
              AND t.name = ''XStudio_Attribute_Mst_Tbl''
        )
            THROW 55012, ''XStudio_Attribute_Mst_Tbl missing in ConfigDB.'', 1;

        IF
        (
            SELECT COUNT(1)
            FROM ' + QUOTENAME(@ConfigDB) + N'.sys.columns c
            JOIN ' + QUOTENAME(@ConfigDB) + N'.sys.tables t
              ON t.object_id = c.object_id
            JOIN ' + QUOTENAME(@ConfigDB) + N'.sys.schemas s
              ON s.schema_id = t.schema_id
            WHERE s.name = ''dbo''
              AND t.name = ''XStudio_EditControls_Mst_Tbl''
              AND c.name IN
              (
                  ''ParentID'',
                  ''ControlType'',
                  ''ControlName'',
                  ''ControlProperties'',
                  ''IsDeleted''
              )
        ) < 5
            THROW 55013, ''EditControls core columns missing or renamed.'', 1;

        IF
        (
            SELECT COUNT(1)
            FROM ' + QUOTENAME(@ConfigDB) + N'.sys.columns c
            JOIN ' + QUOTENAME(@ConfigDB) + N'.sys.tables t
              ON t.object_id = c.object_id
            JOIN ' + QUOTENAME(@ConfigDB) + N'.sys.schemas s
              ON s.schema_id = t.schema_id
            WHERE s.name = ''dbo''
              AND t.name = ''XStudio_Attribute_Mst_Tbl''
              AND c.name IN
              (
                  ''ID'',
                  ''Name'',
                  ''DisplayName'',
                  ''DataType'',
                  ''ParentID'',
                  ''IsDeleted''
              )
        ) < 6
            THROW 55014, ''Attribute core columns missing or renamed.'', 1;';

    EXEC sys.sp_executesql @SQL;

    -- Ensure target entity/edit exist.
    SET @SQL = N'
        IF NOT EXISTS
        (
            SELECT 1
            FROM ' + QUOTENAME(@ConfigDB) + N'.dbo.XStudio_Entities_Mst_Tbl WITH (NOLOCK)
            WHERE ID = @EntityID
              AND IsDeleted = 0
        )
            THROW 55015, ''Entity not found in ConfigDB.'', 1;

        IF NOT EXISTS
        (
            SELECT 1
            FROM ' + QUOTENAME(@ConfigDB) + N'.dbo.XStudio_Edit_Mst_Tbl WITH (NOLOCK)
            WHERE ID = @EditID
              AND ParentID = @EntityID
              AND IsDeleted = 0
        )
            THROW 55016, ''Edit not found for EntityID in ConfigDB.'', 1;';

    EXEC sys.sp_executesql
        @SQL,
        N'@EntityID varchar(36), @EditID varchar(36)',
        @EntityID = @EntityID,
        @EditID = @EditID;

    -- ============================================================
    -- 5. CALL PLATFORM EDIT-CONTROL GENERATION SP
    -- ============================================================

    IF @GenerateMissingControls = 1
    BEGIN
        EXEC XStudio_Configuration.dbo.Xstudio_Insert_Edit_Columns_Info_Usp
            @EntityID = @EntityID,
            @EditID = @EditID,
            @PkColumn = @PkColumn,
            @DatabaseName = @ConfigDB;
    END;

    -- ============================================================
    -- 6. SAFE JSON TEMPLATES
    -- ============================================================
    -- The JSON is intentionally broad because BindControls.js reads many
    -- keys depending on control type and XStudio version. Values are strings
    -- where the renderer commonly expects strings.

    DECLARE @BaseJSON nvarchar(max) = N'{
  "DefaultValue": "",
  "IsRequired": "False",
  "IsUnique": "False",
  "IsVisible": "True",
  "IsReadOnly": "False",
  "IsReadOnlyOnEdit": "False",
  "IsSaveOrNot": "True",
  "IsSaveAsXml": "False",
  "XmlAttributeID": "",
  "IsSaveAsJson": "False",
  "JsonAttributeID": "",
  "JsonPath": "",
  "LabelFormat": "",
  "IsLabelTitleVisible": "True",
  "TextBoxType": "text",
  "TextBoxPasswordType": "False",
  "NumericMinValue": "0",
  "NumericMaxValue": "99999999",
  "NumericStepValue": "1",
  "NumericInputMask": "",
  "TextAreaRowNumber": "3",
  "IsTextEditor": "False",
  "DropdownType": "",
  "DropdownDatabase": "",
  "DropdownEntityID": "",
  "DropdownTextField": "",
  "DropdownValueField": "",
  "DropdownImageField": "",
  "DropdownEntryMode": "single",
  "DropdownInsertionMode": "",
  "DropdownFilterCondition": "",
  "DropdownOrderBy": "",
  "IsDropdownPreloaded": "False",
  "DropdownSearchAfterCharacter": "",
  "DropdownTextSeperator": "",
  "IsDropdownAddButton": "False",
  "DropdownAddEditorID": "",
  "DateTimePickerMinTime": "",
  "DateTimePickerMaxTime": "",
  "IsDateTimePickerBlockPastTime": "False",
  "IsDateTimePickerBlockFutureTime": "False",
  "IsDateTimePickerSelectCurrentTime": "False",
  "DateTimePickerFormat": "dd/MM/yyyy HH:mm",
  "DateTimePickerOutputFormat": "",
  "DateTimePickerType": "datetime",
  "IsDateRangePicker": "False",
  "ParentDatePickerId": "",
  "FileBrowseMode": "",
  "FileBrowseType": "",
  "FileBrowseTypeFilterMode": "",
  "FileBrowseTypeFilter": "",
  "FileBrowseMaxSize": "",
  "FileNameSaveBy": "",
  "FileUploadPath": "",
  "IsMaintainFileRevision": "False",
  "CheckboxThemeType": "default",
  "IsCheckboxDefaultChecked": "False",
  "IsSwitch": "False",
  "Rating": "0",
  "RatingMaxValue": "",
  "RatingPrecision": "",
  "Slider": "0",
  "SliderMinRange": "",
  "SliderMaxRange": "",
  "SliderRangeSmallStep": "",
  "SliderRangeLargeStep": "",
  "AutoIncrementPrefix": "",
  "AutoIncrementSuffix": "",
  "AutoIncrementLength": "0",
  "AutoIncrementSeedValue": "",
  "AutoIncrementIncrementRate": "",
  "ImageUrl": "",
  "ImageboxType": "",
  "MapCenterPoint": "",
  "MapZoomLevel": "10",
  "MapLayer": "",
  "DataReturnType": "",
  "LongitudeField": "",
  "LatitudeField": "",
  "IsSeparateLatLong": "False",
  "IsNeedUpload": "False",
  "NoOfImageDisplay": "3",
  "LabelSize": "4",
  "IsShowPreviousValue": "True",
  "IsShowRange": "True",
  "IsShowInstrumentTag": "True",
  "InstrumentTagPosition": ""
}';

    IF ISJSON(@BaseJSON) <> 1
        THROW 55017, 'Internal error: @BaseJSON is not valid JSON.', 1;

    -- ============================================================
    -- 7. BUILD DROPDOWN METADATA FROM ATTRIBUTE PROPERTIES
    -- ============================================================

    IF OBJECT_ID('tempdb..#DropdownMeta') IS NOT NULL
        DROP TABLE #DropdownMeta;

    CREATE TABLE #DropdownMeta
    (
        AttributeID varchar(36) NOT NULL PRIMARY KEY,
        AttributeName varchar(200) NOT NULL,
        ControlName varchar(200) NOT NULL,
        DropdownDatabase varchar(200) NOT NULL,
        DropdownEntityID varchar(36) NOT NULL,
        DropdownTextField varchar(500) NOT NULL,
        DropdownValueField varchar(36) NOT NULL,
        DropdownEntryMode varchar(100) NOT NULL
    );

    SET @SQL = N'
        INSERT INTO #DropdownMeta
        (
            AttributeID,
            AttributeName,
            ControlName,
            DropdownDatabase,
            DropdownEntityID,
            DropdownTextField,
            DropdownValueField,
            DropdownEntryMode
        )
        SELECT
            CAST(a.ID AS varchar(36)) AS AttributeID,
            a.Name AS AttributeName,
            ''cmb_'' + a.Name AS ControlName,
            MAX(CASE WHEN ap.Name = ''DropdownDatabase'' THEN CONVERT(varchar(200), ap.Value) END) AS DropdownDatabase,
            MAX(CASE WHEN ap.Name = ''DropdownEntity'' THEN CONVERT(varchar(36), ap.Value) END) AS DropdownEntityID,
            MAX(CASE WHEN ap.Name = ''DropdownTextField'' THEN CONVERT(varchar(500), ap.Value) END) AS DropdownTextField,
            MAX(CASE WHEN ap.Name = ''DropdownValueField'' THEN CONVERT(varchar(36), ap.Value) END) AS DropdownValueField,
            ISNULL(MAX(CASE WHEN ap.Name = ''DropdownEntryMode'' THEN CONVERT(varchar(100), ap.Value) END), ''single'') AS DropdownEntryMode
        FROM ' + QUOTENAME(@ConfigDB) + N'.dbo.XStudio_Attribute_Mst_Tbl a WITH (NOLOCK)
        JOIN XStudio_Configuration.dbo.XStudio_DataType_Mst_Tbl dt WITH (NOLOCK)
          ON dt.ID = a.DataType
         AND dt.IsDeleted = 0
        LEFT JOIN ' + QUOTENAME(@ConfigDB) + N'.dbo.XStudio_AttributeProperties_Mst_Tbl ap WITH (NOLOCK)
          ON ap.ParentID = a.ID
         AND ap.IsDeleted = 0
        WHERE a.ParentID = @EntityID
          AND a.IsDeleted = 0
          AND dt.Name = ''Dropdown''
        GROUP BY a.ID, a.Name
        HAVING
            MAX(CASE WHEN ap.Name = ''DropdownDatabase'' THEN CONVERT(varchar(200), ap.Value) END) IS NOT NULL
        AND MAX(CASE WHEN ap.Name = ''DropdownEntity'' THEN CONVERT(varchar(36), ap.Value) END) IS NOT NULL
        AND MAX(CASE WHEN ap.Name = ''DropdownTextField'' THEN CONVERT(varchar(500), ap.Value) END) IS NOT NULL
        AND MAX(CASE WHEN ap.Name = ''DropdownValueField'' THEN CONVERT(varchar(36), ap.Value) END) IS NOT NULL;';

    EXEC sys.sp_executesql
        @SQL,
        N'@EntityID varchar(36)',
        @EntityID = @EntityID;

    -- Fail if dropdown attributes exist but are missing dropdown metadata.
    SET @SQL = N'
        IF EXISTS
        (
            SELECT 1
            FROM ' + QUOTENAME(@ConfigDB) + N'.dbo.XStudio_Attribute_Mst_Tbl a WITH (NOLOCK)
            JOIN XStudio_Configuration.dbo.XStudio_DataType_Mst_Tbl dt WITH (NOLOCK)
              ON dt.ID = a.DataType
             AND dt.IsDeleted = 0
            LEFT JOIN #DropdownMeta dm
              ON dm.AttributeID = CAST(a.ID AS varchar(36))
            WHERE a.ParentID = @EntityID
              AND a.IsDeleted = 0
              AND dt.Name = ''Dropdown''
              AND dm.AttributeID IS NULL
        )
            THROW 55018, ''One or more Dropdown attributes are missing complete dropdown properties. Run dropdown_properties_template.sql first.'', 1;';

    EXEC sys.sp_executesql
        @SQL,
        N'@EntityID varchar(36)',
        @EntityID = @EntityID;

    -- ============================================================
    -- 8. DOCUMENTED FALLBACK UPDATE FOR EDIT CONTROL SAFETY
    -- ============================================================
    -- No generic save-edit-control SP is present in the current SP catalog.
    -- The platform generation SP is run above. The following update is a
    -- narrow, schema-inspected fallback for renderer safety only.

    IF @AllowDocumentedFallback = 0
    BEGIN
        SET @SQL = N'
            IF EXISTS
            (
                SELECT 1
                FROM ' + QUOTENAME(@ConfigDB) + N'.dbo.XStudio_EditControls_Mst_Tbl ec WITH (NOLOCK)
                WHERE ec.ParentID = @EditID
                  AND ec.IsDeleted = 0
                  AND
                  (
                       ec.ControlProperties IS NULL
                    OR LTRIM(RTRIM(CONVERT(nvarchar(max), ec.ControlProperties))) = ''''
                    OR ISJSON(CONVERT(nvarchar(max), ec.ControlProperties)) <> 1
                  )
            )
                THROW 55019, ''Edit controls need ControlProperties repair but @AllowDocumentedFallback = 0.'', 1;';
        EXEC sys.sp_executesql
            @SQL,
            N'@EditID varchar(36)',
            @EditID = @EditID;
    END;

    IF @AllowDocumentedFallback = 1
    BEGIN
        DECLARE
            @HasName bit = 0,
            @HasControlLabel bit = 0,
            @HasAttributeID bit = 0,
            @HasColumnName bit = 0,
            @HasControlType bit = 0,
            @HasControlName bit = 0,
            @HasDropdownType bit = 0,
            @HasDropDownEntityID bit = 0,
            @HasDropdownTextField bit = 0,
            @HasDropdownValueField bit = 0,
            @HasDropdownDatabase bit = 0,
            @HasControlProperties bit = 0,
            @HasModifiedBy bit = 0,
            @HasModifiedOn bit = 0,
            @HasSource bit = 0,
            @HasHostAddress bit = 0;

        SET @SQL = N'
            SELECT
                @HasName = MAX(CASE WHEN c.name = ''Name'' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END),
                @HasControlLabel = MAX(CASE WHEN c.name = ''ControlLabel'' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END),
                @HasAttributeID = MAX(CASE WHEN c.name = ''AttributeID'' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END),
                @HasColumnName = MAX(CASE WHEN c.name = ''ColumnName'' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END),
                @HasControlType = MAX(CASE WHEN c.name = ''ControlType'' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END),
                @HasControlName = MAX(CASE WHEN c.name = ''ControlName'' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END),
                @HasDropdownType = MAX(CASE WHEN c.name = ''DropdownType'' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END),
                @HasDropDownEntityID = MAX(CASE WHEN c.name = ''DropDownEntityID'' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END),
                @HasDropdownTextField = MAX(CASE WHEN c.name = ''DropdownTextField'' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END),
                @HasDropdownValueField = MAX(CASE WHEN c.name = ''DropdownValueField'' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END),
                @HasDropdownDatabase = MAX(CASE WHEN c.name = ''DropdownDatabase'' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END),
                @HasControlProperties = MAX(CASE WHEN c.name = ''ControlProperties'' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END),
                @HasModifiedBy = MAX(CASE WHEN c.name = ''ModifiedBy'' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END),
                @HasModifiedOn = MAX(CASE WHEN c.name = ''ModifiedOn'' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END),
                @HasSource = MAX(CASE WHEN c.name = ''Source'' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END),
                @HasHostAddress = MAX(CASE WHEN c.name = ''HostAddress'' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END)
            FROM ' + QUOTENAME(@ConfigDB) + N'.sys.columns c
            JOIN ' + QUOTENAME(@ConfigDB) + N'.sys.tables t
              ON t.object_id = c.object_id
            JOIN ' + QUOTENAME(@ConfigDB) + N'.sys.schemas s
              ON s.schema_id = t.schema_id
            WHERE s.name = ''dbo''
              AND t.name = ''XStudio_EditControls_Mst_Tbl'';';

        EXEC sys.sp_executesql
            @SQL,
            N'@HasName bit OUTPUT,
              @HasControlLabel bit OUTPUT,
              @HasAttributeID bit OUTPUT,
              @HasColumnName bit OUTPUT,
              @HasControlType bit OUTPUT,
              @HasControlName bit OUTPUT,
              @HasDropdownType bit OUTPUT,
              @HasDropDownEntityID bit OUTPUT,
              @HasDropdownTextField bit OUTPUT,
              @HasDropdownValueField bit OUTPUT,
              @HasDropdownDatabase bit OUTPUT,
              @HasControlProperties bit OUTPUT,
              @HasModifiedBy bit OUTPUT,
              @HasModifiedOn bit OUTPUT,
              @HasSource bit OUTPUT,
              @HasHostAddress bit OUTPUT',
            @HasName = @HasName OUTPUT,
            @HasControlLabel = @HasControlLabel OUTPUT,
            @HasAttributeID = @HasAttributeID OUTPUT,
            @HasColumnName = @HasColumnName OUTPUT,
            @HasControlType = @HasControlType OUTPUT,
            @HasControlName = @HasControlName OUTPUT,
            @HasDropdownType = @HasDropdownType OUTPUT,
            @HasDropDownEntityID = @HasDropDownEntityID OUTPUT,
            @HasDropdownTextField = @HasDropdownTextField OUTPUT,
            @HasDropdownValueField = @HasDropdownValueField OUTPUT,
            @HasDropdownDatabase = @HasDropdownDatabase OUTPUT,
            @HasControlProperties = @HasControlProperties OUTPUT,
            @HasModifiedBy = @HasModifiedBy OUTPUT,
            @HasModifiedOn = @HasModifiedOn OUTPUT,
            @HasSource = @HasSource OUTPUT,
            @HasHostAddress = @HasHostAddress OUTPUT;

        IF @HasControlProperties = 0
            THROW 55020, 'XStudio_EditControls_Mst_Tbl has no ControlProperties column.', 1;

        DECLARE @AuditSet nvarchar(max) = N'';
        IF @HasModifiedBy = 1 SET @AuditSet += N', ModifiedBy = @UserID';
        IF @HasModifiedOn = 1 SET @AuditSet += N', ModifiedOn = GETDATE()';
        IF @HasSource = 1 SET @AuditSet += N', Source = ''System''';
        IF @HasHostAddress = 1 SET @AuditSet += N', HostAddress = @IPAddress';

        -- 8a. Fill null/blank/invalid JSON with full base JSON.
        SET @SQL = N'
            UPDATE ec
            SET ControlProperties = @BaseJSON' + @AuditSet + N'
            FROM ' + QUOTENAME(@ConfigDB) + N'.dbo.XStudio_EditControls_Mst_Tbl ec
            WHERE ec.ParentID = @EditID
              AND ec.IsDeleted = 0
              AND
              (
                   ec.ControlProperties IS NULL
                OR LTRIM(RTRIM(CONVERT(nvarchar(max), ec.ControlProperties))) = ''''
                OR ISJSON(CONVERT(nvarchar(max), ec.ControlProperties)) <> 1
              );';

        EXEC sys.sp_executesql
            @SQL,
            N'@EditID varchar(36), @BaseJSON nvarchar(max), @UserID varchar(36), @IPAddress varchar(100)',
            @EditID = @EditID,
            @BaseJSON = @BaseJSON,
            @UserID = @UserID,
            @IPAddress = @IPAddress;

        -- 8b. Normalize dropdown controls and dropdown JSON.
        -- Match EditControls to attributes using AttributeID when present,
        -- otherwise ColumnName when present.
        DECLARE @JoinPredicate nvarchar(max) = N'';
        IF @HasAttributeID = 1
            SET @JoinPredicate = N'CAST(ec.AttributeID AS varchar(36)) = dm.AttributeID';
        ELSE IF @HasColumnName = 1
            SET @JoinPredicate = N'ec.ColumnName = dm.AttributeName';
        ELSE
            THROW 55021, 'Cannot map edit controls to attributes: neither AttributeID nor ColumnName exists.';

        DECLARE @DropdownSet nvarchar(max) = N'
              ControlType = ''selectbox'',
              ControlName = dm.ControlName,
              ControlProperties =
                    JSON_MODIFY
                    (
                        JSON_MODIFY
                        (
                            JSON_MODIFY
                            (
                                JSON_MODIFY
                                (
                                    JSON_MODIFY
                                    (
                                        JSON_MODIFY
                                        (
                                            CASE
                                                WHEN ec.ControlProperties IS NULL
                                                  OR LTRIM(RTRIM(CONVERT(nvarchar(max), ec.ControlProperties))) = ''''
                                                  OR ISJSON(CONVERT(nvarchar(max), ec.ControlProperties)) <> 1
                                                THEN @BaseJSON
                                                ELSE CONVERT(nvarchar(max), ec.ControlProperties)
                                            END,
                                            ''$.DropdownType'',
                                            ''dynamic''
                                        ),
                                        ''$.DropdownDatabase'',
                                        dm.DropdownDatabase
                                    ),
                                    ''$.DropdownEntityID'',
                                    dm.DropdownEntityID
                                ),
                                ''$.DropdownTextField'',
                                dm.DropdownTextField
                            ),
                            ''$.DropdownValueField'',
                            dm.DropdownValueField
                        ),
                        ''$.DropdownEntryMode'',
                        dm.DropdownEntryMode
                    )';

        IF @HasDropdownType = 1
            SET @DropdownSet += N', DropdownType = ''dynamic''';

        IF @HasDropDownEntityID = 1
            SET @DropdownSet += N', DropDownEntityID = dm.DropdownEntityID';

        IF @HasDropdownTextField = 1
            SET @DropdownSet += N', DropdownTextField = dm.DropdownTextField';

        IF @HasDropdownValueField = 1
            SET @DropdownSet += N', DropdownValueField = dm.DropdownValueField';

        IF @HasDropdownDatabase = 1
            SET @DropdownSet += N', DropdownDatabase = dm.DropdownDatabase';

        SET @SQL = N'
            UPDATE ec
            SET ' + @DropdownSet + @AuditSet + N'
            FROM ' + QUOTENAME(@ConfigDB) + N'.dbo.XStudio_EditControls_Mst_Tbl ec
            JOIN #DropdownMeta dm
              ON ' + @JoinPredicate + N'
            WHERE ec.ParentID = @EditID
              AND ec.IsDeleted = 0;';

        EXEC sys.sp_executesql
            @SQL,
            N'@EditID varchar(36), @BaseJSON nvarchar(max), @UserID varchar(36), @IPAddress varchar(100)',
            @EditID = @EditID,
            @BaseJSON = @BaseJSON,
            @UserID = @UserID,
            @IPAddress = @IPAddress;

        -- 8c. Optional label/name consistency for controls where columns exist.
        IF @HasName = 1 AND @HasControlLabel = 1 AND @HasColumnName = 1
        BEGIN
            SET @SQL = N'
                UPDATE ec
                SET [Name] = ISNULL(NULLIF(ec.[Name], ''''), ec.ColumnName),
                    ControlLabel = ISNULL(NULLIF(ec.ControlLabel, ''''), ec.ColumnName)' + @AuditSet + N'
                FROM ' + QUOTENAME(@ConfigDB) + N'.dbo.XStudio_EditControls_Mst_Tbl ec
                WHERE ec.ParentID = @EditID
                  AND ec.IsDeleted = 0
                  AND
                  (
                       ec.[Name] IS NULL
                    OR ec.[Name] = ''''
                    OR ec.ControlLabel IS NULL
                    OR ec.ControlLabel = ''''
                  );';

            EXEC sys.sp_executesql
                @SQL,
                N'@EditID varchar(36), @UserID varchar(36), @IPAddress varchar(100)',
                @EditID = @EditID,
                @UserID = @UserID,
                @IPAddress = @IPAddress;
        END;
    END;

    -- ============================================================
    -- 9. POSTFLIGHT
    -- ============================================================

    DECLARE @NullJsonCount int = 0;
    DECLARE @InvalidJsonCount int = 0;
    DECLARE @BadDropdownCount int = 0;
    DECLARE @ControlCount int = 0;

    SET @SQL = N'
        SELECT @ControlCount = COUNT(1)
        FROM ' + QUOTENAME(@ConfigDB) + N'.dbo.XStudio_EditControls_Mst_Tbl ec WITH (NOLOCK)
        WHERE ec.ParentID = @EditID
          AND ec.IsDeleted = 0;

        SELECT @NullJsonCount = COUNT(1)
        FROM ' + QUOTENAME(@ConfigDB) + N'.dbo.XStudio_EditControls_Mst_Tbl ec WITH (NOLOCK)
        WHERE ec.ParentID = @EditID
          AND ec.IsDeleted = 0
          AND
          (
               ec.ControlProperties IS NULL
            OR LTRIM(RTRIM(CONVERT(nvarchar(max), ec.ControlProperties))) = ''''
          );

        SELECT @InvalidJsonCount = COUNT(1)
        FROM ' + QUOTENAME(@ConfigDB) + N'.dbo.XStudio_EditControls_Mst_Tbl ec WITH (NOLOCK)
        WHERE ec.ParentID = @EditID
          AND ec.IsDeleted = 0
          AND ec.ControlProperties IS NOT NULL
          AND LTRIM(RTRIM(CONVERT(nvarchar(max), ec.ControlProperties))) <> ''''
          AND ISJSON(CONVERT(nvarchar(max), ec.ControlProperties)) <> 1;';

    EXEC sys.sp_executesql
        @SQL,
        N'@EditID varchar(36),
          @ControlCount int OUTPUT,
          @NullJsonCount int OUTPUT,
          @InvalidJsonCount int OUTPUT',
        @EditID = @EditID,
        @ControlCount = @ControlCount OUTPUT,
        @NullJsonCount = @NullJsonCount OUTPUT,
        @InvalidJsonCount = @InvalidJsonCount OUTPUT;

    IF @ControlCount = 0
        THROW 55022, 'Postflight failed: edit has no active controls.', 1;

    IF @NullJsonCount > 0
        THROW 55023, 'Postflight failed: active edit controls still have NULL/blank ControlProperties.', 1;

    IF @InvalidJsonCount > 0
        THROW 55024, 'Postflight failed: active edit controls still have invalid JSON ControlProperties.', 1;

    SET @SQL = N'
        SELECT @BadDropdownCount = COUNT(1)
        FROM #DropdownMeta dm
        WHERE NOT EXISTS
        (
            SELECT 1
            FROM ' + QUOTENAME(@ConfigDB) + N'.dbo.XStudio_EditControls_Mst_Tbl ec WITH (NOLOCK)
            WHERE ec.ParentID = @EditID
              AND ec.IsDeleted = 0
              AND ec.ControlType = ''selectbox''
              AND
              (
                    (COL_LENGTH(@ConfigDB + ''.dbo.XStudio_EditControls_Mst_Tbl'', ''AttributeID'') IS NOT NULL AND CAST(ec.AttributeID AS varchar(36)) = dm.AttributeID)
                 OR (COL_LENGTH(@ConfigDB + ''.dbo.XStudio_EditControls_Mst_Tbl'', ''ColumnName'') IS NOT NULL AND ec.ColumnName = dm.AttributeName)
              )
              AND JSON_VALUE(CONVERT(nvarchar(max), ec.ControlProperties), ''$.DropdownEntityID'') = dm.DropdownEntityID
              AND JSON_VALUE(CONVERT(nvarchar(max), ec.ControlProperties), ''$.DropdownValueField'') = dm.DropdownValueField
              AND JSON_VALUE(CONVERT(nvarchar(max), ec.ControlProperties), ''$.DropdownTextField'') = dm.DropdownTextField
              AND JSON_VALUE(CONVERT(nvarchar(max), ec.ControlProperties), ''$.DropdownDatabase'') = dm.DropdownDatabase
        );';

    EXEC sys.sp_executesql
        @SQL,
        N'@EditID varchar(36), @ConfigDB sysname, @BadDropdownCount int OUTPUT',
        @EditID = @EditID,
        @ConfigDB = @ConfigDB,
        @BadDropdownCount = @BadDropdownCount OUTPUT;

    IF @BadDropdownCount > 0
        THROW 55025, 'Postflight failed: one or more dropdown edit controls are missing selectbox type or dropdown JSON values.', 1;

    -- ============================================================
    -- 10. RESULT
    -- ============================================================

    SET @SQL = N'
        SELECT
            ec.ID,
            ec.ParentID,
            ec.ControlType,
            ec.ControlName,
            CASE WHEN ec.ControlProperties IS NULL THEN 1 ELSE 0 END AS IsControlPropertiesNull,
            ISJSON(CONVERT(nvarchar(max), ec.ControlProperties)) AS IsValidJson,
            JSON_VALUE(CONVERT(nvarchar(max), ec.ControlProperties), ''$.DropdownEntityID'') AS DropdownEntityID,
            JSON_VALUE(CONVERT(nvarchar(max), ec.ControlProperties), ''$.DropdownValueField'') AS DropdownValueField,
            JSON_VALUE(CONVERT(nvarchar(max), ec.ControlProperties), ''$.DropdownTextField'') AS DropdownTextField
        FROM ' + QUOTENAME(@ConfigDB) + N'.dbo.XStudio_EditControls_Mst_Tbl ec WITH (NOLOCK)
        WHERE ec.ParentID = @EditID
          AND ec.IsDeleted = 0
        ORDER BY ec.ControlType, ec.ControlName;';

    EXEC sys.sp_executesql
        @SQL,
        N'@EditID varchar(36)',
        @EditID = @EditID;

    COMMIT TRANSACTION;

    PRINT 'Edit control safe JSON template completed successfully. Next: bind the edit to a page through XStudio_Save_Page_Control_Configuration_Usp.';

END TRY
BEGIN CATCH

    IF @@TRANCOUNT > 0
        ROLLBACK TRANSACTION;

    DECLARE @ErrorMessage nvarchar(4000) = ERROR_MESSAGE();
    DECLARE @ErrorLine int = ERROR_LINE();
    DECLARE @ErrorNumber int = ERROR_NUMBER();

    RAISERROR('edit_control_safe_json_template.sql failed. Error %d at line %d: %s', 16, 1, @ErrorNumber, @ErrorLine, @ErrorMessage);

END CATCH;
```

## 37. Entity Create Template

**Source file:** `03_SQL_TEMPLATES/entity_create_template.sql`  
**File type:** `.sql`

**SHA256:** `75a4e20a7f56`  
**Encoding used:** `utf-8`

> Start of imported document.

```sql
-- ============================================================
-- Entity Create Template
-- Replace all <PLACEHOLDER> values before executing
-- Run from any database context with access to XStudio_Configuration.
-- ============================================================
--
-- Purpose:
--   Create or validate one XStudio entity and its business attributes
--   correctly through XStudio stored procedures.
--
-- Hard rule:
--   Use XStudio stored procedures for entity and attribute creation.
--
-- This template intentionally DOES NOT create:
--   - LV metadata
--   - Edit metadata
--   - Pages
--   - PageControls
--   - Menus
--   - MenuRights
--
-- Those belong in their own templates/playbooks after the entity and
-- attributes are correct.
--
-- Correct SP surface for this template:
--   - XStudio_Configuration.dbo.XStudio_SaveEntity_Usp
--   - XStudio_Configuration.dbo.XStudio_AddAttribute_Usp
--
-- Do not directly create the physical table.
-- Do not directly insert into XStudio_Entities_Mst_Tbl.
-- Do not directly insert into XStudio_Attribute_Mst_Tbl.
-- Do not directly alter the physical entity table for attributes.
--
-- XStudio_SaveEntity_Usp creates/updates entity metadata and creates
-- the physical table as part of the platform path.
--
-- XStudio_AddAttribute_Usp creates attribute metadata and adds/alters
-- the physical column as part of the platform path.
--
-- ============================================================

SET NOCOUNT ON;
SET XACT_ABORT ON;

BEGIN TRY

    -- ============================================================
    -- 0. CONFIGURATION VARIABLES
    -- ============================================================

    DECLARE @SystemID       varchar(36)  = '6C8C0070-60B5-4474-BEC8-5C02197B32E6';
    DECLARE @ApplicationID  varchar(36)  = '29123B0B-3872-41CF-B5E1-67C4A8F0D4BC';

    DECLARE @EntityName     varchar(100) = '<EntityTableName>';       -- e.g. XMES_MyEntity_Mst_Tbl
    DECLARE @EntityLabel    varchar(200) = '<Entity Display Name>';   -- retained for docs/downstream templates

    DECLARE @TableType      varchar(50)  = 'Master';                  -- Master or Transaction
    DECLARE @IsAuditLog     bit          = 1;
    DECLARE @IsTimeLine     bit          = 0;
    DECLARE @SyncType       varchar(100) = 'None';
    DECLARE @IsChild        bit          = 0;
    DECLARE @ParentEntityID varchar(50)  = NULL;
    DECLARE @IsGroupNotification bit     = 0;
    DECLARE @WorkFlowId     varchar(50)  = NULL;
    DECLARE @WorkFlowStatusId varchar(50)= NULL;
    DECLARE @IsAutoRefresh  bit          = 0;
    DECLARE @RefreshInterVal varchar(20) = '5';
    DECLARE @IsEquipmentEntity bit       = 0;
    DECLARE @EquipmentType  varchar(max) = NULL;

    -- Extended/summary/data-removal/indexing/event/import-export flags.
    -- Keep explicit so the call remains compatible with the 2026-05-08
    -- 34-parameter XStudio_SaveEntity_Usp signature.
    DECLARE @HaveExendendEntity bit = 0;
    DECLARE @ExtendedEntityId varchar(50) = NULL;
    DECLARE @ExtendedEntityReferenceAttributeId varchar(50) = NULL;
    DECLARE @SummaryInterval varchar(50) = '15';
    DECLARE @SummaryScheduleID varchar(50) = NULL;
    DECLARE @BlockType varchar(100) = NULL;
    DECLARE @EnableSummarySchedule bit = 0;
    DECLARE @IsEnableDataRemovingMode bit = 0;
    DECLARE @DataRemovingType varchar(50) = NULL;
    DECLARE @DataRetentionPeriod varchar(20) = '2';
    DECLARE @DataRemovingWhereCondition varchar(max) = NULL;
    DECLARE @EnableDataIndexing bit = 0;
    DECLARE @IsEventEntity bit = 0;
    DECLARE @EventType varchar(100) = NULL;
    DECLARE @EnableImportExportLog bit = 0;
    DECLARE @ImportExportLogAttribute varchar(40) = NULL;
    DECLARE @IsSummaryEntity bit = 0;

    DECLARE @ConfigDB sysname;
    DECLARE @DataDB   sysname;
    DECLARE @EntityID varchar(36);
    DECLARE @SQL      nvarchar(max);

    -- ============================================================
    -- 1. BUSINESS ATTRIBUTE DEFINITIONS
    -- ============================================================
    -- Add only business columns here.
    -- Do not add platform/system columns such as ID, CreatedBy, CreatedOn,
    -- ModifiedBy, ModifiedOn, IsDeleted, IsSystem, Source, HostAddress.
    --
    -- DataTypeID may be supplied directly. If NULL, DataTypeName is resolved
    -- from XStudio_Configuration.dbo.XStudio_DataType_Mst_Tbl.
    --
    -- Common DataTypeName examples must be confirmed in the live DB:
    --   varchar / nvarchar / int / decimal / datetime / bit / Dropdown
    --
    -- For dropdown attributes, create only the base attribute here. Then use
    -- dropdown_properties_template.sql to create the complete dropdown property
    -- and relation set.

    DECLARE @AttributeDef TABLE
    (
        SortNo             int IDENTITY(1,1) NOT NULL,
        Name               varchar(100) NOT NULL,
        DisplayName        varchar(200) NOT NULL,
        DataTypeName       varchar(100) NULL,
        DataTypeID         varchar(36) NULL,
        IsRequired         bit NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
        IsUnique           bit NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
        IsSearchable       bit NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
        IsCalculatedColumn bit NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
        Description        varchar(max) NULL
    );

    -- TODO: Replace sample rows.
    INSERT INTO @AttributeDef
    (
        Name,
        DisplayName,
        DataTypeName,
        DataTypeID,
        IsRequired,
        IsUnique,
        IsSearchable,
        IsCalculatedColumn,
        Description
    )
    VALUES
        ('Code',        'Code',        'varchar', NULL, 1, 1, 1, 0, 'Business code'),
        ('Description', 'Description', 'varchar', NULL, 0, 0, 1, 0, 'Description');

    -- ============================================================
    -- 2. BASIC INPUT VALIDATION
    -- ============================================================

    IF @SystemID IS NULL OR TRY_CONVERT(uniqueidentifier, @SystemID) IS NULL
        THROW 53000, 'Invalid @SystemID.', 1;

    IF @ApplicationID IS NULL OR TRY_CONVERT(uniqueidentifier, @ApplicationID) IS NULL
        THROW 53001, 'Invalid @ApplicationID.', 1;

    IF @EntityName IS NULL OR LTRIM(RTRIM(@EntityName)) = ''
        THROW 53002, 'Entity name is required.', 1;

    IF LEN(@EntityName) > 100
        THROW 53003, 'Entity name exceeds XStudio_SaveEntity_Usp @EntityName varchar(100).', 1;

    IF @EntityName LIKE '%[^A-Za-z0-9_]%'
        THROW 53004, 'Entity name must contain only letters, numbers, and underscores.', 1;

    IF @EntityName IN
    (
        'ID',
        'CreatedBy',
        'CreatedOn',
        'ModifiedBy',
        'ModifiedOn',
        'IsDeleted',
        'IsSystem',
        'Source',
        'HostAddress'
    )
        THROW 53005, 'Entity name cannot be a platform column name.', 1;

    IF @TableType NOT IN ('Master', 'Transaction')
        THROW 53006, '@TableType must be Master or Transaction.', 1;

    IF EXISTS
    (
        SELECT 1
        FROM @AttributeDef
        WHERE Name IS NULL
           OR LTRIM(RTRIM(Name)) = ''
           OR LEN(Name) > 100
           OR Name LIKE '%[^A-Za-z0-9_]%'
    )
        THROW 53007, 'Every attribute Name must be non-empty, <=100 chars, and contain only letters, numbers, and underscores.', 1;

    IF EXISTS
    (
        SELECT Name
        FROM @AttributeDef
        GROUP BY Name
        HAVING COUNT(*) > 1
    )
        THROW 53008, 'Duplicate attribute names in @AttributeDef.', 1;

    IF EXISTS
    (
        SELECT 1
        FROM @AttributeDef
        WHERE Name IN
        (
            'ID',
            'CreatedBy',
            'CreatedOn',
            'ModifiedBy',
            'ModifiedOn',
            'IsDeleted',
            'IsSystem',
            'Source',
            'HostAddress'
        )
    )
        THROW 53009, 'Do not define platform/system columns in @AttributeDef.', 1;

    -- ============================================================
    -- 3. RESOLVE LIVE XSTUDIO DATABASES
    -- ============================================================

    SELECT
        @ConfigDB = ConfigurationDatabase,
        @DataDB = DataDatabase
    FROM XStudio_Configuration.dbo.XStudio_GetDataSourceInfoFromSystemId_Fun(@SystemID);

    IF @ConfigDB IS NULL OR @DataDB IS NULL
        THROW 53010, 'Could not resolve ConfigDB/DataDB from @SystemID.', 1;

    IF DB_ID(@ConfigDB) IS NULL
        THROW 53011, 'Resolved configuration database does not exist.', 1;

    IF DB_ID(@DataDB) IS NULL
        THROW 53012, 'Resolved data database does not exist.', 1;

    IF NOT EXISTS
    (
        SELECT 1
        FROM XStudio_Configuration.dbo.XStudio_Application_Mst_Tbl
        WHERE ID = @ApplicationID
          AND IsDeleted = 0
    )
    BEGIN
        SET @SQL = N'
            IF NOT EXISTS
            (
                SELECT 1
                FROM ' + QUOTENAME(@ConfigDB) + N'.dbo.XStudio_Application_Mst_Tbl
                WHERE ID = @ApplicationID
                  AND IsDeleted = 0
            )
                THROW 53013, ''ApplicationID not found in the resolved configuration database.'', 1;';

        EXEC sys.sp_executesql
            @SQL,
            N'@ApplicationID varchar(36)',
            @ApplicationID = @ApplicationID;
    END;

    -- ============================================================
    -- 4. LIVE SCHEMA AND SP SIGNATURE PREFLIGHT
    -- ============================================================
    -- These checks intentionally inspect the live XStudio schema before
    -- relying on column names or SP parameters.

    IF
    (
        SELECT COUNT(1)
        FROM XStudio_Configuration.sys.parameters p
        JOIN XStudio_Configuration.sys.procedures pr
          ON pr.object_id = p.object_id
        WHERE pr.name = 'XStudio_SaveEntity_Usp'
          AND p.name IN
          (
              '@EntityName',
              '@SystemId',
              '@ApplicationId',
              '@EntityID',
              '@IsAuditLog',
              '@IsTimeLine',
              '@SyncType',
              '@TableType',
              '@IsChild',
              '@ParentEntityID',
              '@IsGroupNotification',
              '@WorkFlowId',
              '@WorkFlowStatusId',
              '@RefreshInterVal',
              '@IsAutoRefresh',
              '@IsEquipmentEntity',
              '@EquipmentType'
          )
    ) < 17
        THROW 53014, 'XStudio_SaveEntity_Usp signature does not match the expected entity-create surface.', 1;

    IF
    (
        SELECT COUNT(1)
        FROM XStudio_Configuration.sys.parameters p
        JOIN XStudio_Configuration.sys.procedures pr
          ON pr.object_id = p.object_id
        WHERE pr.name = 'XStudio_AddAttribute_Usp'
          AND p.name IN
          (
              '@ID',
              '@EntityID',
              '@Name',
              '@DataTypeID',
              '@IsRequired',
              '@IsUnique',
              '@IsSearchable',
              '@IsCalculatedColumn',
              '@DisplayName',
              '@SystemID',
              '@Description'
          )
    ) <> 11
        THROW 53015, 'XStudio_AddAttribute_Usp signature does not match the expected 11-parameter surface.', 1;

    SET @SQL = N'
        IF NOT EXISTS
        (
            SELECT 1
            FROM ' + QUOTENAME(@ConfigDB) + N'.sys.tables t
            JOIN ' + QUOTENAME(@ConfigDB) + N'.sys.schemas s
              ON s.schema_id = t.schema_id
            WHERE s.name = ''dbo''
              AND t.name = ''XStudio_Entities_Mst_Tbl''
        )
            THROW 53016, ''XStudio_Entities_Mst_Tbl missing in resolved ConfigDB.'', 1;

        IF NOT EXISTS
        (
            SELECT 1
            FROM ' + QUOTENAME(@ConfigDB) + N'.sys.tables t
            JOIN ' + QUOTENAME(@ConfigDB) + N'.sys.schemas s
              ON s.schema_id = t.schema_id
            WHERE s.name = ''dbo''
              AND t.name = ''XStudio_Attribute_Mst_Tbl''
        )
            THROW 53017, ''XStudio_Attribute_Mst_Tbl missing in resolved ConfigDB.'', 1;

        IF
        (
            SELECT COUNT(1)
            FROM ' + QUOTENAME(@ConfigDB) + N'.sys.columns c
            JOIN ' + QUOTENAME(@ConfigDB) + N'.sys.tables t
              ON t.object_id = c.object_id
            JOIN ' + QUOTENAME(@ConfigDB) + N'.sys.schemas s
              ON s.schema_id = t.schema_id
            WHERE s.name = ''dbo''
              AND t.name = ''XStudio_Entities_Mst_Tbl''
              AND c.name IN
              (
                  ''ID'',
                  ''Name'',
                  ''ParentID'',
                  ''IsDeleted'',
                  ''TableType'',
                  ''IsChild'',
                  ''ParentEntityID'',
                  ''IsAuditLog'',
                  ''IsTimeLine'',
                  ''IsAutoRefresh'',
                  ''RefreshInterVal''
              )
        ) < 11
            THROW 53018, ''XStudio_Entities_Mst_Tbl core columns missing or renamed.'', 1;

        IF
        (
            SELECT COUNT(1)
            FROM ' + QUOTENAME(@ConfigDB) + N'.sys.columns c
            JOIN ' + QUOTENAME(@ConfigDB) + N'.sys.tables t
              ON t.object_id = c.object_id
            JOIN ' + QUOTENAME(@ConfigDB) + N'.sys.schemas s
              ON s.schema_id = t.schema_id
            WHERE s.name = ''dbo''
              AND t.name = ''XStudio_Attribute_Mst_Tbl''
              AND c.name IN
              (
                  ''ID'',
                  ''Name'',
                  ''DisplayName'',
                  ''DataType'',
                  ''ParentID'',
                  ''IsRequired'',
                  ''IsUnique'',
                  ''IsSearchable'',
                  ''IsCalculatedColumn'',
                  ''IsDeleted''
              )
        ) < 10
            THROW 53019, ''XStudio_Attribute_Mst_Tbl core columns missing or renamed.'', 1;';

    EXEC sys.sp_executesql @SQL;

    -- ============================================================
    -- 5. RESOLVE DATA TYPES
    -- ============================================================

    DECLARE @ResolvedAttribute TABLE
    (
        SortNo             int NOT NULL PRIMARY KEY,
        Name               varchar(100) NOT NULL,
        DisplayName        varchar(200) NOT NULL,
        DataTypeName       varchar(100) NULL,
        DataTypeID         varchar(36) NOT NULL,
        IsRequired         bit NOT NULL,
        IsUnique           bit NOT NULL,
        IsSearchable       bit NOT NULL,
        IsCalculatedColumn bit NOT NULL,
        Description        varchar(max) NULL
    );

    INSERT INTO @ResolvedAttribute
    (
        SortNo,
        Name,
        DisplayName,
        DataTypeName,
        DataTypeID,
        IsRequired,
        IsUnique,
        IsSearchable,
        IsCalculatedColumn,
        Description
    )
    SELECT
        ad.SortNo,
        ad.Name,
        ad.DisplayName,
        ad.DataTypeName,
        COALESCE
        (
            ad.DataTypeID,
            CAST(dt_by_name.ID AS varchar(36)),
            CAST(dt_by_actual.ID AS varchar(36))
        ) AS DataTypeID,
        ad.IsRequired,
        ad.IsUnique,
        ad.IsSearchable,
        ad.IsCalculatedColumn,
        ad.Description
    FROM @AttributeDef ad
    LEFT JOIN XStudio_Configuration.dbo.XStudio_DataType_Mst_Tbl dt_by_name
      ON dt_by_name.Name = ad.DataTypeName
     AND dt_by_name.IsDeleted = 0
    LEFT JOIN XStudio_Configuration.dbo.XStudio_DataType_Mst_Tbl dt_by_actual
      ON dt_by_actual.ActualDataType = ad.DataTypeName
     AND dt_by_actual.IsDeleted = 0;

    IF EXISTS
    (
        SELECT 1
        FROM @ResolvedAttribute
        WHERE DataTypeID IS NULL
           OR TRY_CONVERT(uniqueidentifier, DataTypeID) IS NULL
    )
        THROW 53020, 'One or more attributes could not resolve to a valid XStudio data type.', 1;

    -- ============================================================
    -- 6. GUARD AGAINST SPLIT-BRAIN ENTITY/TABLE STATES
    -- ============================================================
    -- Correct path creates both metadata and physical table through
    -- XStudio_SaveEntity_Usp. If a physical table already exists but
    -- metadata does not, stop instead of repairing by direct insert.

    SET @SQL = N'
        DECLARE @MetadataExists bit = 0;
        DECLARE @PhysicalExists bit = 0;

        IF EXISTS
        (
            SELECT 1
            FROM ' + QUOTENAME(@ConfigDB) + N'.dbo.XStudio_Entities_Mst_Tbl WITH (NOLOCK)
            WHERE Name = @EntityName
              AND IsDeleted = 0
        )
            SET @MetadataExists = 1;

        IF OBJECT_ID(QUOTENAME(@DataDB) + ''.dbo.'' + QUOTENAME(@EntityName), ''U'') IS NOT NULL
            SET @PhysicalExists = 1;

        IF @MetadataExists = 0 AND @PhysicalExists = 1
            THROW 53021, ''Physical table exists but XStudio entity metadata is missing. Stop and use exceptional recovery, not this create template.'', 1;';

    EXEC sys.sp_executesql
        @SQL,
        N'@EntityName varchar(100), @DataDB sysname',
        @EntityName = @EntityName,
        @DataDB = @DataDB;

    -- ============================================================
    -- 7. CREATE OR UPDATE ENTITY THROUGH XSTUDIO SP
    -- ============================================================

    EXEC XStudio_Configuration.dbo.XStudio_SaveEntity_Usp
        @EntityName = @EntityName,
        @SystemId = @SystemID,
        @ApplicationId = @ApplicationID,
        @EntityID = N'0',
        @IsAuditLog = @IsAuditLog,
        @IsTimeLine = @IsTimeLine,
        @SyncType = @SyncType,
        @TableType = @TableType,
        @IsChild = @IsChild,
        @ParentEntityID = @ParentEntityID,
        @IsGroupNotification = @IsGroupNotification,
        @WorkFlowId = @WorkFlowId,
        @WorkFlowStatusId = @WorkFlowStatusId,
        @RefreshInterVal = @RefreshInterVal,
        @IsAutoRefresh = @IsAutoRefresh,
        @IsEquipmentEntity = @IsEquipmentEntity,
        @EquipmentType = @EquipmentType,
        @HaveExendendEntity = @HaveExendendEntity,
        @ExtendedEntityId = @ExtendedEntityId,
        @ExtendedEntityReferenceAttributeId = @ExtendedEntityReferenceAttributeId,
        @SummaryInterval = @SummaryInterval,
        @SummaryScheduleID = @SummaryScheduleID,
        @BlockType = @BlockType,
        @EnableSummarySchedule = @EnableSummarySchedule,
        @IsEnableDataRemovingMode = @IsEnableDataRemovingMode,
        @DataRemovingType = @DataRemovingType,
        @DataRetentionPeriod = @DataRetentionPeriod,
        @DataRemovingWhereCondition = @DataRemovingWhereCondition,
        @EnableDataIndexing = @EnableDataIndexing,
        @IsEventEntity = @IsEventEntity,
        @EventType = @EventType,
        @EnableImportExportLog = @EnableImportExportLog,
        @ImportExportLogAttribute = @ImportExportLogAttribute,
        @IsSummaryEntity = @IsSummaryEntity;

    SET @SQL = N'
        SELECT TOP (1)
            @EntityID = CAST(ID AS varchar(36))
        FROM ' + QUOTENAME(@ConfigDB) + N'.dbo.XStudio_Entities_Mst_Tbl WITH (NOLOCK)
        WHERE Name = @EntityName
          AND IsDeleted = 0;';

    EXEC sys.sp_executesql
        @SQL,
        N'@EntityName varchar(100), @EntityID varchar(36) OUTPUT',
        @EntityName = @EntityName,
        @EntityID = @EntityID OUTPUT;

    IF @EntityID IS NULL
        THROW 53022, 'XStudio_SaveEntity_Usp completed but entity metadata could not be resolved.', 1;

    -- ============================================================
    -- 8. ADD MISSING ATTRIBUTES THROUGH XSTUDIO SP
    -- ============================================================
    -- Existing attributes are validated, not silently changed. Changing
    -- DataType or Name after creation is a migration and should be handled
    -- by a dedicated migration script, not by this create template.

    DECLARE
        @AttrSortNo int,
        @AttrName varchar(100),
        @AttrDisplayName varchar(200),
        @AttrDataTypeID varchar(36),
        @AttrIsRequired bit,
        @AttrIsUnique bit,
        @AttrIsSearchable bit,
        @AttrIsCalculatedColumn bit,
        @AttrDescription varchar(max),
        @ExistingAttrID varchar(36),
        @ExistingDataTypeID varchar(36);

    DECLARE attr_cursor CURSOR LOCAL FAST_FORWARD FOR
        SELECT
            SortNo,
            Name,
            DisplayName,
            DataTypeID,
            IsRequired,
            IsUnique,
            IsSearchable,
            IsCalculatedColumn,
            Description
        FROM @ResolvedAttribute
        ORDER BY SortNo;

    OPEN attr_cursor;

    FETCH NEXT FROM attr_cursor INTO
        @AttrSortNo,
        @AttrName,
        @AttrDisplayName,
        @AttrDataTypeID,
        @AttrIsRequired,
        @AttrIsUnique,
        @AttrIsSearchable,
        @AttrIsCalculatedColumn,
        @AttrDescription;

    WHILE @@FETCH_STATUS = 0
    BEGIN
        SET @ExistingAttrID = NULL;
        SET @ExistingDataTypeID = NULL;

        SET @SQL = N'
            SELECT TOP (1)
                @ExistingAttrID = CAST(ID AS varchar(36)),
                @ExistingDataTypeID = CAST(DataType AS varchar(36))
            FROM ' + QUOTENAME(@ConfigDB) + N'.dbo.XStudio_Attribute_Mst_Tbl WITH (NOLOCK)
            WHERE ParentID = @EntityID
              AND Name = @AttrName
              AND IsDeleted = 0;';

        EXEC sys.sp_executesql
            @SQL,
            N'@EntityID varchar(36), @AttrName varchar(100), @ExistingAttrID varchar(36) OUTPUT, @ExistingDataTypeID varchar(36) OUTPUT',
            @EntityID = @EntityID,
            @AttrName = @AttrName,
            @ExistingAttrID = @ExistingAttrID OUTPUT,
            @ExistingDataTypeID = @ExistingDataTypeID OUTPUT;

        IF @ExistingAttrID IS NULL
        BEGIN
            EXEC XStudio_Configuration.dbo.XStudio_AddAttribute_Usp
                @ID = '0',
                @EntityID = @EntityID,
                @Name = @AttrName,
                @DataTypeID = @AttrDataTypeID,
                @IsRequired = @AttrIsRequired,
                @IsUnique = @AttrIsUnique,
                @IsSearchable = @AttrIsSearchable,
                @IsCalculatedColumn = @AttrIsCalculatedColumn,
                @DisplayName = @AttrDisplayName,
                @SystemID = @SystemID,
                @Description = @AttrDescription;
        END
        ELSE IF @ExistingDataTypeID <> @AttrDataTypeID
        BEGIN
            DECLARE @MismatchMessage nvarchar(4000) =
                N'Existing attribute DataType mismatch for ' + @AttrName
                + N'. Existing=' + ISNULL(@ExistingDataTypeID, N'<NULL>')
                + N', Requested=' + ISNULL(@AttrDataTypeID, N'<NULL>')
                + N'. Stop and use an explicit migration script.';
            THROW 53023, @MismatchMessage, 1;
        END;

        FETCH NEXT FROM attr_cursor INTO
            @AttrSortNo,
            @AttrName,
            @AttrDisplayName,
            @AttrDataTypeID,
            @AttrIsRequired,
            @AttrIsUnique,
            @AttrIsSearchable,
            @AttrIsCalculatedColumn,
            @AttrDescription;
    END;

    CLOSE attr_cursor;
    DEALLOCATE attr_cursor;

    -- ============================================================
    -- 9. POSTFLIGHT
    -- ============================================================

    SET @SQL = N'
        IF NOT EXISTS
        (
            SELECT 1
            FROM ' + QUOTENAME(@ConfigDB) + N'.dbo.XStudio_Entities_Mst_Tbl WITH (NOLOCK)
            WHERE ID = @EntityID
              AND Name = @EntityName
              AND ParentID = @ApplicationID
              AND IsDeleted = 0
        )
            THROW 53024, ''Postflight failed: entity metadata missing or ParentID is not ApplicationID.'', 1;

        IF OBJECT_ID(QUOTENAME(@DataDB) + ''.dbo.'' + QUOTENAME(@EntityName), ''U'') IS NULL
            THROW 53025, ''Postflight failed: physical entity table was not created in DataDB.'', 1;';

    EXEC sys.sp_executesql
        @SQL,
        N'@EntityID varchar(36), @EntityName varchar(100), @ApplicationID varchar(36), @DataDB sysname',
        @EntityID = @EntityID,
        @EntityName = @EntityName,
        @ApplicationID = @ApplicationID,
        @DataDB = @DataDB;

    DECLARE
        @MissingAttrCount int = 0,
        @MissingColumnCount int = 0;

    SET @SQL = N'
        SELECT @MissingAttrCount = COUNT(1)
        FROM @Attr AS src
        WHERE NOT EXISTS
        (
            SELECT 1
            FROM ' + QUOTENAME(@ConfigDB) + N'.dbo.XStudio_Attribute_Mst_Tbl a WITH (NOLOCK)
            WHERE a.ParentID = @EntityID
              AND a.Name = src.Name
              AND a.IsDeleted = 0
        );';

    -- Table-valued variables cannot be passed to dynamic SQL without a type,
    -- so use a temp table for postflight checks.
    IF OBJECT_ID('tempdb..#ExpectedAttribute') IS NOT NULL
        DROP TABLE #ExpectedAttribute;

    SELECT
        Name,
        DisplayName,
        DataTypeID
    INTO #ExpectedAttribute
    FROM @ResolvedAttribute;

    SET @SQL = N'
        SELECT @MissingAttrCount = COUNT(1)
        FROM #ExpectedAttribute src
        WHERE NOT EXISTS
        (
            SELECT 1
            FROM ' + QUOTENAME(@ConfigDB) + N'.dbo.XStudio_Attribute_Mst_Tbl a WITH (NOLOCK)
            WHERE a.ParentID = @EntityID
              AND a.Name = src.Name
              AND a.IsDeleted = 0
        );

        SELECT @MissingColumnCount = COUNT(1)
        FROM #ExpectedAttribute src
        WHERE COL_LENGTH(QUOTENAME(@DataDB) + ''.dbo.'' + QUOTENAME(@EntityName), src.Name) IS NULL;';

    EXEC sys.sp_executesql
        @SQL,
        N'@EntityID varchar(36), @EntityName varchar(100), @DataDB sysname, @MissingAttrCount int OUTPUT, @MissingColumnCount int OUTPUT',
        @EntityID = @EntityID,
        @EntityName = @EntityName,
        @DataDB = @DataDB,
        @MissingAttrCount = @MissingAttrCount OUTPUT,
        @MissingColumnCount = @MissingColumnCount OUTPUT;

    IF @MissingAttrCount > 0
        THROW 53026, 'Postflight failed: one or more expected attributes are missing from XStudio_Attribute_Mst_Tbl.', 1;

    IF @MissingColumnCount > 0
        THROW 53027, 'Postflight failed: one or more expected physical columns are missing from the data table.', 1;

    -- ============================================================
    -- 10. RESULT
    -- ============================================================

    SET @SQL = N'
        SELECT
            ''Entity'' AS ObjectType,
            e.ID,
            e.Name,
            e.ParentID,
            e.TableType,
            e.IsAuditLog,
            e.IsTimeLine,
            e.IsAutoRefresh,
            e.RefreshInterVal
        FROM ' + QUOTENAME(@ConfigDB) + N'.dbo.XStudio_Entities_Mst_Tbl e WITH (NOLOCK)
        WHERE e.ID = @EntityID

        UNION ALL

        SELECT
            ''Attribute'' AS ObjectType,
            a.ID,
            a.Name,
            a.ParentID,
            CAST(NULL AS varchar(50)) AS TableType,
            CAST(NULL AS bit) AS IsAuditLog,
            CAST(NULL AS bit) AS IsTimeLine,
            CAST(NULL AS bit) AS IsAutoRefresh,
            CAST(NULL AS varchar(20)) AS RefreshInterVal
        FROM ' + QUOTENAME(@ConfigDB) + N'.dbo.XStudio_Attribute_Mst_Tbl a WITH (NOLOCK)
        JOIN #ExpectedAttribute src
          ON src.Name = a.Name
        WHERE a.ParentID = @EntityID
          AND a.IsDeleted = 0
        ORDER BY ObjectType, Name;';

    EXEC sys.sp_executesql
        @SQL,
        N'@EntityID varchar(36)',
        @EntityID = @EntityID;

    PRINT 'Entity create template completed successfully. Next: configure dropdown properties if needed, then LV/edit/page/menu using the dedicated templates.';

END TRY
BEGIN CATCH

    IF CURSOR_STATUS('local', 'attr_cursor') >= -1
    BEGIN
        CLOSE attr_cursor;
        DEALLOCATE attr_cursor;
    END;

    DECLARE @ErrorMessage nvarchar(4000) = ERROR_MESSAGE();
    DECLARE @ErrorLine int = ERROR_LINE();
    DECLARE @ErrorNumber int = ERROR_NUMBER();

    RAISERROR('entity_create_template.sql failed. Error %d at line %d: %s', 16, 1, @ErrorNumber, @ErrorLine, @ErrorMessage);

END CATCH;
```
