## 38. Lv Link Column Template

**Source file:** `03_SQL_TEMPLATES/lv_link_column_template.sql`  
**File type:** `.sql`  
**Size:** `32037` bytes  
**Modified on:** `2026-05-12 11:24:40`  
**SHA256:** `91fa26d132af`  
**Encoding used:** `utf-8`

> Start of imported document.

```sql
-- ============================================================
-- LV Link Column Template
-- Add or update one LV navigation/action link column safely
-- ============================================================
--
-- Purpose:
--   Add or update a pure navigation/action link column on an existing LV,
--   then regenerate the LV view with the confirmed XStudio_CreateViewForLV_Usp
--   signature.
--
-- This template covers work intentionally removed from
-- entity_create_template.sql:
--   - LV action/navigation link-column configuration
--   - LV column renderer-safe ColumnProperties
--   - LV view regeneration after column changes
--
-- Hard rule:
--   Use XStudio stored procedures where an XStudio SP exists.
--
-- Confirmed SP for this template:
--   XStudio_Configuration.dbo.XStudio_CreateViewForLV_Usp
--
-- Important SP finding from the 2026-05-08 reference:
--   XStudio_CreateViewForLV_Usp has 8 parameters:
--     @LvID
--     @LvName
--     @DataDatabase
--     @ColumnQuery
--     @RelationQuery
--     @GroupByQuery
--     @WhereCondition
--     @IsCalculatedView
--
--   Its body concatenates @ColumnQuery + @RelationQuery + @WhereCondition
--   into CREATE OR ALTER VIEW. Therefore NULL fragments are unsafe.
--
-- No complete generic "save LV column configuration" SP was found in the
-- current stored-procedure reference. Therefore the LV column write below is
-- a documented exception: guarded, schema-inspected DML against
-- XStudio_LvColumns_Mst_Tbl.
--
-- Do not use this template to create the entity, attributes, LV header, page,
-- PageControl, menu, or menu rights.
--
-- Execution sequence:
--   1. Create entity/attributes with entity_create_template.sql.
--   2. Create/resolve LV header and normal LV columns with the LV template/playbook.
--   3. Run this file for each action/navigation link column.
--   4. Bind the LV to a page using page_menu_template.sql.
--
-- ============================================================

SET NOCOUNT ON;
SET XACT_ABORT ON;

BEGIN TRY
    BEGIN TRANSACTION;

    -- ============================================================
    -- 0. INPUTS
    -- ============================================================

    DECLARE @SystemID varchar(36) = '6C8C0070-60B5-4474-BEC8-5C02197B32E6';

    DECLARE @LVID varchar(36) = '<lv_guid>';

    -- Link column identity.
    -- Common action names: Edit, Delete, Open, WOProduction, WOConsumption.
    DECLARE @ColName varchar(100) = 'Edit';
    DECLARE @ColHeader varchar(200) = 'Edit';
    DECLARE @LinkLabel varchar(100) = 'Edit';
    DECLARE @SerialNumber int = 999;

    -- Role visibility. NULL or empty = unrestricted.
    DECLARE @RoleIDList varchar(max) = NULL;

    -- Link target behavior.
    -- Hard rule for Edit/Delete action columns in this KB:
    --   if @ColName is Edit or Delete, @CustomLink should be the target page ID.
    -- For other navigation columns, either use a target page ID or a platform URL.
    DECLARE @TargetPageID varchar(36) = '<target_page_guid>';
    DECLARE @UsePageIdAsCustomLink bit = 1;

    -- Optional URL mode for non-Edit/Delete navigation columns.
    DECLARE @SystemName varchar(100) = 'XMES';
    DECLARE @ApplicationName varchar(100) = 'DEFAULT';
    DECLARE @TargetPageName varchar(200) = '<target_page_name>';
    DECLARE @URLParam varchar(500) = 'ID={ID}';
    DECLARE @TargetMode varchar(50) = 'Inside'; -- Inside or Popup

    -- Explicit LV view fragments.
    -- These must be the full fragments for the LV after this link column exists.
    -- Do not pass NULL. Do not rely on @LvID-only regeneration.
    --
    -- @ColumnQuery must include an alias for @ColName, for example:
    --   SELECT t.[ID], t.[WONo], 'Edit' AS [Edit]
    --
    -- @RelationQuery example:
    --   FROM [XStudio_XMES].dbo.[XMES_Work_Order_Mst_Tbl] t
    --
    -- @WhereCondition example:
    --   WHERE ISNULL(t.IsDeleted,0)=0
    DECLARE @ColumnQuery varchar(max) = '<SELECT ... including link column alias>';
    DECLARE @RelationQuery varchar(max) = '<FROM ...>';
    DECLARE @GroupByQuery varchar(max) = '';
    DECLARE @WhereCondition varchar(max) = '<WHERE ...>';
    DECLARE @IsCalculatedView bit = 0;

    -- Use a real XStudio user GUID. Do not use 'sa'.
    DECLARE @UserID varchar(36) = '<deployment_user_guid>';
    DECLARE @IPAddress varchar(100) = 'T-SQL';

    DECLARE @ConfigDB sysname;
    DECLARE @DataDB sysname;
    DECLARE @SQL nvarchar(max);

    -- ============================================================
    -- 1. RESOLVE LIVE DATABASES
    -- ============================================================

    SELECT
        @ConfigDB = ConfigurationDatabase,
        @DataDB = DataDatabase
    FROM XStudio_Configuration.dbo.XStudio_GetDataSourceInfoFromSystemId_Fun(@SystemID);

    IF @ConfigDB IS NULL OR @DataDB IS NULL
        THROW 56000, 'Could not resolve ConfigDB/DataDB from @SystemID.', 1;

    IF DB_ID(@ConfigDB) IS NULL
        THROW 56001, 'Resolved configuration database does not exist.', 1;

    IF DB_ID(@DataDB) IS NULL
        THROW 56002, 'Resolved data database does not exist.', 1;

    -- ============================================================
    -- 2. INPUT VALIDATION
    -- ============================================================

    IF TRY_CONVERT(uniqueidentifier, @SystemID) IS NULL
        THROW 56003, 'Invalid @SystemID.', 1;

    IF TRY_CONVERT(uniqueidentifier, @LVID) IS NULL
        THROW 56004, 'Invalid @LVID.', 1;

    IF @UserID IS NULL OR @UserID = '<deployment_user_guid>' OR TRY_CONVERT(uniqueidentifier, @UserID) IS NULL
        THROW 56005, 'Set @UserID to a real XStudio user GUID before running this template.', 1;

    IF @ColName IS NULL OR LTRIM(RTRIM(@ColName)) = '' OR @ColName LIKE '%[^A-Za-z0-9_]%'
        THROW 56006, '@ColName must be a simple column identifier with no spaces.', 1;

    IF @ColHeader IS NULL OR LTRIM(RTRIM(@ColHeader)) = ''
        THROW 56007, '@ColHeader is required.', 1;

    IF @LinkLabel IS NULL OR LTRIM(RTRIM(@LinkLabel)) = ''
        THROW 56008, '@LinkLabel is required.', 1;

    IF @SerialNumber IS NULL OR @SerialNumber < 0
        THROW 56009, '@SerialNumber must be >= 0.', 1;

    IF @TargetMode NOT IN ('Inside', 'Popup')
        THROW 56010, '@TargetMode must be Inside or Popup.', 1;

    IF LOWER(@ColName) IN ('edit', 'delete')
       AND (TRY_CONVERT(uniqueidentifier, @TargetPageID) IS NULL OR @UsePageIdAsCustomLink <> 1)
        THROW 56011, 'Edit/Delete action columns must use the target page ID as CustomLink.', 1;

    IF @UsePageIdAsCustomLink = 1 AND TRY_CONVERT(uniqueidentifier, @TargetPageID) IS NULL
        THROW 56012, '@TargetPageID must be a page GUID when @UsePageIdAsCustomLink = 1.', 1;

    IF @UsePageIdAsCustomLink = 0
    BEGIN
        IF @SystemName IS NULL OR @ApplicationName IS NULL OR @TargetPageName IS NULL OR @URLParam IS NULL
            THROW 56013, 'URL mode requires @SystemName, @ApplicationName, @TargetPageName, and @URLParam.', 1;
    END;

    IF @ColumnQuery IS NULL
       OR @RelationQuery IS NULL
       OR @WhereCondition IS NULL
       OR @ColumnQuery LIKE '<SELECT%'
       OR @RelationQuery LIKE '<FROM%'
       OR @WhereCondition LIKE '<WHERE%'
        THROW 56014, 'Set explicit non-placeholder @ColumnQuery, @RelationQuery, and @WhereCondition before running.', 1;

    IF @ColumnQuery NOT LIKE '%[' + @ColName + ']%'
        THROW 56015, '@ColumnQuery must include an alias for the link column @ColName.', 1;

    -- ============================================================
    -- 3. SP AND SCHEMA PREFLIGHT
    -- ============================================================

    IF OBJECT_ID('XStudio_Configuration.dbo.XStudio_CreateViewForLV_Usp', 'P') IS NULL
        THROW 56016, 'XStudio_CreateViewForLV_Usp is missing.', 1;

    IF
    (
        SELECT COUNT(1)
        FROM XStudio_Configuration.sys.parameters p
        WHERE p.object_id = OBJECT_ID('XStudio_Configuration.dbo.XStudio_CreateViewForLV_Usp')
          AND p.name IN
          (
              '@LvID',
              '@LvName',
              '@DataDatabase',
              '@ColumnQuery',
              '@RelationQuery',
              '@GroupByQuery',
              '@WhereCondition',
              '@IsCalculatedView'
          )
    ) <> 8
        THROW 56017, 'XStudio_CreateViewForLV_Usp signature does not match the confirmed 8-parameter surface.', 1;

    -- Fail closed if a real LV-column save SP exists in a future build.
    -- Current 2026-05-08 reference does not expose a complete generic save SP
    -- for XStudio_LvColumns_Mst_Tbl, so guarded DML below is the documented exception.
    IF EXISTS
    (
        SELECT 1
        FROM XStudio_Configuration.sys.procedures
        WHERE
        (
              name LIKE '%Save%Lv%Column%'
           OR name LIKE '%Save%LV%Column%'
           OR name LIKE '%Save%LvColumns%'
           OR name LIKE '%Save%LVColumns%'
        )
        AND name NOT LIKE '%Get%'
    )
        THROW 56018, 'A possible LV-column save SP exists in this environment. Inspect and use that SP instead of this guarded DML template.', 1;

    SET @SQL = N'
        IF NOT EXISTS
        (
            SELECT 1
            FROM ' + QUOTENAME(@ConfigDB) + N'.sys.tables t
            JOIN ' + QUOTENAME(@ConfigDB) + N'.sys.schemas s
              ON s.schema_id = t.schema_id
            WHERE s.name = ''dbo''
              AND t.name = ''XStudio_LvColumns_Mst_Tbl''
        )
            THROW 56019, ''XStudio_LvColumns_Mst_Tbl missing in ConfigDB.'', 1;

        IF
        (
            SELECT COUNT(1)
            FROM ' + QUOTENAME(@ConfigDB) + N'.sys.columns c
            JOIN ' + QUOTENAME(@ConfigDB) + N'.sys.tables t
              ON t.object_id = c.object_id
            JOIN ' + QUOTENAME(@ConfigDB) + N'.sys.schemas s
              ON s.schema_id = t.schema_id
            WHERE s.name = ''dbo''
              AND t.name = ''XStudio_LvColumns_Mst_Tbl''
              AND c.name IN
              (
                  ''ParentID'',
                  ''Name'',
                  ''Header'',
                  ''Type'',
                  ''SerialNumber'',
                  ''IsVisible'',
                  ''IsCustomField'',
                  ''ColumnProperties'',
                  ''IsDeleted''
              )
        ) < 9
            THROW 56020, ''LvColumns core columns missing or renamed.'', 1;

        IF NOT EXISTS
        (
            SELECT 1
            FROM ' + QUOTENAME(@ConfigDB) + N'.dbo.XStudio_LV_Mst_Tbl WITH (NOLOCK)
            WHERE ID = @LVID
              AND IsDeleted = 0
        )
            THROW 56021, ''LV not found in ConfigDB.'', 1;';

    EXEC sys.sp_executesql
        @SQL,
        N'@LVID varchar(36)',
        @LVID = @LVID;

    IF @UsePageIdAsCustomLink = 1
    BEGIN
        SET @SQL = N'
            IF NOT EXISTS
            (
                SELECT 1
                FROM ' + QUOTENAME(@ConfigDB) + N'.dbo.XStudio_Page_Mst_Tbl WITH (NOLOCK)
                WHERE ID = @TargetPageID
                  AND IsDeleted = 0
            )
                THROW 56022, ''Target page ID not found in ConfigDB.'', 1;';
        EXEC sys.sp_executesql
            @SQL,
            N'@TargetPageID varchar(36)',
            @TargetPageID = @TargetPageID;
    END;

    -- ============================================================
    -- 4. PREPARE LINK COLUMN VALUES
    -- ============================================================

    DECLARE @CustomLink varchar(max);

    IF @UsePageIdAsCustomLink = 1
        SET @CustomLink = @TargetPageID;
    ELSE
        SET @CustomLink = @SystemName + '/' + @ApplicationName + '/' + @TargetPageName + '?' + @URLParam;

    DECLARE @ColumnProperties nvarchar(max) = N'{
  "IsConditionalFormatting": false,
  "IsContextMenuEnableForApproval": false,
  "IsDefaultSearch": false,
  "Groupable": false,
  "IsPivotColumn": false,
  "ColumnUnit": "",
  "Title": "",
  "TitleAlignment": "left"
}';

    IF ISJSON(@ColumnProperties) <> 1
        THROW 56023, 'Internal error: @ColumnProperties is not valid JSON.', 1;

    -- ============================================================
    -- 5. SCHEMA-INSPECTED UPSERT INTO LV COLUMNS
    -- ============================================================
    -- Documented exception: no complete generic LV-column save SP was found.
    -- This upsert handles schema variants and never writes unsafe link-column
    -- metadata.

    DECLARE
        @HasID bit = 0,
        @IDIsIdentity bit = 0,
        @HasHeader bit = 0,
        @HasType bit = 0,
        @HasLinkType bit = 0,
        @HasLinkValue bit = 0,
        @HasCustomLink bit = 0,
        @HasColumnCustomLink bit = 0,
        @HasCustomLinkTargetMode bit = 0,
        @HasAttributeID bit = 0,
        @HasRelationID bit = 0,
        @HasRelationIDList bit = 0,
        @HasRoleIDList bit = 0,
        @HasIsVisible bit = 0,
        @HasIsCustomField bit = 0,
        @HasAlignment bit = 0,
        @HasIsRestricted bit = 0,
        @HasSerialNumber bit = 0,
        @HasColumnProperties bit = 0,
        @HasCreatedBy bit = 0,
        @HasModifiedBy bit = 0,
        @HasCreatedOn bit = 0,
        @HasModifiedOn bit = 0,
        @HasIsDeleted bit = 0,
        @HasIsSystem bit = 0,
        @HasSource bit = 0,
        @HasHostAddress bit = 0;

    SET @SQL = N'
        SELECT
            @HasID = MAX(CASE WHEN c.name = ''ID'' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END),
            @IDIsIdentity = MAX(CASE WHEN c.name = ''ID'' AND c.is_identity = 1 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END),
            @HasHeader = MAX(CASE WHEN c.name = ''Header'' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END),
            @HasType = MAX(CASE WHEN c.name = ''Type'' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END),
            @HasLinkType = MAX(CASE WHEN c.name = ''LinkType'' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END),
            @HasLinkValue = MAX(CASE WHEN c.name = ''LinkValue'' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END),
            @HasCustomLink = MAX(CASE WHEN c.name = ''CustomLink'' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END),
            @HasColumnCustomLink = MAX(CASE WHEN c.name = ''ColumnCustomLink'' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END),
            @HasCustomLinkTargetMode = MAX(CASE WHEN c.name = ''CustomLinkTargetMode'' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END),
            @HasAttributeID = MAX(CASE WHEN c.name = ''AttributeID'' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END),
            @HasRelationID = MAX(CASE WHEN c.name = ''RelationID'' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END),
            @HasRelationIDList = MAX(CASE WHEN c.name = ''RelationIDList'' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END),
            @HasRoleIDList = MAX(CASE WHEN c.name = ''RoleIDList'' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END),
            @HasIsVisible = MAX(CASE WHEN c.name = ''IsVisible'' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END),
            @HasIsCustomField = MAX(CASE WHEN c.name = ''IsCustomField'' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END),
            @HasAlignment = MAX(CASE WHEN c.name = ''Alignment'' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END),
            @HasIsRestricted = MAX(CASE WHEN c.name = ''IsRestricted'' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END),
            @HasSerialNumber = MAX(CASE WHEN c.name = ''SerialNumber'' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END),
            @HasColumnProperties = MAX(CASE WHEN c.name = ''ColumnProperties'' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END),
            @HasCreatedBy = MAX(CASE WHEN c.name = ''CreatedBy'' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END),
            @HasModifiedBy = MAX(CASE WHEN c.name = ''ModifiedBy'' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END),
            @HasCreatedOn = MAX(CASE WHEN c.name = ''CreatedOn'' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END),
            @HasModifiedOn = MAX(CASE WHEN c.name = ''ModifiedOn'' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END),
            @HasIsDeleted = MAX(CASE WHEN c.name = ''IsDeleted'' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END),
            @HasIsSystem = MAX(CASE WHEN c.name = ''IsSystem'' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END),
            @HasSource = MAX(CASE WHEN c.name = ''Source'' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END),
            @HasHostAddress = MAX(CASE WHEN c.name = ''HostAddress'' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END)
        FROM ' + QUOTENAME(@ConfigDB) + N'.sys.columns c
        JOIN ' + QUOTENAME(@ConfigDB) + N'.sys.tables t
          ON t.object_id = c.object_id
        JOIN ' + QUOTENAME(@ConfigDB) + N'.sys.schemas s
          ON s.schema_id = t.schema_id
        WHERE s.name = ''dbo''
          AND t.name = ''XStudio_LvColumns_Mst_Tbl'';';

    EXEC sys.sp_executesql
        @SQL,
        N'@HasID bit OUTPUT,
          @IDIsIdentity bit OUTPUT,
          @HasHeader bit OUTPUT,
          @HasType bit OUTPUT,
          @HasLinkType bit OUTPUT,
          @HasLinkValue bit OUTPUT,
          @HasCustomLink bit OUTPUT,
          @HasColumnCustomLink bit OUTPUT,
          @HasCustomLinkTargetMode bit OUTPUT,
          @HasAttributeID bit OUTPUT,
          @HasRelationID bit OUTPUT,
          @HasRelationIDList bit OUTPUT,
          @HasRoleIDList bit OUTPUT,
          @HasIsVisible bit OUTPUT,
          @HasIsCustomField bit OUTPUT,
          @HasAlignment bit OUTPUT,
          @HasIsRestricted bit OUTPUT,
          @HasSerialNumber bit OUTPUT,
          @HasColumnProperties bit OUTPUT,
          @HasCreatedBy bit OUTPUT,
          @HasModifiedBy bit OUTPUT,
          @HasCreatedOn bit OUTPUT,
          @HasModifiedOn bit OUTPUT,
          @HasIsDeleted bit OUTPUT,
          @HasIsSystem bit OUTPUT,
          @HasSource bit OUTPUT,
          @HasHostAddress bit OUTPUT',
        @HasID = @HasID OUTPUT,
        @IDIsIdentity = @IDIsIdentity OUTPUT,
        @HasHeader = @HasHeader OUTPUT,
        @HasType = @HasType OUTPUT,
        @HasLinkType = @HasLinkType OUTPUT,
        @HasLinkValue = @HasLinkValue OUTPUT,
        @HasCustomLink = @HasCustomLink OUTPUT,
        @HasColumnCustomLink = @HasColumnCustomLink OUTPUT,
        @HasCustomLinkTargetMode = @HasCustomLinkTargetMode OUTPUT,
        @HasAttributeID = @HasAttributeID OUTPUT,
        @HasRelationID = @HasRelationID OUTPUT,
        @HasRelationIDList = @HasRelationIDList OUTPUT,
        @HasRoleIDList = @HasRoleIDList OUTPUT,
        @HasIsVisible = @HasIsVisible OUTPUT,
        @HasIsCustomField = @HasIsCustomField OUTPUT,
        @HasAlignment = @HasAlignment OUTPUT,
        @HasIsRestricted = @HasIsRestricted OUTPUT,
        @HasSerialNumber = @HasSerialNumber OUTPUT,
        @HasColumnProperties = @HasColumnProperties OUTPUT,
        @HasCreatedBy = @HasCreatedBy OUTPUT,
        @HasModifiedBy = @HasModifiedBy OUTPUT,
        @HasCreatedOn = @HasCreatedOn OUTPUT,
        @HasModifiedOn = @HasModifiedOn OUTPUT,
        @HasIsDeleted = @HasIsDeleted OUTPUT,
        @HasIsSystem = @HasIsSystem OUTPUT,
        @HasSource = @HasSource OUTPUT,
        @HasHostAddress = @HasHostAddress OUTPUT;

    IF @HasCustomLink = 0 AND @HasColumnCustomLink = 0
        THROW 56024, 'Neither CustomLink nor ColumnCustomLink exists on XStudio_LvColumns_Mst_Tbl.', 1;

    DECLARE @ExistingColumnID varchar(36);

    SET @SQL = N'
        SELECT TOP (1)
            @ExistingColumnID = CAST(ID AS varchar(36))
        FROM ' + QUOTENAME(@ConfigDB) + N'.dbo.XStudio_LvColumns_Mst_Tbl WITH (NOLOCK)
        WHERE ParentID = @LVID
          AND Name = @ColName
          AND IsDeleted = 0;';

    EXEC sys.sp_executesql
        @SQL,
        N'@LVID varchar(36), @ColName varchar(100), @ExistingColumnID varchar(36) OUTPUT',
        @LVID = @LVID,
        @ColName = @ColName,
        @ExistingColumnID = @ExistingColumnID OUTPUT;

    DECLARE @UpdateSet nvarchar(max) = N'Name = @ColName';

    IF @HasHeader = 1 SET @UpdateSet += N', Header = @ColHeader';
    IF @HasType = 1 SET @UpdateSet += N', [Type] = ''link''';
    IF @HasLinkType = 1 SET @UpdateSet += N', LinkType = ''fixedtext''';
    IF @HasLinkValue = 1 SET @UpdateSet += N', LinkValue = @LinkLabel';
    IF @HasCustomLink = 1 SET @UpdateSet += N', CustomLink = @CustomLink';
    IF @HasColumnCustomLink = 1 SET @UpdateSet += N', ColumnCustomLink = @CustomLink';
    IF @HasCustomLinkTargetMode = 1 SET @UpdateSet += N', CustomLinkTargetMode = @TargetMode';
    IF @HasAttributeID = 1 SET @UpdateSet += N', AttributeID = NULL';
    IF @HasRelationID = 1 SET @UpdateSet += N', RelationID = NULL';
    IF @HasRelationIDList = 1 SET @UpdateSet += N', RelationIDList = NULL';
    IF @HasRoleIDList = 1 SET @UpdateSet += N', RoleIDList = NULLIF(@RoleIDList, '''')';
    IF @HasIsVisible = 1 SET @UpdateSet += N', IsVisible = 1';
    IF @HasIsCustomField = 1 SET @UpdateSet += N', IsCustomField = 1';
    IF @HasAlignment = 1 SET @UpdateSet += N', Alignment = ''center''';
    IF @HasIsRestricted = 1 SET @UpdateSet += N', IsRestricted = CASE WHEN NULLIF(@RoleIDList, '''') IS NULL THEN 0 ELSE 1 END';
    IF @HasSerialNumber = 1 SET @UpdateSet += N', SerialNumber = @SerialNumber';
    IF @HasColumnProperties = 1 SET @UpdateSet += N', ColumnProperties = @ColumnProperties';
    IF @HasModifiedBy = 1 SET @UpdateSet += N', ModifiedBy = @UserID';
    IF @HasModifiedOn = 1 SET @UpdateSet += N', ModifiedOn = GETDATE()';
    IF @HasIsSystem = 1 SET @UpdateSet += N', IsSystem = 0';
    IF @HasSource = 1 SET @UpdateSet += N', Source = ''System''';
    IF @HasHostAddress = 1 SET @UpdateSet += N', HostAddress = @IPAddress';

    IF @ExistingColumnID IS NOT NULL
    BEGIN
        SET @SQL = N'
            UPDATE lvc
            SET ' + @UpdateSet + N'
            FROM ' + QUOTENAME(@ConfigDB) + N'.dbo.XStudio_LvColumns_Mst_Tbl lvc
            WHERE lvc.ID = @ExistingColumnID
              AND lvc.IsDeleted = 0;';

        EXEC sys.sp_executesql
            @SQL,
            N'@ExistingColumnID varchar(36),
              @ColName varchar(100),
              @ColHeader varchar(200),
              @LinkLabel varchar(100),
              @CustomLink varchar(max),
              @TargetMode varchar(50),
              @RoleIDList varchar(max),
              @SerialNumber int,
              @ColumnProperties nvarchar(max),
              @UserID varchar(36),
              @IPAddress varchar(100)',
            @ExistingColumnID = @ExistingColumnID,
            @ColName = @ColName,
            @ColHeader = @ColHeader,
            @LinkLabel = @LinkLabel,
            @CustomLink = @CustomLink,
            @TargetMode = @TargetMode,
            @RoleIDList = @RoleIDList,
            @SerialNumber = @SerialNumber,
            @ColumnProperties = @ColumnProperties,
            @UserID = @UserID,
            @IPAddress = @IPAddress;
    END
    ELSE
    BEGIN
        DECLARE @InsertColumns nvarchar(max) = N'';
        DECLARE @InsertValues nvarchar(max) = N'';

        IF @HasID = 1 AND @IDIsIdentity = 0
        BEGIN
            SET @InsertColumns += N'[ID],';
            SET @InsertValues += N'NEWID(),';
        END;

        SET @InsertColumns += N'[Name],[ParentID]';
        SET @InsertValues += N'@ColName,@LVID';

        IF @HasHeader = 1 BEGIN SET @InsertColumns += N',[Header]'; SET @InsertValues += N',@ColHeader'; END;
        IF @HasType = 1 BEGIN SET @InsertColumns += N',[Type]'; SET @InsertValues += N',''link'''; END;
        IF @HasLinkType = 1 BEGIN SET @InsertColumns += N',[LinkType]'; SET @InsertValues += N',''fixedtext'''; END;
        IF @HasLinkValue = 1 BEGIN SET @InsertColumns += N',[LinkValue]'; SET @InsertValues += N',@LinkLabel'; END;
        IF @HasCustomLink = 1 BEGIN SET @InsertColumns += N',[CustomLink]'; SET @InsertValues += N',@CustomLink'; END;
        IF @HasColumnCustomLink = 1 BEGIN SET @InsertColumns += N',[ColumnCustomLink]'; SET @InsertValues += N',@CustomLink'; END;
        IF @HasCustomLinkTargetMode = 1 BEGIN SET @InsertColumns += N',[CustomLinkTargetMode]'; SET @InsertValues += N',@TargetMode'; END;
        IF @HasAttributeID = 1 BEGIN SET @InsertColumns += N',[AttributeID]'; SET @InsertValues += N',NULL'; END;
        IF @HasRelationID = 1 BEGIN SET @InsertColumns += N',[RelationID]'; SET @InsertValues += N',NULL'; END;
        IF @HasRelationIDList = 1 BEGIN SET @InsertColumns += N',[RelationIDList]'; SET @InsertValues += N',NULL'; END;
        IF @HasRoleIDList = 1 BEGIN SET @InsertColumns += N',[RoleIDList]'; SET @InsertValues += N',NULLIF(@RoleIDList, '''')'; END;
        IF @HasIsVisible = 1 BEGIN SET @InsertColumns += N',[IsVisible]'; SET @InsertValues += N',1'; END;
        IF @HasIsCustomField = 1 BEGIN SET @InsertColumns += N',[IsCustomField]'; SET @InsertValues += N',1'; END;
        IF @HasAlignment = 1 BEGIN SET @InsertColumns += N',[Alignment]'; SET @InsertValues += N',''center'''; END;
        IF @HasIsRestricted = 1 BEGIN SET @InsertColumns += N',[IsRestricted]'; SET @InsertValues += N',CASE WHEN NULLIF(@RoleIDList, '''') IS NULL THEN 0 ELSE 1 END'; END;
        IF @HasSerialNumber = 1 BEGIN SET @InsertColumns += N',[SerialNumber]'; SET @InsertValues += N',@SerialNumber'; END;
        IF @HasColumnProperties = 1 BEGIN SET @InsertColumns += N',[ColumnProperties]'; SET @InsertValues += N',@ColumnProperties'; END;
        IF @HasCreatedBy = 1 BEGIN SET @InsertColumns += N',[CreatedBy]'; SET @InsertValues += N',@UserID'; END;
        IF @HasModifiedBy = 1 BEGIN SET @InsertColumns += N',[ModifiedBy]'; SET @InsertValues += N',@UserID'; END;
        IF @HasCreatedOn = 1 BEGIN SET @InsertColumns += N',[CreatedOn]'; SET @InsertValues += N',GETDATE()'; END;
        IF @HasModifiedOn = 1 BEGIN SET @InsertColumns += N',[ModifiedOn]'; SET @InsertValues += N',GETDATE()'; END;
        IF @HasIsDeleted = 1 BEGIN SET @InsertColumns += N',[IsDeleted]'; SET @InsertValues += N',0'; END;
        IF @HasIsSystem = 1 BEGIN SET @InsertColumns += N',[IsSystem]'; SET @InsertValues += N',0'; END;
        IF @HasSource = 1 BEGIN SET @InsertColumns += N',[Source]'; SET @InsertValues += N',''System'''; END;
        IF @HasHostAddress = 1 BEGIN SET @InsertColumns += N',[HostAddress]'; SET @InsertValues += N',@IPAddress'; END;

        SET @SQL = N'
            INSERT INTO ' + QUOTENAME(@ConfigDB) + N'.dbo.XStudio_LvColumns_Mst_Tbl
            (' + @InsertColumns + N')
            SELECT ' + @InsertValues + N';';

        EXEC sys.sp_executesql
            @SQL,
            N'@LVID varchar(36),
              @ColName varchar(100),
              @ColHeader varchar(200),
              @LinkLabel varchar(100),
              @CustomLink varchar(max),
              @TargetMode varchar(50),
              @RoleIDList varchar(max),
              @SerialNumber int,
              @ColumnProperties nvarchar(max),
              @UserID varchar(36),
              @IPAddress varchar(100)',
            @LVID = @LVID,
            @ColName = @ColName,
            @ColHeader = @ColHeader,
            @LinkLabel = @LinkLabel,
            @CustomLink = @CustomLink,
            @TargetMode = @TargetMode,
            @RoleIDList = @RoleIDList,
            @SerialNumber = @SerialNumber,
            @ColumnProperties = @ColumnProperties,
            @UserID = @UserID,
            @IPAddress = @IPAddress;
    END;

    -- ============================================================
    -- 6. POSTFLIGHT BEFORE VIEW REGENERATION
    -- ============================================================

    DECLARE @LinkColumnProblemCount int = 0;
    DECLARE @LvName varchar(100);

    SET @SQL = N'
        SELECT @LvName = CONVERT(varchar(100), Name)
        FROM ' + QUOTENAME(@ConfigDB) + N'.dbo.XStudio_LV_Mst_Tbl WITH (NOLOCK)
        WHERE ID = @LVID
          AND IsDeleted = 0;

        SELECT @LinkColumnProblemCount = COUNT(1)
        FROM ' + QUOTENAME(@ConfigDB) + N'.dbo.XStudio_LvColumns_Mst_Tbl lvc WITH (NOLOCK)
        WHERE lvc.ParentID = @LVID
          AND lvc.Name = @ColName
          AND lvc.IsDeleted = 0
          AND
          (
                 (COL_LENGTH(@ConfigDB + ''.dbo.XStudio_LvColumns_Mst_Tbl'', ''Type'') IS NOT NULL AND ISNULL(CONVERT(varchar(50), lvc.[Type]), '''') <> ''link'')
              OR (COL_LENGTH(@ConfigDB + ''.dbo.XStudio_LvColumns_Mst_Tbl'', ''IsCustomField'') IS NOT NULL AND ISNULL(lvc.IsCustomField, 0) <> 1)
              OR (COL_LENGTH(@ConfigDB + ''.dbo.XStudio_LvColumns_Mst_Tbl'', ''IsVisible'') IS NOT NULL AND ISNULL(lvc.IsVisible, 0) <> 1)
              OR (COL_LENGTH(@ConfigDB + ''.dbo.XStudio_LvColumns_Mst_Tbl'', ''AttributeID'') IS NOT NULL AND lvc.AttributeID IS NOT NULL)
              OR (COL_LENGTH(@ConfigDB + ''.dbo.XStudio_LvColumns_Mst_Tbl'', ''RelationID'') IS NOT NULL AND lvc.RelationID IS NOT NULL)
              OR (COL_LENGTH(@ConfigDB + ''.dbo.XStudio_LvColumns_Mst_Tbl'', ''ColumnProperties'') IS NOT NULL AND (lvc.ColumnProperties IS NULL OR ISJSON(CONVERT(nvarchar(max), lvc.ColumnProperties)) <> 1))
          );';

    EXEC sys.sp_executesql
        @SQL,
        N'@LVID varchar(36), @ColName varchar(100), @ConfigDB sysname, @LvName varchar(100) OUTPUT, @LinkColumnProblemCount int OUTPUT',
        @LVID = @LVID,
        @ColName = @ColName,
        @ConfigDB = @ConfigDB,
        @LvName = @LvName OUTPUT,
        @LinkColumnProblemCount = @LinkColumnProblemCount OUTPUT;

    IF @LvName IS NULL
        THROW 56025, 'LV name could not be resolved for view regeneration.', 1;

    IF @LinkColumnProblemCount > 0
        THROW 56026, 'Postflight failed: link column metadata is not safe.', 1;

    -- ============================================================
    -- 7. REGENERATE LV VIEW USING CONFIRMED SP
    -- ============================================================

    EXEC XStudio_Configuration.dbo.XStudio_CreateViewForLV_Usp
        @LvID = @LVID,
        @LvName = @LvName,
        @DataDatabase = @DataDB,
        @ColumnQuery = @ColumnQuery,
        @RelationQuery = @RelationQuery,
        @GroupByQuery = @GroupByQuery,
        @WhereCondition = @WhereCondition,
        @IsCalculatedView = @IsCalculatedView;

    -- ============================================================
    -- 8. POSTFLIGHT AFTER VIEW REGENERATION
    -- ============================================================

    SET @SQL = N'
        IF OBJECT_ID(QUOTENAME(@DataDB) + ''.dbo.'' + QUOTENAME(''XStudio_'' + @LvName + ''_Vw''), ''V'') IS NULL
            THROW 56027, ''Postflight failed: LV view was not created.'', 1;

        IF COL_LENGTH(QUOTENAME(@DataDB) + ''.dbo.'' + QUOTENAME(''XStudio_'' + @LvName + ''_Vw''), @ColName) IS NULL
            THROW 56028, ''Postflight failed: generated LV view does not expose the link column alias.'', 1;';

    EXEC sys.sp_executesql
        @SQL,
        N'@DataDB sysname, @LvName varchar(100), @ColName varchar(100)',
        @DataDB = @DataDB,
        @LvName = @LvName,
        @ColName = @ColName;

    -- ============================================================
    -- 9. RESULT
    -- ============================================================

    SET @SQL = N'
        SELECT
            lvc.ID,
            lvc.ParentID,
            lvc.Name,
            lvc.Header,
            lvc.[Type],
            lvc.LinkType,
            lvc.LinkValue,
            CASE
                WHEN COL_LENGTH(@ConfigDB + ''.dbo.XStudio_LvColumns_Mst_Tbl'', ''CustomLink'') IS NOT NULL THEN CONVERT(varchar(max), lvc.CustomLink)
                WHEN COL_LENGTH(@ConfigDB + ''.dbo.XStudio_LvColumns_Mst_Tbl'', ''ColumnCustomLink'') IS NOT NULL THEN CONVERT(varchar(max), lvc.ColumnCustomLink)
                ELSE NULL
            END AS EffectiveCustomLink,
            lvc.CustomLinkTargetMode,
            lvc.IsVisible,
            lvc.IsCustomField,
            lvc.SerialNumber,
            lvc.RoleIDList,
            lvc.ColumnProperties
        FROM ' + QUOTENAME(@ConfigDB) + N'.dbo.XStudio_LvColumns_Mst_Tbl lvc WITH (NOLOCK)
        WHERE lvc.ParentID = @LVID
          AND lvc.Name = @ColName
          AND lvc.IsDeleted = 0;';

    EXEC sys.sp_executesql
        @SQL,
        N'@LVID varchar(36), @ColName varchar(100), @ConfigDB sysname',
        @LVID = @LVID,
        @ColName = @ColName,
        @ConfigDB = @ConfigDB;

    COMMIT TRANSACTION;

    PRINT 'LV link column template completed successfully. Next: run postflight_checks.sql and verify the LV page in the UI.';

END TRY
BEGIN CATCH

    IF @@TRANCOUNT > 0
        ROLLBACK TRANSACTION;

    DECLARE @ErrorMessage nvarchar(4000) = ERROR_MESSAGE();
    DECLARE @ErrorLine int = ERROR_LINE();
    DECLARE @ErrorNumber int = ERROR_NUMBER();

    RAISERROR('lv_link_column_template.sql failed. Error %d at line %d: %s', 16, 1, @ErrorNumber, @ErrorLine, @ErrorMessage);

END CATCH;
```

## 39. Page Menu Template

**Source file:** `03_SQL_TEMPLATES/page_menu_template.sql`  
**File type:** `.sql`  
**Size:** `19668` bytes  
**Modified on:** `2026-05-12 08:51:00`  
**SHA256:** `fac5cd4e181f`  
**Encoding used:** `utf-8`

> Start of imported document.

```sql
-- ============================================================
-- Page and Menu Template
-- ============================================================
--
-- Purpose:
--   Create/resolve a list page and popup edit page for an entity,
--   bind the LV/edit controls to those pages, wire edit navigation,
--   create/update a menu item for the list page, and assign menu
--   role visibility.
--
-- Hard rule:
--   Use XStudio stored procedures for all configuration actions
--   where a stored procedure exists.
--
-- Direct-write exception in this template:
--   Brand-new page header rows are created by guarded insert into
--   XStudio_Page_Mst_Tbl because the current XStudio SP catalog does
--   not expose a generic XStudio_Save_Page_Configuration_Usp /
--   XStudio_Create_Page_Usp for creating new page headers.
--
-- Forbidden in this template:
--   - direct writes to XStudio_PageControls_Mst_Tbl
--   - direct writes to XStudio_Menu_Mst_Tbl
--   - direct writes to XStudio_MenuRights_Mst_Tbl
--   - direct updates to XStudio_Menu_Mst_Tbl.Sn
--
-- Required SPs:
--   - XStudio_Configuration.dbo.XStudio_Save_Page_Control_Configuration_Usp
--   - XStudio_Configuration.dbo.XStudio_Save_Menu_Configuration_Usp
--   - XStudio_Configuration.dbo.XStudio_Save_Menu_Rights_Usp
--   - XStudio_Configuration.dbo.XStudio_Save_Menu_Sequence_Usp
--
-- Notes:
--   1. Page header rows are only containers.
--   2. PageControls bind the actual LV/edit/control to the page.
--   3. MenuRights make the menu visible to roles.
--   4. For non-LV/edit controls, reuse the PageControl SP block with
--      the correct @ControlType and @ControlID.
--   5. For tab-container pages, bind the tab container as a PageControl
--      and then use tab SPs separately.
--
-- ============================================================

SET NOCOUNT ON;
SET XACT_ABORT ON;

BEGIN TRY
    BEGIN TRANSACTION;

    -- ============================================================
    -- REQUIRED INPUTS
    -- ============================================================

    DECLARE @SystemID       varchar(36)      = '6C8C0070-60B5-4474-BEC8-5C02197B32E6';
    DECLARE @ConfigDB       sysname          = 'XStudio_Configuration_XMES';
    DECLARE @AppID          varchar(36)      = '29123B0B-3872-41CF-B5E1-67C4A8F0D4BC';

    -- Must be a real XStudio user ID from XStudio_Configuration.dbo.XStudio_User_Mst_Tbl.
    -- Do not use 'sa' or a login string for CreatedBy/ModifiedBy.
    DECLARE @UserID         varchar(36)      = '<deployment_user_guid>';
    DECLARE @IPAddress      varchar(20)      = 'T-SQL';

    DECLARE @LVID           varchar(36)      = '<lv_guid>';
    DECLARE @EditID         varchar(36)      = '<edit_guid>';

    DECLARE @EntityName     varchar(100)     = '<EntityTableName>';
    DECLARE @Label          varchar(100)     = '<Display Label>';
    DECLARE @MenuSn         int              = 100;
    DECLARE @ParentMenuID   varchar(36)      = NULL;  -- NULL for top-level, or set to parent menu GUID.

    DECLARE @ListPageName   varchar(200)     = 'List_Page_' + @EntityName;
    DECLARE @EditPageName   varchar(200)     = 'Edit_Page_' + @EntityName;

    DECLARE @ListPageID     varchar(36);
    DECLARE @EditPageID     varchar(36);
    DECLARE @ListPageControlID varchar(36);
    DECLARE @EditPageControlID varchar(36);
    DECLARE @MenuID         varchar(36);
    DECLARE @MenuMode       char(1);

    -- Replace with actual role GUIDs from XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_Role_Mst_Tbl.
    DECLARE @Roles TABLE
    (
        RoleID varchar(36) NOT NULL PRIMARY KEY
    );

    INSERT INTO @Roles (RoleID)
    VALUES
        ('<role_guid_1>'),
        ('<role_guid_2>');

    -- ============================================================
    -- PREFLIGHT
    -- ============================================================

    IF NOT EXISTS
    (
        SELECT 1
        FROM XStudio_Configuration.dbo.XStudio_User_Mst_Tbl
        WHERE ID = @UserID
          AND IsDeleted = 0
    )
    BEGIN
        THROW 51000, 'Deployment user ID not found in XStudio_Configuration.dbo.XStudio_User_Mst_Tbl.', 1;
    END;

    IF NOT EXISTS
    (
        SELECT 1
        FROM XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_Application_Mst_Tbl
        WHERE ID = @AppID
          AND IsDeleted = 0
    )
    BEGIN
        THROW 51001, 'Application ID not found in XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_Application_Mst_Tbl.', 1;
    END;

    IF NOT EXISTS
    (
        SELECT 1
        FROM XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_LV_Mst_Tbl
        WHERE ID = @LVID
          AND IsDeleted = 0
    )
    BEGIN
        THROW 51002, 'LV ID not found in XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_LV_Mst_Tbl.', 1;
    END;

    IF NOT EXISTS
    (
        SELECT 1
        FROM XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_Edit_Mst_Tbl
        WHERE ID = @EditID
          AND IsDeleted = 0
    )
    BEGIN
        THROW 51003, 'Edit ID not found in XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_Edit_Mst_Tbl.', 1;
    END;

    IF EXISTS
    (
        SELECT 1
        FROM @Roles r
        LEFT JOIN XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_Role_Mst_Tbl role_mst
          ON role_mst.ID = r.RoleID
         AND role_mst.IsDeleted = 0
        WHERE role_mst.ID IS NULL
    )
    BEGIN
        THROW 51004, 'One or more RoleID values do not exist in XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_Role_Mst_Tbl.', 1;
    END;

    IF
    (
        SELECT COUNT(1)
        FROM XStudio_Configuration.sys.procedures
        WHERE name IN
        (
            'XStudio_Save_Page_Control_Configuration_Usp',
            'XStudio_Save_Menu_Configuration_Usp',
            'XStudio_Save_Menu_Rights_Usp',
            'XStudio_Save_Menu_Sequence_Usp'
        )
    ) <> 4
    BEGIN
        THROW 51005, 'One or more required XStudio configuration SPs are missing.', 1;
    END;

    IF NOT EXISTS
    (
        SELECT 1
        FROM XStudio_Configuration.sys.table_types
        WHERE name = 'Menu'
    )
    BEGIN
        THROW 51006, 'Required XStudio_Configuration.dbo.Menu table type is missing.', 1;
    END;

    -- Optional inspection query for live environments.
    -- The INSERT INTO @MenuTable column list below must match dbo.Menu.
    /*
    SELECT
        c.column_id,
        c.name AS ColumnName,
        TYPE_NAME(c.user_type_id) AS DataType,
        c.max_length,
        c.is_nullable
    FROM XStudio_Configuration.sys.table_types tt
    JOIN XStudio_Configuration.sys.columns c
      ON c.object_id = tt.type_table_object_id
    WHERE tt.name = 'Menu'
    ORDER BY c.column_id;
    */

    -- ============================================================
    -- PAGES
    -- ============================================================
    -- No generic page-create SP is present in the current SP catalog.
    -- Therefore this guarded direct insert is the only allowed exception.

    SELECT @ListPageID = CAST(ID AS varchar(36))
    FROM XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_Page_Mst_Tbl
    WHERE Name = @ListPageName
      AND ParentID = @AppID
      AND IsDeleted = 0;

    IF @ListPageID IS NULL
    BEGIN
        SET @ListPageID = CAST(NEWID() AS varchar(36));

        INSERT INTO XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_Page_Mst_Tbl
        (
            ID,
            Name,
            DisplayName,
            Description,
            IsActive,
            IsPopup,
            ParentID,
            CreatedBy,
            ModifiedBy,
            CreatedOn,
            ModifiedOn,
            IsDeleted,
            IsSystem,
            Source,
            HostAddress
        )
        VALUES
        (
            @ListPageID,
            @ListPageName,
            @Label,
            '',
            1,
            0,
            @AppID,
            @UserID,
            @UserID,
            GETDATE(),
            GETDATE(),
            0,
            0,
            'System',
            @IPAddress
        );
    END;

    SELECT @EditPageID = CAST(ID AS varchar(36))
    FROM XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_Page_Mst_Tbl
    WHERE Name = @EditPageName
      AND ParentID = @AppID
      AND IsDeleted = 0;

    IF @EditPageID IS NULL
    BEGIN
        SET @EditPageID = CAST(NEWID() AS varchar(36));

        INSERT INTO XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_Page_Mst_Tbl
        (
            ID,
            Name,
            DisplayName,
            Description,
            IsActive,
            IsPopup,
            ParentID,
            CreatedBy,
            ModifiedBy,
            CreatedOn,
            ModifiedOn,
            IsDeleted,
            IsSystem,
            Source,
            HostAddress
        )
        VALUES
        (
            @EditPageID,
            @EditPageName,
            @Label + ' Edit',
            '',
            1,
            1,
            @AppID,
            @UserID,
            @UserID,
            GETDATE(),
            GETDATE(),
            0,
            0,
            'System',
            @IPAddress
        );
    END;

    -- ============================================================
    -- PAGE CONTROLS
    -- ============================================================
    -- PageControls are the Page -> Control binding.
    -- Use XStudio_Save_Page_Control_Configuration_Usp only.

    SELECT @ListPageControlID = CAST(ID AS varchar(36))
    FROM XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_PageControls_Mst_Tbl
    WHERE ParentID = @ListPageID
      AND ControlID = @LVID
      AND ControlType = 'grid'
      AND IsDeleted = 0;

    IF @ListPageControlID IS NULL
        SET @ListPageControlID = CAST(NEWID() AS varchar(36));

    EXEC XStudio_Configuration.dbo.XStudio_Save_Page_Control_Configuration_Usp
        @SystemId          = @SystemID,
        @Id                = @ListPageControlID,
        @Name              = @Label + ' List',
        @ParentId          = @ListPageID,
        @RowId             = '1',
        @DivId             = '1',
        @ControlType       = 'grid',
        @ControlId         = @LVID,
        @ControlUrl        = '',
        @CustomCss         = '',
        @LoadUrlAsIFrame   = 0,
        @Description       = '',
        @ControlProperties = '{"IpFiltering":"False"}',
        @UserId            = @UserID,
        @IPAddress         = @IPAddress;

    SELECT @EditPageControlID = CAST(ID AS varchar(36))
    FROM XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_PageControls_Mst_Tbl
    WHERE ParentID = @EditPageID
      AND ControlID = @EditID
      AND ControlType = 'edit'
      AND IsDeleted = 0;

    IF @EditPageControlID IS NULL
        SET @EditPageControlID = CAST(NEWID() AS varchar(36));

    EXEC XStudio_Configuration.dbo.XStudio_Save_Page_Control_Configuration_Usp
        @SystemId          = @SystemID,
        @Id                = @EditPageControlID,
        @Name              = @Label + ' Edit',
        @ParentId          = @EditPageID,
        @RowId             = '1',
        @DivId             = '1',
        @ControlType       = 'edit',
        @ControlId         = @EditID,
        @ControlUrl        = '',
        @CustomCss         = '',
        @LoadUrlAsIFrame   = 0,
        @Description       = '',
        @ControlProperties = '{"IpFiltering":"False"}',
        @UserId            = @UserID,
        @IPAddress         = @IPAddress;

    -- Generic PageControl pattern for any other supported control:
    --
    -- EXEC XStudio_Configuration.dbo.XStudio_Save_Page_Control_Configuration_Usp
    --     @SystemId          = @SystemID,
    --     @Id                = '<page_control_guid>',
    --     @Name              = '<control display name>',
    --     @ParentId          = '<page_guid>',
    --     @RowId             = '<row>',
    --     @DivId             = '<div>',
    --     @ControlType       = '<grid|edit|inlinegrid|tab|dashboard|html...>',
    --     @ControlId         = '<target_control_guid>',
    --     @ControlUrl        = '',
    --     @CustomCss         = '',
    --     @LoadUrlAsIFrame   = 0,
    --     @Description       = '',
    --     @ControlProperties = '{"IpFiltering":"False"}',
    --     @UserId            = @UserID,
    --     @IPAddress         = @IPAddress;
    --
    -- If @ControlType = 'tab', bind the tab container here first, then use
    -- XStudio_Save_Tab_Configuration_Usp and XStudio_Save_Tab_Control_Configuration_Usp
    -- to configure children inside the tab container.

    -- ============================================================
    -- EDIT NAVIGATION
    -- ============================================================
    -- Preserve original template behavior: after save/cancel on the edit
    -- popup, return to the list page.
    --
    -- No generic edit-navigation save SP is confirmed in the current
    -- SP catalog. This guarded update is therefore an exception.
    -- If a live environment exposes a suitable edit-navigation SP, use it
    -- instead of this update.

    UPDATE XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_Edit_Mst_Tbl
    SET AfterSavePageID   = @ListPageID,
        AfterCancelPageID = @ListPageID,
        Source            = 'System',
        ModifiedBy        = @UserID,
        ModifiedOn        = GETDATE(),
        HostAddress       = @IPAddress
    WHERE ID = @EditID
      AND IsDeleted = 0;

    -- ============================================================
    -- MENU
    -- ============================================================
    -- Use XStudio_Save_Menu_Configuration_Usp only.

    SELECT @MenuID = CAST(ID AS varchar(36))
    FROM XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_Menu_Mst_Tbl
    WHERE PageID = @ListPageID
      AND ParentID = @AppID
      AND ISNULL(CAST(SubMenuOfID AS varchar(36)), '') = ISNULL(@ParentMenuID, '')
      AND IsDeleted = 0;

    IF @MenuID IS NULL
    BEGIN
        SET @MenuID = CAST(NEWID() AS varchar(36));
        SET @MenuMode = 'I';
    END
    ELSE
    BEGIN
        SET @MenuMode = 'U';
    END;

    DECLARE @MenuTable XStudio_Configuration.dbo.Menu;

    -- This column list matches the Menu TVP used by XStudio_Save_Menu_Configuration_Usp
    -- in the 2026-05-08 SP reference. If the live type changes, update this list
    -- after inspecting XStudio_Configuration.sys.table_types / sys.columns.
    INSERT INTO @MenuTable
    (
        ID,
        Name,
        ParentID,
        PageID,
        SubMenuOfID,
        MenuTooltip,
        MenuType,
        ControlType,
        IsVisible,
        IconID,
        ImageID,
        LinkTarget,
        FilterCondition,
        CreatedBy,
        ModifiedBy
    )
    VALUES
    (
        @MenuID,
        @Label,
        @AppID,
        @ListPageID,
        @ParentMenuID,
        @Label,
        '',
        '',
        1,
        NULL,
        NULL,
        '',
        '',
        @UserID,
        @UserID
    );

    EXEC XStudio_Configuration.dbo.XStudio_Save_Menu_Configuration_Usp
        @SystemId   = @SystemID,
        @Id         = @MenuID,
        @IPAddress  = @IPAddress,
        @Mode       = @MenuMode,
        @TempTable  = @MenuTable;

    -- Preserve original template behavior: explicit menu sequence.
    -- Use the SP; do not update XStudio_Menu_Mst_Tbl.Sn directly.
    EXEC XStudio_Configuration.dbo.XStudio_Save_Menu_Sequence_Usp
        @SystemId     = @SystemID,
        @Id           = @MenuID,
        @Sn           = @MenuSn,
        @SubMenuOfID  = @ParentMenuID,
        @UserId       = @UserID,
        @IPAddress    = @IPAddress;

    -- ============================================================
    -- MENU RIGHTS
    -- ============================================================
    -- Use XStudio_Save_Menu_Rights_Usp once per role.

    DECLARE @RoleID varchar(36);

    DECLARE role_cursor CURSOR LOCAL FAST_FORWARD FOR
        SELECT RoleID
        FROM @Roles;

    OPEN role_cursor;
    FETCH NEXT FROM role_cursor INTO @RoleID;

    WHILE @@FETCH_STATUS = 0
    BEGIN
        IF NOT EXISTS
        (
            SELECT 1
            FROM XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_MenuRights_Mst_Tbl
            WHERE ParentID = @MenuID
              AND RoleID = @RoleID
              AND IsDeleted = 0
        )
        BEGIN
            EXEC XStudio_Configuration.dbo.XStudio_Save_Menu_Rights_Usp
                @SystemId   = @SystemID,
                @Id         = @MenuID,
                @RoleID     = @RoleID,
                @UserId     = @UserID,
                @IPAddress  = @IPAddress;
        END;

        FETCH NEXT FROM role_cursor INTO @RoleID;
    END;

    CLOSE role_cursor;
    DEALLOCATE role_cursor;

    -- If this is a submenu, parent menu must also be visible to each role.
    -- Use XStudio_AddRoleToParentMenu_USP only after confirming live behavior
    -- in the target system.
    /*
    EXEC XStudio_Configuration.dbo.XStudio_AddRoleToParentMenu_USP
        @SystemId = @SystemID,
        @MenuId   = @ParentMenuID,
        @RoleId   = @RoleID;
    */

    -- ============================================================
    -- POSTFLIGHT
    -- ============================================================

    IF NOT EXISTS
    (
        SELECT 1
        FROM XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_Page_Mst_Tbl
        WHERE ID IN (@ListPageID, @EditPageID)
          AND IsDeleted = 0
        HAVING COUNT(1) = 2
    )
    BEGIN
        THROW 51007, 'Postflight failed: list/edit page rows were not found.', 1;
    END;

    IF EXISTS
    (
        SELECT p.ID
        FROM XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_Page_Mst_Tbl p
        LEFT JOIN XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_PageControls_Mst_Tbl pc
          ON pc.ParentID = p.ID
         AND pc.IsDeleted = 0
        WHERE p.ID IN (@ListPageID, @EditPageID)
          AND p.IsDeleted = 0
        GROUP BY p.ID
        HAVING COUNT(pc.ID) = 0
    )
    BEGIN
        THROW 51008, 'Postflight failed: one or more pages have no active PageControl binding.', 1;
    END;

    IF NOT EXISTS
    (
        SELECT 1
        FROM XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_Menu_Mst_Tbl
        WHERE ID = @MenuID
          AND PageID = @ListPageID
          AND IsDeleted = 0
    )
    BEGIN
        THROW 51009, 'Postflight failed: menu row was not found or does not point to the list page.', 1;
    END;

    IF NOT EXISTS
    (
        SELECT 1
        FROM XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_MenuRights_Mst_Tbl
        WHERE ParentID = @MenuID
          AND IsDeleted = 0
    )
    BEGIN
        THROW 51010, 'Postflight failed: menu has no active role rights.', 1;
    END;

    COMMIT TRANSACTION;

    -- ============================================================
    -- SUMMARY
    -- ============================================================

    SELECT 'ListPage' AS [Type], ID, Name
    FROM XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_Page_Mst_Tbl
    WHERE ID = @ListPageID

    UNION ALL

    SELECT 'EditPage', ID, Name
    FROM XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_Page_Mst_Tbl
    WHERE ID = @EditPageID

    UNION ALL

    SELECT 'ListPageControl', ID, Name
    FROM XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_PageControls_Mst_Tbl
    WHERE ID = @ListPageControlID

    UNION ALL

    SELECT 'EditPageControl', ID, Name
    FROM XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_PageControls_Mst_Tbl
    WHERE ID = @EditPageControlID

    UNION ALL

    SELECT 'Menu', ID, Name
    FROM XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_Menu_Mst_Tbl
    WHERE ID = @MenuID;

END TRY
BEGIN CATCH
    IF @@TRANCOUNT > 0
        ROLLBACK TRANSACTION;

    DECLARE @ErrorMessage nvarchar(4000) = ERROR_MESSAGE();
    DECLARE @ErrorSeverity int = ERROR_SEVERITY();
    DECLARE @ErrorState int = ERROR_STATE();

    RAISERROR(@ErrorMessage, @ErrorSeverity, @ErrorState);
END CATCH;
```

## 40. Postflight Checks

**Source file:** `03_SQL_TEMPLATES/postflight_checks.sql`  
**File type:** `.sql`  
**Size:** `5792` bytes  
**Modified on:** `2026-05-09 10:10:16`  
**SHA256:** `3f46e4c66f99`  
**Encoding used:** `utf-8`

> Start of imported document.

```sql
-- ============================================================
-- Postflight Checks
-- Run after creating or modifying any XStudio module
-- ============================================================

-- Set this to the entity you just created/modified
DECLARE @EntityName varchar(100) = '<EntityTableName>';
DECLARE @EntityID   uniqueidentifier = (
    SELECT ID FROM XStudio_Entities_Mst_Tbl WHERE Name = @EntityName AND IsDeleted = 0
);

-- ---- 1. ENTITY ----
SELECT 'ENTITY' as Check, ID, Name, TableType, IsChild, WorkFlowId
FROM XStudio_Entities_Mst_Tbl WHERE ID = @EntityID;

-- ---- 2. ATTRIBUTES ----
SELECT 'ATTRIBUTE COUNT' as Check, COUNT(*) as [Count]
FROM XStudio_Attribute_Mst_Tbl WHERE ParentID = @EntityID AND IsDeleted = 0;

-- ---- 3. DROPDOWN PROPERTIES (must be 6 per dropdown attribute) ----
SELECT
    'DROPDOWN PROPS' as Check,
    a.Name as AttributeName,
    COUNT(p.ID) as PropertyCount,
    CASE WHEN COUNT(p.ID) = 6 THEN 'OK' ELSE 'MISSING ' + CAST(6 - COUNT(p.ID) AS varchar) + ' PROPERTIES' END as Status
FROM XStudio_Attribute_Mst_Tbl a
JOIN XStudio_Configuration.dbo.XStudio_DataType_Mst_Tbl dt ON dt.ID = a.DataType
LEFT JOIN XStudio_AttributeProperties_Mst_Tbl p ON p.ParentID = a.ID AND p.IsDeleted = 0
WHERE a.ParentID = @EntityID
  AND a.IsDeleted = 0
  AND dt.Name = 'Dropdown'
GROUP BY a.Name;

-- ---- 4. ENTITY RELATIONS (must exist per dropdown attribute) ----
SELECT 'ENTITY RELATIONS' as Check, COUNT(*) as [Count],
       CASE WHEN COUNT(*) > 0 THEN 'OK' ELSE 'MISSING' END as Status
FROM XStudio_EntityRelations_Mst_Tbl
WHERE EntityID = @EntityID AND IsDeleted = 0;

-- ---- 5. LV ----
SELECT 'LV' as Check, ID, Name, SPName, FilterCondition
FROM XStudio_LV_Mst_Tbl WHERE ParentID = @EntityID AND IsDeleted = 0;

-- ---- 6. LV COLUMN COUNT ----
SELECT 'LV COLUMNS' as Check, l.Name as LVName, COUNT(c.ID) as ColumnCount
FROM XStudio_LV_Mst_Tbl l
LEFT JOIN XStudio_LvColumns_Mst_Tbl c ON c.ParentID = l.ID AND c.IsDeleted = 0
WHERE l.ParentID = @EntityID AND l.IsDeleted = 0
GROUP BY l.Name;

-- ---- 7. EDIT ----
SELECT 'EDIT' as Check, ID, Name, AfterSavePageID, AfterCancelPageID
FROM XStudio_Edit_Mst_Tbl WHERE ParentID = @EntityID AND IsDeleted = 0;

-- ---- 8. EDIT CONTROLS: NULL ControlProperties ----
SELECT 'NULL CONTROL PROPS' as Check, ControlName, ControlType
FROM XStudio_EditControls_Mst_Tbl ec
JOIN XStudio_Edit_Mst_Tbl e ON e.ID = ec.ParentID
WHERE e.ParentID = @EntityID AND ec.IsDeleted = 0 AND ec.ControlProperties IS NULL;
-- Should return 0 rows

-- ---- 9. DROPDOWN CONTROLS WITH WRONG TYPE ----
SELECT 'WRONG CONTROLTYPE FOR DROPDOWN' as Check, ec.ControlName, ec.ControlType
FROM XStudio_EditControls_Mst_Tbl ec
JOIN XStudio_Edit_Mst_Tbl e ON e.ID = ec.ParentID
JOIN XStudio_Attribute_Mst_Tbl a ON a.ID = ec.AttributeID
JOIN XStudio_Configuration.dbo.XStudio_DataType_Mst_Tbl dt ON dt.ID = a.DataType
WHERE e.ParentID = @EntityID
  AND ec.IsDeleted = 0
  AND dt.Name = 'Dropdown'
  AND ec.ControlType NOT IN ('selectbox', 'dropdown');
-- Should return 0 rows

-- ---- 10. PAGES ----
SELECT 'PAGES' as Check, ID, Name, IsPopup
FROM XStudio_Page_Mst_Tbl WHERE IsDeleted = 0
AND Name LIKE '%' + @EntityName + '%';

-- ---- 11. MENU ----
SELECT 'MENU' as Check, m.Name, m.PageID, COUNT(mr.RoleID) as RoleCount
FROM XStudio_Menu_Mst_Tbl m
LEFT JOIN XStudio_MenuRights_Mst_Tbl mr ON mr.ParentID = m.ID AND mr.IsDeleted = 0
JOIN XStudio_Page_Mst_Tbl p ON p.ID = m.PageID
WHERE p.Name LIKE '%' + @EntityName + '%'
GROUP BY m.Name, m.PageID;

-- ---- 12. GENERATED LV VIEW (run in data DB context) ----
SELECT 'LV VIEW' as Check, TABLE_NAME
FROM XStudio_XMES.INFORMATION_SCHEMA.TABLES
WHERE TABLE_NAME LIKE 'XStudio_List_' + @EntityName + '%';

-- ---- 13. PHYSICAL TABLE ----
SELECT 'PHYSICAL TABLE' as Check, TABLE_NAME
FROM XStudio_XMES.INFORMATION_SCHEMA.TABLES
WHERE TABLE_NAME = @EntityName;

-- ---- 14. ORPHANED OBJECTS CHECK ----
-- Objects whose ParentID doesn't resolve to an existing object
SELECT 'ORPHANED EDIT CONTROLS' as Check, COUNT(*) as [Count]
FROM XStudio_EditControls_Mst_Tbl ec
WHERE ec.IsDeleted = 0
AND NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM XStudio_Edit_Mst_Tbl e WHERE e.ID = ec.ParentID AND e.IsDeleted = 0);

SELECT 'ORPHANED LV COLUMNS' as Check, COUNT(*) as [Count]
FROM XStudio_LvColumns_Mst_Tbl lc
WHERE lc.IsDeleted = 0
AND NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM XStudio_LV_Mst_Tbl lv WHERE lv.ID = lc.ParentID AND lv.IsDeleted = 0);

-- ---- 15. CUSTOM LV LINK/ACTION COLUMNS ----
SELECT 'UNSAFE LINK COLUMN' as Check, lv.Name as LVName, lc.Name as ColumnName,
       lc.Type, lc.IsCustomField, lc.AttributeID, lc.RelationID, lc.RelationIDList
FROM XStudio_LvColumns_Mst_Tbl lc
JOIN XStudio_LV_Mst_Tbl lv ON lv.ID = lc.ParentID AND lv.IsDeleted = 0
WHERE lc.IsDeleted = 0
  AND (lc.Type = 'link' OR lc.Name IN ('Edit','Delete'))
  AND (
        ISNULL(lc.IsCustomField,0) <> 1
     OR ISNULL(lc.AttributeID,'') <> ''
     OR ISNULL(lc.RelationID,'') <> ''
     OR ISNULL(lc.RelationIDList,'') <> ''
  );
-- Should return 0 rows for pure navigation/action links

-- ---- 16. PAGE CONTROL BINDING ----
SELECT 'BROKEN PAGE CONTROL' as Check, p.Name as PageName, pc.ControlType, pc.ControlID
FROM XStudio_PageControls_Mst_Tbl pc
JOIN XStudio_Page_Mst_Tbl p ON p.ID = pc.ParentID AND p.IsDeleted = 0
WHERE pc.IsDeleted = 0
  AND (
       (LOWER(pc.ControlType) IN ('grid','lv','listview') AND NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM XStudio_LV_Mst_Tbl lv WHERE lv.ID = pc.ControlID AND lv.IsDeleted = 0))
    OR (LOWER(pc.ControlType) = 'edit' AND NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM XStudio_Edit_Mst_Tbl e WHERE e.ID = pc.ControlID AND e.IsDeleted = 0))
  );
-- Should return 0 rows

-- ---- 17. WORKFLOW SIDE-EFFECT ACTIONS ----
SELECT 'WORKFLOW ACTIONS' as Check, COUNT(*) as [Count]
FROM XStudio_WorkFlow_Action_Mst_Tbl
WHERE IsDeleted = 0;
```
