## 41. Repair Scripts

**Source file:** `03_SQL_TEMPLATES/repair_scripts.sql`  
**File type:** `.sql`  
**Size:** `20614` bytes  
**Modified on:** `2026-05-12 11:35:21`  
**SHA256:** `102c1adc2b8f`  
**Encoding used:** `utf-8`

> Start of imported document.

```sql
-- ============================================================
-- Repair Scripts
-- Exceptional recovery diagnostics for broken XStudio configuration
-- ============================================================
--
-- Purpose:
--   Diagnose broken XStudio configuration and route repairs through
--   the correct XStudio stored procedures or corrected SQL templates.
--
-- This file is NOT part of the normal build flow.
--
-- Normal build flow:
--   1. entity_create_template.sql
--   2. dropdown_properties_template.sql, only for dropdown attributes
--   3. edit_control_safe_json_template.sql
--   4. lv_link_column_template.sql, only for LV action/navigation links
--   5. page_menu_template.sql
--   6. postflight_checks.sql
--
-- Hard rule:
--   Do not repair by ad-hoc direct DML when an XStudio SP or corrected
--   template exists.
--
-- This file intentionally avoids executable INSERT/UPDATE/DELETE/MERGE
-- statements against XStudio configuration tables. It is read-only except
-- for commented SP calls where the SP signature is known.
--
-- If a direct fallback is absolutely required:
--   - stop;
--   - document why no SP/template covers the case;
--   - inspect live sys.columns/sys.parameters;
--   - take a backup/snapshot;
--   - write a one-off guarded script;
--   - rerun postflight_checks.sql.
--
-- ============================================================

SET NOCOUNT ON;

-- ============================================================
-- 0. INPUTS
-- ============================================================

DECLARE @SystemID varchar(36) = '6C8C0070-60B5-4474-BEC8-5C02197B32E6';
DECLARE @ConfigDB sysname;
DECLARE @DataDB sysname;

DECLARE @EntityID varchar(36) = '<entity_guid>';
DECLARE @EntityName varchar(100) = '<EntityTableName>';
DECLARE @EditID varchar(36) = '<edit_guid>';
DECLARE @LVID varchar(36) = '<lv_guid>';
DECLARE @PageID varchar(36) = '<page_guid>';
DECLARE @MenuID varchar(36) = '<menu_guid>';
DECLARE @RoleID varchar(36) = '<role_guid>';

-- Use a real XStudio user GUID for any SP-backed recovery.
DECLARE @UserID varchar(36) = '<deployment_user_guid>';
DECLARE @IPAddress varchar(100) = 'T-SQL';

SELECT
    @ConfigDB = ConfigurationDatabase,
    @DataDB = DataDatabase
FROM XStudio_Configuration.dbo.XStudio_GetDataSourceInfoFromSystemId_Fun(@SystemID);

SELECT
    'CONTEXT' AS [Check],
    @SystemID AS SystemID,
    @ConfigDB AS ConfigDB,
    @DataDB AS DataDB,
    @EntityID AS EntityID,
    @EntityName AS EntityName,
    @EditID AS EditID,
    @LVID AS LVID,
    @PageID AS PageID,
    @MenuID AS MenuID,
    @RoleID AS RoleID;

-- ============================================================
-- 1. REPAIR CATEGORY: NULL / INVALID EDIT CONTROL JSON
-- ============================================================
--
-- Old unsafe pattern removed:
--   UPDATE XStudio_EditControls_Mst_Tbl SET ControlProperties = ...
--
-- Correct route:
--   Run 03_SQL_TEMPLATES/edit_control_safe_json_template.sql
--
-- That template:
--   - calls Xstudio_Insert_Edit_Columns_Info_Usp first;
--   - checks live schema;
--   - completes ControlProperties with documented fallback only because
--     no generic save-edit-control SP is confirmed;
--   - validates dropdown JSON.

DECLARE @SQL nvarchar(max);

SET @SQL = N'
SELECT
    ''EDIT CONTROL JSON ISSUE'' AS [Check],
    ec.ID,
    ec.ParentID,
    ec.ControlType,
    ec.ControlName,
    CASE
        WHEN ec.ControlProperties IS NULL THEN ''NULL''
        WHEN LTRIM(RTRIM(CONVERT(nvarchar(max), ec.ControlProperties))) = '''' THEN ''BLANK''
        WHEN ISJSON(CONVERT(nvarchar(max), ec.ControlProperties)) <> 1 THEN ''INVALID_JSON''
        ELSE ''OK''
    END AS Status
FROM ' + QUOTENAME(@ConfigDB) + N'.dbo.XStudio_EditControls_Mst_Tbl ec WITH (NOLOCK)
WHERE ec.ParentID = @EditID
  AND ec.IsDeleted = 0
  AND
  (
       ec.ControlProperties IS NULL
    OR LTRIM(RTRIM(CONVERT(nvarchar(max), ec.ControlProperties))) = ''''
    OR ISJSON(CONVERT(nvarchar(max), ec.ControlProperties)) <> 1
);';

EXEC sys.sp_executesql
    @SQL,
    N'@EditID varchar(36)',
    @EditID = @EditID;

-- Recovery:
--   Open edit_control_safe_json_template.sql.
--   Set @SystemID, @EntityID, @EditID, @UserID.
--   Run it.
--   Then run postflight_checks.sql.

-- ============================================================
-- 2. REPAIR CATEGORY: DROPDOWN CONTROLTYPE / DROPDOWN JSON
-- ============================================================
--
-- Old unsafe pattern removed:
--   UPDATE XStudio_EditControls_Mst_Tbl
--   SET ControlType = 'selectbox', ControlName = 'cmb_' + AttributeName ...
--
-- Correct route:
--   1. Run dropdown_properties_template.sql for each dropdown attribute.
--   2. Run edit_control_safe_json_template.sql for the edit form.

SET @SQL = N'
SELECT
    ''DROPDOWN EDIT CONTROL ISSUE'' AS [Check],
    ec.ID,
    ec.ControlName,
    ec.ControlType,
    a.ID AS AttributeID,
    a.Name AS AttributeName,
    dt.Name AS DataType,
    JSON_VALUE(CONVERT(nvarchar(max), ec.ControlProperties), ''$.DropdownEntityID'') AS DropdownEntityID,
    JSON_VALUE(CONVERT(nvarchar(max), ec.ControlProperties), ''$.DropdownValueField'') AS DropdownValueField,
    JSON_VALUE(CONVERT(nvarchar(max), ec.ControlProperties), ''$.DropdownTextField'') AS DropdownTextField
FROM ' + QUOTENAME(@ConfigDB) + N'.dbo.XStudio_EditControls_Mst_Tbl ec WITH (NOLOCK)
JOIN ' + QUOTENAME(@ConfigDB) + N'.dbo.XStudio_Attribute_Mst_Tbl a WITH (NOLOCK)
  ON a.ID = ec.AttributeID
 AND a.IsDeleted = 0
JOIN XStudio_Configuration.dbo.XStudio_DataType_Mst_Tbl dt WITH (NOLOCK)
  ON dt.ID = a.DataType
 AND dt.IsDeleted = 0
WHERE ec.ParentID = @EditID
  AND ec.IsDeleted = 0
  AND dt.Name = ''Dropdown''
  AND
  (
       ec.ControlType <> ''selectbox''
    OR ec.ControlName <> ''cmb_'' + a.Name
    OR ec.ControlProperties IS NULL
    OR ISJSON(CONVERT(nvarchar(max), ec.ControlProperties)) <> 1
    OR ISNULL(JSON_VALUE(CONVERT(nvarchar(max), ec.ControlProperties), ''$.DropdownEntityID''), '''') = ''''
    OR ISNULL(JSON_VALUE(CONVERT(nvarchar(max), ec.ControlProperties), ''$.DropdownValueField''), '''') = ''''
    OR ISNULL(JSON_VALUE(CONVERT(nvarchar(max), ec.ControlProperties), ''$.DropdownTextField''), '''') = ''''
  );';

EXEC sys.sp_executesql
    @SQL,
    N'@EditID varchar(36)',
    @EditID = @EditID;

-- Recovery:
--   Run dropdown_properties_template.sql first for missing property/relation metadata.
--   Then run edit_control_safe_json_template.sql.

-- ============================================================
-- 3. REPAIR CATEGORY: MISSING / PARTIAL DROPDOWN PROPERTIES
-- ============================================================
--
-- Old unsafe pattern removed:
--   INSERT six rows directly into XStudio_AttributeProperties_Mst_Tbl.
--
-- Correct route:
--   Run dropdown_properties_template.sql.
--
-- That template:
--   - calls XStudio_Add_DropDown_Attribute_Properties_And_Relation_Usp;
--   - validates 9-param or 12-param signature;
--   - enforces the six-property contract;
--   - uses documented fallback only for incomplete SP output.

SET @SQL = N'
SELECT
    ''DROPDOWN PROPERTY ISSUE'' AS [Check],
    a.ID AS AttributeID,
    a.Name AS AttributeName,
    COUNT(p.ID) AS ActivePropertyCount,
    SUM(CASE WHEN p.Name = ''DropdownDatabase'' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS HasDropdownDatabase,
    SUM(CASE WHEN p.Name = ''DropdownEntity'' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS HasDropdownEntity,
    SUM(CASE WHEN p.Name = ''DropdownEntryMode'' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS HasDropdownEntryMode,
    SUM(CASE WHEN p.Name = ''DropdownTextField'' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS HasDropdownTextField,
    SUM(CASE WHEN p.Name = ''DropdownValueField'' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS HasDropdownValueField,
    SUM(CASE WHEN p.Name = ''TextCharacterLength'' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS HasTextCharacterLength
FROM ' + QUOTENAME(@ConfigDB) + N'.dbo.XStudio_Attribute_Mst_Tbl a WITH (NOLOCK)
JOIN XStudio_Configuration.dbo.XStudio_DataType_Mst_Tbl dt WITH (NOLOCK)
  ON dt.ID = a.DataType
 AND dt.IsDeleted = 0
LEFT JOIN ' + QUOTENAME(@ConfigDB) + N'.dbo.XStudio_AttributeProperties_Mst_Tbl p WITH (NOLOCK)
  ON p.ParentID = a.ID
 AND p.IsDeleted = 0
WHERE a.ParentID = @EntityID
  AND a.IsDeleted = 0
  AND dt.Name = ''Dropdown''
GROUP BY a.ID, a.Name
HAVING
       SUM(CASE WHEN p.Name = ''DropdownDatabase'' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) <> 1
    OR SUM(CASE WHEN p.Name = ''DropdownEntity'' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) <> 1
    OR SUM(CASE WHEN p.Name = ''DropdownEntryMode'' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) <> 1
    OR SUM(CASE WHEN p.Name = ''DropdownTextField'' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) <> 1
    OR SUM(CASE WHEN p.Name = ''DropdownValueField'' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) <> 1
    OR SUM(CASE WHEN p.Name = ''TextCharacterLength'' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) <> 1;';

EXEC sys.sp_executesql
    @SQL,
    N'@EntityID varchar(36)',
    @EntityID = @EntityID;

-- Recovery:
--   Run dropdown_properties_template.sql for each AttributeID returned.

-- ============================================================
-- 4. REPAIR CATEGORY: BROKEN / MISSING LV VIEW
-- ============================================================
--
-- Correct route:
--   Use XStudio_CreateViewForLV_Usp with explicit non-null fragments.
--
-- Confirmed signature:
--   @LvID
--   @LvName
--   @DataDatabase
--   @ColumnQuery
--   @RelationQuery
--   @GroupByQuery
--   @WhereCondition
--   @IsCalculatedView
--
-- Use lv_link_column_template.sql when the problem involves an action/link
-- column and view regeneration.

SET @SQL = N'
SELECT
    ''LV VIEW CONTEXT'' AS [Check],
    lv.ID AS LVID,
    lv.Name AS LVName,
    lv.ParentID AS EntityID,
    @DataDB AS DataDB,
    ''Check expected view name manually from LV generation conventions'' AS Action
FROM ' + QUOTENAME(@ConfigDB) + N'.dbo.XStudio_LV_Mst_Tbl lv WITH (NOLOCK)
WHERE lv.ID = @LVID
  AND lv.IsDeleted = 0;';

EXEC sys.sp_executesql
    @SQL,
    N'@LVID varchar(36), @DataDB sysname',
    @LVID = @LVID,
    @DataDB = @DataDB;

-- SP-backed recovery template:
-- Replace all fragments before uncommenting.
--
-- EXEC XStudio_Configuration.dbo.XStudio_CreateViewForLV_Usp
--     @LvID = @LVID,
--     @LvName = '<lv_name>',
--     @DataDatabase = @DataDB,
--     @ColumnQuery = '<SELECT ...>',
--     @RelationQuery = '<FROM/JOIN ...>',
--     @GroupByQuery = '',
--     @WhereCondition = '<WHERE ...>',
--     @IsCalculatedView = 0;

-- ============================================================
-- 5. REPAIR CATEGORY: UNSAFE LV LINK / ACTION COLUMN
-- ============================================================
--
-- Old unsafe pattern removed:
--   UPDATE XStudio_LvColumns_Mst_Tbl
--   SET Type='link', AttributeID='', RelationID='', ...
--
-- Correct route:
--   Run lv_link_column_template.sql.
--
-- That template:
--   - checks for a future LV-column save SP;
--   - uses guarded schema-inspected DML only because no such SP is confirmed;
--   - sets pure action links without AttributeID/RelationID binding;
--   - calls XStudio_CreateViewForLV_Usp.

SET @SQL = N'
SELECT
    ''UNSAFE LV LINK COLUMN'' AS [Check],
    lvc.ID,
    lvc.ParentID AS LVID,
    lvc.Name,
    lvc.Header,
    lvc.[Type],
    lvc.IsCustomField,
    lvc.AttributeID,
    lvc.RelationID,
    lvc.RelationIDList,
    lvc.ColumnProperties
FROM ' + QUOTENAME(@ConfigDB) + N'.dbo.XStudio_LvColumns_Mst_Tbl lvc WITH (NOLOCK)
WHERE lvc.ParentID = @LVID
  AND lvc.IsDeleted = 0
  AND
  (
       lvc.[Type] = ''link''
    OR lvc.Name IN (''Edit'', ''Delete'', ''Open'')
  )
  AND
  (
        ISNULL(lvc.IsCustomField, 0) <> 1
     OR lvc.AttributeID IS NOT NULL
     OR lvc.RelationID IS NOT NULL
     OR ISNULL(lvc.RelationIDList, '''') <> ''''
     OR lvc.ColumnProperties IS NULL
     OR ISJSON(CONVERT(nvarchar(max), lvc.ColumnProperties)) <> 1
  );';

EXEC sys.sp_executesql
    @SQL,
    N'@LVID varchar(36)',
    @LVID = @LVID;

-- Recovery:
--   Run lv_link_column_template.sql for each unsafe link/action column.

-- ============================================================
-- 6. REPAIR CATEGORY: DUPLICATE MENU ITEMS
-- ============================================================
--
-- Old unsafe pattern removed:
--   UPDATE XStudio_Menu_Mst_Tbl SET IsDeleted = 1 ...
--
-- Correct route:
--   Prefer correcting/recreating menu through page_menu_template.sql.
--   If a duplicate must be removed, inspect and use:
--     XStudio_Delete_Menu_Configuration_Usp
--
-- This file does not guess the delete SP signature. Inspect it live first.

SET @SQL = N'
SELECT
    ''DUPLICATE MENU'' AS [Check],
    m.Name,
    m.PageID,
    COUNT(*) AS ActiveCount
FROM ' + QUOTENAME(@ConfigDB) + N'.dbo.XStudio_Menu_Mst_Tbl m WITH (NOLOCK)
WHERE m.IsDeleted = 0
GROUP BY m.Name, m.PageID
HAVING COUNT(*) > 1;';

EXEC sys.sp_executesql @SQL;

SELECT
    'MENU DELETE SP SIGNATURE' AS [Check],
    p.parameter_id,
    p.name,
    TYPE_NAME(p.user_type_id) AS ParameterType,
    p.max_length
FROM XStudio_Configuration.sys.parameters p
WHERE p.object_id = OBJECT_ID('XStudio_Configuration.dbo.XStudio_Delete_Menu_Configuration_Usp')
ORDER BY p.parameter_id;

-- Recovery:
--   1. Identify the duplicate MenuID to remove.
--   2. Inspect the signature above.
--   3. Call XStudio_Delete_Menu_Configuration_Usp with the live signature.
--   4. Rerun postflight_checks.sql.

-- ============================================================
-- 7. REPAIR CATEGORY: MENU WITHOUT RIGHTS / ROLE CANNOT SEE MENU
-- ============================================================
--
-- Correct route:
--   XStudio_Save_Menu_Rights_Usp
--
-- Confirmed signature:
--   @SystemId
--   @Id       -- MenuID
--   @RoleID
--   @UserId
--   @IPAddress

SET @SQL = N'
SELECT
    ''MENU WITHOUT RIGHTS'' AS [Check],
    m.ID AS MenuID,
    m.Name AS MenuName,
    m.PageID
FROM ' + QUOTENAME(@ConfigDB) + N'.dbo.XStudio_Menu_Mst_Tbl m WITH (NOLOCK)
LEFT JOIN ' + QUOTENAME(@ConfigDB) + N'.dbo.XStudio_MenuRights_Mst_Tbl mr WITH (NOLOCK)
  ON mr.ParentID = m.ID
 AND mr.IsDeleted = 0
WHERE m.IsDeleted = 0
  AND (@MenuID = ''<menu_guid>'' OR m.ID = @MenuID)
GROUP BY m.ID, m.Name, m.PageID
HAVING COUNT(mr.ID) = 0;';

EXEC sys.sp_executesql
    @SQL,
    N'@MenuID varchar(36)',
    @MenuID = @MenuID;

-- SP-backed recovery:
-- Uncomment only after setting @MenuID, @RoleID, @UserID.
--
-- IF NOT EXISTS
-- (
--     SELECT 1
--     FROM XStudio_MenuRights_Mst_Tbl
--     WHERE ParentID = @MenuID
--       AND RoleID = @RoleID
--       AND IsDeleted = 0
-- )
-- BEGIN
--     EXEC XStudio_Configuration.dbo.XStudio_Save_Menu_Rights_Usp
--         @SystemId  = @SystemID,
--         @Id        = @MenuID,
--         @RoleID    = @RoleID,
--         @UserId    = @UserID,
--         @IPAddress = @IPAddress;
-- END;

-- ============================================================
-- 8. REPAIR CATEGORY: PAGE EXISTS BUT BLANK
-- ============================================================
--
-- Correct route:
--   XStudio_Save_Page_Control_Configuration_Usp
--
-- Confirmed signature:
--   @SystemId
--   @Id
--   @Name
--   @ParentId
--   @RowId
--   @DivId
--   @ControlType
--   @ControlId
--   @ControlUrl
--   @CustomCss
--   @LoadUrlAsIFrame
--   @Description
--   @ControlProperties
--   @UserId
--   @IPAddress

SET @SQL = N'
SELECT
    ''BLANK PAGE'' AS [Check],
    p.ID AS PageID,
    p.Name AS PageName,
    COUNT(pc.ID) AS ActivePageControlCount
FROM ' + QUOTENAME(@ConfigDB) + N'.dbo.XStudio_Page_Mst_Tbl p WITH (NOLOCK)
LEFT JOIN ' + QUOTENAME(@ConfigDB) + N'.dbo.XStudio_PageControls_Mst_Tbl pc WITH (NOLOCK)
  ON pc.ParentID = p.ID
 AND pc.IsDeleted = 0
WHERE p.IsDeleted = 0
  AND (@PageID = ''<page_guid>'' OR p.ID = @PageID)
GROUP BY p.ID, p.Name
HAVING COUNT(pc.ID) = 0;';

EXEC sys.sp_executesql
    @SQL,
    N'@PageID varchar(36)',
    @PageID = @PageID;

-- SP-backed recovery template:
-- Set values before uncommenting.
--
-- DECLARE @PageControlID varchar(36) = CAST(NEWID() AS varchar(36));
-- DECLARE @ControlID varchar(36) = '<lv_or_edit_or_control_guid>';
-- DECLARE @ControlType varchar(50) = '<grid|edit|tab|inlinegrid|html|dashboard>';
--
-- EXEC XStudio_Configuration.dbo.XStudio_Save_Page_Control_Configuration_Usp
--     @SystemId          = @SystemID,
--     @Id                = @PageControlID,
--     @Name              = '<page control name>',
--     @ParentId          = @PageID,
--     @RowId             = '1',
--     @DivId             = '1',
--     @ControlType       = @ControlType,
--     @ControlId         = @ControlID,
--     @ControlUrl        = '',
--     @CustomCss         = '',
--     @LoadUrlAsIFrame   = 0,
--     @Description       = '',
--     @ControlProperties = '{"IpFiltering":"False"}',
--     @UserId            = @UserID,
--     @IPAddress         = @IPAddress;

-- ============================================================
-- 9. REPAIR CATEGORY: EDIT AfterSavePageID / AfterCancelPageID BROKEN
-- ============================================================
--
-- No generic edit-navigation save SP has been confirmed in the current
-- SP reference. This remains an exceptional case.
--
-- Normal prevention:
--   Set edit navigation correctly in the edit creation/template flow.
--
-- Recovery approach:
--   1. diagnose here;
--   2. if no SP exists in the live environment, create a one-off guarded
--      exception script with live schema inspection;
--   3. do not place direct UPDATE in this generic KB repair file.

SET @SQL = N'
SELECT
    ''BROKEN EDIT NAVIGATION'' AS [Check],
    e.ID,
    e.Name,
    e.AfterSavePageID,
    save_page.Name AS AfterSavePageName,
    e.AfterCancelPageID,
    cancel_page.Name AS AfterCancelPageName
FROM ' + QUOTENAME(@ConfigDB) + N'.dbo.XStudio_Edit_Mst_Tbl e WITH (NOLOCK)
LEFT JOIN ' + QUOTENAME(@ConfigDB) + N'.dbo.XStudio_Page_Mst_Tbl save_page WITH (NOLOCK)
  ON save_page.ID = e.AfterSavePageID
 AND save_page.IsDeleted = 0
LEFT JOIN ' + QUOTENAME(@ConfigDB) + N'.dbo.XStudio_Page_Mst_Tbl cancel_page WITH (NOLOCK)
  ON cancel_page.ID = e.AfterCancelPageID
 AND cancel_page.IsDeleted = 0
WHERE e.IsDeleted = 0
  AND (@EditID = ''<edit_guid>'' OR e.ID = @EditID)
  AND
  (
       (e.AfterSavePageID IS NOT NULL AND save_page.ID IS NULL)
    OR (e.AfterCancelPageID IS NOT NULL AND cancel_page.ID IS NULL)
  );';

EXEC sys.sp_executesql
    @SQL,
    N'@EditID varchar(36)',
    @EditID = @EditID;

-- ============================================================
-- 10. REPAIR CATEGORY: ORPHANED OBJECTS
-- ============================================================

SET @SQL = N'
SELECT ''ORPHANED EDIT CONTROLS'' AS [Check], COUNT(*) AS [Count]
FROM ' + QUOTENAME(@ConfigDB) + N'.dbo.XStudio_EditControls_Mst_Tbl ec WITH (NOLOCK)
WHERE ec.IsDeleted = 0
  AND NOT EXISTS
  (
      SELECT 1
      FROM ' + QUOTENAME(@ConfigDB) + N'.dbo.XStudio_Edit_Mst_Tbl e WITH (NOLOCK)
      WHERE e.ID = ec.ParentID
        AND e.IsDeleted = 0
  )

UNION ALL

SELECT ''ORPHANED LV COLUMNS'', COUNT(*)
FROM ' + QUOTENAME(@ConfigDB) + N'.dbo.XStudio_LvColumns_Mst_Tbl lc WITH (NOLOCK)
WHERE lc.IsDeleted = 0
  AND NOT EXISTS
  (
      SELECT 1
      FROM ' + QUOTENAME(@ConfigDB) + N'.dbo.XStudio_LV_Mst_Tbl lv WITH (NOLOCK)
      WHERE lv.ID = lc.ParentID
        AND lv.IsDeleted = 0
  )

UNION ALL

SELECT ''ORPHANED PAGE CONTROLS'', COUNT(*)
FROM ' + QUOTENAME(@ConfigDB) + N'.dbo.XStudio_PageControls_Mst_Tbl pc WITH (NOLOCK)
WHERE pc.IsDeleted = 0
  AND NOT EXISTS
  (
      SELECT 1
      FROM ' + QUOTENAME(@ConfigDB) + N'.dbo.XStudio_Page_Mst_Tbl p WITH (NOLOCK)
      WHERE p.ID = pc.ParentID
        AND p.IsDeleted = 0
  )

UNION ALL

SELECT ''ORPHANED MENU RIGHTS'', COUNT(*)
FROM ' + QUOTENAME(@ConfigDB) + N'.dbo.XStudio_MenuRights_Mst_Tbl mr WITH (NOLOCK)
WHERE mr.IsDeleted = 0
  AND NOT EXISTS
  (
      SELECT 1
      FROM ' + QUOTENAME(@ConfigDB) + N'.dbo.XStudio_Menu_Mst_Tbl m WITH (NOLOCK)
      WHERE m.ID = mr.ParentID
        AND m.IsDeleted = 0
  );';

EXEC sys.sp_executesql @SQL;

-- Recovery:
--   PageControl orphan -> XStudio_Delete_Page_Control_Configuration_Usp
--   MenuRights orphan -> XStudio_Delete_Menu_Rights_Usp
--   Menu orphan/wrong -> XStudio_Delete_Menu_Configuration_Usp or XStudio_Save_Menu_Configuration_Usp
--   LV/Edit orphan    -> use the owning LV/Edit template or one-off exceptional recovery after SP inspection

-- ============================================================
-- 11. FINAL STEP
-- ============================================================
--
-- After any recovery:
--   1. run postflight_checks.sql;
--   2. test the UI with the affected role;
--   3. if the fix required a direct fallback not present in a corrected
--      template, add that case to the KB gap table instead of normalizing it
--      here.
```

> End of imported document.

## 42. Broken Link Columns

**Source file:** `04_DEBUGGING/broken_link_columns.md`  
**File type:** `.md`  
**Size:** `9846` bytes  
**Modified on:** `2026-05-12 16:55:03`  
**SHA256:** `778909c87b28`  
**Encoding used:** `utf-8`

> Start of imported document.

### Debugging: Broken Link Columns

#### Symptoms

- Link column appears in grid but clicking does nothing
- Link column navigates to wrong page
- Link column shows raw JSON instead of clickable text
- Link column throws “page not found”
- Link column does not appear at all
- Grid crashes when link column exists
- Generated LV view fails after a link/action column is added

#### Correct repair rule

```text
Use XStudio SPs wherever a suitable SP exists.
If no suitable SP exists, preserve the operation as a documented no-SP exception with live schema inspection, guards, real user audit values, and postflight.
```

For LV link/action columns:

```text
No generic save-LV-column SP is currently confirmed.
```

Therefore, correcting `XStudio_LvColumns_Mst_Tbl` link-column metadata remains a **documented no-SP exception**. The generated view must be regenerated through:

```text
XStudio_CreateViewForLV_Usp
```

Do not remove the link-column field knowledge. Make it safe.

#### Link-column contract

A working pure navigation/action column normally has:

| Field | Required value | Notes |
|---|---|---|
| `Name` | no spaces, e.g. `WOProduction` | Internal identifier and view alias |
| `Header` | display header | Grid header text |
| `Type` | `link` | Required |
| `LinkType` | `fixedtext` | Uses `LinkValue` as visible link text |
| `LinkValue` | `Open`, `Edit`, etc. | Visible clickable text |
| `CustomLink` / `ColumnCustomLink` | URL, page ID, or platform action target | Column name varies by schema |
| `CustomLinkTargetMode` | `Inside` or `Popup` | Match live examples |
| `RoleIDList` | role GUIDs or blank | Optional visibility restriction |
| `IsVisible` | `1` | Required to show |
| `IsCustomField` | `1` | Prevents physical-column binding |
| `AttributeID` | `NULL` or blank | Must not point to a real attribute |
| `RelationID` | `NULL` or blank | Must be blank for pure link/action column |
| `RelationIDList` | `NULL` or blank | Must be blank |
| `Alignment` | `center` | Common working pattern |
| `IsRestricted` | `1` only if role-restricted | Otherwise `0` |
| `SerialNumber` | column order | Place after normal columns |
| `ParentID` | LV ID | Owning LV |
| `ColumnProperties` | valid JSON | Required |

Minimum `ColumnProperties`:

```json
{
  "IsConditionalFormatting": false,
  "IsContextMenuEnableForApproval": false,
  "IsDefaultSearch": false,
  "Groupable": true,
  "IsPivotColumn": true,
  "ColumnUnit": "",
  "Title": "",
  "TitleAlignment": "left"
}
```

#### Preflight before any no-SP repair

Inspect the live LV column schema:

```sql
SELECT
    c.name,
    TYPE_NAME(c.user_type_id) AS DataType,
    c.max_length,
    c.is_nullable,
    c.is_identity
FROM XStudio_Configuration_XMES.sys.columns c
JOIN XStudio_Configuration_XMES.sys.tables t
  ON t.object_id = c.object_id
JOIN XStudio_Configuration_XMES.sys.schemas s
  ON s.schema_id = t.schema_id
WHERE s.name = 'dbo'
  AND t.name = 'XStudio_LvColumns_Mst_Tbl'
ORDER BY c.column_id;
```

Resolve the target row:

```sql
SELECT
    ID,
    Name,
    Header,
    Type,
    LinkType,
    LinkValue,
    CustomLink,
    CustomLinkTargetMode,
    AttributeID,
    RelationID,
    RelationIDList,
    RoleIDList,
    IsVisible,
    IsCustomField,
    ColumnProperties
FROM XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_LvColumns_Mst_Tbl
WHERE Name = 'WOProduction'
  AND ParentID = '<lv_guid>'
  AND IsDeleted = 0;
```

Use a real user:

```sql
DECLARE @UserID varchar(36) = '<deployment_user_guid>';
DECLARE @IPAddress varchar(100) = 'T-SQL';
```

Do not use `sa`.

---

#### Cause 1 — Wrong `Type`

If `Type` is `varchar`, `text`, or blank, the grid treats the column as data instead of a link.

Diagnosis:

```sql
SELECT
    ID,
    Name,
    [Type],
    LinkType,
    IsCustomField,
    AttributeID
FROM XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_LvColumns_Mst_Tbl
WHERE Name = 'WOProduction'
  AND ParentID = '<lv_guid>'
  AND IsDeleted = 0;
```

Correct no-SP exception repair:

```sql
UPDATE XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_LvColumns_Mst_Tbl
SET [Type]       = 'link',
    LinkType     = 'fixedtext',
    IsCustomField = 1,
    AttributeID  = NULL,
    RelationID   = NULL,
    RelationIDList = NULL,
    ModifiedBy   = @UserID,
    ModifiedOn   = GETDATE()
WHERE ID = '<column_guid>'
  AND IsDeleted = 0;
```

If the local schema stores blank strings instead of nullable GUID fields, use the live schema’s expected representation.

Then regenerate the LV view.

---

#### Cause 2 — `IsCustomField = 0` with nonblank `AttributeID`

When `IsCustomField = 0` and `AttributeID` has a value, XStudio may look for a physical attribute column. If the action column has no physical attribute, the generated view can fail.

Diagnosis:

```sql
SELECT
    Name,
    IsCustomField,
    AttributeID,
    RelationID,
    RelationIDList
FROM XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_LvColumns_Mst_Tbl
WHERE ParentID = '<lv_guid>'
  AND Name = 'WOProduction'
  AND IsDeleted = 0;
```

Fix contract:

```text
IsCustomField = 1
AttributeID = NULL/blank
RelationID = NULL/blank
RelationIDList = NULL/blank
```

Do this as a guarded no-SP exception and regenerate the view.

---

#### Cause 3 — `CustomLink` URL format wrong

URL mode format:

```text
{SystemName}/DEFAULT/{PageName}?{Param}={Token}
```

Examples:

```text
XMES/DEFAULT/List_Page_XMES_Production_Trn_Tbl?WOID={ID}
XMES/DEFAULT/List_Page_XMES_Consumption_Trn_Tbl?WOID={ID}
```

Common mistakes:

| Mistake | Correct |
|---|---|
| Leading slash `/XMES/...` | `XMES/...` |
| Token `{WOID}` when LV exposes `ID` | use `{ID}` or expose `WOID` |
| Page name not exact | match `XStudio_Page_Mst_Tbl.Name` |
| URL target used where page ID is expected | use target page ID for that action type |

Diagnosis:

```sql
SELECT
    ID,
    Name,
    DisplayName,
    IsDeleted
FROM XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_Page_Mst_Tbl
WHERE Name = 'List_Page_XMES_Production_Trn_Tbl'
  AND IsDeleted = 0;

SELECT
    Name,
    CustomLink,
    CustomLinkTargetMode
FROM XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_LvColumns_Mst_Tbl
WHERE ID = '<column_guid>'
  AND IsDeleted = 0;
```

---

#### Cause 4 — `{COLUMN_NAME}` token not in LV result set

The token in `CustomLink` is replaced from the LV result row. If the view does not expose that column, the token remains unreplaced or becomes empty.

Diagnosis:

```sql
SELECT
    COLUMN_NAME
FROM XStudio_XMES.INFORMATION_SCHEMA.COLUMNS
WHERE TABLE_NAME = 'XStudio_List_MyEntity_Mst_Tbl_Vw'
ORDER BY ORDINAL_POSITION;
```

If `{ID}` is used, the generated LV view must expose `ID`.

Fix options:

```text
Add ID as a hidden LV column
or change CustomLink token to an exposed column
then regenerate the LV view
```

---

#### Cause 5 — `ColumnProperties` is NULL, blank, or malformed

Diagnosis:

```sql
SELECT
    ID,
    Name,
    ColumnProperties,
    ISJSON(CONVERT(nvarchar(max), ColumnProperties)) AS IsValidJson
FROM XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_LvColumns_Mst_Tbl
WHERE ID = '<column_guid>'
  AND IsDeleted = 0;
```

Correct no-SP exception repair:

```sql
UPDATE XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_LvColumns_Mst_Tbl
SET ColumnProperties =
    '{"IsConditionalFormatting":false,"IsContextMenuEnableForApproval":false,"IsDefaultSearch":false,"Groupable":true,"IsPivotColumn":true,"ColumnUnit":"","Title":"","TitleAlignment":"left"}',
    ModifiedBy = @UserID,
    ModifiedOn = GETDATE()
WHERE ID = '<column_guid>'
  AND IsDeleted = 0
  AND
  (
       ColumnProperties IS NULL
    OR LTRIM(RTRIM(CONVERT(nvarchar(max), ColumnProperties))) = ''
    OR ISJSON(CONVERT(nvarchar(max), ColumnProperties)) <> 1
  );
```

---

#### Cause 6 — LV view not regenerated after link-column change

After any LV column insert/update/delete, regenerate:

```sql
EXEC XStudio_Configuration.dbo.XStudio_CreateViewForLV_Usp
    @LvID             = '<lv_guid>',
    @LvName           = '<lv_name>',
    @DataDatabase     = 'XStudio_XMES',
    @ColumnQuery      = '<SELECT including JSON alias for link/action column>',
    @RelationQuery    = '<FROM/JOIN ...>',
    @GroupByQuery     = '',
    @WhereCondition   = '<WHERE ...>',
    @IsCalculatedView = 0;
```

Do not pass null query fragments.

Verify:

```sql
SELECT TOP 1
    WOProduction
FROM XStudio_XMES.dbo.XStudio_List_XMES_SMS_Work_Order_Vw;
```

Expected: a clickable value or JSON/action descriptor appropriate for the renderer.

---

#### Cause 7 — Generated view alias does not match the metadata row

For link/action columns, the LV metadata and generated view alias must agree.

The view should expose an alias with the same name as `XStudio_LvColumns_Mst_Tbl.Name`, for example:

```text
WOProduction
```

For URL links, the alias may carry a JSON string such as:

```json
{
  "Link": "XMES/DEFAULT/List_Page_XMES_Production_Trn_Tbl?WOID={ID}",
  "LinkTarget": "inside",
  "LinkValue": "Open"
}
```

For Edit/Delete action columns, validate against live generated examples. Some builds expect:

```text
PageId
RecordId
Action
LinkTarget
```

---

#### Quick checklist

1. [ ] `Type = 'link'`
2. [ ] `LinkType = 'fixedtext'`
3. [ ] `LinkValue` set, for example `Open`
4. [ ] `CustomLink` / `ColumnCustomLink` target is correct for the action type
5. [ ] `CustomLinkTargetMode = Inside` or `Popup`
6. [ ] `IsCustomField = 1`
7. [ ] `AttributeID`, `RelationID`, `RelationIDList` blank/null
8. [ ] `ColumnProperties` valid JSON
9. [ ] Target page exists when target is page/URL-based
10. [ ] Link token column exists in LV view output
11. [ ] LV view regenerated with `XStudio_CreateViewForLV_Usp`
12. [ ] Generated view exposes the link alias

#### See also

- [create_lv_grid.md](../02_TASK_PLAYBOOKS/create_lv_grid.md)
- [create_lv_link_column.md](../02_TASK_PLAYBOOKS/create_lv_link_column.md)
- [lv_view_failures.md](lv_view_failures.md)
- [lv_link_column_template.sql](../03_SQL_TEMPLATES/lv_link_column_template.sql)

> End of imported document.

## 43. Dropdown Failures

**Source file:** `04_DEBUGGING/dropdown_failures.md`  
**File type:** `.md`  
**Size:** `8760` bytes  
**Modified on:** `2026-05-12 16:54:58`  
**SHA256:** `f60eeaa2a33f`  
**Encoding used:** `utf-8`

> Start of imported document.

### Debugging: Dropdown Failures

#### Symptoms

- Dropdown shows no options
- Dropdown saves blank or empty GUID
- Dropdown renders as textbox
- Dropdown crashes the edit form / `BindControls.js`
- Dropdown works in edit form but LV shows GUID
- Dropdown displays the wrong text
- LV view fails after adding dropdown display column

#### Correct repair rule

```text
Use XStudio SPs wherever a suitable SP exists.
If no suitable SP exists, preserve the operation as a documented no-SP exception with live schema inspection, guards, real user audit values, and postflight.
```

For dropdown metadata, use:

```text
XStudio_Add_DropDown_Attribute_Properties_And_Relation_Usp
dropdown_properties_template.sql
```

The template calls the SP first, then verifies/completes the six-property contract only if the SP output is incomplete.

#### Dropdown completeness contract

A dropdown attribute is complete only when all of these are true:

| Requirement | Description |
|---|---|
| Base attribute exists | Created through `XStudio_AddAttribute_Usp` |
| Physical column exists | Created by the attribute SP path |
| Six property rows exist | `DropdownDatabase`, `DropdownEntity`, `DropdownEntryMode`, `DropdownTextField`, `DropdownValueField`, `TextCharacterLength` |
| Entity relation exists | One active `XStudio_EntityRelations_Mst_Tbl` row |
| Edit control is safe | `ControlType = selectbox`, full JSON |
| LV relation is set where needed | LV column uses `RelationID` for display text |
| LV view regenerated | `XStudio_CreateViewForLV_Usp` after LV changes |

#### Diagnosis: all dropdown properties

```sql
SELECT
    Name,
    Value
FROM XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_AttributeProperties_Mst_Tbl
WHERE ParentID = '<dropdown_attr_guid>'
  AND IsDeleted = 0
ORDER BY Name;
```

Expected property names:

```text
DropdownDatabase
DropdownEntity
DropdownEntryMode
DropdownTextField
DropdownValueField
TextCharacterLength
```

Property-count check:

```sql
SELECT
    COUNT(*) AS ActivePropertyCount
FROM XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_AttributeProperties_Mst_Tbl
WHERE ParentID = '<dropdown_attr_guid>'
  AND IsDeleted = 0;
```

Expected:

```text
6
```

---

#### Dropdown shows no options

Possible causes:

1. `DropdownDatabase` wrong
2. `DropdownTextField` wrong
3. `DropdownEntity` points to wrong entity GUID
4. target entity/table has no active rows
5. `DropdownFilterCondition` filters out all rows
6. edit-control JSON is missing dropdown fields

Diagnosis:

```sql
-- Target entity from DropdownEntity
SELECT
    e.ID,
    e.Name,
    e.ParentID
FROM XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_Entities_Mst_Tbl e
WHERE e.ID =
(
    SELECT TRY_CONVERT(uniqueidentifier, Value)
    FROM XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_AttributeProperties_Mst_Tbl
    WHERE ParentID = '<dropdown_attr_guid>'
      AND Name = 'DropdownEntity'
      AND IsDeleted = 0
)
AND e.IsDeleted = 0;

-- Verify target table has rows
SELECT TOP 5 *
FROM XStudio_XMES.dbo.<TargetTable>
WHERE ISNULL(IsDeleted, 0) = 0;

-- Verify display column exists
SELECT COLUMN_NAME
FROM XStudio_XMES.INFORMATION_SCHEMA.COLUMNS
WHERE TABLE_NAME = '<TargetTable>'
  AND COLUMN_NAME = '<ColumnName>';
```

Fix path:

```text
Run dropdown_properties_template.sql with the correct target entity/value/display fields.
Then run edit_control_safe_json_template.sql if the dropdown is on an edit form.
```

---

#### Dropdown saves empty GUID

Cause:

```text
DropdownValueField is missing or points to the wrong GUID.
```

`DropdownValueField` must be the **attribute GUID** of the target value field, normally the target entity’s `ID` attribute. It is not the target entity ID itself.

Diagnosis:

```sql
SELECT
    Value AS DropdownValueField
FROM XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_AttributeProperties_Mst_Tbl
WHERE ParentID = '<dropdown_attr_guid>'
  AND Name = 'DropdownValueField'
  AND IsDeleted = 0;

SELECT
    ID,
    Name,
    DisplayName,
    ParentID
FROM XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_Attribute_Mst_Tbl
WHERE ID = '<dropdown_value_field_value>'
  AND IsDeleted = 0;
```

Expected:

```text
Name = ID, or the intended target value attribute
ParentID = target entity ID
```

Fix:

```text
Run dropdown_properties_template.sql with the correct target value attribute.
```

---

#### Dropdown crash / BindControls.js crash

Common causes:

```text
ControlProperties is NULL/blank/invalid
ControlType is textbox instead of selectbox
DropdownEntityID / DropdownValueField / DropdownTextField missing in edit-control JSON
dropdown property rows incomplete
```

Fix sequence:

```text
1. dropdown_properties_template.sql
2. edit_control_safe_json_template.sql
3. postflight_checks.sql
```

See:

```text
renderer_crashes.md
```

---

#### Dropdown shows GUIDs instead of display text in grid

Cause:

```text
Missing or wrong entity relation for the dropdown attribute
or LV column has empty/wrong RelationID.
```

Diagnosis:

```sql
SELECT
    ID,
    EntityID,
    AttributeID,
    Cardinality,
    RelationEntityID,
    RelationAttributeID,
    RelationName,
    RelationEntity,
    RelationAttribute
FROM XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_EntityRelations_Mst_Tbl
WHERE EntityID = '<owning_entity_guid>'
  AND AttributeID = '<dropdown_attr_guid>'
  AND IsDeleted = 0;
```

If no relation exists, do not manually insert it as the normal repair path. Use:

```text
dropdown_properties_template.sql
```

because it calls the dropdown SP and completes relation/property fallback safely.

---

#### LV column bound to dropdown but `RelationID` is blank

Diagnosis:

```sql
SELECT
    ID,
    Name,
    AttributeID,
    RelationID
FROM XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_LvColumns_Mst_Tbl
WHERE AttributeID = '<dropdown_attr_guid>'
  AND ParentID = '<lv_guid>'
  AND IsDeleted = 0;
```

Expected:

```text
RelationID = active XStudio_EntityRelations_Mst_Tbl.ID for this dropdown attribute
```

No generic save-LV-column SP is currently confirmed, so fixing `RelationID` is a documented no-SP exception.

Guarded repair:

```sql
DECLARE @RelationID uniqueidentifier = '<entity_relation_guid>';
DECLARE @UserID varchar(36) = '<deployment_user_guid>';

UPDATE XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_LvColumns_Mst_Tbl
SET RelationID = @RelationID,
    ModifiedBy = @UserID,
    ModifiedOn = GETDATE()
WHERE AttributeID = '<dropdown_attr_guid>'
  AND ParentID = '<lv_guid>'
  AND IsDeleted = 0;
```

Then regenerate the LV view:

```sql
EXEC XStudio_Configuration.dbo.XStudio_CreateViewForLV_Usp
    @LvID             = '<lv_guid>',
    @LvName           = '<lv_name>',
    @DataDatabase     = 'XStudio_XMES',
    @ColumnQuery      = '<SELECT ... with dropdown display alias>',
    @RelationQuery    = '<FROM/JOIN ... target dropdown table ...>',
    @GroupByQuery     = '',
    @WhereCondition   = '<WHERE ...>',
    @IsCalculatedView = 0;
```

---

#### Edit control dropdown metadata mismatch

Diagnosis:

```sql
SELECT
    ec.ID,
    ec.ControlName,
    ec.ControlType,
    JSON_VALUE(CONVERT(nvarchar(max), ec.ControlProperties), '$.DropdownDatabase') AS DropdownDatabase,
    JSON_VALUE(CONVERT(nvarchar(max), ec.ControlProperties), '$.DropdownEntityID') AS DropdownEntityID,
    JSON_VALUE(CONVERT(nvarchar(max), ec.ControlProperties), '$.DropdownTextField') AS DropdownTextField,
    JSON_VALUE(CONVERT(nvarchar(max), ec.ControlProperties), '$.DropdownValueField') AS DropdownValueField
FROM XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_EditControls_Mst_Tbl ec
WHERE ec.AttributeID = '<dropdown_attr_guid>'
  AND ec.IsDeleted = 0;
```

Expected:

```text
ControlType = selectbox
ControlName usually cmb_<AttributeName>
all dropdown JSON fields non-empty
```

Fix path:

```text
edit_control_safe_json_template.sql
```

---

#### Quick checklist

1. [ ] Base attribute exists and physical column exists
2. [ ] 6 dropdown properties exist
3. [ ] `DropdownEntity` = target entity GUID
4. [ ] `DropdownValueField` = target value attribute GUID, normally target `ID` attribute
5. [ ] `DropdownDatabase` = target data DB
6. [ ] `DropdownTextField` references an existing target display column/expression
7. [ ] Entity relation exists
8. [ ] LV column has non-empty `RelationID` when display text is needed
9. [ ] LV view regenerated after LV relation changes
10. [ ] Edit control uses `selectbox`
11. [ ] Edit-control JSON contains dropdown fields
12. [ ] `postflight_checks.sql` passes

#### See also

- [renderer_crashes.md](renderer_crashes.md)
- [create_dropdown_attribute.md](../02_TASK_PLAYBOOKS/create_dropdown_attribute.md)
- [dropdown_properties_template.sql](../03_SQL_TEMPLATES/dropdown_properties_template.sql)
- [edit_control_safe_json_template.sql](../03_SQL_TEMPLATES/edit_control_safe_json_template.sql)
- [postflight_checks.sql](../03_SQL_TEMPLATES/postflight_checks.sql)

> End of imported document.

## 44. Lv View Failures

**Source file:** `04_DEBUGGING/lv_view_failures.md`  
**File type:** `.md`  
**Size:** `6319` bytes  
**Modified on:** `2026-05-12 16:55:22`  
**SHA256:** `6cf5a3096b83`  
**Encoding used:** `utf-8`

> Start of imported document.

### Debugging: LV View Failures

#### Symptoms

- Grid shows no data even though rows exist
- Browser or SQL shows `Invalid column name 'ColumnName'`
- Grid shows stale columns after LV column changes
- Grid spinner never resolves
- New link columns do not appear
- Dropdown columns show GUIDs instead of display text
- Custom-SP-backed LV returns unexpected shape

#### Correct repair rule

```text
Use XStudio SPs wherever a suitable SP exists.
If no suitable SP exists, preserve the operation as a documented no-SP exception with live schema inspection, guards, real user audit values, and postflight.
```

For generated LV views, use:

```text
XStudio_CreateViewForLV_Usp
```

For LV header/columns/filter changes:

```text
No generic save-LV-header or save-LV-column SP is currently confirmed.
```

So LV metadata repairs remain documented no-SP exceptions, but view regeneration is SP-backed.

#### Root cause: view not regenerated

The LV view in the data DB is not automatically updated after all manual LV metadata changes.

Typical generated view name:

```text
XStudio_XMES.dbo.XStudio_<LVName>_Vw
```

Regenerate with the live 8-param signature:

```sql
EXEC XStudio_Configuration.dbo.XStudio_CreateViewForLV_Usp
    @LvID             = '<lv_guid>',
    @LvName           = '<lv_name>',
    @DataDatabase     = 'XStudio_XMES',
    @ColumnQuery      = '<SELECT ...>',
    @RelationQuery    = '<FROM/JOIN ...>',
    @GroupByQuery     = '',
    @WhereCondition   = '<WHERE ...>',
    @IsCalculatedView = 0;
```

Do not pass null query fragments.

#### Diagnosis: does the view exist?

```sql
SELECT
    TABLE_NAME
FROM XStudio_XMES.INFORMATION_SCHEMA.TABLES
WHERE TABLE_TYPE = 'VIEW'
  AND TABLE_NAME = 'XStudio_<LVName>_Vw';
```

#### Diagnosis: view definition

```sql
SELECT
    OBJECT_DEFINITION(OBJECT_ID('XStudio_XMES.dbo.XStudio_<LVName>_Vw')) AS ViewDefinition;
```

Look for:

```text
expected columns
datetime formatting
dropdown joins
link/action JSON aliases
correct WHERE clause
```

#### Diagnosis: actual output

```sql
SELECT TOP 3 *
FROM XStudio_XMES.dbo.XStudio_<LVName>_Vw;
```

#### Invalid column name

Cause:

```text
LV column references an AttributeID whose Attribute.Name does not exist as a physical column.
```

Diagnosis:

```sql
SELECT
    lc.ID AS LVColumnID,
    lc.Name AS LVColName,
    a.Name AS AttributeName,
    lc.AttributeID
FROM XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_LvColumns_Mst_Tbl lc
JOIN XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_Attribute_Mst_Tbl a
  ON a.ID = lc.AttributeID
WHERE lc.ParentID = '<lv_guid>'
  AND lc.IsDeleted = 0
  AND ISNULL(lc.IsCustomField, 0) = 0
  AND lc.AttributeID IS NOT NULL
  AND NOT EXISTS
  (
      SELECT 1
      FROM XStudio_XMES.INFORMATION_SCHEMA.COLUMNS c
      WHERE c.TABLE_NAME = '<EntityTableName>'
        AND c.COLUMN_NAME = a.Name
  );
```

Fix options:

```text
If the attribute should exist: repair entity/attribute/physical column mismatch.
If the LV column is stale: remove/disable the LV column through guarded no-SP exception.
If it is an action link: set Type=link, IsCustomField=1, AttributeID blank/null.
```

Then regenerate the view.

#### Link column not appearing

Diagnosis:

```sql
SELECT
    Name,
    [Type],
    IsCustomField,
    AttributeID,
    IsVisible,
    ColumnProperties
FROM XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_LvColumns_Mst_Tbl
WHERE ParentID = '<lv_guid>'
  AND Name = 'WOProduction'
  AND IsDeleted = 0;
```

Required:

```text
Type = link
IsCustomField = 1
AttributeID blank/null
IsVisible = 1
ColumnProperties valid JSON
```

Fix through `lv_link_column_template.sql` or guarded no-SP metadata repair. Then regenerate the view.

#### Dropdown displays GUID

Cause:

```text
LV column is bound to FK attribute but missing RelationID or query fragments do not join the target table.
```

Diagnosis:

```sql
SELECT
    lc.Name,
    lc.AttributeID,
    lc.RelationID,
    er.RelationEntity,
    er.RelationAttribute
FROM XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_LvColumns_Mst_Tbl lc
LEFT JOIN XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_EntityRelations_Mst_Tbl er
  ON er.ID = lc.RelationID
 AND er.IsDeleted = 0
WHERE lc.ParentID = '<lv_guid>'
  AND lc.AttributeID = '<dropdown_attr_guid>'
  AND lc.IsDeleted = 0;
```

Fix:

```text
1. Run dropdown_properties_template.sql if relation is missing.
2. Set LV column RelationID through guarded no-SP exception if needed.
3. Regenerate the view with target-table JOIN and display alias.
```

#### View regeneration fails or stays stale

Check:

1. SP output/errors
2. `OBJECT_DEFINITION` after running the SP
3. another LV column referencing a missing physical column
4. query fragments still placeholders or null
5. target data DB name wrong
6. LV is actually custom-SP-backed

#### Custom-SP-backed LV

If `XStudio_LV_Mst_Tbl.SPName` is set, the grid may read from a stored procedure instead of the generated view.

Diagnosis:

```sql
SELECT
    ID,
    Name,
    SPName,
    FilterCondition
FROM XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_LV_Mst_Tbl
WHERE ID = '<lv_guid>'
  AND IsDeleted = 0;
```

Verify the SP exists:

```sql
SELECT OBJECT_ID('XStudio_XMES.dbo.<SPName>') AS ObjectId;
```

The custom SP must return columns matching `XStudio_LvColumns_Mst_Tbl.Name` values.

#### FilterCondition syntax

For request-token filters:

```text
(WorkOrderID='<request.WOID>' OR IIF('<request.WOID>'='',1,0)=1)
```

If updating `FilterCondition`, this is a no-SP exception. Use guarded update with real `@UserID`, then validate behavior.

#### Quick checklist

1. [ ] Run live `XStudio_CreateViewForLV_Usp` after LV changes
2. [ ] Do not pass null query fragments
3. [ ] Query `SELECT TOP 1 * FROM ..._Vw`
4. [ ] Check physical column existence for every normal LV attribute
5. [ ] Link columns use `Type=link`, `IsCustomField=1`, blank/null `AttributeID`
6. [ ] Dropdown display columns have valid `RelationID`
7. [ ] Custom-SP-backed LVs have valid `SPName` and output shape
8. [ ] `FilterCondition` syntax is valid and token names match URL/request context

#### See also

- [broken_link_columns.md](broken_link_columns.md)
- [dropdown_failures.md](dropdown_failures.md)
- [create_lv_grid.md](../02_TASK_PLAYBOOKS/create_lv_grid.md)
- [lv_link_column_template.sql](../03_SQL_TEMPLATES/lv_link_column_template.sql)
- [postflight_checks.sql](../03_SQL_TEMPLATES/postflight_checks.sql)

> End of imported document.

[Back to Table of Contents](#table-of-contents)

## 45. Orphaned Objects

**Source file:** `04_DEBUGGING/orphaned_objects.md`  
**File type:** `.md`  
**Size:** `12339` bytes  
**Modified on:** `2026-05-12 16:54:54`  
**SHA256:** `7ee7fcd1fe8a`  
**Encoding used:** `utf-8`

> Start of imported document.

### Debugging: Orphaned Objects

#### Symptom

Configuration rows exist in XStudio tables, but the object does not appear or does not render correctly in the UI.

Common examples:

| Symptom | Typical cause |
|---|---|
| Menu item missing | Missing MenuRights, wrong parent menu, invisible menu, wrong PageID |
| Page opens blank | Page has no active PageControl |
| Grid not showing | PageControl points to missing/deleted LV or wrong ControlType |
| Edit form empty | PageControl points to missing/deleted Edit, or edit controls are missing/broken |
| Tab opens blank | Tab container bound but tabs or tab controls missing |
| Config rows exist but invisible | Wrong ParentID, deleted parent, missing rights, wrong binding |

#### Correct repair rule

```text
Use XStudio SPs wherever a suitable SP exists.
If no suitable SP exists, preserve the operation as a documented no-SP exception with live schema inspection, guards, real user audit values, and postflight.
```

#### Correct repair paths

| Problem area | Correct path |
|---|---|
| PageControl missing/wrong | `XStudio_Save_Page_Control_Configuration_Usp` |
| PageControl must be removed | `XStudio_Delete_Page_Control_Configuration_Usp` |
| Menu missing/wrong | `XStudio_Save_Menu_Configuration_Usp` |
| Menu rights missing | `XStudio_Save_Menu_Rights_Usp` |
| Menu rights must be removed | `XStudio_Delete_Menu_Rights_Usp` |
| Menu sequence wrong | `XStudio_Save_Menu_Sequence_Usp` |
| Tab missing | `XStudio_Save_Tab_Configuration_Usp` |
| Tab child control missing | `XStudio_Save_Tab_Control_Configuration_Usp` |
| HTML control missing/wrong | `XStudio_Save_HTMLControl_Usp` / datasource SPs |
| Brand-new page header missing | Guarded no-SP insert into `XStudio_Page_Mst_Tbl` if no page-create SP exists |
| LV/Edit/header/columns broken | Use relevant playbook/template; no-SP exceptions only where no SP exists |

This document is diagnostic-first. SELECTs are safe. Repairs should follow the listed SP/template paths.

#### ParentID contract

| Table | Required parent |
|---|---|
| `XStudio_Entities_Mst_Tbl.ParentID` | Application ID |
| `XStudio_Attribute_Mst_Tbl.ParentID` | Entity ID |
| `XStudio_LV_Mst_Tbl.ParentID` | Entity ID |
| `XStudio_LvColumns_Mst_Tbl.ParentID` | LV ID |
| `XStudio_Edit_Mst_Tbl.ParentID` | Entity ID |
| `XStudio_EditControls_Mst_Tbl.ParentID` | Edit ID |
| `XStudio_Page_Mst_Tbl.ParentID` | Application ID |
| `XStudio_PageControls_Mst_Tbl.ParentID` | Page ID |
| `XStudio_Menu_Mst_Tbl.ParentID` | Application ID |
| `XStudio_MenuRights_Mst_Tbl.ParentID` | Menu ID |
| `XStudio_Tab_Container_Mst_Tbl.ParentID` | Application ID |
| `XStudio_Tab_Mst_Tbl.ParentID` | Tab container ID |
| `XStudio_Tab_Dtl_Tbl.ParentID` | Tab ID |

#### Required input context

```sql
DECLARE @EntityName varchar(100) = '<EntityTableName>';
DECLARE @AppID uniqueidentifier = '29123B0B-3872-41CF-B5E1-67C4A8F0D4BC';
DECLARE @RoleID uniqueidentifier = NULL;
DECLARE @PageName varchar(200) = NULL;
DECLARE @MenuName varchar(200) = NULL;

DECLARE @EntityID uniqueidentifier =
(
    SELECT ID
    FROM XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_Entities_Mst_Tbl
    WHERE Name = @EntityName
      AND IsDeleted = 0
);
```

#### Entity parent check

```sql
SELECT
    e.ID,
    e.Name,
    e.ParentID,
    a.ID AS AppID,
    a.Name AS AppName,
    CASE WHEN a.ID IS NULL THEN 'BROKEN: ParentID does not resolve to active application' ELSE 'OK' END AS Status
FROM XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_Entities_Mst_Tbl e
LEFT JOIN XStudio_Configuration.dbo.XStudio_Application_Mst_Tbl a
  ON a.ID = e.ParentID
 AND a.IsDeleted = 0
WHERE e.Name = @EntityName
  AND e.IsDeleted = 0;
```

Fix path: entity save/update SP where possible. Direct ParentID patch only as documented no-SP exception.

#### Attribute parent check

```sql
SELECT
    a.ID,
    a.Name,
    a.ParentID,
    e.Name AS EntityName,
    CASE WHEN e.ID IS NULL THEN 'BROKEN: ParentID does not resolve to active entity' ELSE 'OK' END AS Status
FROM XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_Attribute_Mst_Tbl a
LEFT JOIN XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_Entities_Mst_Tbl e
  ON e.ID = a.ParentID
 AND e.IsDeleted = 0
WHERE a.ParentID = @EntityID;
```

Fix path: attribute/schema SPs such as `XStudio_AddAttribute_Usp`, or documented exception only if no SP exists.

#### LV columns missing/orphaned

```sql
SELECT
    lv.ID AS LVID,
    lv.Name AS LVName,
    lc.ID AS LVColumnID,
    lc.Name AS LVColumnName,
    CASE
        WHEN lc.ID IS NULL THEN 'BROKEN: LV has no active columns'
        ELSE 'OK'
    END AS Status
FROM XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_LV_Mst_Tbl lv
LEFT JOIN XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_LvColumns_Mst_Tbl lc
  ON lc.ParentID = lv.ID
 AND lc.IsDeleted = 0
WHERE lv.ParentID = @EntityID
  AND lv.IsDeleted = 0
ORDER BY lv.Name, lc.Name;
```

Fix path: LV playbook/template; regenerate with `XStudio_CreateViewForLV_Usp`.

#### Edit controls missing/orphaned

```sql
SELECT
    e.ID AS EditID,
    e.Name AS EditName,
    ec.ID AS EditControlID,
    ec.ControlName,
    ec.ControlType,
    CASE
        WHEN ec.ID IS NULL THEN 'BROKEN: Edit has no active controls'
        ELSE 'OK'
    END AS Status
FROM XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_Edit_Mst_Tbl e
LEFT JOIN XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_EditControls_Mst_Tbl ec
  ON ec.ParentID = e.ID
 AND ec.IsDeleted = 0
WHERE e.ParentID = @EntityID
  AND e.IsDeleted = 0
ORDER BY e.Name, ec.ControlName;
```

Fix path: `Xstudio_Insert_Edit_Columns_Info_Usp`, then `edit_control_safe_json_template.sql`.

#### Page blank: no PageControls

```sql
SELECT
    p.ID AS PageID,
    p.Name AS PageName,
    COUNT(pc.ID) AS ActivePageControlCount,
    CASE WHEN COUNT(pc.ID) = 0 THEN 'BROKEN: Page has no active PageControls' ELSE 'OK' END AS Status
FROM XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_Page_Mst_Tbl p
LEFT JOIN XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_PageControls_Mst_Tbl pc
  ON pc.ParentID = p.ID
 AND pc.IsDeleted = 0
WHERE p.IsDeleted = 0
  AND (@PageName IS NULL OR p.Name = @PageName)
GROUP BY p.ID, p.Name
HAVING COUNT(pc.ID) = 0;
```

Fix path:

```text
XStudio_Save_Page_Control_Configuration_Usp
```

#### PageControl points to missing target

```sql
SELECT
    p.Name AS PageName,
    pc.ID AS PageControlID,
    pc.ControlType,
    pc.ControlID,
    CASE
        WHEN LOWER(pc.ControlType) IN ('grid', 'lv', 'listview') AND lv.ID IS NULL THEN 'BROKEN: LV missing'
        WHEN LOWER(pc.ControlType) = 'edit' AND e.ID IS NULL THEN 'BROKEN: Edit missing'
        WHEN LOWER(pc.ControlType) = 'tab' AND tc.ID IS NULL THEN 'BROKEN: Tab container missing'
        ELSE 'OK or requires control-specific resolver'
    END AS Status
FROM XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_PageControls_Mst_Tbl pc
JOIN XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_Page_Mst_Tbl p
  ON p.ID = pc.ParentID
 AND p.IsDeleted = 0
LEFT JOIN XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_LV_Mst_Tbl lv
  ON lv.ID = pc.ControlID
 AND lv.IsDeleted = 0
LEFT JOIN XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_Edit_Mst_Tbl e
  ON e.ID = pc.ControlID
 AND e.IsDeleted = 0
LEFT JOIN XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_Tab_Container_Mst_Tbl tc
  ON tc.ID = pc.ControlID
 AND tc.IsDeleted = 0
WHERE pc.IsDeleted = 0
  AND (@PageName IS NULL OR p.Name = @PageName);
```

Fix path: resave PageControl with correct ControlType/ControlID.

#### Menu not visible

```sql
SELECT
    m.ID AS MenuID,
    m.Name AS MenuName,
    m.PageID,
    p.Name AS PageName,
    m.SubMenuOfID,
    m.IsVisible,
    COUNT(mr.ID) AS ActiveRightCount,
    CASE
        WHEN m.PageID IS NOT NULL AND p.ID IS NULL THEN 'BROKEN: PageID does not resolve'
        WHEN ISNULL(m.IsVisible,0) = 0 THEN 'BROKEN: Menu hidden'
        WHEN COUNT(mr.ID) = 0 THEN 'BROKEN: No active rights'
        ELSE 'OK'
    END AS Status
FROM XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_Menu_Mst_Tbl m
LEFT JOIN XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_Page_Mst_Tbl p
  ON p.ID = m.PageID
 AND p.IsDeleted = 0
LEFT JOIN XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_MenuRights_Mst_Tbl mr
  ON mr.ParentID = m.ID
 AND mr.IsDeleted = 0
WHERE m.IsDeleted = 0
  AND (@MenuName IS NULL OR m.Name = @MenuName)
GROUP BY m.ID, m.Name, m.PageID, p.ID, p.Name, m.SubMenuOfID, m.IsVisible;
```

Fix path:

```text
XStudio_Save_Menu_Configuration_Usp
XStudio_Save_Menu_Rights_Usp
XStudio_Save_Menu_Sequence_Usp
```

#### Duplicate PageControl binding

```sql
SELECT
    pc.ParentID,
    p.Name AS PageName,
    pc.ControlType,
    pc.ControlID,
    COUNT(*) AS ActiveDuplicateCount
FROM XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_PageControls_Mst_Tbl pc
JOIN XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_Page_Mst_Tbl p
  ON p.ID = pc.ParentID
 AND p.IsDeleted = 0
WHERE pc.IsDeleted = 0
GROUP BY pc.ParentID, p.Name, pc.ControlType, pc.ControlID
HAVING COUNT(*) > 1;
```

Fix path: delete wrong duplicate through `XStudio_Delete_Page_Control_Configuration_Usp`.

#### PageCollection confusion

PageCollection SPs manage collections/bookmarks. They do not create runtime pages or menus.

If a page collection exists but no runtime page/menu appears, check:

```sql
SELECT 'PAGE' AS ObjectType, ID, Name
FROM XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_Page_Mst_Tbl
WHERE IsDeleted = 0
  AND Name LIKE '%' + @EntityName + '%'

UNION ALL

SELECT 'MENU' AS ObjectType, ID, Name
FROM XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_Menu_Mst_Tbl
WHERE IsDeleted = 0
  AND Name LIKE '%' + @EntityName + '%';
```

#### Tab diagnostics

Use only when a tab container is expected.

```sql
SELECT
    p.Name AS PageName,
    pc.ID AS PageControlID,
    pc.ControlType,
    pc.ControlID AS TabContainerID,
    tc.Name AS TabContainerName
FROM XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_PageControls_Mst_Tbl pc
JOIN XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_Page_Mst_Tbl p
  ON p.ID = pc.ParentID
 AND p.IsDeleted = 0
LEFT JOIN XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_Tab_Container_Mst_Tbl tc
  ON tc.ID = pc.ControlID
 AND tc.IsDeleted = 0
WHERE pc.IsDeleted = 0
  AND LOWER(pc.ControlType) = 'tab'
  AND (@PageName IS NULL OR p.Name = @PageName);
```

Fix paths:

```text
XStudio_Save_Page_Control_Configuration_Usp
XStudio_Save_Tab_Configuration_Usp
XStudio_Save_Tab_Control_Configuration_Usp
```

#### Global orphan scan

```sql
SELECT 'ORPHANED_EDIT_CONTROLS' AS CheckName, COUNT(*) AS CountValue
FROM XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_EditControls_Mst_Tbl ec
WHERE ec.IsDeleted = 0
  AND NOT EXISTS
  (
      SELECT 1 FROM XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_Edit_Mst_Tbl e
      WHERE e.ID = ec.ParentID AND e.IsDeleted = 0
  )

UNION ALL

SELECT 'ORPHANED_LV_COLUMNS', COUNT(*)
FROM XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_LvColumns_Mst_Tbl lc
WHERE lc.IsDeleted = 0
  AND NOT EXISTS
  (
      SELECT 1 FROM XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_LV_Mst_Tbl lv
      WHERE lv.ID = lc.ParentID AND lv.IsDeleted = 0
  )

UNION ALL

SELECT 'ORPHANED_PAGECONTROLS', COUNT(*)
FROM XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_PageControls_Mst_Tbl pc
WHERE pc.IsDeleted = 0
  AND NOT EXISTS
  (
      SELECT 1 FROM XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_Page_Mst_Tbl p
      WHERE p.ID = pc.ParentID AND p.IsDeleted = 0
  )

UNION ALL

SELECT 'ORPHANED_MENURIGHTS', COUNT(*)
FROM XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_MenuRights_Mst_Tbl mr
WHERE mr.IsDeleted = 0
  AND NOT EXISTS
  (
      SELECT 1 FROM XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_Menu_Mst_Tbl m
      WHERE m.ID = mr.ParentID AND m.IsDeleted = 0
  );
```

#### Anti-pattern

Do not repair ParentID or visibility by casual direct updates such as:

```text
UPDATE <config_table> SET ParentID = ..., ModifiedBy = 'sa'
```

Use the object-family SP where one exists. If no SP exists, document the no-SP exception, inspect schema, guard the exact row, use a real user, and rerun postflight.

#### Correct repair order

```text
1. Identify symptom.
2. Confirm parent chain.
3. Confirm binding layer:
   Page -> PageControls
   Menu -> MenuRights
   TabContainer -> Tabs -> TabControls
4. Confirm target LV/Edit/HTML/Dashboard/InlineGrid exists.
5. Repair through the relevant XStudio SP where available.
6. For no-SP exception surfaces, use guarded schema-inspected DML only.
7. Run postflight_checks.sql.
8. Test with affected role.
```

#### See also

- [renderer_crashes.md](renderer_crashes.md)
- [page_menu_template.sql](../03_SQL_TEMPLATES/page_menu_template.sql)
- [postflight_checks.sql](../03_SQL_TEMPLATES/postflight_checks.sql)

> End of imported document.

## 46. Physical Column Mismatch

**Source file:** `04_DEBUGGING/physical_column_mismatch.md`  
**File type:** `.md`  
**Size:** `6365` bytes  
**Modified on:** `2026-05-12 16:54:48`  
**SHA256:** `3b6b5b5a73f1`  
**Encoding used:** `utf-8`

> Start of imported document.

### Debugging: Physical Column Mismatch

#### What it is

`XStudio_Attribute_Mst_Tbl.Name` is the physical SQL column name.

Changing `Name` can trigger XStudio schema logic and rename the physical column in the data DB. If config name and physical column name diverge, stored procedures, LV views, edit controls, and domain SQL can break.

Use `DisplayName` for UI labels. Do not change `Name` for label changes.

#### Incident pattern

1. `Name` changed from `WorkOrderID` to `Work Order`
2. XStudio renamed physical column from `WorkOrderID` to `Work Order`
3. SPs referencing `WorkOrderID` failed with `Invalid column name 'WorkOrderID'`
4. Reverting `Name` triggered a second physical rename
5. SPs worked again only after config and physical column aligned

#### Correct repair rule

```text
Use XStudio SPs wherever a suitable SP exists.
If no suitable SP exists, preserve the operation as a documented no-SP exception with live schema inspection, guards, real user audit values, backup/transaction plan, and postflight.
```

For normal attribute creation, use:

```text
XStudio_AddAttribute_Usp
```

Do not manually insert attributes or run `ALTER TABLE` for normal creation.

For physical column mismatch repair, there may be no single safe generic SP. Treat repair as exceptional and validate carefully.

#### Diagnosis: attributes whose physical columns do not exist

```sql
DECLARE @EntityID uniqueidentifier = '<entity_guid>';

SELECT
    e.Name AS TableName,
    a.ID AS AttributeID,
    a.Name AS ConfigName,
    a.DisplayName,
    CASE
        WHEN c.COLUMN_NAME IS NULL THEN 'MISSING_PHYSICAL_COLUMN'
        ELSE 'OK'
    END AS Status
FROM XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_Attribute_Mst_Tbl a
JOIN XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_Entities_Mst_Tbl e
  ON e.ID = a.ParentID
 AND e.IsDeleted = 0
LEFT JOIN XStudio_XMES.INFORMATION_SCHEMA.COLUMNS c
  ON c.TABLE_NAME = e.Name
 AND c.COLUMN_NAME = a.Name
WHERE a.ParentID = @EntityID
  AND a.IsDeleted = 0
ORDER BY a.Name;
```

#### Diagnosis: physical columns with no matching attribute

```sql
DECLARE @EntityID uniqueidentifier = '<entity_guid>';
DECLARE @EntityTableName varchar(100) =
(
    SELECT Name
    FROM XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_Entities_Mst_Tbl
    WHERE ID = @EntityID
      AND IsDeleted = 0
);

SELECT
    c.COLUMN_NAME AS PhysicalColumn
FROM XStudio_XMES.INFORMATION_SCHEMA.COLUMNS c
WHERE c.TABLE_NAME = @EntityTableName
  AND NOT EXISTS
  (
      SELECT 1
      FROM XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_Attribute_Mst_Tbl a
      WHERE a.ParentID = @EntityID
        AND a.Name = c.COLUMN_NAME
        AND a.IsDeleted = 0
  )
ORDER BY c.COLUMN_NAME;
```

#### Causes

| Cause | Description |
|---|---|
| Direct update to `Attribute_Mst_Tbl.Name` | Triggers or bypasses physical rename behavior depending on path |
| Attribute schema SP/build bug | Some builds observed using display name in physical creation path |
| Bulk scripts | Metadata and physical columns created by different logic |
| Manual `sp_rename` | Physical schema changed without metadata update |
| Entity name rename | Can also rename physical table and break dependencies |

#### Prevention

1. Use `XStudio_AddAttribute_Usp` for new attributes.
2. Do not manually `ALTER TABLE` for normal attribute creation.
3. Do not update `Attribute.Name` for UI label changes.
4. Use `DisplayName` for labels.
5. After bulk/schema work, run mismatch diagnostics.
6. Regenerate affected LV views after repair.
7. Recompile/verify business SPs referencing the column.

#### Safe label change

Preferred label-only change:

```sql
DECLARE @UserID varchar(36) = '<deployment_user_guid>';

UPDATE XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_Attribute_Mst_Tbl
SET DisplayName = 'Work Order',
    ModifiedBy = @UserID,
    ModifiedOn = GETDATE()
WHERE ID = '<attr_guid>'
  AND IsDeleted = 0;
```

This preserves `Name = WorkOrderID`.

Do not do this for a label change:

```text
UPDATE XStudio_Attribute_Mst_Tbl SET Name = 'Work Order'
```

#### Fix option A — Rename physical column to match config

Use only if:

```text
config Attribute.Name is correct
physical column is wrong
dependent SQL should use config name
you have a backup/rollback plan
```

Example:

```sql
USE XStudio_XMES;
EXEC sp_rename 'dbo.MyTable.[Work Order]', 'WorkOrderID', 'COLUMN';
```

After repair:

```text
run mismatch diagnostic
regenerate affected LV views
verify custom SPs compile/run
test edit save/load
```

#### Fix option B — Update config to match physical column

Use only if:

```text
physical column name is the intended canonical name
config Attribute.Name is wrong
you understand whether the XStudio path will trigger physical rename
```

Because updating `Attribute.Name` can trigger a physical rename, do not perform this casually. Prefer a controlled repair script with:

```text
schema inspection
transaction/backup strategy
exact-row guard
real @UserID
postflight
```

#### Fix option C — Recreate missing attribute through SP

If the physical column and metadata are both incomplete, use:

```text
XStudio_AddAttribute_Usp
```

Do not directly insert attribute metadata plus manual `ALTER TABLE`.

#### Postflight

```sql
-- Config/physical alignment
-- Expect zero MISSING_PHYSICAL_COLUMN rows
<run the mismatch diagnostic above>

-- LV views using the column
EXEC XStudio_Configuration.dbo.XStudio_CreateViewForLV_Usp
    @LvID             = '<lv_guid>',
    @LvName           = '<lv_name>',
    @DataDatabase     = 'XStudio_XMES',
    @ColumnQuery      = '<SELECT ...>',
    @RelationQuery    = '<FROM/JOIN ...>',
    @GroupByQuery     = '',
    @WhereCondition   = '<WHERE ...>',
    @IsCalculatedView = 0;

SELECT TOP 1 *
FROM XStudio_XMES.dbo.XStudio_<LVName>_Vw;
```

#### Common mistakes

| Mistake | Effect |
|---|---|
| Updating `Name` for label change | Physical column rename / SP breakage |
| Fixing physical column but not LV view | Grid still broken |
| Fixing metadata but not custom SPs | Domain logic still fails |
| Using `sa` audit values | Non-traceable repair |
| Guessing column names | Creates second mismatch |

#### See also

- [lv_view_failures.md](lv_view_failures.md)
- [create_dropdown_attribute.md](../02_TASK_PLAYBOOKS/create_dropdown_attribute.md)
- [entity_create_template.sql](../03_SQL_TEMPLATES/entity_create_template.sql)
- [postflight_checks.sql](../03_SQL_TEMPLATES/postflight_checks.sql)

## 47. Renderer Crashes

**Source file:** `04_DEBUGGING/renderer_crashes.md`  
**File type:** `.md`  
**Size:** `9307` bytes  
**Modified on:** `2026-05-12 16:54:46`  
**SHA256:** `40aba5108b70`  
**Encoding used:** `utf-8`

> Start of imported document.

### Debugging: Renderer Crashes (BindControls.js)

#### Symptoms

Browser console shows:

- `TypeError: Cannot read properties of null (reading 'toLowerCase')`
- `BindControls.js line 1149`
- Edit form opens blank
- Edit form spinner never resolves
- Page opens blank
- Dropdown shows no options
- Tab content does not render
- HTML/dashboard/inlinegrid area appears empty

#### Correct repair rule

```text
Use XStudio SPs wherever a suitable SP exists.
If no suitable SP exists, preserve the operation as a documented no-SP exception with live schema inspection, guards, real user audit values, and postflight.
```

This page is diagnostic-first. Do not do casual direct repairs.

#### Mental model

Renderer failures usually come from:

```text
Page layer:
  Page -> PageControl -> target control

Control configuration:
  LV / Edit / HTML / Dashboard / InlineGrid / TabContainer

Attribute/dropdown JSON:
  EditControls.ControlProperties
  AttributeProperties
  EntityRelations
```

Important:

```text
Page header alone renders nothing.
PageControls bind visible controls.
EditControls.ControlProperties must be full valid JSON.
Dropdowns need both AttributeProperties and edit-control JSON.
Tabs are optional, not default.
```

#### Correct repair paths

| Problem | Correct path |
|---|---|
| Page missing control | `XStudio_Save_Page_Control_Configuration_Usp` |
| PageControl wrong/deleted | delete/save PageControl SPs |
| Menu opens wrong page | `XStudio_Save_Menu_Configuration_Usp` |
| Menu invisible | `XStudio_Save_Menu_Rights_Usp` |
| Tab missing | `XStudio_Save_Tab_Configuration_Usp` |
| Tab child control missing | `XStudio_Save_Tab_Control_Configuration_Usp` |
| HTML config missing | `XStudio_Save_HTMLControl_Usp` / datasource SP |
| Edit controls missing | `Xstudio_Insert_Edit_Columns_Info_Usp` |
| Edit JSON unsafe | `edit_control_safe_json_template.sql` |
| Dropdown metadata incomplete | dropdown SP/template |

#### Cause 1 — Edit control `ControlProperties` is NULL/blank/invalid

Diagnosis:

```sql
SELECT
    ec.ID,
    ec.ControlName,
    ec.ControlType,
    ec.ControlProperties
FROM XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_EditControls_Mst_Tbl ec
WHERE ec.ParentID = '<edit_guid>'
  AND ec.IsDeleted = 0
  AND
  (
       ec.ControlProperties IS NULL
    OR LTRIM(RTRIM(CONVERT(nvarchar(max), ec.ControlProperties))) = ''
    OR ISJSON(CONVERT(nvarchar(max), ec.ControlProperties)) <> 1
  );
```

Expected:

```text
0 rows
```

Fix:

```text
Xstudio_Insert_Edit_Columns_Info_Usp
edit_control_safe_json_template.sql
```

Direct JSON update is allowed only inside the approved safe template or as a documented no-SP exception.

#### Cause 2 — PageControl properties are NULL/blank

Diagnosis:

```sql
SELECT
    p.Name AS PageName,
    pc.ID,
    pc.Name,
    pc.ControlType,
    pc.ControlID,
    pc.ControlProperties
FROM XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_PageControls_Mst_Tbl pc
JOIN XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_Page_Mst_Tbl p
  ON p.ID = pc.ParentID
WHERE p.Name = '<page_name>'
  AND pc.IsDeleted = 0
  AND
  (
       pc.ControlProperties IS NULL
    OR LTRIM(RTRIM(CONVERT(nvarchar(max), pc.ControlProperties))) = ''
  );
```

Fix:

```text
XStudio_Save_Page_Control_Configuration_Usp
```

Simple LV/edit bindings usually need at least:

```json
{"IpFiltering":"False"}
```

#### Cause 3 — Page has no PageControls

Diagnosis:

```sql
SELECT
    p.ID,
    p.Name,
    COUNT(pc.ID) AS ActivePageControlCount
FROM XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_Page_Mst_Tbl p
LEFT JOIN XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_PageControls_Mst_Tbl pc
  ON pc.ParentID = p.ID
 AND pc.IsDeleted = 0
WHERE p.Name = '<page_name>'
  AND p.IsDeleted = 0
GROUP BY p.ID, p.Name
HAVING COUNT(pc.ID) = 0;
```

Fix:

```text
XStudio_Save_Page_Control_Configuration_Usp
```

#### Cause 4 — PageControl `ControlType` does not match target control

Diagnosis:

```sql
SELECT
    p.Name AS PageName,
    pc.ID,
    pc.ControlType,
    pc.ControlID,
    CASE
        WHEN LOWER(pc.ControlType) IN ('grid', 'lv', 'listview') AND lv.ID IS NULL THEN 'LV missing'
        WHEN LOWER(pc.ControlType) = 'edit' AND e.ID IS NULL THEN 'Edit missing'
        WHEN LOWER(pc.ControlType) = 'tab' AND tc.ID IS NULL THEN 'Tab container missing'
        ELSE 'OK or requires specific resolver'
    END AS Status
FROM XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_PageControls_Mst_Tbl pc
JOIN XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_Page_Mst_Tbl p
  ON p.ID = pc.ParentID
 AND p.IsDeleted = 0
LEFT JOIN XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_LV_Mst_Tbl lv
  ON lv.ID = pc.ControlID
 AND lv.IsDeleted = 0
LEFT JOIN XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_Edit_Mst_Tbl e
  ON e.ID = pc.ControlID
 AND e.IsDeleted = 0
LEFT JOIN XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_Tab_Container_Mst_Tbl tc
  ON tc.ID = pc.ControlID
 AND tc.IsDeleted = 0
WHERE p.Name = '<page_name>'
  AND pc.IsDeleted = 0;
```

Fix: re-save PageControl with correct `ControlType` and `ControlID`.

#### Cause 5 — Dropdown property rows incomplete

Diagnosis:

```sql
SELECT
    Name,
    Value
FROM XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_AttributeProperties_Mst_Tbl
WHERE ParentID = '<attr_guid>'
  AND IsDeleted = 0
ORDER BY Name;
```

Expected:

```text
DropdownDatabase
DropdownEntity
DropdownEntryMode
DropdownTextField
DropdownValueField
TextCharacterLength
```

Fix:

```text
dropdown_properties_template.sql
```

#### Cause 6 — Dropdown edit control is textbox or missing JSON values

Diagnosis:

```sql
SELECT
    ec.ID,
    ec.ControlName,
    ec.ControlType,
    JSON_VALUE(CONVERT(nvarchar(max), ec.ControlProperties), '$.DropdownEntityID') AS DropdownEntityID,
    JSON_VALUE(CONVERT(nvarchar(max), ec.ControlProperties), '$.DropdownValueField') AS DropdownValueField,
    JSON_VALUE(CONVERT(nvarchar(max), ec.ControlProperties), '$.DropdownTextField') AS DropdownTextField,
    JSON_VALUE(CONVERT(nvarchar(max), ec.ControlProperties), '$.DropdownDatabase') AS DropdownDatabase
FROM XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_EditControls_Mst_Tbl ec
WHERE ec.ParentID = '<edit_guid>'
  AND ec.IsDeleted = 0
  AND ec.ControlType IN ('selectbox', 'textbox');
```

Dropdown controls should normally be:

```text
ControlType = selectbox
ControlName = cmb_<AttributeName>
Dropdown JSON fields non-empty
```

Fix:

```text
dropdown_properties_template.sql
edit_control_safe_json_template.sql
```

#### Cause 7 — Tab container is bound but tabs/children are missing

Use only when page intentionally binds a tab container.

Diagnosis:

```sql
SELECT
    tc.ID AS TabContainerID,
    tc.Name AS TabContainerName,
    t.ID AS TabID,
    t.Name AS TabName,
    td.ID AS TabControlID,
    td.ControlType,
    td.ControlID
FROM XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_Tab_Container_Mst_Tbl tc
LEFT JOIN XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_Tab_Mst_Tbl t
  ON t.ParentID = tc.ID
 AND t.IsDeleted = 0
LEFT JOIN XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_Tab_Dtl_Tbl td
  ON td.ParentID = t.ID
 AND td.IsDeleted = 0
WHERE tc.ID = '<tab_container_guid>'
  AND tc.IsDeleted = 0;
```

Fix:

```text
XStudio_Save_Tab_Configuration_Usp
XStudio_Save_Tab_Control_Configuration_Usp
```

#### Cause 8 — HTML/dashboard/inlinegrid target config missing

Diagnosis:

```sql
SELECT
    p.Name AS PageName,
    pc.ID,
    pc.ControlType,
    pc.ControlID,
    pc.ControlProperties
FROM XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_PageControls_Mst_Tbl pc
JOIN XStudio_Configuration_XMES.dbo.XStudio_Page_Mst_Tbl p
  ON p.ID = pc.ParentID
 AND p.IsDeleted = 0
WHERE p.Name = '<page_name>'
  AND pc.IsDeleted = 0
  AND LOWER(pc.ControlType) NOT IN ('grid', 'lv', 'listview', 'edit', 'tab');
```

Fix:

```text
Use matching control-family SP first.
Then bind through XStudio_Save_Page_Control_Configuration_Usp.
```

Examples:

```text
HTML: XStudio_Save_HTMLControl_Usp
HTML datasource: XStudio_Save_HTMLControl_DataSource_Usp
Inlinegrid binding: PageControl SP, ControlID should point to child LV where that is the live pattern
```

#### Quick triage checklist

1. [ ] Page has active PageControls
2. [ ] PageControls resolve to live targets
3. [ ] PageControl properties safe
4. [ ] Edit controls exist
5. [ ] Edit `ControlProperties` valid JSON
6. [ ] Dropdown properties count = 6
7. [ ] Dropdown edit controls are `selectbox`
8. [ ] Dropdown JSON values non-empty
9. [ ] EntityRelation exists for dropdown
10. [ ] Tabs checked only if page uses tabs
11. [ ] HTML/dashboard/inlinegrid target config exists
12. [ ] `postflight_checks.sql` passes

#### Anti-patterns

Do not repair with casual direct updates using `sa`.

Examples of old unsafe patterns:

```text
UPDATE XStudio_EditControls_Mst_Tbl SET ControlProperties = ...
UPDATE XStudio_EditControls_Mst_Tbl SET ControlType = 'selectbox'
INSERT INTO XStudio_PageControls_Mst_Tbl
```

Use SP/template paths first. Direct fallback only where no complete SP exists and the exception is documented.
