## 48. Campaign Bom Workflow

**Source file:** `05_XMES_DOMAIN/campaign_bom_workflow.md`  
**File type:** `.md`  
**Size:** `3231` bytes  
**Modified on:** `2026-05-09 10:11:02`  
**SHA256:** `7ba50efe1836`  
**Encoding used:** `utf-8`

> Start of imported document.

### Campaign and BOM Workflow

#### Campaign lifecycle

A Campaign is the planning unit for a heat campaign. It drives everything downstream.

```

The campaign model must feed the broader digital thread:

```text
Sales order
-> production order
-> campaign/grade sequence
-> SMS/RM plans
-> BOM explosion
-> work orders and requirements
-> heat/cast/billet/rolling execution
-> genealogy, inventory, quality, and SAP posting
```
Campaign created (XMES_Campaign_Mst_Tbl)
  ↓
Grade Sequence configured (XMES_Campaign_Grade_Seq_Tbl)
  → Which steel grades, in what order, how many heats per grade
  ↓
SMS Plan created (XMES_Campaign_SMS_Plan_Trn_tbl)
  → Planned LRF heats per grade
  → SP: usp_SMS_Create_WorkOrders
  → Creates XMES_Work_Order_Mst_Tbl rows (type = SMS/LRF)
  ↓
RM Sequence configured (XMES_Campaign_RM_Seq_Tbl)
  → Which raw materials to charge, in what sequence
  → SP: USP_XMES_Create_RM_WorkOrders_From_RM_Sequence
  → Creates XMES_Work_Order_Mst_Tbl rows (type = RM)
  ↓
BOM Explosion (BOM_Exploded)
  → SP: usp_Build_Campaign_BOM_Exploded
  → Materialises BOM tree for campaign into BOM_Exploded
  → SP: USP_XMES_Create_WorkOrders_From_BOM_Exploded
  → Creates work order requirement rows (XMES_Work_Order_Requirement_Trn_Tbl)
```

#### Key tables

| Table | Purpose |
|---|---|
| `XMES_Campaign_Mst_Tbl` | Campaign header |
| `XMES_Campaign_Dtl_tbl` | Campaign detail lines (IsChild=1) |
| `XMES_Campaign_Grade_Seq_Tbl` | Grade heat sequence |
| `XMES_Campaign_SMS_Plan_Trn_tbl` | LRF heat plan |
| `XMES_Campaign_RM_Plan_Trn_Tbl` | RM charge plan |
| `XMES_Campaign_RM_Seq_Tbl` | RM sequence |
| `XMES_BOM_Mst_Tbl` | BOM header (links material to components) |
| `BOM_Exploded` | Pre-exploded BOM per campaign |

#### Campaign ID generation

`XMES_CampaignId_generation_usp` — auto-numbers the next campaign ID.

#### Grade Sequence HTML screen

The `GradeSequence` custom HTML control (ID: `C6798D31-EF14-4645-9CB2-81BF14529E5D`) provides the interactive grade sequencing UI.

#### Menu structure

```
Campaign Masters → XMES_Campaign_Mst_Tbl list page
SMS Campaign    → XMES_Campaign_SMS_Plan list page
SMS Work Order  → XMES_Work_Order_Mst_Tbl (filtered to SMS type)
RM Campaign     → XMES_Campaign_RM_Plan list page
RM Work Order   → XMES_Work_Order_Mst_Tbl (filtered to RM type)
```

#### BOM explosion SP

`usp_Build_Campaign_BOM_Exploded` takes a campaign ID and recursively walks the BOM tree, materializing all components (with yield and scrap factors applied) into `BOM_Exploded`.

Input: `CampaignID`  
Output: rows in `BOM_Exploded` with `CampaignID`, `MaterialID`, `ComponentID`, `PlannedQty`, `YieldFactor`, `ScrapFactor`

#### Work Order creation from BOM

`USP_XMES_Create_WorkOrders_From_BOM_Exploded` walks `BOM_Exploded` and creates:
- `XMES_Work_Order_Mst_Tbl` rows (the WO)
- `XMES_Work_Order_Requirement_Trn_Tbl` rows (the planned material requirements)

#### Reconciliation rule

Campaign planning is incomplete until the downstream actuals can reconcile back to plan:

```text
PlannedQty
vs RequirementQty
vs ConsumedQty
vs ProducedQty
vs AcceptedQty
vs PostedQty
```

Use SPs/views for reconciliation rather than embedding these calculations only in screens.

> End of imported document.

## 49. Plan Vs Actual

**Source file:** `05_XMES_DOMAIN/plan_vs_actual.md`  
**File type:** `.md`  
**Size:** `5071` bytes  
**Modified on:** `2026-05-09 10:11:02`  
**SHA256:** `5be6d458ba87`  
**Encoding used:** `utf-8`

> Start of imported document.

### Plan vs Actual

#### Purpose

Compare what was planned (BOM-derived WO requirements) against what was actually consumed, for each Work Order.

Plan vs Actual is also the base for reconciliation and performance analytics. It should support work order, heat, cast, rolling, inventory, and SAP posting comparisons.

#### Data sources

| Source | Table | Role |
|---|---|---|
| Plan | `XMES_Work_Order_Requirement_Trn_Tbl` | BOM-derived planned quantities |
| Actual | `XMES_Consumption_Trn_Tbl` | Actual consumption records |
| WO | `XMES_Work_Order_Mst_Tbl` | Work Order context |

#### Current state of WO Requirement LV (confirmed 2026-04-28)

The live view `XStudio_List_XMES_Work_Order_Requirement_Trn_Tbl_Vw` contains:

Plan columns: `WorkOrderNumber`, `ID`, `RequirementSourceType`, `Name`, `CampaignID`, `WorkOrderID`, `MaterialCode`, `Material`, `RequirementSourceRefID`, `MaterialID`, `RequirementType`, `PlannedQty`, `UOM`, `YieldFactor`, `ScrapFactor`, `ConsumptionTolerancePct`, `SequenceNo`, `PlannedStartDate`, `PlannedCompletionDate`, `Remarks`

**Missing (not yet implemented):** actual execution columns.

#### Required Plan vs Actual SP

The Plan vs Actual view needs a custom SP (SP-backed LV). Specification:

```sql
CREATE OR ALTER PROCEDURE dbo.usp_XMES_WO_Plan_Vs_Actual
    @WOID nvarchar(36) = NULL
AS
BEGIN
    SELECT
        r.ID,
        r.WorkOrderID,
        wo.WorkOrderNumber,
        r.MaterialID,
        r.Name as Material,
        r.MaterialCode,
        r.RequirementType,
        r.PlannedQty,
        r.UOM,
        r.YieldFactor,
        r.ScrapFactor,
        r.ConsumptionTolerancePct,
        -- Actual consumption (aggregated)
        ISNULL(c.ConsumedQty, 0) as ConsumedQty,
        -- Variance calculations
        r.PlannedQty - ISNULL(c.ConsumedQty, 0) as RemainingQty,
        ISNULL(c.ConsumedQty, 0) - r.PlannedQty as VarianceQty,
        CASE
            WHEN r.PlannedQty = 0 THEN NULL
            ELSE ROUND(((ISNULL(c.ConsumedQty, 0) - r.PlannedQty) / r.PlannedQty) * 100, 2)
        END as VariancePct,
        -- Status
        CASE
            WHEN ISNULL(c.ConsumedQty, 0) = 0 THEN 'Not Started'
            WHEN ISNULL(c.ConsumedQty, 0) >= r.PlannedQty * (1 + r.ConsumptionTolerancePct/100) THEN 'Over Consumed'
            WHEN ISNULL(c.ConsumedQty, 0) >= r.PlannedQty * (1 - r.ConsumptionTolerancePct/100) THEN 'Within Tolerance'
            ELSE 'Partial'
        END as ConsumptionStatus,
        c.LastConsumedOn,
        r.PlannedStartDate,
        r.PlannedCompletionDate
    FROM XMES_Work_Order_Requirement_Trn_Tbl r
    JOIN XMES_Work_Order_Mst_Tbl wo ON wo.ID = r.WorkOrderID
    LEFT JOIN (
        SELECT
            WorkOrderID,
            MaterialID,
            SUM(ConsumedQty) as ConsumedQty,
            MAX(RecordedOn) as LastConsumedOn
        FROM XMES_Consumption_Trn_Tbl
        WHERE IsDeleted = 0
        GROUP BY WorkOrderID, MaterialID
    ) c ON c.WorkOrderID = r.WorkOrderID AND c.MaterialID = r.MaterialID
    WHERE r.IsDeleted = 0
      AND (@WOID IS NULL OR r.WorkOrderID = @WOID)
    ORDER BY r.SequenceNo;
END
```

#### Unplanned consumption

Consumption rows where `WorkOrderID` matches but no `XMES_Work_Order_Requirement_Trn_Tbl` row with the same `MaterialID` exist = unplanned consumption.

```sql
-- Identify unplanned consumption
SELECT c.WorkOrderID, c.MaterialID, SUM(c.ConsumedQty) as UnplannedQty
FROM XMES_Consumption_Trn_Tbl c
WHERE c.IsDeleted = 0
  AND NOT EXISTS (
    SELECT 1 FROM XMES_Work_Order_Requirement_Trn_Tbl r
    WHERE r.WorkOrderID = c.WorkOrderID AND r.MaterialID = c.MaterialID
  )
GROUP BY c.WorkOrderID, c.MaterialID;
```

#### Production actuals

For output/byproduct requirements, link production records via `WorkOrderID + MaterialID + RecordType = 'Output'`:

```sql
LEFT JOIN (
    SELECT WorkOrderID, MaterialID, SUM(Quantity) as ProducedQty
    FROM XMES_Production_Trn_Tbl
    WHERE IsDeleted = 0 AND RecordType = 'Output'
    GROUP BY WorkOrderID, MaterialID
) p ON p.WorkOrderID = r.WorkOrderID AND p.MaterialID = r.MaterialID
```

#### Reconciliation views and SPs

Add reconciliation layers as the MES matures:

```text
usp_XMES_Reconcile_WorkOrder_Actuals
usp_XMES_Reconcile_Heat_Balance
usp_XMES_Reconcile_Cast_Billet_Yield
usp_XMES_Reconcile_RM_Yield
usp_XMES_Reconcile_Inventory_Balance
usp_XMES_Reconcile_SAP_Posting_Qty
```

Core checks:

```text
Work order: PlannedQty vs RequirementQty vs ConsumedQty vs ProducedQty vs AcceptedQty vs PostedQty
Heat: MetallicChargeQty + AlloyQty + FluxQty -> LiquidSteelQty -> BilletQty -> AcceptedBilletQty
Rolling: ChargedBilletQty -> RolledQty -> PrimeQty -> ScrapQty -> FGQty
Inventory: CurrentQty = OpeningQty + Receipts - Issues - Transfers - Dispatches - Reversals
```

#### LV configuration for Plan vs Actual

```sql
-- Set the custom SP on the Plan vs Actual LV
UPDATE XStudio_LV_Mst_Tbl
SET SPName = 'usp_XMES_WO_Plan_Vs_Actual',
    FilterCondition = NULL,  -- filtering done by SP via @WOID parameter
    ModifiedBy = 'sa', ModifiedOn = GETDATE()
WHERE Name = 'List_XMES_Plan_vs_Actual';
```

## 50. Work Order Execution

**Source file:** `05_XMES_DOMAIN/work_order_execution.md`  
**File type:** `.md`  
**Size:** `4199` bytes  
**Modified on:** `2026-05-09 10:11:02`  
**SHA256:** `e1e96790de45`  
**Encoding used:** `utf-8`

> Start of imported document.

### Work Order Execution

#### Overview

Work Orders are the execution unit. Operators start a WO, record what was consumed and produced, and log process events. The execution layer consists of 3 transaction tables.

WO execution is the bridge between planning and the material digital thread. A WO should connect to equipment, job/operation, lot, quality, inventory, and workflow status.

#### Execution tables

| Table | Purpose |
|---|---|
| `XMES_Production_Trn_Tbl` | What was produced: quantities, grade, timestamps |
| `XMES_Consumption_Trn_Tbl` | What was consumed: materials, quantities, lots |
| `XMES_WO_Event_Trn_Tbl` | Process events: equipment states, operator actions |

These replace the legacy `XMES_Material_Production_Trn_Tbl` for new screens.

#### Navigation path

```
SMS WO List / RM WO List
  → link column "WO Production" → List_Page_XMES_Production_Trn_Tbl?WOID={ID}
  → link column "WO Consumption" → List_Page_XMES_Consumption_Trn_Tbl?WOID={ID}
  → link column "WO Events" → List_Page_XMES_WO_Event_Trn_Tbl?WOID={ID}

All three pages are tabs inside: Tab_Page_XMES_WO_Execution
Tab Container: 590CBC0D-CCA7-4AC8-A0AF-B4A3C086A480
```

#### Edit form canonical pattern (from Consumption — working reference)

The `Edit_XMES_Consumption_Trn_Tbl` is the reference. Any new execution edit form must match this pattern.

| Field | ControlType | ControlName | DefaultValue source |
|---|---|---|---|
| WorkOrderID | `selectbox` | `cmb_WorkOrderID` | `<request.WOID>` |
| MaterialID | `selectbox` | `cmb_MaterialID` | — |
| UOMID | `selectbox` | `cmb_UOMID` | — |
| ConsumedQty | `number` | `num_ConsumedQty` | — |
| RecordedOn | `datetimepicker` | `dtp_RecordedOn` | — |

**Key**: `WorkOrderID` is pre-populated from the URL via `DefaultValue = '<request.WOID>'`.

#### Production edit repair (2026-04-28)

The `Edit_XMES_Production_Trn_Tbl` had broken controls (textbox type for dropdowns, minimal ControlProperties). Fixed by `scripts/fix_production_edit_controls.sql`:

- Dropdown fields changed to `selectbox` with full `DropdownType='dynamic'` JSON
- `RecordedOn` → `datetimepicker` with `dtp_RecordedOn` naming
- All controls now have full renderer-safe JSON (3.5–4.9 KB)
- Zero postflight issues after repair

#### FilterCondition on execution LVs

All 3 execution LVs use the dual-mode filter:

```sql
(WorkOrderID='<request.WOID>' OR IIF('<request.WOID>'='',1,0)=1)
```

- From WO link → shows only that WO's records
- From nav menu (no param) → shows all records

#### Link columns on WO lists (confirmed 2026-04-27)

Added to both `List_XMES_SMS_Work_Order` and `List_XMES_RM_Work_Order`:

| Serial | Column Name | Target |
|---|---|---|
| 45 | WOProduction | `List_Page_XMES_Production_Trn_Tbl?WOID={ID}` |
| 46 | WOConsumption | `List_Page_XMES_Consumption_Trn_Tbl?WOID={ID}` |
| 47 | WOEvents | `List_Page_XMES_WO_Event_Trn_Tbl?WOID={ID}` |

#### Custom SPs

| SP | Purpose |
|---|---|
| `usp_SMS_Create_WorkOrders` | Creates SMS (LRF) WOs from campaign plan |
| `USP_XMES_Create_RM_WorkOrders_From_RM_Sequence` | Creates RM WOs from charge sequence |
| `USP_XMES_Create_WorkOrders_From_BOM_Exploded` | Creates WOs from BOM explosion |
| `XSTUDIO_WORKFLOW_*_SP` (×23) | Auto-generated status transition SPs |

#### Plant-specific execution extension

Do not create separate execution islands per plant. Plant-specific tables and SPs should extend core WO/job/event execution:

```text
SMS heat and cast actuals -> WO/job + lot/genealogy
RM billet charge and rolling actuals -> WO/job + lot/genealogy
CO/PP/DRI/BF production actuals -> WO/job + lot/genealogy
CPP generation actuals -> WO/job or utility performance model
```

Recommended SP responsibilities:

```text
validate WO status and equipment availability
record consumption/production/byproduct/delay
create or transform lots
link genealogy
update inventory balances
calculate yield/reconciliation
move workflow status when appropriate
```

#### Plan vs Actual

The `XMES_Work_Order_Requirement_Trn_Tbl` holds the BOM-derived plan. The actual execution comes from `XMES_Consumption_Trn_Tbl`. A Plan vs Actual view needs to join these with aggregation. See [plan_vs_actual.md](plan_vs_actual.md).

> End of imported document.

## 51. Xmes Domain Model

**Source file:** `05_XMES_DOMAIN/xmes_domain_model.md`  
**File type:** `.md`  
**Size:** `10365` bytes  
**Modified on:** `2026-05-09 10:10:50`  
**SHA256:** `fd9a7fc04c34`  
**Encoding used:** `utf-8`

> Start of imported document.

### XMES Domain Model

#### System context

XMES is an Integrated Steel Plant MES built on XStudio. It should model the full digital thread:

```text
Raw materials
-> Coke / Pellet / DRI / BF
-> SMS / EAF / LRF / VD
-> CCM / Strand / Billet
-> Reheating / Rolling
-> Bundle / Coil / FG lot
-> Dispatch / SAP posting
```

The design must use XStudio-native primitives:

```text
Asset Structure = equipment + historian + event + summary backbone
XMES transaction tables = production, quality, inventory, genealogy, SAP execution truth
XMES SPs = business interpretation, validation, transformation, posting, reconciliation
XStudio workflows = controlled lifecycle transitions
Views/LVs/pages = operational screens, passports, Sankey, Gantt, SPC, OEE/TEEP, reports
```

Do not design XMES as screens per table. Design it as material flow, time, quality, equipment state, and genealogy as one product.

#### Architecture by MES feature

| MES feature | XStudio design | Source of truth |
|---|---|---|
| Asset hierarchy | Asset Structure hierarchy, equipment types, templates, instances | Asset Structure |
| Historian data | Equipment tag mapping plus current/block tables | Asset Structure |
| Event detection | `<EquipmentType>_Event_*` tables and event actions | Asset Structure |
| Production execution | WO/job/consumption/production/delay/event tables | XMES |
| Plant-specific execution | CO/PP/DRI/BF/SMS/RM/CPP transaction tables | XMES |
| Quality | Samples, results, specifications, disposition, NCR, holds | XMES QM |
| Genealogy | Lot, lot event, genealogy, movement, balance | XMES inventory/genealogy |
| SAP posting | Posting queue/status/result SP chain | XMES integration |
| OEE/TEEP | Equipment states + delays + production + quality + summary | Asset Structure + XMES |
| SPC | QM result views + spec limits + summary | XMES QM |
| Sankey/mass balance | Standard flow views over lot/consumption/production | XMES views |
| Gantt | Sales order/campaign/heat/job planned vs actual views | XMES views |

#### Product creation sequence

Use this order for an integrated steel MES product:

```text
01 Asset Structure foundation
02 Equipment types + templates + instances
03 Tag attributes + historian mapping
04 Event definitions + event actions
05 XMES master/planning/execution/QM/inventory entities
06 Business SPs
07 Workflows
08 Summary definitions
09 Views/LVs/reports
10 Postflight validation
```

Asset Structure must be configured before execution tables depend on equipment. Equipment type creation is structural: it generates tables, triggers, views, current/block/historian objects, and optional event objects.

#### Asset Structure equipment backbone

Use Asset Structure for physical equipment that has tags, states, operating events, templates, or multiple instances.

Recommended event-enabled equipment types:

| Plant | Equipment types |
|---|---|
| CO | `CokeOvenBattery`, `CoalCrusher`, `CokePusher`, `QuenchingCar` |
| Pellet/DRI | `PelletIndurationFurnace`, `PelletBallingDisc`, `DRIKiln`, `ProductCooler` |
| BF | `BlastFurnace`, `HotBlastStove`, `CastHouse`, `HMLadle` |
| SMS | `EAF`, `LRF`, `VD`, `CCM`, `CCMStrand`, `Tundish`, `Ladle` |
| RM | `ReheatingFurnace`, `RollingMillStand`, `Shear`, `CoolingBed`, `BundlingMachine`, `WeighBridge` |
| CPP | `Boiler`, `Turbine`, `Generator`, `Compressor`, `Pump`, `Transformer` |

Passive reference lists remain normal XMES master entities: grade, material, UOM, reason code, defect code, customer, route, BOM, specification, shift, crew.

#### Planning to execution flow

```text
Sales Order
-> Production Order
-> Campaign
-> Campaign grade sequence
-> Campaign RM/SMS plan
-> BOM explosion
-> Work orders
-> Jobs/operations/requirements
-> Consumption/production/byproduct/delay events
-> Quality samples/results
-> Inventory lot/genealogy updates
-> SAP posting workflow trigger
```

Core planning entities include:

```text
XMES_Sales_Order_Mst_Tbl
XMES_Sales_Order_Dtl_Tbl
XMES_Production_Order_Mst_Tbl
XMES_Campaign_Mst_Tbl
XMES_Campaign_Dtl_tbl
XMES_Campaign_Grade_Seq_Tbl
XMES_Campaign_SMS_Plan_Trn_tbl
XMES_Campaign_RM_Plan_Trn_Tbl
XMES_Campaign_RM_Seq_Tbl
XMES_BOM_Mst_Tbl
XMES_BOM_Dtl_Tbl
BOM_Exploded
```

#### Core execution model

Work orders are the execution unit. Operators or event actions record actual consumption, production, byproducts, delays, and process events.

```text
XMES_Work_Order_Mst_Tbl
XMES_Work_Order_Requirement_Trn_Tbl
XMES_Job_Trn_Tbl
XMES_Consumption_Trn_Tbl
XMES_Production_Trn_Tbl
XMES_Byproduct_Event_Trn_Tbl
XMES_Delay_Event_Trn_Tbl
XMES_WO_Event_Trn_Tbl
XMES_Work_Order_Status_History_Trn_Tbl
```

Execution SPs must enforce lifecycle, requirement sync, production/consumption validation, status movement, and reconciliation. Do not bury these rules only in edit screens.

#### Plant-specific execution depth

Plant extensions should extend the core execution model, not duplicate generic WO/event ledgers.

##### CO / Pellet / DRI / BF

```text
Coal -> Coke batch
Iron ore / concentrate -> Pellet lot
Pellet / coal -> DRI lot
Coke + pellet + ore + flux -> Hot metal ladle
Hot metal ladle -> SMS heat
```

Expected SP families:

```text
usp_XMES_CO_Record_CokeBatch
usp_XMES_PP_Record_PelletLot
usp_XMES_DRI_Record_DRILot
usp_XMES_BF_Record_Tap
usp_XMES_BF_Create_HotMetalLadle
```

##### SMS / CCM

```text
Hot metal + DRI + scrap + alloy + flux
-> EAF heat
-> LRF/VD treatment
-> CCM cast
-> strand billet cuts
-> billet lots
```

Expected SP families:

```text
usp_XMES_SMS_Create_Heat
usp_XMES_SMS_Record_EAF_Tap
usp_XMES_SMS_Record_LRF_Treatment
usp_XMES_SMS_Record_VD_Treatment
usp_XMES_SMS_Start_Cast
usp_XMES_SMS_Record_CCM_Cast
usp_XMES_SMS_Generate_Billets_From_Cast
usp_XMES_SMS_Close_Heat
```

##### RM

```text
Billet lot
-> yard / direct hot charge
-> reheating furnace
-> rolling
-> cobble / cutback / scrap exceptions
-> bundle / coil / FG lot
```

Expected SP families:

```text
usp_XMES_RM_Charge_Billet
usp_XMES_RM_Start_Rolling
usp_XMES_RM_Record_Rolling
usp_XMES_RM_Generate_FG_Lots
usp_XMES_RM_Record_Cut_Bundle
usp_XMES_RM_Close_Rolling_Job
```

#### Genealogy and lot architecture

Use one common genealogy spine across all plants.

```text
XMES_Lot_Mst_Tbl
XMES_Lot_Event_Trn_Tbl
XMES_Lot_Genealogy_Trn_Tbl
XMES_Inventory_Balance_Trn_Tbl
XMES_Inventory_Movement_Trn_Tbl
XMES_Yard_Stock_Trn_Tbl
```

Canonical lot SP layer:

```text
usp_XMES_Create_Lot
usp_XMES_Record_Lot_Event
usp_XMES_Link_Genealogy
usp_XMES_Transform_Lot
usp_XMES_Split_Lot
usp_XMES_Merge_Lots
usp_XMES_Reverse_Lot_Event
usp_XMES_Recalculate_Lot_Balance
usp_XMES_Rebuild_Genealogy_For_Lot
```

Transformation patterns:

```text
One-to-one: HOT_METAL_LADLE -> HEAT
Many-to-one: CokeBatch + PelletLot + FluxLot -> HotMetalLadle
One-to-many: HEAT -> Billet1, Billet2, Billet3
Many-to-many: Billets -> Coil / Bundle / FG lot
```

Traceability must support backward and forward navigation:

```text
Finished bundle -> billet -> strand -> cast -> heat -> charge materials
Raw material lot -> affected heats -> billets -> bundles -> dispatches -> customers
```

#### Billet and bundle passport model

Billet tracking should capture the full lifecycle:

```text
Heat -> Cast -> Strand -> Billet
-> Yard / Direct Hot Charge
-> Reheating Furnace
-> Rolling
-> Cobble / Scrap / Bundle / Coil / FG Lot
-> Dispatch / Passport
```

Passport views should include:

```text
Order: sales order, production order, campaign, customer, grade, standard
SMS: heat, EAF/LRF/VD route, cast, CCM, strand, sequence, billet
Quality: chemistry, ladle/tundish/billet/RM results, usage decision, NCR/hold
RM: charge mode, furnace, rolling job, line, yield, cobble/scrap exceptions
Inventory: yard location, movement history, hold/release status
Dispatch: bundle, FG lot, dispatch order, SAP posting status
```

Suggested views:

```text
V_XMES_Billet_Digital_Passport
V_XMES_Bundle_Passport
V_XMES_Bundle_To_Billet_Genealogy
V_XMES_Dispatch_Bundle_Trace
```

#### Mass balance and Sankey model

Use views, not dashboard-specific tables, for Sankey and mass balance.

Standard Sankey columns:

```text
FlowDate
ShiftID
PlantID
AreaID
EquipmentID
SourceNode
TargetNode
MaterialID
MaterialStage
LotID
HeatNo
BilletNo
BundleNo
Quantity
UOMID
LossType
WorkOrderID
DrilldownKey
```

The plant-wide Sankey must show losses explicitly: coking loss, BF slag/gas, SMS slag, CCM crop loss, billet rejection, RHF scale loss, RM cobble, scrap, cutting loss, and dispatch shortages.

#### OEE / TEEP architecture

OEE/TEEP must be calculated from numerator/denominator facts, not averaged percentages.

Inputs:

```text
Asset Structure equipment states and event intervals
XMES production quantities
XMES delay/cobble/scrap events
QM accepted/rejected quantities
energy/fuel/steam/gas measurements where relevant
```

Core formulas:

```text
Availability = RunTime / PlannedProductionTime
Performance = ActualQty / TheoreticalQty, or ActualRate / StandardRate
Quality = GoodQty / TotalProducedQty
OEE = Availability * Performance * Quality
Loading = PlannedProductionTime / CalendarTime
TEEP = OEE * Loading
```

For plant/ISP rollup, recalculate from summed raw facts:

```text
Availability = SUM(RunTimeMin) / SUM(PlannedProductionTimeMin)
Performance = SUM(ActualQty) / SUM(TheoreticalQty)
Quality = SUM(GoodQty) / SUM(TotalProducedQty)
```

Do not average equipment OEE/TEEP upward.

#### Visualization view suite

Create canonical views that screens can drill from:

```text
V_XMES_Sankey_Integrated_SteelPlant
V_XMES_Genealogy_Graph
V_XMES_Product_Passport
V_XMES_Order_Gantt
V_XMES_Operations_Performance
V_XMES_OEE_Equipment_Shift
V_XMES_TEEP_Plant_Day
V_XMES_SPC_Quality
V_XMES_Exception_CommandCenter
```

Every screen should drill from one of these product surfaces:

```text
Sankey -> mass balance and losses
Genealogy graph -> traceability and root cause
Passport -> audit/customer documentation
Gantt -> planning/execution time
Performance -> OEE/TEEP/SPC/losses
```

#### Design rule

Every MES feature must land in one of four layers:

```text
1. Asset Structure detects equipment state, captures historian data, and runs event/summary logic.
2. XMES transactions store production, quality, inventory, genealogy, and SAP truth.
3. XMES SPs enforce domain rules and convert events/actions into MES records.
4. XStudio UI/views expose execution screens, passport, Sankey, Gantt, SPC, OEE, and reports.
```

> End of imported document.

### Working Example: Dropdown Attribute

#### Source

`MaterialID` on `XMES_Consumption_Trn_Tbl` → `XMES_Material_Mst_Tbl`  
Confirmed working as of 2026-04-28.

#### Attribute

```
Table:          XMES_Consumption_Trn_Tbl
Column name:    MaterialID
Physical col:   varchar(36)
DataType:       Dropdown (D313A2F3-9299-4AAB-9A5A-ACC8B055BDA9)
```

#### All 6 AttributeProperties rows

| Name | Value |
|---|---|
| `DropdownEntity` | `<XMES_Material_Mst_Tbl entity GUID>` |
| `DropdownDatabase` | `XStudio_XMES` |
| `DropdownTextField` | `[XMES_Material_Mst_Tbl].[Name]` |
| `DropdownValueField` | `<ID attribute GUID on XMES_Material_Mst_Tbl>` |
| `DropdownEntryMode` | `single` |
| `TextCharacterLength` | `36` |

#### EntityRelation row

```
EntityID          = <XMES_Consumption_Trn_Tbl entity GUID>
AttributeID       = <MaterialID attribute GUID>
Cardinality       = Many
RelationEntityID  = <XMES_Material_Mst_Tbl entity GUID>
RelationAttributeID = <ID attribute GUID on XMES_Material_Mst_Tbl>
RelationName      = MaterialID-XMES_Material_Mst_Tbl
RelationEntity    = XMES_Material_Mst_Tbl
RelationAttribute = ID
```

#### EditControl (canonical working pattern)

```
ControlType:  selectbox           ← not textbox
ControlName:  cmb_MaterialID      ← cmb_ prefix convention

Physical columns:
  DropdownType:        dynamic
  DropDownEntityID:    <XMES_Material_Mst_Tbl entity GUID>
  DropdownTextField:   [XMES_Material_Mst_Tbl].[Name]
  DropdownValueField:  <ID attribute GUID>
  DropdownDatabase:    XStudio_XMES

ControlProperties JSON:
  "DropdownType":        "dynamic"
  "DropdownDatabase":    "XStudio_XMES"
  "DropdownEntityID":    "<XMES_Material_Mst_Tbl entity GUID>"
  "DropdownTextField":   "[XMES_Material_Mst_Tbl].[Name]"
  "DropdownValueField":  "<ID attribute GUID>"
  "DropdownEntryMode":   "single"
```

#### LV column (shows material name, not GUID)

```
Header:      Material
AttributeID: <MaterialID attribute GUID>
RelationID:  <entity relation GUID>   ← required to show display text
```

#### Key lessons

1. Both physical columns AND JSON must be populated (renderer reads both)
2. `ControlType = 'selectbox'` not `'textbox'` or `'dropdown'`
3. `DropdownValueField` = the ID attribute GUID, not the entity GUID
4. `TextCharacterLength = '36'` for GUID FK columns
5. EntityRelation row needed for LV display text resolution

#### Key lessons

1. `ControlType = 'selectbox'` for dropdowns (not `textbox`, not `dropdown`)
2. Naming convention: `cmb_` for dropdowns, `dtp_` for datetime, `num_` for number, `txt_` for text
3. `DefaultValue = '<request.WOID>'` pre-populates parent FK from URL
4. Both physical columns AND JSON must contain dropdown config
5. Full ControlProperties JSON (never minimal stub)

## 54. Working Lv Link Example

**Source file:** `06_EXAMPLES/working_lv_link_example.md`  
**File type:** `.md`  
**Size:** `2379` bytes  
**Modified on:** `2026-04-28 14:44:12`  
**SHA256:** `dfe3b76cf0d5`  
**Encoding used:** `utf-8`

> Start of imported document.

### Working Example: LV Link Column

#### Source

`WOProduction` link column on `List_XMES_SMS_Work_Order`  
Confirmed working as of 2026-04-28.

#### Purpose

From the SMS Work Order list, operator clicks "Open" → opens the Production records for that specific WO.

#### Column definition

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| `Name` | `WOProduction` |
| `Header` | `WO Production` |
| `Type` | `link` |
| `LinkType` | `fixedtext` |
| `LinkValue` | `Open` |
| `CustomLink` | `XMES/DEFAULT/List_Page_XMES_Production_Trn_Tbl?WOID={ID}` |
| `CustomLinkTargetMode` | `Inside` |
| `IsCustomField` | `1` |
| `IsVisible` | `1` |
| `AttributeID` | `` (empty) |
| `RelationID` | `` (empty) |
| `RelationIDList` | `` (empty) |
| `Alignment` | `center` |
| `SerialNumber` | `45` |
| `ColumnProperties` | `{"IsConditionalFormatting":false,...}` |

#### Target page

`List_Page_XMES_Production_Trn_Tbl` — exists in `XStudio_Page_Mst_Tbl`, `IsPopup=0`.

#### FilterCondition on target LV

```sql
(WorkOrderID='<request.WOID>' OR IIF('<request.WOID>'='',1,0)=1)
```

- When arrived from WO link (`?WOID=<guid>`) → shows only production for that WO
- When arrived from nav menu → shows all production records

#### DefaultValue on target edit form's WorkOrderID field

```json
{ "DefaultValue": "<request.WOID>" }
```

Set on the `cmb_WorkOrderID` selectbox control. When Add is clicked in the filtered production list, the WorkOrderID is pre-selected.

#### Generated view column

After `XStudio_CreateViewForLV_Usp`, the `WOProduction` column in the view returns:

```json
{
  "Link": "XMES/DEFAULT/List_Page_XMES_Production_Trn_Tbl?WOID=<row_id>",
  "LinkTarget": "inside",
  "LinkValue": "Open",
  "RecordId": "<row_id>"
}
```

#### What broke before it worked

1. Initially `Type='varchar'` with a stale `AttributeID` — grid tried to read `WOExecution` column from entity table → view error
2. `AttributeID` had a real GUID — XStudio tried to join the relation → wrong data
3. `IsCustomField=0` — grid treated column as bound data
4. `ColumnProperties` was NULL — grid features broke

#### The fix sequence

1. Set `Type='link'`, `IsCustomField=1`, `AttributeID=''`
2. Set `ColumnProperties` to the required JSON
3. Set `LinkType='fixedtext'`, `LinkValue='Open'`
4. Set `CustomLinkTargetMode='Inside'`
5. Regenerate LV view
6. Verified: `SELECT TOP 1 WOProduction FROM ...Vw` returns JSON string

> End of imported document.

## 55. Working Parent Child Example

**Source file:** `06_EXAMPLES/working_parent_child_example.md`  
**File type:** `.md`  
**Size:** `2407` bytes  
**Modified on:** `2026-04-28 14:44:49`  
**SHA256:** `fabf9e60c54c`  
**Encoding used:** `utf-8`

> Start of imported document.

### Working Example: Parent-Child Navigation

#### Source

Work Order → Production/Consumption/WO Events  
Confirmed working as of 2026-04-27.

#### Pattern

```
List_XMES_SMS_Work_Order (parent LV page)
  ├── link col 45: WOProduction  → List_Page_XMES_Production_Trn_Tbl?WOID={ID}
  ├── link col 46: WOConsumption → List_Page_XMES_Consumption_Trn_Tbl?WOID={ID}
  └── link col 47: WOEvents      → List_Page_XMES_WO_Event_Trn_Tbl?WOID={ID}

Child LV FilterCondition (all 3):
  (WorkOrderID='<request.WOID>' OR IIF('<request.WOID>'='',1,0)=1)

Child edit DefaultValue on WorkOrderID field:
  <request.WOID>

Child edit AfterSavePageID:
  → returns to the filtered child list (keeps WOID context)

Child edit AfterSaveAndContinuePageID:
  → same edit page (opens fresh blank form, still in WOID context)
```

#### Why the dual-mode FilterCondition matters

```sql
(WorkOrderID='<request.WOID>' OR IIF('<request.WOID>'='',1,0)=1)
```

- Menu navigation → `?WOID=` is absent → `IIF('','',1,0)=1` → shows all records
- WO link navigation → `?WOID=<guid>` → `WorkOrderID='<guid>'` → shows only that WO's records

Without this pattern, the LV would either always require a WOID (breaking menu access) or never filter (losing drill-down focus).

#### URL parameter flow

```
1. User on SMS WO list page (no WOID param in URL)
2. Clicks "Open" link in WOProduction column of row X
3. Browser navigates to: List_Page_XMES_Production_Trn_Tbl?WOID=<row_X_ID>
4. Production LV FilterCondition substitutes: WorkOrderID='<row_X_ID>'
5. User clicks Add button on the production list
6. Edit form opens as popup — URL context still has ?WOID=<row_X_ID>
7. cmb_WorkOrderID DefaultValue = '<request.WOID>' → pre-selected
8. User fills other fields, clicks Save & Add Another
9. Fresh blank form opens — still in the same popup context with WOID
10. cmb_WorkOrderID again pre-populated
11. User clicks Save
12. Returns to List_Page_XMES_Production_Trn_Tbl?WOID=<row_X_ID> (filtered list)
```

#### The complete pattern for any parent-child flow

1. Parent LV: add link column(s) pointing to child list page with `?ParentParam={ID}`
2. Child LV: set `FilterCondition` with the dual-mode pattern
3. Child edit: set `DefaultValue = '<request.ParentParam>'` on FK field
4. Child edit: `AfterSavePageID` = child list page, `AfterSaveAndContinuePageID` = same edit page
5. Regenerate all LV views

> End of imported document.

**Encoding used:** `utf-8`

> Start of imported document.

### Working Example: WO Execution Tab Navigation

#### Source

WO Execution tab page — confirmed working as of 2026-04-28.

#### Architecture

```
Menu: "WO Execution" → Tab_Page_XMES_WO_Execution

Tab_Page_XMES_WO_Execution:
  PageControl: ControlType='tab', ControlID=590CBC0D-CCA7-4AC8-A0AF-B4A3C086A480

Tab Container: "WO Execution" (ID: 590CBC0D-CCA7-4AC8-A0AF-B4A3C086A480)
  ParentID: 29123B0B-3872-41CF-B5E1-67C4A8F0D4BC (ApplicationID)
  Tabs:
    - Consumption  → grid + edit for XMES_Consumption_Trn_Tbl
    - Production   → grid + edit for XMES_Production_Trn_Tbl
    - WO Events    → grid + edit for XMES_WO_Event_Trn_Tbl
    - Plan vs Actual → SP-backed LV (usp_XMES_WO_Plan_Vs_Actual)
```

#### Key IDs

| Object | ID |
|---|---|
| System ID | `6C8C0070-60B5-4474-BEC8-5C02197B32E6` |
| Application DEFAULT | `29123B0B-3872-41CF-B5E1-67C4A8F0D4BC` |
| Tab Container "WO Execution" | `590CBC0D-CCA7-4AC8-A0AF-B4A3C086A480` |

#### How WOID flows from WO list to execution tabs

1. SMS WO List page: user clicks "WO Production" link column
2. URL: `XMES/DEFAULT/List_Page_XMES_Production_Trn_Tbl?WOID={ID}`
3. The `?WOID=` is available on the Production list page
4. FilterCondition on Production LV: `(WorkOrderID='<request.WOID>' OR ...)`
5. Add button opens popup for `Edit_Page_XMES_Production_Trn_Tbl`
6. Popup inherits `?WOID=` from parent frame
7. `cmb_WorkOrderID` DefaultValue = `<request.WOID>` → auto-selected

#### Edit form repair summary

`Edit_XMES_Production_Trn_Tbl` was repaired by `scripts/fix_production_edit_controls.sql` to match the Consumption pattern:

- `WorkOrderID`, `CampaignID`, `MaterialID`, `UOMID` → `selectbox` with `DropdownType='dynamic'`
- Full renderer-safe JSON (~3.5–4.9 KB per control)
- `RecordedOn` → `dtp_RecordedOn` with `DateTimePickerOutputFormat='DD-MM-yyyy HH:mm:ss'`
- Zero postflight issues after repair

#### Consumption edit AfterSave configuration

```sql
AfterSavePageID                = <List_Page_XMES_Consumption_Trn_Tbl page GUID>
AfterCancelPageID              = <List_Page_XMES_Consumption_Trn_Tbl page GUID>
AfterSaveAndContinuePageID     = <Edit_Page_XMES_Consumption_Trn_Tbl page GUID>
IsSaveAndContinueButtonVisible = 1
SaveAndContinueButtonName      = 'Save & Add Another'
```

This allows operators to:
1. Click Save → return to filtered consumption list
2. Click Save & Add Another → fresh blank form (WOID still pre-populated)

#### LV views regenerated

After all configuration:
```sql
-- Regenerate each LV with the live XStudio_CreateViewForLV_Usp signature.
-- Current shared-config exports require @LvName, @DataDatabase, query fragments,
-- and @IsCalculatedView in addition to @LvID.
```

Verified: `SELECT TOP 3 * FROM XStudio_XMES.dbo.XStudio_List_XMES_Consumption_Trn_Tbl_Vw` returns rows.

{
  "meta": {

    "always_load": [
      "00_START_HERE/00_mental_model.md",
      "00_START_HERE/01_safety_rules.md"
    ],
    "deprecated_routes": {
      "old page/menu playbook": "Use 02_TASK_PLAYBOOKS/create_page_bind_controls_menu_playbook.md and 03_SQL_TEMPLATES/page_menu_template.sql instead."

### XStudio Knowledge Base

Multi-layer KB for XStudio platform configuration. Supports Haiku, Sonnet, Opus, and GPT-5.5.

#### Start here

**Every agent must load:**
1. [00_START_HERE/00_mental_model.md](00_START_HERE/00_mental_model.md) — one-page platform overview
2. [00_START_HERE/01_safety_rules.md](00_START_HERE/01_safety_rules.md) — **critical rules that prevent damage**
3. [00_START_HERE/02_task_router.md](00_START_HERE/02_task_router.md) — maps task → playbook

#### Retrieval pattern

```
Intent → task_router → safety_rules → canonical reference → playbook → SQL template/script → preflight → execution → postflight → repair path
```

Do not answer or generate SQL from memory when a playbook, script, or canonical reference already exists.

#### Structure

| Layer | Contents |
|---|---|
| `00_START_HERE/` | Mental model, safety rules, task router |
| `01_CANONICAL_REFERENCE/` | Database arch, object hierarchy, tokens, table reference, SPs |
| `02_TASK_PLAYBOOKS/` | Step-by-step SQL instructions per task |
| `03_SQL_TEMPLATES/` | Ready-to-run SQL templates and repair scripts |
| `04_DEBUGGING/` | Symptom → cause → fix guides |
| `05_XMES_DOMAIN/` | XMES steel plant domain knowledge |
| `06_EXAMPLES/` | Working confirmed examples from live system |
| `07_INDEXES/` | task_router.json, error_to_fix_map.json, table_to_feature_map.json, dependency_graph.json |

#### Model loading guide

| Model | Minimum load | Use for |
|---|---|---|
| Haiku | mental_model.md + task_router.json | Classify task, validate config, run checklist |
| Sonnet | safety_rules.md + playbook + SQL template + 1 example | Module generation, SQL drafts, debugging |
| Opus | mental_model.md + safety_rules.md + canonical refs + domain docs | Architecture, migration, hard debugging |
| GPT-5.5 | Playbooks first, reference second | Planning + execution |

#### Current consolidated stance (2026-05-09)

XStudio should be treated as a metadata-driven SQL execution platform. A working product such as XMES is not a set of tables; it is a complete stack:

```text
XStudio system/application
-> configuration metadata
-> generated or controlled physical data objects
-> LVs/views/forms/pages/menus/rights
-> workflows and action hooks
-> business SPs
-> postflight validation
```

For integrated steel MES work, use this product architecture:

```text
Asset Structure = equipment, hierarchy, historian tags, equipment events, block/history capture
XMES transactions = production, quality, inventory, genealogy, SAP execution truth
XMES SPs = business interpretation, validation, posting, transformation, reconciliation
XStudio workflow = governed lifecycle transitions
Summary/views/LVs = operational screens, Sankey, Gantt, SPC, OEE/TEEP, passports, reports
```

#### New in 01_CANONICAL_REFERENCE (2026-05-09)

The following files were added from the full SP dump (`XStudio_Configuration` DB, ~370 SPs, generated 2026-04-15) and new asset structure deep-dive docs:

| File | What it covers |
|---|---|
| `asset_structure.md` | Full Asset Structure object model, lifecycle, triggers, views, event schema, LMEL patterns |
| `asset_structure_deep_dive.md` | SP-level implementation detail for Asset Structure, from profiler + SP inspection |
| `asset_structure_api_surface.md` | Asset Structure REST API endpoints (including known URL misspelling) |
| `asset_structure_historian_blocks.md` | Block/historian tables: naming, row generation, ReportDate logic, recalculation |
| `asset_structure_runtime_views.md` | Generated union views: Equipment, Template, Tag Mst Vw |
| `asset_structure_triggers_and_mapping.md` | Trigger generator, template/instance/mapping sync behavior |
| `sp_catalog_complete.md` | Full SP catalog — all ~370 SPs organized by feature family with purpose and param counts |
| `dow_logsheet_feature.md` | DOW Digital Operator Workbench: logsheet, versioning, sections, parameters |
| `downtime_feature.md` | DownTime monitoring: config, tags, conditions, event log, full-replace save pattern |
